	public static class LayoutControlStrategyRegistrator
	{
		public static void RegisterFlowLayoutControlStrategy()
		{
			StrategyManager.Default.RegisterStrategy<FlowLayoutControl, ItemsControlStrategy<FlowLayoutControl, FlowLayoutControlWrapper>>();
		}

		public static void RegisterTileLayoutControlStrategy()
		{
			StrategyManager.Default.RegisterStrategy<TileLayoutControl, TileLayoutControlStrategy>();
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutControlWrapper : IItemsControlWrapper<FlowLayoutControl>, ITargetWrapper<FlowLayoutControl>
	{
		public FlowLayoutControl Target { get; set; }

		public object ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return Target.ItemsSource;
			}
			set
			{
				Target.ItemsSource = (IEnumerable)value;
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate ItemTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return Target.ItemTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				Target.ItemTemplate = value;
			}
		}

		public DataTemplateSelector ItemTemplateSelector
		{
			get
			{
				return Target.ItemTemplateSelector;
			}
			set
			{
				Target.ItemTemplateSelector = value;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutControlWrapper : FlowLayoutControlWrapper, IItemsControlWrapper<TileLayoutControl>, ITargetWrapper<TileLayoutControl>
	{
		public new TileLayoutControl Target
		{
			get

				return (TileLayoutControl)base.Target;
			}
			set
			{
				base.Target = value;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutControlStrategy : ItemsControlStrategy<TileLayoutControl, TileLayoutControlWrapper>
	{
		private DataTemplate tileItemTemplate;

		private DataTemplate tileContentTemplate;

		private DataTemplate GetTileItemTemplate()
		{
			if (tileContentTemplate == null)
			{
				FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ContentPresenter));
				frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, new Binding());
				frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(ContentPresenter.ContentTemplateSelectorProperty, ((BaseStrategy<TileLayoutControl>)(object)this).ViewSelector);
				tileContentTemplate = new DataTemplate
				{
					VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory
				};
				tileContentTemplate.Seal();
			}
			if (tileItemTemplate == null)
			{
				FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory2 = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Tile));
				frameworkElementFactory2.SetBinding(ContentControlBase.ContentProperty, new Binding());
				frameworkElementFactory2.SetValue(ContentControlBase.ContentTemplateProperty, tileContentTemplate);
				tileItemTemplate = new DataTemplate
				{
					VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory2
				};
				tileItemTemplate.Seal();
			}
			return tileItemTemplate;
		}

		protected override void InitItemTemplate()
		{
			if (((CommonStrategyBase<TileLayoutControl, TileLayoutControlWrapper>)(object)this).Wrapper.ItemTemplate == null && ((CommonStrategyBase<TileLayoutControl, TileLayoutControlWrapper>)(object)this).Wrapper.ItemTemplateSelector == null)
			{
				((CommonStrategyBase<TileLayoutControl, TileLayoutControlWrapper>)(object)this).Wrapper.ItemTemplate = GetTileItemTemplate();
			}
		}
	}

		{
			public static readonly DependencyProperty SourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Source", typeof(object), typeof(CurrentValueProvider), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnSourceChanged));

			public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Value", typeof(object), typeof(CurrentValueProvider));

			public object MetadataLocator => (Source as MetadataExtendedSource).Return((MetadataExtendedSource x) => x.MetadataLocator, () => (MetadataLocator)null);

			public object Source
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(SourceProperty);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(SourceProperty, value);
				}
			}

			public object Value
			{
				get
				{
					return GetValue(ValueProperty);
				}
				set
				{
					SetValue(ValueProperty, value);
				}
			}

			protected virtual void OnSourceChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
			{
				Value = (Source as MetadataExtendedSource).Return((MetadataExtendedSource x) => x.Source, () => Source);
			}

			private static void OnSourceChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (d is CurrentValueProvider currentValueProvider)
				{
					currentValueProvider.OnSourceChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
				}
			}
		}

		public event ValueChangedEventHandler<object> CurrentItemChanged
		{
			[CompilerGenerated]
			add
			{
				ValueChangedEventHandler<object> val = this.m_CurrentItemChanged;
				ValueChangedEventHandler<object> val2;
				do
				{
					val2 = val;
					ValueChangedEventHandler<object> value2 = (ValueChangedEventHandler<object>)(object)Delegate.Combine((Delegate)(object)val2, (Delegate)(object)value);
					val = Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref this.m_CurrentItemChanged, value2, val2);
				}
				while (val != val2);
			}
			[CompilerGenerated]
			remove
			{
				ValueChangedEventHandler<object> val = this.m_CurrentItemChanged;
				ValueChangedEventHandler<object> val2;
				do
				{
					val2 = val;
					ValueChangedEventHandler<object> value2 = (ValueChangedEventHandler<object>)(object)Delegate.Remove((Delegate)(object)val2, (Delegate)(object)value);
					val = Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref this.m_CurrentItemChanged, value2, val2);
				}
				while (val != val2);
			}
		}

			LayoutGroup.OrientationProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical));
		}

		public DataLayoutControl()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			GeneratedItems = new List<DataLayoutItem>();
			ValueProvider = new CurrentValueProvider();
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new DataLayoutControlController(this);
		}

		private bool TryFindItemName(DataLayoutItem item)
		{
			try
			{
				return ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).FindName(((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Name) == item;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected void ClearItem(DataLayoutItem item)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Name) && TryFindItemName(item))
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)this).UnregisterName(((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Name);
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Name = "";
			((DependencyObject)(object)item).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty);
			((DependencyObject)(object)item).ClearValue(LayoutItem.AddColonToLabelProperty);
			item.DataLayoutControl = null;
		}

		protected void ClearUI()
		{
			((PanelBase)this).DeleteChildren();
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).BeginUpdate();
			foreach (DataLayoutItem generatedItem in GeneratedItems)
			{
				((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).Remove((FrameworkElement)(object)generatedItem);
			}
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).EndUpdate();
			GeneratedItems.ForEach(ClearItem);
			GeneratedItems.Clear();
		}

		protected internal virtual DataLayoutItem CreateItem()
		{
			return new DataLayoutItem();
		}

		protected virtual LayoutGroupInfo CreateRootGroupInfo()
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected O, but got Unknown
			return new LayoutGroupInfo((string)null, (GroupView?)GroupView.Group, (Orientation?)base.Orientation);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement FindControlByName(string id)
		{
			return (FrameworkElement)(((object)base.FindControlByName(id)) ?? ((object)GeneratedItems.FirstOrDefault((DataLayoutItem x) => object.Equals(((FrameworkElement)(object)x).Name, id))));
		}

		protected internal DataLayoutItem GenerateItem(Type valueType, IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo, Func<DataLayoutItem> createItem = null)
		{
			DataLayoutItem item = createItem.Return((Func<DataLayoutItem> x) => x(), CreateItem);
			if (propertyInfo != null)
			{
				GenerateItemName(item, propertyInfo.Name);
			}
			item.Binding = CreateBinding(propertyInfo);
			((FrameworkElement)(object)item).SetBinding(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath("ValueProvider.Value"),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			((FrameworkElement)(object)item).SetBinding(LayoutItem.AddColonToLabelProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(AddColonToItemLabelsProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			OnAutoGeneratingItem(valueType, propertyInfo, ref item);
			if (item != null)
			{
				GeneratedItems.Add(item);
				item.DataLayoutControl = this;
			}
			return item;
		}

		private Binding CreateBinding(IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = ((propertyInfo != null) ? (propertyInfo.ContextObject as PropertyDescriptor) : null);
			Binding binding;
			if (propertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor dependencyPropertyDescriptor = DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(propertyDescriptor);
				binding = ((dependencyPropertyDescriptor != null && dependencyPropertyDescriptor.IsAttached) ? new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(propertyDescriptor)
				} : new Binding(propertyInfo.Name));
			}
			else
			{
				binding = new Binding(string.Empty);
			}
			binding.UpdateSourceTrigger = ItemUpdateSourceTrigger;
			return binding;
		}

		protected void GenerateItemName(DataLayoutItem item, string propertyName)
		{
			propertyName = propertyName.Replace(".", "_");
			propertyName = propertyName.Replace(" ", "_");
			((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Name = GetUniqueName("li" + propertyName);
			if (FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)(object)this) && FrameworkElementExtensions.HasNameScope((DependencyObject)(object)this))
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)this).RegisterName(((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Name, (object)item);
			}
		}

		protected void GenerateUI()
		{
			if (CurrentItemType.IsSimple())
			{
				GenerateUIForSimpleType();
			}
			else
			{
				GenerateUIForComplexType();
			}
			OnAutoGeneratedUI();
		}

		protected virtual void GenerateUIForComplexType()
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0054: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).BeginUpdate();
			RuntimeEditingContext context = new RuntimeEditingContext((object)this, (EditingContextTrace)null);
			IEnumerable<IEdmPropertyInfo> currentItemTypeVisibleProperties = GetCurrentItemTypeVisibleProperties();
			LayoutGroupGenerator val = new LayoutGroupGenerator(ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)(object)context), (Func<IModelItem, IModelItem>)((IModelItem x) => ((EditingContextBase)context).CreateModelItem((object)((LayoutGroup)x.GetCurrentValue()).CreateGroup(), (IModelItem)null)), (Action<IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem x)
			{
				OnAutoGeneratedGroup((LayoutGroup)x.GetCurrentValue());
			}, (Func<IModelItem, EditorsGeneratorBase>)((IModelItem x) => (EditorsGeneratorBase)(object)new DataLayoutControlGenerator(this, delegate(FrameworkElement y)
			{
				((Panel)(object)(LayoutGroup)x.GetCurrentValue()).Children.Add(y);
			})));
			EditorsSource.GenerateEditors(CreateRootGroupInfo(), currentItemTypeVisibleProperties, (IGroupGenerator)(object)val, (EditorsGeneratorBase)(object)new DataLayoutControlGenerator(this, delegate(FrameworkElement x)
			{
				((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).Add(x);
			}), GenerateEditorOptions.ForLayoutRuntime(), AutoGeneratedItemsLocation == DataLayoutControlAutoGeneratedItemsLocation.AvailableItems, true, (Func<IEdmPropertyInfo, ForeignKeyInfo>)null, false);
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).EndUpdate();
		}

		protected virtual void GenerateUIForSimpleType()
		{
			DataLayoutItem dataLayoutItem = GenerateItem(CurrentItemType, null);
			if (dataLayoutItem != null)
			{
				if (AutoGeneratedItemsLocation == DataLayoutControlAutoGeneratedItemsLocation.Control)
				{
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)dataLayoutItem);
				}
				else
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.AvailableItems).Add((FrameworkElement)(object)dataLayoutItem);
				}
			}
		}

		protected Type GetCurrentItemType(object currentItem)
		{
			return currentItem?.GetType();
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<IEdmPropertyInfo> GetCurrentItemTypeSupportedProperties()
		{
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0034: Expected O, but got Unknown
			object metadataLocator = ValueProvider.MetadataLocator;
			IEntityProperties val = (IEntityProperties)new ReflectionEntityProperties(TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(ValueProvider.Value).Cast<PropertyDescriptor>(), CurrentItemType, true, (IMetadataLocator)(metadataLocator as MetadataLocator));
			return val.AllProperties;
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<IEdmPropertyInfo> GetCurrentItemTypeVisibleProperties()
		{
			return EditorsGeneratorBase.GetFilteredAndSortedProperties(GetCurrentItemTypeSupportedProperties(), false, false, LayoutType.DataForm);
		}

		protected string GetUniqueName(string baseName)
		{
			string text = baseName;
			int num = 0;
			while (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).FindName(text) != null)
			{
				int num2 = num + 1;
				num = num2;
				text = baseName + num2;
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected virtual void OnAutoGeneratedItemsLocationChanged()
		{
			UpdateUI();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAutoGeneratedGroup(LayoutGroup group)
		{
			if (this.AutoGeneratedGroup != null)
			{
				this.AutoGeneratedGroup(this, new DataLayoutControlAutoGeneratedGroupEventArgs(group));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAutoGeneratedUI()
		{
			if (this.AutoGeneratedUI != null)
			{
				this.AutoGeneratedUI(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAutoGeneratingItem(Type valueType, IEdmPropertyInfo property, ref DataLayoutItem item)
		{
			if (this.AutoGeneratingItem != null)
			{
				DataLayoutControlAutoGeneratingItemEventArgs e = new DataLayoutControlAutoGeneratingItemEventArgs((property != null) ? property.Name : null, valueType, item);
				this.AutoGeneratingItem(this, e);
				item = e.Item;
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					item = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCurrentItemChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			bool flag = GetCurrentItemType(newValue) != GetCurrentItemType(oldValue);
			if (flag && AutoGenerateItems)
			{
				ClearUI();
			}
			ValueProvider.Source = newValue;
			currentItemChangedAction = delegate(bool itemTypeChanged)
			{
				if (itemTypeChanged)
				{
					UpdateUI();
				}
				if (this.CurrentItemChanged != null)
				{
					this.CurrentItemChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<object>(oldValue, newValue));
				}
			};
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).IsInitialized)
			{
				currentItemChangedAction(flag);
				currentItemChangedAction = null;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			if (currentItemChangedAction != null)
			{
				currentItemChangedAction(obj: true);
				currentItemChangedAction = null;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsReadOnlyChanged()
		{
			GeneratedItems.ForEach(delegate(DataLayoutItem item)
			{
				item.OnDataLayoutControlIsReadOnlyChanged();
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnAutoGenerateItemsChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
			{
				ClearUI();
				if (base.Root != null)
				{
					base.Root.ModelChanged(new LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs((DependencyObject)(object)this, "AutoGenerateItems", AutoGenerateItemsProperty));
				}
			}
			if (newValue)
			{
				UpdateUI();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemUpdateSourceTriggerChanged(UpdateSourceTrigger oldValue, UpdateSourceTrigger newValue)
		{
			UpdateUI();
		}

		protected void UpdateUI()
		{
			if (!AutoGenerateItems)
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = updateLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				ClearUI();
				if (CurrentItem != null)
				{
					GenerateUI();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnVisualChildrenChanged(DependencyObject visualAdded, DependencyObject visualRemoved)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnVisualChildrenChanged(visualAdded, visualRemoved);
			if (AutoGenerateItems && DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this) && !Locker.op_Implicit(updateLocker) && visualAdded is UIElement uIElement && !((PanelBase)this).IsInternalElement(uIElement))
			{
				UpdateUI();
			}
		}
	}
	public class DataLayoutControlController : LayoutControlController

		protected override bool ForwardDesignTimeInput => (((Controller)this).Control as DataLayoutControl).Return((DataLayoutControl x) => !x.AutoGenerateItems, () => false);

		public DataLayoutControlController(ILayoutControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}
	}

		[Description("Gets or sets whether the layout item’s editor is read-only.\nThis is a dependency property.")]

		[Description("Gets or sets the Binding object for the current DataLayoutItem.")]
		public Binding Binding
		{
			get
			{
				return _Binding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Binding == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value != null)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ElementName))
					{
						throw new NotSupportedException("Binding.ElementName is not supported.");
					}
					if (value.RelativeSource != null)
					{
						throw new NotSupportedException("Binding.RelativeSource is not supported.");
					}
				}
				_Binding = value;
				OnBindingChanged();
			}
		}

		protected Type BindingSourceType
		{
			get
			{
				string bindingPropertyPath = GetBindingPropertyPath();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(bindingPropertyPath) || !bindingPropertyPath.Contains(".") || Binding.Path.PathParameters.Count > 0)
				{
					return GetBindingSourceType(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContext);
				}
				object bindingSource = GetBindingSource(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContext);
				if (bindingSource == null)
				{
					return GetBindingSourceType(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContext);
				}
				return ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx.GetPropertyType(bindingSource, bindingPropertyPath) ?? GetBindingSourceType(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContext);
			}
		}

		protected internal DataLayoutControl DataLayoutControl
		{

					_DataLayoutControl = value;
					OnDataLayoutControlChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected IEdmPropertyInfo PropertyInfo { get; private set; }

		internal static bool IsPropertyHasDefaultValue(DependencyObject dObj, DependencyProperty property)
		{
			return DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dObj, property).BaseValueSource < BaseValueSource.Inherited;
		}

		private static bool IsPropertyHasDefaultOrInheritedValue(DependencyObject dObj, DependencyProperty property)
		{
			return DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dObj, property).BaseValueSource <= BaseValueSource.Inherited;
		}

		public static object GetUnderlyingObject(DependencyObject obj)

		}

		}

		[DXHelpExclude(true)]

		public DataLayoutItem()
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			UpdateIsActuallyReadOnly();
			UpdateIsActuallyRequired();
			ShowEmptyItemDesignTimeMessage();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContextChanged += delegate(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnDataContextChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
			};
		}

		protected override void OnVisualParentChanged(DependencyObject oldParent)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).OnVisualParentChanged(oldParent);
			if (((Visual)(object)this).VisualParent != null)
			{
				EnsureDataLayoutControlReference();
			}
			else
			{
				ClearDataLayoutControlReference();
			}
		}

		private void EnsureDataLayoutControlReference()
		{
			if (GetDataLayoutControl() == null && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetLayoutControl() is DataLayoutControl dataLayoutControl)
			{
				_DataLayoutControlReference = new WeakReference(dataLayoutControl);
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultOrInheritedValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty))
				{
					dataContextBindingExpression = ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ValueProvider.Value")
					{
						Source = dataLayoutControl,
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
				}
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, LayoutItem.AddColonToLabelProperty))
				{
					addColonToItemLabelsBindingExpression = ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(LayoutItem.AddColonToLabelProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("AddColonToItemLabels")
					{
						Source = dataLayoutControl,
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
				}
			}
		}

		private void ClearDataLayoutControlReference()
		{
			if (_DataLayoutControlReference != null)
			{
				_DataLayoutControlReference = null;
				if (dataContextBindingExpression == ((DependencyObject)(object)this).ReadLocalValue(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty))
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty);
				}
				if (addColonToItemLabelsBindingExpression == ((DependencyObject)(object)this).ReadLocalValue(LayoutItem.AddColonToLabelProperty))
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(LayoutItem.AddColonToLabelProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		internal DataLayoutControl GetDataLayoutControl()
		{
			return DataLayoutControl ?? (_DataLayoutControlReference.With((WeakReference x) => x.Target) as DataLayoutControl);
		}

		private bool HasCustomEditorAttribute()
		{
			Lazy<CommonEditorAttributeBase> lazy = PropertyInfo.Attributes.ReadAttributeProperty<CommonEditorAttributeBase, CommonEditorAttributeBase>(typeof(LayoutControlEditorAttribute), (Func<CommonEditorAttributeBase, CommonEditorAttributeBase>)((CommonEditorAttributeBase y) => y), (CommonEditorAttributeBase)null);
			object obj = lazy.Value.With((CommonEditorAttributeBase x) => x.TemplateKey);
			return obj != null;
		}

		protected virtual bool GetIsActuallyReadOnly()
		{
			if (PropertyInfo == null || (!PropertyInfo.PropertyType.IsEditable() && !HasCustomEditorAttribute()))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (GetDataLayoutControl().Return((DataLayoutControl x) => x.IsReadOnly, () => false))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				return true;
			}
			ReadOnlyAttribute readOnlyAttribute = ((BindingSourceType != null) ? BindingSourceType.GetAttribute<ReadOnlyAttribute>() : null);
			if (!GetAttributeSettings((DependencyObject)(object)this).IsReadOnly)
			{
				return readOnlyAttribute?.IsReadOnly ?? false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected void CheckIsActuallyReadOnlyChanged()
		{
			if (IsActuallyReadOnly != _IsActuallyReadOnly)
			{
				_IsActuallyReadOnly = IsActuallyReadOnly;
				OnIsActuallyReadOnlyChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsActuallyReadOnlyChanged()
		{
			UpdateIsActuallyRequired();
			if (base.Content != null)
			{
				UpdateContentValueBinding((FrameworkElement)base.Content);
				UpdateContentIsReadOnly((FrameworkElement)base.Content);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsReadOnlyChanged()
		{
			CheckIsActuallyReadOnlyChanged();
		}

		protected void UpdateIsActuallyReadOnly()
		{
			_IsActuallyReadOnly = IsActuallyReadOnly;
		}

		protected virtual PropertyDescriptor GetBindingProperty()
		{
			string bindingPropertyPath = GetBindingPropertyPath();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(bindingPropertyPath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			object bindingSource = GetBindingSource(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContext);
			if (Binding.Path.PathParameters.Count > 0)
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = Binding.Path.PathParameters[0] as PropertyDescriptor;
				return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(bindingSource)[propertyDescriptor.Name];
			}
			if (bindingPropertyPath.Contains("."))
			{
				return ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx.GetIsReady(bindingSource, bindingPropertyPath);
			}
			return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(bindingSource)[bindingPropertyPath];
		}

		protected string GetBindingPropertyPath()
		{
			if (Binding.Path == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return Binding.Path.Path;
		}

		protected object GetBindingSource(object dataContext)
		{
			return ((Binding != null) ? Binding.Source : null) ?? dataContext;
		}

		protected Type GetBindingSourceType(object dataContext)
		{
			return GetBindingSource(dataContext)?.GetType();
		}

		protected void UpdateUI()
		{
			if (Binding == null || BindingSourceType == null)
			{
				ClearUI();
			}
			else
			{
				GenerateUI();
			}
		}

		protected void GenerateUI()
		{
			//IL_0041: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (Binding != null && !(BindingSourceType == null))
			{
				PropertyDescriptor bindingProperty = GetBindingProperty();
				IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo;
				if (bindingProperty == null)
				{
					IEdmPropertyInfo val = (IEdmPropertyInfo)new EmptyEdmPropertyInfo(BindingSourceType);
					propertyInfo = val;
				}
				else
				{
					IEdmPropertyInfo val = (IEdmPropertyInfo)new EdmPropertyInfo(bindingProperty, DataColumnAttributesProvider.GetAttributes(bindingProperty, BindingSourceType, (IAttributesProvider)null), false, false);
					propertyInfo = val;
				}
				PropertyInfo = propertyInfo;
				SetAttributeSettings((DependencyObject)(object)this, DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings.Create(PropertyInfo));
				UpdateIsActuallyReadOnly();
				UpdateIsActuallyRequired();
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, LayoutItem.LabelProperty))
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(LayoutItem.LabelProperty, GetLabel());
				}
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty))
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty, GetToolTip());
				}
				if (base.Content == emptyItemDesignTimeMessage)
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(LayoutItem.ContentProperty);
				}
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, LayoutItem.ContentProperty))
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = CreateContentAndInitializeUI();
					UpdateContentValueBinding(frameworkElement);
					UpdateContentIsReadOnly(frameworkElement);
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(LayoutItem.ContentProperty, (object)frameworkElement);
				}
				else
				{
					UpdateUnderlyingObject(base.Content as FrameworkElement);
					DoApplySettingsForCustomContent();
					UnsubscribeEditorEvents(base.Content);
					SubscribeEditorEvents(base.Content);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void ClearUI()
		{
			PropertyInfo = null;
			UpdateIsActuallyReadOnly();
			UpdateIsActuallyRequired();
			Locker val = labelLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, LayoutItem.LabelProperty))
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(LayoutItem.LabelProperty);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
			if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty))
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty);
			}
			Locker val2 = contentLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, LayoutItem.ContentProperty))
				{
					UnsubscribeEditorEvents(base.Content);
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(LayoutItem.ContentProperty);
					_contentBinding = null;
				}
				FinalizeUI();
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val2)?.Dispose();
			}
			if (base.Content == null)
			{
				ShowEmptyItemDesignTimeMessage();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void FinalizeUI()
		{
			((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty);
			((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty);
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement CreateContentAndInitializeUI()
		{
			DataLayoutItemGenerator dataLayoutItemGenerator = new DataLayoutItemGenerator(this, allowGenerateContent: true);
			EditorsSource.GenerateEditor(PropertyInfo, (EditorsGeneratorBase)(object)dataLayoutItemGenerator, (Func<IEdmPropertyInfo, ForeignKeyInfo>)null, (IEnumerable<TypeNamePropertyPair>)null, false, false, false);
			return dataLayoutItemGenerator.Content;
		}

		private void DoApplySettingsForCustomContent()
		{
			if (ApplySettingsForCustomContent)
			{
				DataLayoutItemGenerator dataLayoutItemGenerator = new DataLayoutItemGenerator(this, allowGenerateContent: false);
				EditorsSource.GenerateEditor(PropertyInfo, (EditorsGeneratorBase)(object)dataLayoutItemGenerator, (Func<IEdmPropertyInfo, ForeignKeyInfo>)null, (IEnumerable<TypeNamePropertyPair>)null, false, false, false);
			}
		}

		private void OnEditorEditValueChanged(object sender, EditValueChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BindingExpression bindingExpression = ((FrameworkElement)((sender is BaseEdit) ? sender : null))?.GetBindingExpression(BaseEdit.EditValueProperty);
			if (bindingExpression != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding.UpdateSourceTrigger != UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged)
			{
				((DispatcherObject)(object)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetLayoutControl()?.RequestEditorValidation();
				}, DispatcherPriority.Background);
			}
			else if (DataLayoutControl == null || DataLayoutControl.EditorValidationOrder == EditorValidationOrder.PostAndValidate)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetLayoutControl()?.RequestEditorValidation();
			}
		}

		private void OnEditorLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DoValidate();
		}

		private void OnEditorLostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DataLayoutControl == null || DataLayoutControl.EditorValidationOrder == EditorValidationOrder.PostAndValidate)
			{
				editorLostKeyboardOperation?.Abort();
				editorLostKeyboardOperation = ((DispatcherObject)(object)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetLayoutControl()?.RequestEditorValidation();
				});
			}
		}

		private void OnEditorValidate(object sender, ValidationEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DataLayoutControl != null && DataLayoutControl.EditorValidationOrder != EditorValidationOrder.PostAndValidate)
			{
				bool isValid = DoValidate((BaseEdit)((sender is BaseEdit) ? sender : null), e.Value);
				if (DataLayoutControl.EditorValidationOrder == EditorValidationOrder.ValidateAndPostValidValue)
				{
					e.ErrorContent = GetValidationErrorText(e.Value);
					e.IsValid = isValid;
				}
				((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private bool DoValidate(BaseEdit editor, object value)
		{
			//IL_0039: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0043: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (editor == null || !((FrameworkElement)(object)editor).IsLoaded || PropertyValidator == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			BaseEditHelper.SetValidationError((DependencyObject)(object)editor, (BaseValidationError)null);
			string validationErrorText = GetValidationErrorText(value);
			bool flag = string.IsNullOrEmpty(validationErrorText);
			if (flag)
			{
				BaseEditHelper.SetValidationError((DependencyObject)(object)editor, (BaseValidationError)null);
			}
			else
			{
				BaseEditHelper.SetValidationError((DependencyObject)(object)editor, new BaseValidationError((object)validationErrorText));
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private string GetValidationErrorText(object value)
		{
			return PropertyValidator?.GetErrorText(value, GetDataLayoutControl().Return((DataLayoutControl x) => x.CurrentItem, () => ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).DataContext));
		}

			if (IsActuallyReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}

				return true;

		}

		}

		protected virtual void OnBindingChanged()
		{
			UpdateUI();
		}

				UpdateUI();
			}
		}

		}

		}

		protected void UpdateContentIsReadOnly(FrameworkElement content)
		{
			if (GetContentIsReadOnlyProperty(content) != null)
			{
				content.SetValue(GetContentIsReadOnlyProperty(content), IsActuallyReadOnly);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateContentValueBinding(FrameworkElement content)
		{
			UpdateUnderlyingObject(content);
			DependencyProperty contentValueProperty = GetContentValueProperty(content);
			if (contentValueProperty != null)
			{
				BindingExpression bindingExpression = content.GetBindingExpression(contentValueProperty);
				if (bindingExpression == null || bindingExpression == _contentBinding)
				{
					content.SetBinding(contentValueProperty, CreateContentValueBinding(IsActuallyReadOnly));
					_contentBinding = content.GetBindingExpression(contentValueProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateUnderlyingObject(FrameworkElement content)
		{
			content?.SetBinding(UnderlyingObjectProperty, CreateContentValueBindingCore(isReadOnly: true, allowValidation: false));
		}

		protected virtual DependencyProperty GetContentValueProperty(FrameworkElement content)
		{
			if (!(content is BaseEdit))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return BaseEdit.EditValueProperty;
		}

		protected virtual Binding CreateContentValueBinding(bool isReadOnly)
		{
			return CreateContentValueBindingCore(isReadOnly, allowValidation: true);
		}

		private Binding CreateContentValueBindingCore(bool isReadOnly, bool allowValidation)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			if (Binding.Source != null)
			{
				binding.Source = Binding.Source;
			}
			if (Binding.Path != null)
			{
				binding.Path = Binding.Path;
			}
			binding.Mode = (isReadOnly ? BindingMode.OneWay : BindingMode.TwoWay);
			binding.Converter = Binding.Converter;
			binding.ConverterCulture = Binding.ConverterCulture;
			binding.ConverterParameter = Binding.ConverterParameter;
			binding.FallbackValue = Binding.FallbackValue;
			binding.StringFormat = Binding.StringFormat;
			binding.TargetNullValue = Binding.TargetNullValue;
			binding.UpdateSourceTrigger = Binding.UpdateSourceTrigger;
			if (allowValidation)
			{
				binding.ValidatesOnDataErrors = true;
				binding.ValidatesOnExceptions = true;
				binding.NotifyOnValidationError = true;
				binding.NotifyOnSourceUpdated = true;
			}
			return binding;
		}

		protected override void OnLabelChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			base.OnLabelChanged(oldValue);
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this) || Locker.op_Implicit(labelLocker))
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = labelLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				if (base.Label == null)
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(LayoutItem.LabelProperty, GetLabel());
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(UIElement oldValue, UIElement newValue)
		{
			base.OnContentChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
			{
				if (Locker.op_Implicit(contentLocker))
				{
					return;
				}
				Locker val = contentLocker.Lock();
				try
				{
					if (base.Content == null)
					{
						GenerateUI();
					}
					if (base.Content == null)
					{
						ShowEmptyItemDesignTimeMessage();
					}
					return;
				}
				finally
				{
					((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
				}
			}
			UnsubscribeEditorEvents(oldValue);
			SubscribeEditorEvents(newValue);
		}

		internal override void OnEndDeserializing()
		{
			base.OnEndDeserializing();
			if (IsPropertyHasDefaultValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, LayoutItem.LabelProperty))
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(LayoutItem.LabelProperty, GetLabel());
			}
		}

		private void UnsubscribeEditorEvents(UIElement element)
		{
			//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0024: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			BaseEdit val = (BaseEdit)(object)((element is BaseEdit) ? element : null);
			if (PropertyValidator != null && val != null)
			{
				val.EditValueChanged -= new EditValueChangedEventHandler(OnEditorEditValueChanged);
				((UIElement)(object)val).LostKeyboardFocus -= OnEditorLostKeyboardFocus;
				((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Loaded -= OnEditorLoaded;
				val.Validate -= new ValidateEventHandler(OnEditorValidate);
			}
			editorLostKeyboardOperation?.Abort();
		}

		private void SubscribeEditorEvents(UIElement element)
		{
			//IL_0049: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0053: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0065: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bf: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00c7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00d1: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (Binding == null || BindingSourceType == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			PropertyDescriptor bindingProperty = GetBindingProperty();
			if (bindingProperty != null)
			{
				PropertyValidator = DataColumnAttributesExtensions.CreatePropertyValidator(bindingProperty, BindingSourceType);
				BaseEdit val = (BaseEdit)(object)((element is BaseEdit) ? element : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					val.EditValueChanged -= new EditValueChangedEventHandler(OnEditorEditValueChanged);
					val.EditValueChanged += new EditValueChangedEventHandler(OnEditorEditValueChanged);
					((UIElement)(object)val).LostKeyboardFocus -= OnEditorLostKeyboardFocus;
					((UIElement)(object)val).LostKeyboardFocus += OnEditorLostKeyboardFocus;
					((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Loaded -= OnEditorLoaded;
					((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Loaded += OnEditorLoaded;
					val.Validate -= new ValidateEventHandler(OnEditorValidate);
					val.Validate += new ValidateEventHandler(OnEditorValidate);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowEmptyItemDesignTimeMessage()
		{
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(LayoutItem.ContentProperty, (object)emptyItemDesignTimeMessage);
			}
		}

		internal void DoValidate()
		{
			UIElement content = base.Content;
			BaseEdit val = (BaseEdit)(object)((content is BaseEdit) ? content : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				DoValidate(val, val.EditValue);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx : ComplexPropertyDescriptorReflection
	{
		private class PD : PropertyDescriptorWrapper
		{
			public Type OwnerPropertyType { get; private set; }

			public PD(PropertyDescriptor dp, Type ownerPropertyType)
				: base(dp)
			{
				OwnerPropertyType = ownerPropertyType;
			}
		}

		public ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx(object sourceObject, string path)
			: base(sourceObject, path)
		{
		}

		public static PropertyDescriptor GetIsReady(object sourceObject, string path)
		{
			ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx complexPropertyDescriptorEx = new ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx(sourceObject, path);
			return ((ComplexPropertyDescriptorReflection)complexPropertyDescriptorEx).Last;
		}

		public static Type GetPropertyType(object sourceObject, string path)
		{
			if (!(((ComplexPropertyDescriptorReflection)new ComplexPropertyDescriptorEx(sourceObject, path)).Last is PD pD))
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (pD.OwnerPropertyType != null && pD.OwnerPropertyType.IsSubclassOf(((PropertyDescriptor)(object)pD).ComponentType))
			{
				return pD.OwnerPropertyType;
			}
			return ((PropertyDescriptor)(object)pD).ComponentType;
		}

		protected override PropertyDescriptor GetDescriptor(string name, object obj, Type type)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor descriptor = ((ComplexPropertyDescriptorReflection)this).GetDescriptor(name, obj, type);
			return (PropertyDescriptor)(object)new PD(descriptor, type);
		}
	}

			AttributesApplier.ApplyDisplayFormatAttributesForEditor(property, (Func<IModelItem>)(() => (!customizeEditor) ? null : edit), (Action<IModelItem, DependencyProperty, object>)null);
		}
	}

			object resourceContent = EditorsGeneratorBase.GetResourceContent<DataLayoutItem, BaseEditSettings, BaseEdit>(template);
			if (resourceContent is DataLayoutItem)

					DataLayoutItem dataLayoutItem2 = control.CreateItem();
					dataLayoutItem2.editorTemplate = template;
					return dataLayoutItem2;
				});
			}
		}

				break;
			case 2:
				filteringTemplate = GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.BooleanToggle);
				initializer = CustomizeInitializer(initializer, clearLabel: true, clearToolTip: true);
				break;
			case 4:
				filteringTemplate = GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.BooleanDropDown);
				initializer = CustomizeInitializer(initializer, clearLabel: true, clearToolTip: true);
				break;
			case 3:
				filteringTemplate = GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.BooleanList);
				initializer = CustomizeInitializer(initializer, clearLabel: true, clearToolTip: true);
				break;
			default:
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
			GenerateEditorFromResourcesCore(property, filteringTemplate, initializer);
		}

		public override void FilterBooleanChoiceProperty(IEdmPropertyInfo property, FilterBooleanEditorSettings settings, Initializer initializer)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override void FilterEnumChoice(IEdmPropertyInfo property, FilterEnumEditorSettings settings, Initializer initializer)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_0080: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			LookupUIEditorType editorType = ((FilterEditorSettingsBase<LookupUIEditorType>)(object)settings).EditorType;
			DataTemplate resource;
			switch ((int)editorType)
			{
			case 0:
			case 1:
				resource = (settings.UseFlags ? GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.EnumCheckedList) : GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.EnumList));
				break;
			case 2:
				resource = (settings.UseFlags ? GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.EnumCheckedDropDown) : GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.EnumDropDown));
				break;
			case 3:
				resource = (settings.UseFlags ? GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.EnumToken) : GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys.EnumList));
				break;
			default:
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
			GenerateEditorFromResourcesCore(property, resource, CustomizeInitializer(initializer, clearLabel: true, clearToolTip: true));
		}

		public override void FilterEnumChoiceProperty(IEdmPropertyInfo property, FilterEnumEditorSettings settings, Initializer initializer)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override void GenerateEditorFromResources(IEdmPropertyInfo property, object resourceKey, Initializer initializer)
		{
			//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			DataTemplate resource = GetResourceTemplate(resourceKey) ?? Owner.editorTemplate;
			GenerateEditorFromResourcesCore(property, resource, initializer);
		}

		private void GenerateEditorFromResourcesCore(IEdmPropertyInfo property, DataTemplate resource, Initializer initializer)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0058: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0062: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0062: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_004c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (resource != null)
			{
				object resourceContent = EditorsGeneratorBase.GetResourceContent<DataLayoutItem, BaseEditSettings, BaseEdit>(resource);
				if (resourceContent is BaseEditSettings)
				{
					IBaseEdit val = ((BaseEditSettings)resourceContent).CreateEditor((EditorOptimizationMode)0);
					GenerateEditor(property, ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)new RuntimeEditingContext((object)val, (EditingContextTrace)null)), initializer, customizeEditor: true);
				}
				else if (resourceContent is BaseEdit)
				{
					GenerateEditor(property, ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)new RuntimeEditingContext(resourceContent, (EditingContextTrace)null)), initializer, customizeEditor: true);
				}
				else
				{
					GenerateEditor(property, ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)new RuntimeEditingContext(resourceContent, (EditingContextTrace)null)), initializer, customizeEditor: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private Initializer CustomizeInitializer(Initializer initializer, bool clearLabel, bool clearToolTip)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			return initializer + new Initializer((Action<IModelItem, IModelItem>)null, (Action<IModelItem>)delegate(IModelItem container)
			{
				if (clearLabel)
				{
					ModelExtensions.SetValue(container, LayoutItem.LabelProperty, (object)null, true);
				}
				if (clearToolTip)
				{
					ModelExtensions.SetValue(container, FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty, (object)null, true);
				}
			});
		}

		private DataTemplate GetFilteringTemplate(FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeys key)
		{
			return GetResourceTemplate(new FilteringUIGeneratorThemeKeyExtension
			{
				IsThemeIndependent = true,
				ResourceKey = key
			});
		}

		private DataTemplate GetResourceTemplate(object key)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)(((object)Owner.GetDataLayoutControl()) ?? ((object)Owner));
			return (DataTemplate)frameworkElement.TryFindResource(key);
		}
	}
	public sealed class DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings
	{
		private class Settings
		{
			public string Label { get; set; }

			public string ToolTip { get; set; }

			public bool IsReadOnly { get; set; }

			public bool IsRequired { get; set; }

			public string NullText { get; set; }

			public bool ShowNullTextForEmptyValue { get; set; }

			public string DisplayFormatString { get; set; }
		}

		private static DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings empty;

		public static DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings Empty => empty ?? (empty = new DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings(null));

		public string Label { get; private set; }

		public string ToolTip { get; private set; }

		public bool IsReadOnly { get; private set; }

		public bool IsRequired { get; private set; }

		public string NullText { get; private set; }

		public bool ShowNullTextForEmptyValue { get; private set; }

		public string DisplayFormatString { get; private set; }

		public static DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings Create(IEdmPropertyInfo propertyInfo)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Settings settings = new Settings();
			RuntimeEditingContext context = new RuntimeEditingContext((object)settings, (EditingContextTrace)null);
			AttributesApplier.ApplyBaseAttributesForLayoutItem(propertyInfo, ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)(object)context), ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)(object)context), (Func<string, string>)null);
			AttributesApplier.ApplyDisplayFormatAttributesForEditor(propertyInfo, (Func<IModelItem>)(() => ModelExtensions.GetRoot((IEditingContext)(object)context)), (Action<IModelItem, DependencyProperty, object>)null);
			return new DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings(settings);
		}

		private DataLayoutItemAttributeSettings(Settings settings)
		{
			if (settings == null)
			{
				Label = null;
				ToolTip = null;
				IsReadOnly = false;
				IsRequired = false;
				NullText = null;
				ShowNullTextForEmptyValue = false;
				DisplayFormatString = null;
			}
			else
			{
				Label = settings.Label;
				ToolTip = settings.ToolTip;
				IsReadOnly = settings.IsReadOnly;
				IsRequired = settings.IsRequired;
				NullText = settings.NullText;
				ShowNullTextForEmptyValue = settings.ShowNullTextForEmptyValue;
				DisplayFormatString = settings.DisplayFormatString;
			}
		}
	}
	internal static class TypeExtensions
	{
		internal static Type GetNonNullableType(this Type type)
		{
			if (!type.IsNullable())
			{
				return type;
			}
			return type.GetGenericArguments()[0];
		}

		internal static bool IsEditable(this Type type)
		{
			return type.IsSimple();
		}

		internal static bool IsNullable(this Type type)
		{
			if (type.IsGenericType)
			{
				return type.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool IsSimple(this Type type)
		{
			type = type.GetNonNullableType();
			if (!type.IsPrimitive && !type.IsEnum && !(type == typeof(string)) && !(type == typeof(DateTime)) && !(type == typeof(decimal)))
			{
				return type == typeof(TimeSpan);
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal static class MemberInfoExtensions
	{
		internal static T GetAttribute<T>(this MemberInfo memberInfo) where T : Attribute
		{
			return (T)memberInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(T), inherit: true).FirstOrDefault();
		}
	}
