	public class LayoutControlCustomizationController
	{
		private Style _CustomizationControlStyle;

		private FrameworkElement _CustomizationToolbarElement;

		private Popup _CustomizationToolbarPopup;

		private Style _ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle;

		private Style _ItemParentIndicatorStyle;

		private Style _ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle;

		private Window _RootVisual;

		public FrameworkElement Control => ((Controller)Controller).Control;

		public Canvas CustomizationCanvas { get; private set; }

		public Style CustomizationControlStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _CustomizationControlStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (CustomizationControlStyle != value)
				{
					_CustomizationControlStyle = value;
					if (CustomizationControl != null)
					{
						((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationControl).Style = CustomizationControlStyle;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public ILayoutControl ILayoutControl => Controller.ILayoutControl;

		public Style ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle != value)
				{
					_ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle = value;
					if (CustomizationToolbar != null)
					{
						((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).Style = ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Style ItemParentIndicatorStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _ItemParentIndicatorStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ItemParentIndicatorStyle != value)
				{
					_ItemParentIndicatorStyle = value;
					if (ParentIndicator != null)
					{
						((FrameworkElement)(object)ParentIndicator).Style = ItemParentIndicatorStyle;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Style ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle != value)
				{
					_ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle = value;
					if (SelectionIndicators != null)
					{
						((ElementPool<LayoutItemSelectionIndicator>)SelectionIndicators).ItemStyle = ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public FrameworkElements SelectedElements { get; private set; }

		protected LayoutControlController Controller { get; private set; }

		protected LayoutControlCustomizationControl CustomizationControl { get; private set; }

		protected LayoutControlCustomizationCover CustomizationCover { get; private set; }

		protected LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar CustomizationToolbar { get; private set; }

		protected FrameworkElement CustomizationToolbarElement
		{
			get
			{
				return _CustomizationToolbarElement;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == Control)
				{
					value = null;
				}
				if (CustomizationToolbarElement != value && CanSetCustomizationToolbarElement(value))
				{
					FrameworkElement customizationToolbarElement = CustomizationToolbarElement;
					_CustomizationToolbarElement = value;
					if (customizationToolbarElement != null)
					{
						HideCustomizationToolbar(remove: false);
					}
					if (CustomizationToolbarElement != null)
					{
						ShowCustomizationToolbar(CustomizationToolbarElement);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected ILayoutGroup CustomizationToolbarElementParent => CustomizationToolbarElement.Parent as ILayoutGroup;

		protected LayoutItemParentIndicator ParentIndicator { get; private set; }

		protected Window RootVisual
		{
			get
			{
				return _RootVisual;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (RootVisual != value)
				{
					if (RootVisual != null)
					{
						RootVisual.Activated -= RootVisualActivated;
						RootVisual.Deactivated -= RootVisualDeactivated;
						RootVisual.LocationChanged -= RootVisualLocationChanged;
						RootVisual.StateChanged -= RootVisualStateChanged;
					}
					_RootVisual = value;
					if (RootVisual != null)
					{
						RootVisual.Activated += RootVisualActivated;
						RootVisual.Deactivated += RootVisualDeactivated;
						RootVisual.LocationChanged += RootVisualLocationChanged;
						RootVisual.StateChanged += RootVisualStateChanged;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected HorizontalAlignment? SelectedElementsHorizontalAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return GetSelectedElementsProperty((FrameworkElement element) => (element.Parent as ILayoutGroup).Return((ILayoutGroup x) => x.GetItemHorizontalAlignment(element), () => HorizontalAlignment.Stretch));
			}
			set
			{
				if (!value.HasValue)
				{
					return;
				}
				SetSelectedElementsProperty(delegate(FrameworkElement element, HorizontalAlignment alignment)
				{
					(element.Parent as ILayoutGroup).Do(delegate(ILayoutGroup x)
					{
						x.SetItemHorizontalAlignment(element, alignment, updateWidth: true);
					});
				}, value.Value);
			}
		}

		protected VerticalAlignment? SelectedElementsVerticalAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return GetSelectedElementsProperty((FrameworkElement element) => (element.Parent as ILayoutGroup).Return((ILayoutGroup x) => x.GetItemVerticalAlignment(element), () => VerticalAlignment.Stretch));
			}
			set
			{
				if (!value.HasValue)
				{
					return;
				}
				SetSelectedElementsProperty(delegate(FrameworkElement element, VerticalAlignment alignment)
				{
					(element.Parent as ILayoutGroup).Do(delegate(ILayoutGroup x)
					{
						x.SetItemVerticalAlignment(element, alignment, updateHeight: true);
					});
				}, value.Value);
			}
		}

		protected LayoutGroup SelectedGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1 && (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0] == Control || ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0].IsLayoutGroup()))
				{
					return (LayoutGroup)(object)((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0];
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		protected LayoutItemSelectionIndicators SelectionIndicators { get; private set; }

		public event EventHandler<LayoutControlSelectionChangedEventArgs> SelectionChanged;

		public LayoutControlCustomizationController(LayoutControlController controller)
		{
			Controller = controller;
		}

		public void CheckSelectedElementsAreInVisualTree()
		{
			if (SelectedElements != null)
			{
				List<FrameworkElement> list = new List<FrameworkElement>(((IEnumerable<FrameworkElement>)SelectedElements).Where((FrameworkElement element) => FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree(element)));
				((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Assign((IList<FrameworkElement>)list);
			}
		}

		public virtual IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			if (CustomizationCover != null)
			{
				yield return CustomizationCover;
			}
			if (CustomizationCanvas != null)
			{
				yield return CustomizationCanvas;
			}
			if (CustomizationControl != null)
			{
				yield return (UIElement)(object)CustomizationControl;
			}
		}

		public virtual FrameworkElement GetSelectableItem(Point p)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = Controller.GetItem(p, ignoreLayoutGroups: false, DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control));
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control) && frameworkElement is LayoutItem)
			{
				frameworkElement = ((LayoutItem)(object)frameworkElement).GetSelectableElement(UIElementExtensions.MapPoint((UIElement)Control, p, (UIElement)frameworkElement));
			}
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control) && frameworkElement == Control)
			{
				frameworkElement = null;
			}
			return frameworkElement;
		}

		public virtual void OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (CustomizationCover != null)
			{
				CustomizationCover.Measure(availableSize);
			}
			if (CustomizationCanvas != null)
			{
				CustomizationCanvas.Measure(availableSize);
			}
		}

		public virtual void OnArrange(Size finalSize)
		{
			if (CustomizationCover != null)
			{
				CustomizationCover.Arrange(((IPanel)ILayoutControl).ClientBounds);
			}
			if (CustomizationCanvas != null)
			{
				CustomizationCanvas.Arrange(((IPanel)ILayoutControl).ClientBounds);
				FrameworkElementExtensions.ClipToBounds((FrameworkElement)CustomizationCanvas);
			}
			if (CustomizationControl != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)CustomizationControl).Measure(RectHelper.Size(((IPanel)ILayoutControl).ClientBounds));
				((UIElement)(object)CustomizationControl).Arrange(((IPanel)ILayoutControl).ClientBounds);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void BeginCustomization()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			SelectedElements = new FrameworkElements();
			((ObservableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).CollectionChanged += delegate(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnSelectedElementsChanged(e);
			};
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control))
			{
				ShowCustomizationCover();
			}
			ShowCustomizationCanvas();
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control))
			{
				SelectionIndicators = CreateSelectionIndicators(CustomizationCanvas);
				((ElementPool<LayoutItemSelectionIndicator>)SelectionIndicators).ItemStyle = ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle;
				ShowCustomizationControl();
				if (((Controller)Controller).IsLoaded)
				{
					RootVisual = DependencyObjectExtensions.FindElementByTypeInParents<Window>((DependencyObject)Control, (DependencyObject)null);
				}
			}
			((ObservableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).CollectionChanged += OnAvailableItemsChanged;
		}

		protected internal virtual void EndCustomization()
		{
			((ObservableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).CollectionChanged -= OnAvailableItemsChanged;
			RootVisual = null;
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Clear();
			if (CustomizationToolbar != null)
			{
				HideCustomizationToolbar(remove: true);
			}
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control))
			{
				HideCustomizationControl();
			}
			SelectionIndicators = null;
			HideCustomizationCanvas();
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control))
			{
				HideCustomizationCover();
			}
			SelectedElements = null;
		}

		protected virtual Canvas CreateCustomizationCanvas()
		{
			return new Canvas();
		}

		protected virtual LayoutControlCustomizationControl CreateCustomizationControl()
		{
			return new LayoutControlCustomizationControl();
		}

		protected virtual LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar CreateCustomizationToolbar()
		{
			return new LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar();
		}

		protected virtual LayoutItemParentIndicator CreateParentIndicator()
		{
			return new LayoutItemParentIndicator();
		}

		protected virtual LayoutItemSelectionIndicators CreateSelectionIndicators(Panel container)
		{
			return new LayoutItemSelectionIndicators(container, ILayoutControl);
		}

		protected virtual void AddNewItem(FrameworkElement container)
		{
			if (container is ILayoutControlCustomizableItem { CanAddNewItems: not false } layoutControlCustomizableItem)
			{
				FrameworkElement element = layoutControlCustomizableItem.AddNewItem();
				ILayoutControl.InitNewElement(element);
				ProcessSelection(element, clearExistingSelection: true);
				Control.UpdateLayout();
				ShowCustomizationToolbar();
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool CanSetCustomizationToolbarElement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (element != null)
			{
				if (CustomizationControl != null && CustomizationControl.IsAvailableItemsListOpen)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (((Controller)Controller).IsDragAndDrop)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (RootVisual != null && (!RootVisual.IsActive || (!BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted && RootVisual.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized)))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void ChangeSelectedGroupPadding(bool isHorizontalChange, bool isIncrease)
		{
			Thickness padding = SelectedGroup.Padding;
			double num = (isHorizontalChange ? padding.Left : padding.Top);
			num = ((!isIncrease) ? (num - 1.0) : (num + 1.0));
			num = Math.Max(0.0, num);
			if (isHorizontalChange)
			{
				double left = (padding.Right = num);
				padding.Left = left;
			}
			else
			{
				double left = (padding.Bottom = num);
				padding.Top = left;
			}
			if (SelectedGroup.Padding != padding)
			{
				SelectedGroup.Padding = padding;
				Controller.OnModelChanged(new LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs((DependencyObject)(object)SelectedGroup, "Padding", LayoutControlBase.PaddingProperty));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DeleteAvailableItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			FocusControl();
			ILayoutControl.DeleteAvailableItem(item);
		}

		protected void FocusControl()
		{
			if (CustomizationControl != null && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationControl))
			{
				((UIElement)(object)CustomizationControl).Focus();
			}
		}

		protected virtual Geometry GetCustomizationCoverHitTestClip()
		{
			PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
			pathGeometry.Figures.Add(GraphicsHelper.CreateRectFigure(SizeHelper.ToRect(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize((FrameworkElement)CustomizationCover))));
			List<Rect> list = new List<Rect>();
			ILayoutControl.GetLiveCustomizationAreas(list, CustomizationCover);
			foreach (Rect item in list)
			{
				pathGeometry.Figures.Add(GraphicsHelper.CreateRectFigure(item));
			}
			return pathGeometry;
		}

		protected FrameworkElement GetCustomizationToolbarElement(Point p)
		{
			if (!((IPanel)ILayoutControl).ClientBounds.Contains(p))
			{
				return null;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)
			{
				if (item != Control && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree(item) && GetCustomizationToolbarElementBounds(item).Contains(p))
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetCustomizationToolbarElementBounds(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(element, Control);
		}

		protected virtual Point GetCustomizationToolbarOffset(Rect elementBounds, Size toolbarSize)
		{
			Rect rect = RectHelper.New(toolbarSize);
			RectHelper.AlignHorizontally(ref rect, elementBounds, HorizontalAlignment.Center);
			rect.Y = elementBounds.Bottom;
			return RectHelper.Location(rect);
		}

		protected virtual LayoutControlNewItemsInfo GetNewItemsInfo()
		{
			return new LayoutControlNewItemsInfo
			{
				new LayoutControlNewItemInfo(LocalizationRes.LayoutControl_Customization_NewGroupBox, LayoutGroupView.GroupBox),
				new LayoutControlNewItemInfo(LocalizationRes.LayoutControl_Customization_NewTabbedGroup, LayoutGroupView.Tabs)
			};
		}

		protected virtual void InitCustomizationControl()
		{
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_007b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0085: Expected O, but got Unknown
			CustomizationControl.AvailableItems = ILayoutControl.VisibleAvailableItems;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationControl).SetBinding(LayoutControlCustomizationControl.AvailableItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization")
			{
				Source = Control,
				Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			CustomizationControl.NewItemsInfo = GetNewItemsInfo();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationControl).SetBinding(LayoutControlCustomizationControl.NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("AllowNewItemsDuringCustomization")
			{
				Source = Control,
				Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationControl).Style = CustomizationControlStyle;
			CustomizationControl.DeleteAvailableItem += DeleteAvailableItem;
			CustomizationControl.IsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged += OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged;
			CustomizationControl.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop += delegate(object o, LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement> e)
			{
				StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(e);
			};
			CustomizationControl.StartNewItemDragAndDrop += delegate(object o, LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo> e)
			{
				StartNewItemDragAndDrop(e);
			};
		}

		protected virtual void FinalizeCustomizationControl()
		{
			CustomizationControl.IsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged -= OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged;
			CustomizationControl.AvailableItems = null;
			CustomizationControl.NewItemsInfo = null;
		}

		protected virtual void InitCustomizationToolbar()
		{
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).SetBinding(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar.AvailableItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization")
			{
				Source = Control,
				Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).Style = ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle;
			UpdateCustomizationToolbarValues();
			CustomizationToolbar.AddNewItem += delegate
			{
				AddNewItem(CustomizationToolbarElement);
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.ItemHeaderChanged += delegate
			{
				((ILayoutControlCustomizableItem)CustomizationToolbarElement).Header = CustomizationToolbar.ItemHeader;
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.ItemHorizontalAlignmentChanged += delegate
			{
				SelectedElementsHorizontalAlignment = CustomizationToolbar.ItemHorizontalAlignment;
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.ItemVerticalAlignmentChanged += delegate
			{
				SelectedElementsVerticalAlignment = CustomizationToolbar.ItemVerticalAlignment;
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.MoveItem += delegate(bool forward)
			{
				MoveItem(CustomizationToolbarElement, forward);
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.MoveItemToAvailableItems += delegate
			{
				MoveItemsToAvailableItems(SelectedElements);
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.ReturnFocus += delegate
			{
				FocusControl();
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.SelectItemParent += delegate
			{
				MoveSelectionToParent(CustomizationToolbarElement);
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.ShowItemParentIndicator += delegate
			{
				ShowParentIndicator(CustomizationToolbarElement);
			};
			CustomizationToolbar.HideItemParentIndicator += delegate
			{
				HideParentIndicator();
			};
		}

		protected virtual void MoveItem(FrameworkElement item, bool forward)
		{
			if (!(item.Parent is ILayoutGroup layoutGroup))
			{
				return;
			}
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((IPanel)layoutGroup).GetLogicalChildren(true);
			int num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).IndexOf(item);
			num = ((!forward) ? (num - 1) : (num + 1));
			if (num >= 0 && num <= ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count - 1)
			{
				UIElement element = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[num];
				((IPanel)layoutGroup).Children.Remove(item);
				num = ((IPanel)layoutGroup).Children.IndexOf(element);
				if (forward)
				{
					num++;
				}
				((IPanel)layoutGroup).Children.Insert(num, item);
				ILayoutControl.MakeControlVisible(item);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void MoveItemsToAvailableItems(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count == 1)
			{
				((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).Add(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[0]);
			}
			else
			{
				((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).BeginUpdate();
				try
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
					{
						((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).Add(item);
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).EndUpdate();
				}
			}
			ILayoutControl.OptimizeLayout(keepEmptyTabs: true);
		}

		protected virtual void MoveSelectionToParent(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).BeginUpdate();
			try
			{
				if (child != null)
				{
					if (child == Control)
					{
						return;
					}
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Clear();
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Add((FrameworkElement)child.Parent);
				}
				else if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count > 1)
				{
					FrameworkElement item = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count - 1];
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Clear();
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Add(item);
				}
				else if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1 && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0] != Control)
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0] = (FrameworkElement)((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0].Parent;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).EndUpdate();
			}
			ShowCustomizationToolbar();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAvailableItemsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> collection = ((IEnumerable<FrameworkElement>)SelectedElements).Where((FrameworkElement element) => !((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)ILayoutControl.AvailableItems).Contains(element));
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Assign((IList<FrameworkElement>)new List<FrameworkElement>(collection));
		}

		protected virtual void OnAvailableItemsListOpened()
		{
			CustomizationToolbarElement = null;
		}

		protected virtual void OnAvailableItemsListClosed()
		{
			ShowCustomizationToolbar();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnControlVisibilityChanged(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			if (control.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1)
				{
					Control.UpdateLayout();
					if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1)
					{
						ILayoutControl.MakeControlVisible(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0]);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Remove(control);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnGroupCollapsed(ILayoutGroup group)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)
			{
				if (DependencyObjectExtensions.FindIsInParents((DependencyObject)item, (DependencyObject)((IControl)group).Control))
				{
					ProcessSelection(((IControl)group).Control, clearExistingSelection: true);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnLayoutUpdated()
		{
			UpdateCustomizationCoverHitTestClip();
			if (SelectionIndicators != null)
			{
				SelectionIndicators.UpdateBounds();
			}
			if (ParentIndicator != null)
			{
				ParentIndicator.UpdateBounds();
			}
			UpdateCustomizationToolbarValues();
			UpdateCustomizationToolbarBounds();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnLoaded()
		{
			RootVisual = DependencyObjectExtensions.FindElementByTypeInParents<Window>((DependencyObject)Control, (DependencyObject)null);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedElementsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (CustomizationToolbarElement != null && !((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Contains(CustomizationToolbarElement))
			{
				CustomizationToolbarElement = null;
			}
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1)
			{
				ILayoutControl.MakeControlVisible(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0]);
				Control.UpdateLayout();
			}
			if (SelectionIndicators != null)
			{
				SelectionIndicators.Update(SelectedElements);
			}
			if (Controller.IsCustomization)
			{
				FocusControl();
			}
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(Control, new LayoutControlSelectionChangedEventArgs(SelectedElements));
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnTabClicked(ILayoutGroup group, FrameworkElement selectedTabChild)
		{
			if (!group.IsLocked)
			{
				ProcessSelection(selectedTabChild ?? ((IControl)group).Control, clearExistingSelection: true);
				ShowCustomizationToolbar();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void ProcessSelection(FrameworkElement element, bool clearExistingSelection)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).BeginUpdate();
			try
			{
				if (clearExistingSelection)
				{
					if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1)
					{
						((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0] = element;
						return;
					}
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Clear();
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Add(element);
				}
				else if (!((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Contains(element))
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Remove(Control);
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Add(element);
				}
				else
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Remove(element);
					if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control) && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 0)
					{
						((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Add(Control);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((LockableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).EndUpdate();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SetSelectedGroupItemSpace(double itemSpace)
		{
			if (SelectedGroup.ItemSpace != itemSpace)
			{
				SelectedGroup.ItemSpace = itemSpace;
				Controller.OnModelChanged(new LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs((DependencyObject)(object)SelectedGroup, "ItemSpace", LayoutControlBase.ItemSpaceProperty));
			}
		}

		protected void ShowCustomizationToolbar()
		{
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count != 0)
			{
				CustomizationToolbarElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0];
			}
		}

		protected void ShowCustomizationToolbar(Point p)
		{
			FrameworkElement customizationToolbarElement = GetCustomizationToolbarElement(p);
			if (customizationToolbarElement != null)
			{
				CustomizationToolbarElement = customizationToolbarElement;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ShowLayoutXML()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			using (XmlWriter xml = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, new XmlWriterSettings
			{
				Indent = true
			}))
			{
				ILayoutControl.WriteToXML(xml);
			}
			TextBox textBox = new TextBox
			{
				FontFamily = new FontFamily("Courier New"),
				FontSize = 12.0,
				IsReadOnly = true
			};
			textBox.KeyDown += delegate(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
			{
				if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
				{
					((Popup)textBox.Parent).IsOpen = false;
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			};
			textBox.LostFocus += delegate
			{
				((Popup)textBox.Parent).IsOpen = false;
			};
			textBox.Text = stringBuilder.ToString();
			textBox.SelectAll();
			Popup popup = new Popup();
			popup.Child = textBox;
			popup.Closed += delegate
			{
				FocusControl();
			};
			popup.IsOpen = true;
			textBox.Focus();
		}

		protected void UpdateCustomizationCoverHitTestClip()
		{
			if (CustomizationCover != null)
			{
				CustomizationCover.HitTestClip = GetCustomizationCoverHitTestClip();
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateCustomizationToolbarBounds()
		{
			if (CustomizationToolbarElement != null)
			{
				UpdateCustomizationToolbarBounds(CustomizationToolbarElement);
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateCustomizationToolbarValues()
		{
			if (CustomizationToolbarElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CustomizationToolbar.IsInitializing = true;
			try
			{
				UpdateCustomizationToolbarValuesCore();
			}
			finally
			{
				CustomizationToolbar.IsInitializing = false;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCustomizationToolbarValuesCore()
		{
			//IL_0051: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_013a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0144: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_018d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0197: Expected O, but got Unknown
			CustomizationToolbar.ItemHorizontalAlignment = SelectedElementsHorizontalAlignment;
			CustomizationToolbar.ItemVerticalAlignment = SelectedElementsVerticalAlignment;
			if (CustomizationToolbarElementParent.HasUniformLayout)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).SetBinding(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar.ItemMovingUIVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("AllowItemMovingDuringCustomization")
				{
					Source = Control,
					Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
				});
			}
			else
			{
				CustomizationToolbar.ItemMovingUIVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((IPanel)CustomizationToolbarElementParent).GetLogicalChildren(true);
			CustomizationToolbar.CanMoveItemBackward = CustomizationToolbarElement != ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[0];
			CustomizationToolbar.CanMoveItemForward = CustomizationToolbarElement != ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count - 1];
			CustomizationToolbar.ItemMovingDirection = CustomizationToolbarElementParent.VisibleOrientation;
			ILayoutControlCustomizableItem layoutControlCustomizableItem = CustomizationToolbarElement as ILayoutControlCustomizableItem;
			if (layoutControlCustomizableItem != null && !layoutControlCustomizableItem.IsLocked && layoutControlCustomizableItem.HasHeader && (layoutControlCustomizableItem.Header == null || layoutControlCustomizableItem.Header is string))
			{
				CustomizationToolbar.ItemHeader = layoutControlCustomizableItem.Header;
				((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).SetBinding(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar.ItemRenamingUIVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("AllowItemRenamingDuringCustomization")
				{
					Source = Control,
					Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
				});
			}
			else
			{
				CustomizationToolbar.ItemRenamingUIVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (layoutControlCustomizableItem != null && !layoutControlCustomizableItem.IsLocked && layoutControlCustomizableItem.CanAddNewItems)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).SetBinding(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar.NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("AllowNewItemsDuringCustomization")
				{
					Source = Control,
					Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
				});
			}
			else
			{
				CustomizationToolbar.NewItemsUIVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		protected T? GetSelectedElementsProperty<T>(Func<FrameworkElement, T> getElementProperty) where T : struct
		{
			T? result = null;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)
			{
				if (item != null && item != Control)
				{
					if (!result.HasValue)
					{
						result = getElementProperty(item);
					}
					else if (!result.Equals(getElementProperty(item)))
					{
						return null;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected void SetSelectedElementsProperty<T>(Action<FrameworkElement, T> setElementProperty, T value)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)
			{
				if (item != Control)
				{
					setElementProperty(item, value);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCustomizationCanvas()
		{
			CustomizationCanvas = CreateCustomizationCanvas();
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)CustomizationCanvas, 1000);
			((IPanel)ILayoutControl).Children.Add(CustomizationCanvas);
		}

		private void HideCustomizationCanvas()
		{
			((IPanel)ILayoutControl).Children.Remove(CustomizationCanvas);
			CustomizationCanvas = null;
		}

		private void ShowCustomizationControl()
		{
			CustomizationControl = CreateCustomizationControl();
			InitCustomizationControl();
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)(object)CustomizationControl, 1000);
			((IPanel)ILayoutControl).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)CustomizationControl);
		}

		private void HideCustomizationControl()
		{
			((IPanel)ILayoutControl).Children.Remove((UIElement)(object)CustomizationControl);
			FinalizeCustomizationControl();
			CustomizationControl = null;
		}

		private void ShowCustomizationCover()
		{
			CustomizationCover = new LayoutControlCustomizationCover();
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)CustomizationCover, 1000);
			((IPanel)ILayoutControl).Children.Add(CustomizationCover);
		}

		private void HideCustomizationCover()
		{
			((IPanel)ILayoutControl).Children.Remove(CustomizationCover);
			CustomizationCover = null;
		}

		private void ShowCustomizationToolbar(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (CustomizationToolbar == null)
			{
				CustomizationToolbar = CreateCustomizationToolbar();
				InitCustomizationToolbar();
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup = new Popup();
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.AllowsTransparency = true;
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.Placement = PlacementMode.Relative;
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.Child = (UIElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar;
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.PlacementTarget = Control;
			}
			else
			{
				UpdateCustomizationToolbarValues();
			}
			UpdateCustomizationToolbarBounds(element);
		}

		private void HideCustomizationToolbar(bool remove)
		{
			_CustomizationToolbarPopup.IsOpen = false;
			if (remove)
			{
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.Child = null;
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup = null;
				CustomizationToolbar.OnHide();
				CustomizationToolbar = null;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateCustomizationToolbarBounds(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			Rect bounds = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(element, Control);
			bounds.Intersect(((IPanel)ILayoutControl).ClientBounds);
			_CustomizationToolbarPopup.IsOpen = FrameworkElementExtensions.IsVisible(Control) && !bounds.IsEmpty;
			if (_CustomizationToolbarPopup.IsOpen)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).Measure(SizeHelper.Infinite);
				Point customizationToolbarOffset = GetCustomizationToolbarOffset(bounds, ((UIElement)(object)CustomizationToolbar).DesiredSize);
				PopupExtensions.MakeVisible(_CustomizationToolbarPopup, (Point?)customizationToolbarOffset, (Rect?)bounds);
			}
		}

		private void ShowParentIndicator(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			ParentIndicator = CreateParentIndicator();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)ParentIndicator).Style = ItemParentIndicatorStyle;
			CustomizationCanvas.Children.Add((UIElement)(object)ParentIndicator);
			ParentIndicator.LayoutControl = ILayoutControl;
			ParentIndicator.Element = (FrameworkElement)element.Parent;
		}

		private void HideParentIndicator()
		{
			CustomizationCanvas.Children.Remove((UIElement)(object)ParentIndicator);
			ParentIndicator = null;
		}

		private void OnAvailableItemsChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnAvailableItemsChanged(e);
		}

		private void OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (CustomizationControl.IsAvailableItemsListOpen)
			{
				OnAvailableItemsListOpened();
			}
			else
			{
				OnAvailableItemsListClosed();
			}
		}

		private void RootVisualActivated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ShowCustomizationToolbar();
		}

		private void RootVisualDeactivated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			FocusControl();
			CustomizationToolbarElement = null;
		}

		private void RootVisualLocationChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateCustomizationToolbarBounds();
			if (_CustomizationToolbarPopup != null)
			{
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.HorizontalOffset += 1.0;
				_CustomizationToolbarPopup.HorizontalOffset -= 1.0;
			}
		}

		private void RootVisualStateChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (RootVisual.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized)
			{
				CustomizationToolbarElement = null;
			}
			else
			{
				ShowCustomizationToolbar();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnKeyDown(DXKeyEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Key)
			{
			case Key.Escape:
				MoveSelectionToParent(null);
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			case Key.D0:
			case Key.D1:
			case Key.D2:
			case Key.D3:
			case Key.D4:
			case Key.D5:
			case Key.D6:
			case Key.D7:
			case Key.D8:
			case Key.D9:
				if (SelectedGroup != null && !SelectedGroup.IsLocked)
				{
					SetSelectedGroupItemSpace((double)(e.Key - 34));
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.Left:
			case Key.Up:
			case Key.Right:
			case Key.Down:
				if (SelectedGroup != null && !SelectedGroup.IsLocked)
				{
					ChangeSelectedGroupPadding(e.Key == Key.Left || e.Key == Key.Right, e.Key == Key.Right || e.Key == Key.Up);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.F2:
				if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)Control))
				{
					ShowLayoutXML();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMouseMove(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Controller.MouseCaptureOwner == null)
			{
				ShowCustomizationToolbar(e.GetPosition((UIElement)Control));
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ProcessSelection(GetSelectableItem(((DXMouseEventArgs)e).GetPosition((UIElement)Control)), Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.None);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ShowCustomizationToolbar(((DXMouseEventArgs)e).GetPosition((UIElement)Control));
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMouseRightButtonDown(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			FrameworkElement selectableItem = GetSelectableItem(((DXMouseEventArgs)e).GetPosition((UIElement)Control));
			if (!((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Contains(selectableItem))
			{
				ProcessSelection(selectableItem, clearExistingSelection: true);
			}
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement CreateNewItem(LayoutControlNewItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			if (!(itemInfo.Data is LayoutGroupView))
			{
				return null;
			}
			LayoutGroup layoutGroup = ILayoutControl.CreateGroup();
			layoutGroup.Header = itemInfo.Label;
			layoutGroup.View = (LayoutGroupView)itemInfo.Data;
			InitNewItem(layoutGroup);
			return (FrameworkElement)(object)layoutGroup;
		}

		protected virtual void InitNewItem(LayoutGroup group)
		{
			if (group.View != LayoutGroupView.Tabs)
			{
				return;
			}
			((Panel)(object)group).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)ILayoutControl.CreateGroup());
			((Panel)(object)group).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)ILayoutControl.CreateGroup());
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)group).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				ILayoutControl.InitNewElement(item);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDropElement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			ProcessSelection(element, clearExistingSelection: true);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnStartDragAndDrop()
		{
			if (SelectionIndicators != null)
			{
				SelectionIndicators.IsVisible = false;
			}
			CustomizationToolbarElement = null;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnEndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			Control.UpdateLayout();
			if (accept && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements).Count == 1)
			{
				ILayoutControl.MakeControlVisible(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)SelectedElements)[0]);
			}
			if (SelectionIndicators != null)
			{
				SelectionIndicators.IsVisible = true;
			}
			ShowCustomizationToolbar();
			FocusControl();
		}

		protected void StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement> arguments)
		{
			Controller.StartItemDragAndDrop(arguments.Data, arguments.MouseEventArgs, arguments.Source);
		}

		protected void StartNewItemDragAndDrop(LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo> arguments)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = CreateNewItem(arguments.Data);
			if (frameworkElement != null)
			{
				ILayoutControl.InitNewElement(frameworkElement);
				Controller.StartItemDragAndDrop(frameworkElement, arguments.MouseEventArgs, arguments.Source);
			}
		}
	}
	public class LayoutControlCustomizationCover : Canvas
	{
		public Geometry HitTestClip { get; set; }

		public LayoutControlCustomizationCover()
		{
			base.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Transparent);
		}

		protected override HitTestResult HitTestCore(PointHitTestParameters hitTestParameters)
		{
			if (HitTestClip == null || HitTestClip.FillContains(hitTestParameters.HitPoint))
			{
				return base.HitTestCore(hitTestParameters);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "LeftSizingElement", Type = typeof(ElementSizer))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "RightSizingElement", Type = typeof(ElementSizer))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TopSizingElement", Type = typeof(ElementSizer))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "BottomSizingElement", Type = typeof(ElementSizer))]
	public class LayoutItemSelectionIndicator : ControlBase
	{
		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowSizingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ElementProperty;

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ElementSizeProperty;

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsSizingProperty;

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsHorizontalSizingProperty;

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsVerticalSizingProperty;

		private ILayoutControl _LayoutControl;

		private const string LeftSizingElementName = "LeftSizingElement";

		private const string RightSizingElementName = "RightSizingElement";

		private const string TopSizingElementName = "TopSizingElement";

		private const string BottomSizingElementName = "BottomSizingElement";

		public bool AllowSizing => (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(AllowSizingProperty);

		public FrameworkElement Element
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ElementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ElementProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public double ElementSize
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ElementSizeProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ElementSizeProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsSizing
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(IsSizingProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(IsSizingProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsHorizontalSizing
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(IsHorizontalSizingProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(IsHorizontalSizingProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsVerticalSizing
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(IsVerticalSizingProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(IsVerticalSizingProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public ILayoutControl LayoutControl
		{
			get
			{
				return _LayoutControl;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (LayoutControl != value)
				{
					_LayoutControl = value;
					SetAllowSizing();
				}
			}
		}

		protected ElementSizer LeftSizingElement { get; private set; }

		protected ElementSizer RightSizingElement { get; private set; }

		protected ElementSizer TopSizingElement { get; private set; }

		protected ElementSizer BottomSizingElement { get; private set; }

		static LayoutItemSelectionIndicator()
		{
			AllowSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator), null);
			ElementProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Element", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItemSelectionIndicator)(object)o).OnElementChanged();
			}));
			ElementSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ElementSize", typeof(object), typeof(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator), null);
			IsSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator), null);
			IsHorizontalSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsHorizontalSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator), null);
			IsVerticalSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsVerticalSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemSelectionIndicator>();
		}

		public void UpdateBounds()
		{
			if (Element != null && ((FrameworkElement)this).Parent != null)
			{
				FrameworkElementExtensions.SetBounds((FrameworkElement)(object)this, CalculateBounds());
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			DetachEventHandlers(LeftSizingElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(RightSizingElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(TopSizingElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(BottomSizingElement);
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			LeftSizingElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("LeftSizingElement") as ElementSizer;
			RightSizingElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("RightSizingElement") as ElementSizer;
			TopSizingElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("TopSizingElement") as ElementSizer;
			BottomSizingElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("BottomSizingElement") as ElementSizer;
			AttachEventHandlers(LeftSizingElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(RightSizingElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(TopSizingElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(BottomSizingElement);
		}

		private void AttachEventHandlers(ElementSizer sizer)
		{
			if (sizer != null)
			{
				sizer.ElementSizeChanging += OnElementSizeChanging;
				sizer.IsSizingChanged += OnIsSizingChanged;
			}
		}

		private void DetachEventHandlers(ElementSizer sizer)
		{
			if (sizer != null)
			{
				sizer.ElementSizeChanging -= OnElementSizeChanging;
				sizer.IsSizingChanged -= OnIsSizingChanged;
			}
		}

		private void OnElementSizeChanging(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnElementSizeChanging(((ElementSizer)sender).ElementSize);
		}

		private void OnIsSizingChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ElementSizer elementSizer = (ElementSizer)sender;
			OnIsSizingChanged(elementSizer.IsSizing, elementSizer.Side, elementSizer.ElementSize);
		}

		protected virtual Rect CalculateBounds()
		{
			if (Element == LayoutControl)
			{
				return UIElementExtensions.MapRect((UIElement)Element, ((IPanel)(ILayoutControl)Element).ContentBounds, (UIElement)(FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).Parent);
			}
			return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetVisualBounds(Element, (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).Parent, false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementChanged()
		{
			SetAllowSizing();
			UpdateBounds();
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementSizeChanging(double elementSize)
		{
			ElementSize = elementSize;
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsSizingChanged(bool isSizing, Side side, double elementSize)
		{
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			IsSizing = isSizing;
			IsHorizontalSizing = isSizing && ((int)side == 0 || (int)side == 2);
			IsVerticalSizing = isSizing && ((int)side == 1 || (int)side == 4);
			ElementSize = elementSize;
		}

		protected virtual void SetAllowSizing()
		{
			if (LayoutControl != null && Element != LayoutControl)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(AllowSizingProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("AllowItemSizingDuringCustomization")
				{
					Source = LayoutControl
				});
			}
			else
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).ClearValue(AllowSizingProperty);
			}
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemSelectionIndicators : ElementPool<LayoutItemSelectionIndicator>
	{
		private bool _IsVisible = true;

		public bool IsVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (IsVisible != value)
				{
					_IsVisible = value;
					base.Items.ForEach(delegate(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator item)
					{
						UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)(object)item, value);
					});
					if (IsVisible)
					{
						UpdateBounds();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public ILayoutControl LayoutControl { get; private set; }

		public LayoutItemSelectionIndicators(Panel container, ILayoutControl layoutControl)
			: base(container)
		{
			LayoutControl = layoutControl;
		}

		public void Update(FrameworkElements selectedElements)
		{
			base.MarkItemsAsUnused();
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)selectedElements)
			{
				base.Add().Element = item;
			}
			base.DeleteUnusedItems();
		}

		public void UpdateBounds()
		{
			if (IsVisible)
			{
				base.Items.ForEach(delegate(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator item)
				{
					item.UpdateBounds();
				});
			}
		}

		protected override LayoutItemSelectionIndicator CreateItem()
		{
			LayoutItemSelectionIndicator layoutItemSelectionIndicator = base.CreateItem();
			layoutItemSelectionIndicator.LayoutControl = LayoutControl;
			UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)(object)layoutItemSelectionIndicator, IsVisible);
			return layoutItemSelectionIndicator;
		}

		protected override void DeleteItem(LayoutItemSelectionIndicator item)
		{
			item.LayoutControl = null;
			base.DeleteItem(item);
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemParentIndicator : LayoutItemSelectionIndicator
	{
		static LayoutItemParentIndicator()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemParentIndicator>();
		}
	}

			{
				((LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar)(object)o).OnItemHeaderChanged();

			ItemMovingDirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemMovingDirection", typeof(Orientation), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{

			ItemMovingForwardDirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemMovingForwardDirection", typeof(Side), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar), null);
			ItemMovingUIVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemMovingUIVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar), null);
			ItemRenamingUIVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemRenamingUIVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar), null);
			NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewItemsUIVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar>();
		}

		public LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar()
		{
			OnItemMovingDirectionChanged();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (AddNewItemElement != null)
			{
				AddNewItemElement.Click -= OnAddNewItemElementClick;
			}
			if (HorizontalAlignmentElement != null)
			{
				HorizontalAlignmentElement.AlignmentChanged -= OnHorizontalAlignmentElementAlignmentChanged;
			}
			if (VerticalAlignmentElement != null)
			{
				VerticalAlignmentElement.AlignmentChanged -= OnVerticalAlignmentElementAlignmentChanged;
			}
			if (MoveItemBackwardElement != null)
			{
				MoveItemBackwardElement.Click -= OnMoveItemBackwardElementClick;
			}
			if (MoveItemForwardElement != null)
			{
				MoveItemForwardElement.Click -= OnMoveItemForwardElementClick;
			}
			if (MoveToAvailableItemsElement != null)
			{
				MoveToAvailableItemsElement.Click -= OnMoveToAvailableItemsElementClick;
			}
			if (RenameElement != null)
			{
				RenameElement.Click -= OnRenameElementClick;
			}
			if (RenamingEditElement != null)
			{
				RenamingEditElement.KeyDown -= OnRenamingEditElementKeyDown;
				RenamingEditElement.LostFocus -= OnRenamingEditElementLostFocus;
				RenamingEditElement.TextChanged -= OnRenamingEditElementTextChanged;
			}
			if (SelectParentElement != null)
			{
				SelectParentElement.Click -= OnSelectParentElementClick;
				SelectParentElement.MouseEnter -= OnSelectParentElementMouseEnter;
				SelectParentElement.MouseLeave -= OnSelectParentElementMouseLeave;
			}
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			AddNewItemElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("AddNewItemElement") as Button;
			HorizontalAlignmentElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalAlignmentElement") as LayoutItemAlignmentControl;
			VerticalAlignmentElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalAlignmentElement") as LayoutItemAlignmentControl;
			MoveItemBackwardElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("MoveItemBackwardElement") as Button;
			MoveItemForwardElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("MoveItemForwardElement") as Button;
			MoveToAvailableItemsElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("MoveToAvailableItemsElement") as Button;
			RenameElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("RenameElement") as Button;
			RenamingEditElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("RenamingEditElement") as TextBox;
			SelectParentElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("SelectParentElement") as Button;
			if (AddNewItemElement != null)
			{
				AddNewItemElement.Click += OnAddNewItemElementClick;
			}
			if (HorizontalAlignmentElement != null)
			{
				HorizontalAlignmentElement.AlignmentChanged += OnHorizontalAlignmentElementAlignmentChanged;
			}
			if (VerticalAlignmentElement != null)
			{
				VerticalAlignmentElement.AlignmentChanged += OnVerticalAlignmentElementAlignmentChanged;
			}
			if (MoveItemBackwardElement != null)
			{
				MoveItemBackwardElement.Click += OnMoveItemBackwardElementClick;
			}
			if (MoveItemForwardElement != null)
			{
				MoveItemForwardElement.Click += OnMoveItemForwardElementClick;
			}
			if (MoveToAvailableItemsElement != null)
			{
				MoveToAvailableItemsElement.Click += OnMoveToAvailableItemsElementClick;
			}
			if (RenameElement != null)
			{
				RenameElement.Click += OnRenameElementClick;
			}
			if (RenamingEditElement != null)
			{
				RenamingEditElement.KeyDown += OnRenamingEditElementKeyDown;
				RenamingEditElement.LostFocus += OnRenamingEditElementLostFocus;
				RenamingEditElement.TextChanged += OnRenamingEditElementTextChanged;
			}
			if (SelectParentElement != null)
			{
				SelectParentElement.Click += OnSelectParentElementClick;
				SelectParentElement.MouseEnter += OnSelectParentElementMouseEnter;
				SelectParentElement.MouseLeave += OnSelectParentElementMouseLeave;
			}
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (HorizontalAlignmentElement != null)
			{
				if (!ItemHorizontalAlignment.HasValue)
				{
					HorizontalAlignmentElement.Alignment = null;
				}
				else
				{
					HorizontalAlignmentElement.Alignment = ItemHorizontalAlignment.Value.GetItemAlignment();
				}
			}
			if (VerticalAlignmentElement != null)
			{
				if (!ItemVerticalAlignment.HasValue)
				{
					VerticalAlignmentElement.Alignment = null;
				}
				else
				{
					VerticalAlignmentElement.Alignment = ItemVerticalAlignment.Value.GetItemAlignment();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnAddNewItemElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnAddNewItem();
		}

		private void OnHorizontalAlignmentElementAlignmentChanged()
		{
			if (!HorizontalAlignmentElement.Alignment.HasValue)
			{
				ItemHorizontalAlignment = null;
			}
			else
			{
				ItemHorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignmentElement.Alignment.Value.GetHorizontalAlignment();
			}
		}

		private void OnVerticalAlignmentElementAlignmentChanged()
		{
			if (!VerticalAlignmentElement.Alignment.HasValue)
			{
				ItemVerticalAlignment = null;
			}
			else
			{
				ItemVerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignmentElement.Alignment.Value.GetVerticalAlignment();
			}
		}

		private void OnMoveItemBackwardElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnMoveItem(forward: false);
		}

		private void OnMoveItemForwardElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnMoveItem(forward: true);
		}

		private void OnMoveToAvailableItemsElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnMoveItemToAvailableItems();
		}

		private void OnRenameElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnRenameItem();
		}

		private void OnRenamingEditElementKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return || e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				HideRenamingEdit(e.Key == Key.Return);
				OnReturnFocus();
			}
		}

		private void OnRenamingEditElementLostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!buttonWasPressed)
			{
				HideRenamingEdit(accept: true);
			}
		}

		private void OnRenamingEditElementTextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			RenamingEditElement.GetBindingExpression(TextBox.TextProperty)?.UpdateSource();
		}

		private void OnSelectParentElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnHideItemParentIndicator();
			OnSelectItemParent();
		}

		private void OnSelectParentElementMouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			OnShowItemParentIndicator();
		}

		private void OnSelectParentElementMouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			OnHideItemParentIndicator();
		}

		internal void OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			buttonWasPressed = true;
		}

		internal void OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			buttonWasPressed = false;
			HideRenamingEdit(accept: true);
		}

		private void ShowRenamingEdit()
		{
			if (RenamingEditElement != null && RenamingEditElement.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
			{
				_OriginalItemHeader = ItemHeader;
				RenamingEditElement.SetBinding(TextBox.TextProperty, new Binding("ItemHeader")
				{
					Source = this,
					Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
				});
				RenamingEditElement.SelectionStart = RenamingEditElement.Text.Length;
				if (RenameElement != null)
				{
					_StoredRenameElementVisibility = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)RenameElement, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
					RenameElement.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				RenamingEditElement.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				RenamingEditElement.Focus();
			}
		}

		private void HideRenamingEdit(bool accept)
		{
			if (RenamingEditElement != null && RenamingEditElement.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
			{
				RenamingEditElement.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				RenamingEditElement.ClearValue(TextBox.TextProperty);
				if (RenameElement != null && _StoredRenameElementVisibility != null)
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)RenameElement, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, _StoredRenameElementVisibility);
					_StoredRenameElementVisibility = null;
				}
				if (!accept)
				{
					ItemHeader = _OriginalItemHeader;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAddNewItem()
		{
			if (this.AddNewItem != null)
			{
				this.AddNewItem();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemHeaderChanged()
		{
			if (!IsInitializing && this.ItemHeaderChanged != null)
			{
				this.ItemHeaderChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemHorizontalAlignmentChanged()
		{
			if (!IsInitializing && this.ItemHorizontalAlignmentChanged != null)
			{
				this.ItemHorizontalAlignmentChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemVerticalAlignmentChanged()
		{
			if (!IsInitializing && this.ItemVerticalAlignmentChanged != null)
			{
				this.ItemVerticalAlignmentChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemMovingDirectionChanged()
		{
			ItemMovingBackwardDirection = (Side)((ItemMovingDirection != Orientation.Horizontal) ? 1 : 0);
			ItemMovingForwardDirection = (Side)((ItemMovingDirection == Orientation.Horizontal) ? 2 : 4);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnHide()
		{
			OnHideItemParentIndicator();
		}

		protected virtual void OnMoveItem(bool forward)
		{
			OnReturnFocus();
			if (this.MoveItem != null)
			{
				this.MoveItem(forward);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMoveItemToAvailableItems()
		{
			if (this.MoveItemToAvailableItems != null)
			{
				this.MoveItemToAvailableItems();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRenameItem()
		{
			ShowRenamingEdit();
		}

		protected virtual void OnReturnFocus()
		{
			if (this.ReturnFocus != null)
			{
				this.ReturnFocus();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectItemParent()
		{
			if (this.SelectItemParent != null)
			{
				this.SelectItemParent();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowItemParentIndicator()
		{
			if (this.ShowItemParentIndicator != null)
			{
				this.ShowItemParentIndicator();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHideItemParentIndicator()
		{
			if (this.HideItemParentIndicator != null)
			{
				this.HideItemParentIndicator();
			}
		}
	}
	public enum LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind
	{
		Left,
		HorizontalCenter,
		Right,
		HorizontalStretch,
		Top,
		VerticalCenter,
		Bottom,
		VerticalStretch
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "RootElement", Type = typeof(Border))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphLeft", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphHorizontalCenter", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphRight", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphHorizontalStretch", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphTop", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphVerticalCenter", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphBottom", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "GlyphVerticalStretch", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	public class LayoutItemAlignmentButton : RadioButton
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CornerRadiusProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty KindProperty;

		private const string RootElementName = "RootElement";

		private const string GlyphBaseName = "Glyph";

		private const string GlyphLeftName = "GlyphLeft";

		private const string GlyphHorizontalCenterName = "GlyphHorizontalCenter";

		private const string GlyphRightName = "GlyphRight";

		private const string GlyphHorizontalStretchName = "GlyphHorizontalStretch";

		private const string GlyphTopName = "GlyphTop";

		private const string GlyphVerticalCenterName = "GlyphVerticalCenter";

		private const string GlyphBottomName = "GlyphBottom";

		private const string GlyphVerticalStretchName = "GlyphVerticalStretch";

		public CornerRadius CornerRadius
		{
			get
			{
				return (CornerRadius)GetValue(CornerRadiusProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CornerRadiusProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind Kind
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind)GetValue(KindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(KindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected Dictionary<LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind, FrameworkElement> Glyphs { get; private set; }

		protected Border RootElement { get; private set; }

		static LayoutItemAlignmentButton()
		{
			CornerRadiusProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CornerRadius", typeof(CornerRadius), typeof(LayoutItemAlignmentButton), null);
			KindProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Kind", typeof(LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind), typeof(LayoutItemAlignmentButton), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItemAlignmentButton)o).OnKindChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemAlignmentButton>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (RootElement != null)
			{
				BorderExtensions.SetClipChild(RootElement, false);
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			RootElement = GetTemplateChild("RootElement") as Border;
			if (Glyphs == null)
			{
				Glyphs = new Dictionary<LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind, FrameworkElement>();
			}
			for (LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind = LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind.Left; layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind <= LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind.VerticalStretch; layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind++)
			{
				Glyphs[layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind] = GetTemplateChild("Glyph" + layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind) as FrameworkElement;
			}
			if (RootElement != null)
			{
				BorderExtensions.SetClipChild(RootElement, true);
			}
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (Glyphs == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind = LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind.Left; layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind <= LayoutItemAlignmentButtonKind.VerticalStretch; layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind++)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = Glyphs[layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind];
				if (frameworkElement != null)
				{
					UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)frameworkElement, layoutItemAlignmentButtonKind == Kind);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnKindChanged()
		{
			UpdateTemplate();
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalStartElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalCenterElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalEndElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalStretchElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalStartElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalCenterElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalEndElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalStretchElement", Type = typeof(RadioButton))]
	public class LayoutItemAlignmentControl : ControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty AlignmentProperty;

		private Orientation _Orientation;

		private const string HorizontalRootElementName = "HorizontalRootElement";

		private const string HorizontalStartElementName = "HorizontalStartElement";

		private const string HorizontalCenterElementName = "HorizontalCenterElement";

		private const string HorizontalEndElementName = "HorizontalEndElement";

		private const string HorizontalStretchElementName = "HorizontalStretchElement";

		private const string VerticalRootElementName = "VerticalRootElement";

		private const string VerticalStartElementName = "VerticalStartElement";

		private const string VerticalCenterElementName = "VerticalCenterElement";

		private const string VerticalEndElementName = "VerticalEndElement";

		private const string VerticalStretchElementName = "VerticalStretchElement";

		public ItemAlignment? Alignment
		{
			get
			{
				return (ItemAlignment?)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(AlignmentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(AlignmentProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return _Orientation;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Orientation != value)
				{
					_Orientation = value;
					UpdateTemplate();
				}
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement HorizontalRootElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton HorizontalStartElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton HorizontalCenterElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton HorizontalEndElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton HorizontalStretchElement { get; private set; }

		protected FrameworkElement VerticalRootElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton VerticalStartElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton VerticalCenterElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton VerticalEndElement { get; private set; }

		protected RadioButton VerticalStretchElement { get; private set; }

		public event Action AlignmentChanged;

		static LayoutItemAlignmentControl()
		{
			AlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Alignment", typeof(ItemAlignment?), typeof(LayoutItemAlignmentControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItemAlignmentControl)(object)o).OnAlignmentChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemAlignmentControl>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			DetachEventHandlers(HorizontalStartElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(HorizontalCenterElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(HorizontalEndElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(HorizontalStretchElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(VerticalStartElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(VerticalCenterElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(VerticalEndElement);
			DetachEventHandlers(VerticalStretchElement);
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			HorizontalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			HorizontalStartElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalStartElement") as RadioButton;
			HorizontalCenterElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalCenterElement") as RadioButton;
			HorizontalEndElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalEndElement") as RadioButton;
			HorizontalStretchElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalStretchElement") as RadioButton;
			VerticalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			VerticalStartElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalStartElement") as RadioButton;
			VerticalCenterElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalCenterElement") as RadioButton;
			VerticalEndElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalEndElement") as RadioButton;
			VerticalStretchElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalStretchElement") as RadioButton;
			AttachEventHandlers(HorizontalStartElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(HorizontalCenterElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(HorizontalEndElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(HorizontalStretchElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(VerticalStartElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(VerticalCenterElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(VerticalEndElement);
			AttachEventHandlers(VerticalStretchElement);
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (HorizontalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)HorizontalRootElement, Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal);
			}
			if (VerticalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)VerticalRootElement, Orientation == Orientation.Vertical);
			}
			UpdateIsChecked(HorizontalStartElement, HorizontalCenterElement, HorizontalEndElement, HorizontalStretchElement);
			UpdateIsChecked(VerticalStartElement, VerticalCenterElement, VerticalEndElement, VerticalStretchElement);
		}

		private void AttachEventHandlers(RadioButton element)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				element.Checked += ElementChecked;
			}
		}

		private void DetachEventHandlers(RadioButton element)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				element.Checked -= ElementChecked;
			}
		}

		private void ElementChecked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender == HorizontalStartElement || sender == VerticalStartElement)
			{
				Alignment = ItemAlignment.Start;
			}
			if (sender == HorizontalCenterElement || sender == VerticalCenterElement)
			{
				Alignment = ItemAlignment.Center;
			}
			if (sender == HorizontalEndElement || sender == VerticalEndElement)
			{
				Alignment = ItemAlignment.End;
			}
			if (sender == HorizontalStretchElement || sender == VerticalStretchElement)
			{
				Alignment = ItemAlignment.Stretch;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateIsChecked(RadioButton start, RadioButton center, RadioButton end, RadioButton stretch)
		{
			UpdateIsChecked(start, ItemAlignment.Start);
			UpdateIsChecked(center, ItemAlignment.Center);
			UpdateIsChecked(end, ItemAlignment.End);
			UpdateIsChecked(stretch, ItemAlignment.Stretch);
		}

		private void UpdateIsChecked(RadioButton element, ItemAlignment alignment)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				element.IsChecked = Alignment == alignment;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAlignmentChanged()
		{
			UpdateTemplate();
			if (this.AlignmentChanged != null)
			{
				this.AlignmentChanged();
			}
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButton : Button
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CornerRadiusProperty;

		public CornerRadius CornerRadius
		{
			get

				return (CornerRadius)GetValue(CornerRadiusProperty);
			}
			set

				SetValue(CornerRadiusProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButton()
		{
			CornerRadiusProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CornerRadius", typeof(CornerRadius), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButton), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButton>();
