	public class LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultDirectionProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DirectionProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty RotateTransformProperty;

		public Side DefaultDirection
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				return (Side)GetValue(DefaultDirectionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				SetValue(DefaultDirectionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Side Direction
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				return (Side)GetValue(DirectionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				SetValue(DirectionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public RotateTransform RotateTransform
		{
			get
			{
				return (RotateTransform)GetValue(RotateTransformProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(RotateTransformProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected double Angle => GetAngle(Direction) - GetAngle(DefaultDirection);

		static LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow()
		{
			DefaultDirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultDirection", typeof(Side), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow)o).OnDefaultDirectionChanged();
			}));
			DirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Direction", typeof(Side), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow)o).OnDirectionChanged();
			}));
			RotateTransformProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RotateTransform", typeof(RotateTransform), typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemCustomizationToolbarButtonArrow>();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAngleChanged()
		{
			UpdateRotateTransform();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefaultDirectionChanged()
		{
			OnAngleChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDirectionChanged()
		{
			OnAngleChanged();
		}

		protected void UpdateRotateTransform()
		{
			RotateTransform = new RotateTransform
			{
				Angle = Angle
			};
		}

		private double GetAngle(Side direction)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			return (int)direction switch
			{
				0 => 0.0, 
				1 => 90.0, 
				2 => 180.0, 
				4 => 270.0, 
				_ => 0.0, 
			};
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "AvailableItemsElement", Type = typeof(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl))]
	public class LayoutControlCustomizationControl : ControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableItemsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableItemsUIVisibilityProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NewItemsInfoProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty;

		private const string AvailableItemsElementName = "AvailableItemsElement";

		public FrameworkElements AvailableItems
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0011: Expected O, but got Unknown
				return (FrameworkElements)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(AvailableItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(AvailableItemsProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public Visibility AvailableItemsUIVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(AvailableItemsUIVisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(AvailableItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsAvailableItemsListOpen
		{
			get
			{
				if (AvailableItemsElement != null)
				{
					return AvailableItemsElement.IsListOpen;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public LayoutControlNewItemsInfo NewItemsInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutControlNewItemsInfo)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(NewItemsInfoProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(NewItemsInfoProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public Visibility NewItemsUIVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl AvailableItemsElement { get; private set; }

		public event Action<FrameworkElement> DeleteAvailableItem;

		public event EventHandler IsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged;

		public event EventHandler<LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement>> StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop;

		public event EventHandler<LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo>> StartNewItemDragAndDrop;

		static LayoutControlCustomizationControl()
		{
			AvailableItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AvailableItems", typeof(FrameworkElements), typeof(LayoutControlCustomizationControl), null);
			AvailableItemsUIVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AvailableItemsUIVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutControlCustomizationControl), null);
			NewItemsInfoProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewItemsInfo", typeof(LayoutControlNewItemsInfo), typeof(LayoutControlCustomizationControl), null);
			NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewItemsUIVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutControlCustomizationControl), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutControlCustomizationControl>();
		}

		public LayoutControlCustomizationControl()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).FocusVisualStyle = null;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (AvailableItemsElement != null)
			{
				AvailableItemsElement.DeleteAvailableItem -= OnDeleteAvailableItem;
				AvailableItemsElement.IsListOpenChanged -= OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged;
				AvailableItemsElement.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop -= OnAvailableItemsStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop;
				AvailableItemsElement.StartNewItemDragAndDrop -= OnAvailableItemsStartNewItemDragAndDrop;
			}
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			AvailableItemsElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("AvailableItemsElement") as LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl;
			if (AvailableItemsElement != null)
			{
				AvailableItemsElement.DeleteAvailableItem += OnDeleteAvailableItem;
				AvailableItemsElement.IsListOpenChanged += OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged;
				AvailableItemsElement.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop += OnAvailableItemsStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop;
				AvailableItemsElement.StartNewItemDragAndDrop += OnAvailableItemsStartNewItemDragAndDrop;
			}
		}

		private void OnAvailableItemsStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(object sender, LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement> e)
		{
			OnStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(e);
		}

		private void OnAvailableItemsStartNewItemDragAndDrop(object sender, LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo> e)
		{
			OnStartNewItemDragAndDrop(e);
		}

		private void OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDeleteAvailableItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (this.DeleteAvailableItem != null)
			{
				this.DeleteAvailableItem(item);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged()
		{
			if (this.IsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsAvailableItemsListOpenChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement> args)
		{
			if (this.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop != null)
			{
				this.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(this, args);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnStartNewItemDragAndDrop(LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo> args)
		{
			if (this.StartNewItemDragAndDrop != null)
			{
				this.StartNewItemDragAndDrop(this, args);
			}
		}
	}
	public interface ILayoutControlAvailableItemsControl : IControl
	{
		bool IsListOpen { get; set; }
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "AvailableItemsListElement", Type = typeof(ListBox))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "NewItemsListElement", Type = typeof(ListBox))]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ListClosed", GroupName = "LayoutStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ListOpen", GroupName = "LayoutStates")]
	public class LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl : ControlBase, ILayoutControlAvailableItemsControl, IControl
	{
		protected class AvailableItemInfo
		{
			public FrameworkElement Item { get; private set; }

			public string Label { get; private set; }

			public static int Compare(AvailableItemInfo info1, AvailableItemInfo info2)
			{
				return string.Compare(info1.Label, info2.Label);
			}

			public static int GetInsertionIndex(List<AvailableItemInfo> sortedList, AvailableItemInfo info)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < sortedList.Count; i++)
				{
					if (Compare(sortedList[i], info) > 0)
					{
						return i;
					}
				}
				return sortedList.Count;
			}

			public AvailableItemInfo(FrameworkElement item, string label)
			{
				Item = item;
				Label = label;
			}
		}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableItemsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsListOpenProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LayoutGroupItemTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NewItemsInfoProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty;

		private const string AvailableItemsListElementName = "AvailableItemsListElement";

		private const string NewItemsListElementName = "NewItemsListElement";

		private const string LayoutStates = "LayoutStates";

		private const string ListClosedLayoutState = "ListClosed";

		private const string ListOpenLayoutState = "ListOpen";

		public FrameworkElements AvailableItems
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0011: Expected O, but got Unknown
				return (FrameworkElements)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(AvailableItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(AvailableItemsProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsListOpen
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(IsListOpenProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(IsListOpenProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate LayoutGroupItemTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(LayoutGroupItemTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(LayoutGroupItemTemplateProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public LayoutControlNewItemsInfo NewItemsInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutControlNewItemsInfo)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(NewItemsInfoProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(NewItemsInfoProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public Visibility NewItemsUIVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected ListBox AvailableItemsListElement { get; private set; }

		protected ListBox NewItemsListElement { get; private set; }

		protected List<AvailableItemInfo> AvailableItemsInfo { get; private set; }

		private int AvailableItemsChangeCount { get; set; }

		public event Action<FrameworkElement> DeleteAvailableItem;

		public event EventHandler IsListOpenChanged;

		public event EventHandler<LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement>> StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop;

		public event EventHandler<LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo>> StartNewItemDragAndDrop;

		static LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl()
		{
			AvailableItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AvailableItems", typeof(FrameworkElements), typeof(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
				((LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl)(object)o).OnAvailableItemsChanged((FrameworkElements)e.OldValue);
			}));
			IsListOpenProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsListOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl)(object)o).OnIsListOpenChanged();
			}));
			LayoutGroupItemTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayoutGroupItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl), null);
			NewItemsInfoProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewItemsInfo", typeof(LayoutControlNewItemsInfo), typeof(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl)(object)o).OnNewItemsInfoChanged();
			}));
			NewItemsUIVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewItemsUIVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			AvailableItemsListElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("AvailableItemsListElement") as ListBox;
			NewItemsListElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("NewItemsListElement") as ListBox;
			UpdateAvailableItemsListElement(null);
			UpdateNewItemsListElement();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected override ControlControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (ControlControllerBase)(object)new LayoutControlAvailableItemsController(this);
		}

		protected virtual AvailableItemInfo CreateAvailableItemInfo(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			return new AvailableItemInfo(item, GetAvailableItemLabel(item));
		}

		protected virtual LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem CreateListItem()
		{
			return new LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem(this);
		}

		protected LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem CreateListItem(AvailableItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem layoutControlAvailableListBoxItem = CreateListItem();
			InitListItem(layoutControlAvailableListBoxItem, itemInfo);
			layoutControlAvailableListBoxItem.Item = itemInfo.Item;
			return layoutControlAvailableListBoxItem;
		}

		protected virtual string GetAvailableItemDefaultLabel(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			return LayoutControl.GetCustomizationDefaultLabel(item);
		}

		protected AvailableItemInfo GetAvailableItemInfo(ListBoxItem listItem)
		{
			int num = AvailableItemsListElement.Items.IndexOf(listItem);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return AvailableItemsInfo[num];
		}

		protected int GetAvailableItemInfoIndex(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < AvailableItemsInfo.Count; i++)
			{
				if (AvailableItemsInfo[i].Item == item)
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		protected virtual string GetAvailableItemLabel(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			string text = LayoutControl.GetCustomizationLabel(item) as string;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = GetAvailableItemDefaultLabel(item);
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected virtual List<AvailableItemInfo> GetAvailableItemsInfo()
		{
			List<AvailableItemInfo> list = new List<AvailableItemInfo>();
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)AvailableItems)
			{
				list.Add(CreateAvailableItemInfo(item));
			}
			list.Sort(AvailableItemInfo.Compare);
			return list;
		}

		protected LayoutControlNewItemInfo GetNewItemInfo(ListBoxItem listItem)
		{
			int num = NewItemsListElement.Items.IndexOf(listItem);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return NewItemsInfo[num];
		}

		protected int GetListItemIndex(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < AvailableItemsListElement.Items.Count; i++)
			{
				if (((LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem)AvailableItemsListElement.Items[i]).Item == item)
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		protected virtual void InitListItem(LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem listItem, AvailableItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			listItem.Content = itemInfo.Label;
			if (itemInfo.Item.IsLayoutGroup() && LayoutGroupItemTemplate != null)
			{
				listItem.ContentTemplate = LayoutGroupItemTemplate;
			}
			if (LayoutControl.GetIsUserDefined(itemInfo.Item))
			{
				listItem.SetBinding(LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem.DeleteElementVisibilityProperty, new Binding("NewItemsUIVisibility")
				{
					Source = this
				});
			}
			listItem.DeleteElementClick += delegate
			{
				OnDeleteAvailableItem(itemInfo.Item);
			};
		}

		protected virtual void OnAvailableItemsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (AvailableItems == null)
			{
				AvailableItemsInfo = null;
			}
			else if (args != null && (args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add || args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove) && AvailableItemsInfo != null)
			{
				if (args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add)
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement newItem in args.NewItems)
					{
						AvailableItemInfo availableItemInfo = CreateAvailableItemInfo(newItem);
						AvailableItemsInfo.Insert(AvailableItemInfo.GetInsertionIndex(AvailableItemsInfo, availableItemInfo), availableItemInfo);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement oldItem in args.OldItems)
					{
						AvailableItemsInfo.RemoveAt(GetAvailableItemInfoIndex(oldItem));
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				AvailableItemsInfo = GetAvailableItemsInfo();
			}
			UpdateAvailableItemsListElement(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDeleteAvailableItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (this.DeleteAvailableItem != null)
			{
				this.DeleteAvailableItem(item);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsListOpenChanged()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(true);
			if (this.IsListOpenChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsListOpenChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnNewItemsInfoChanged()
		{
			UpdateNewItemsListElement();
		}

		protected virtual void OnStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(FrameworkElement item, MouseEventArgs mouseEventArgs, FrameworkElement source)
		{
			OnStartItemDragAndDrop();
			if (this.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop != null)
			{
				this.StartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(this, new LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<FrameworkElement>(item, mouseEventArgs, source));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnStartItemDragAndDrop()
		{
			IsListOpen = false;
			((Controller)((ControlBase)this).Controller).IsMouseEntered = false;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnStartItemDragAndDrop(ListBoxItem listItem, MouseEventArgs arguments)
		{
			AvailableItemInfo availableItemInfo = GetAvailableItemInfo(listItem);
			if (availableItemInfo != null)
			{
				OnStartAvailableItemDragAndDrop(availableItemInfo.Item, arguments, listItem);
				return;
			}
			LayoutControlNewItemInfo newItemInfo = GetNewItemInfo(listItem);
			if (newItemInfo != null)
			{
				OnStartNewItemDragAndDrop(newItemInfo, arguments, listItem);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnStartNewItemDragAndDrop(LayoutControlNewItemInfo itemInfo, MouseEventArgs mouseEventArgs, FrameworkElement source)
		{
			OnStartItemDragAndDrop();
			if (this.StartNewItemDragAndDrop != null)
			{
				this.StartNewItemDragAndDrop(this, new LayoutControlStartDragAndDropEventArgs<LayoutControlNewItemInfo>(itemInfo, mouseEventArgs, source));
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateAvailableItemsListElement(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (AvailableItemsListElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (AvailableItemsInfo == null)
			{
				AvailableItemsListElement.Items.Clear();
				return;
			}
			if (args != null && (args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add || args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove))
			{
				if (args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add)
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement newItem in args.NewItems)
					{
						int availableItemInfoIndex = GetAvailableItemInfoIndex(newItem);
						AvailableItemsListElement.Items.Insert(availableItemInfoIndex, CreateListItem(AvailableItemsInfo[availableItemInfoIndex]));
					}
					return;
				}
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement oldItem in args.OldItems)
					{
						AvailableItemsListElement.Items.RemoveAt(GetListItemIndex(oldItem));
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			AvailableItemsListElement.Items.Clear();
			foreach (AvailableItemInfo item in AvailableItemsInfo)
			{
				AvailableItemsListElement.Items.Add(CreateListItem(item));
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateNewItemsListElement()
		{
			if (NewItemsListElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			NewItemsListElement.Items.Clear();
			if (NewItemsInfo == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (LayoutControlNewItemInfo item in NewItemsInfo)
			{
				ListBoxItem listBoxItem = CreateListItem();
				listBoxItem.Content = item.Label;
				NewItemsListElement.Items.Add(listBoxItem);
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(useTransitions);
			((ControlBase)this).GoToState(IsListOpen ? "ListOpen" : "ListClosed", useTransitions);
		}

		private void OnAvailableItemsChanged(FrameworkElements oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				((ObservableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)oldValue).CollectionChanged -= OnAvailableItemsChanged;
			}
			if (AvailableItems != null)
			{
				((ObservableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)AvailableItems).CollectionChanged += OnAvailableItemsChanged;
			}
			OnAvailableItemsChanged((NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs)null);
		}

		private void OnAvailableItemsChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			AvailableItemsChangeCount++;
			if (AvailableItemsChangeCount == 1)
			{
				((DispatcherObject)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					OnAvailableItemsChanged((AvailableItemsChangeCount == 1) ? e : null);
					AvailableItemsChangeCount = 0;
				}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
			}
		}
	}
	public class LayoutControlAvailableItemsController : ControlControllerBase
	{
		public ILayoutControlAvailableItemsControl IControl => (ILayoutControlAvailableItemsControl)((Controller)this).IControl;

		public LayoutControlAvailableItemsController(ILayoutControlAvailableItemsControl control)
			: base((IControl)control)
		{
		}

		protected override void OnIsMouseEnteredChanged()
		{
			((ControlControllerBase)this).OnIsMouseEnteredChanged();
			IControl.IsListOpen = ((Controller)this).IsMouseEntered;
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "DeleteElement", Type = typeof(Button))]
	public class LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem : ListBoxItem
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DeleteElementVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DeleteElementVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed));

		private Point? _StartDragPoint;

		private const string DeleteElementName = "DeleteElement";

		public Visibility DeleteElementVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)GetValue(DeleteElementVisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DeleteElementVisibilityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected Button DeleteElement { get; private set; }

		protected LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl Owner { get; private set; }

		protected Point? StartDragPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return _StartDragPoint;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (!(StartDragPoint == value))
				{
					_StartDragPoint = value;
					if (StartDragPoint.HasValue)
					{
						CaptureMouse();
					}
					else
					{
						ReleaseMouseCapture();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal FrameworkElement Item { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler DeleteElementClick;

		public LayoutControlAvailableListBoxItem(LayoutControlAvailableItemsControl owner)
		{
			Owner = owner;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (DeleteElement != null)
			{
				DeleteElement.Click -= OnDeleteElementClick;
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			DeleteElement = GetTemplateChild("DeleteElement") as Button;
			if (DeleteElement != null)
			{
				DeleteElement.Click += OnDeleteElementClick;
			}
		}

		private void OnDeleteElementClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnDeleteElementClick();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDeleteElementClick()
		{
			if (this.DeleteElementClick != null)
			{
				this.DeleteElementClick(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			StartDragPoint = null;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			StartDragPoint = e.GetPosition(this);
			e.Handled = true;
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			StartDragPoint = null;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseMove(e);
			if (StartDragPoint.HasValue)
			{
				StartDragPoint = null;
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Normal", useTransitions: false);
				StartDragAndDrop(e);
			}
		}

		protected void StartDragAndDrop(MouseEventArgs arguments)
		{
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				Owner.OnStartItemDragAndDrop(this, arguments);
			}
		}
	}

		None,
		Left,
		Top,
		Right,
		Bottom,
		Client
	}
	public interface IDockLayoutModel : ILayoutModelBase

	public interface IDockLayoutControl : ILayoutControlBase, IScrollControl, IPanel, IControl, ILayoutModelBase, IDockLayoutModel
	{
	}
	[LicenseProvider(typeof(DX_WPF_ControlRequiredForReports_LicenseProvider))]
	[StyleTypedProperty(Property = "ItemSizerStyle", StyleTargetType = typeof(ElementSizer))]
	[DXToolboxBrowsable]
	public class DockLayoutControl : LayoutControlBase, IDockLayoutControl, ILayoutControlBase, IScrollControl, IPanel, IControl, ILayoutModelBase, IDockLayoutModel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowItemSizingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowHorizontalSizingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowVerticalSizingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DockProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemSizerStyleProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightProperty;

		[Description("Gets or sets whether item re-sizing is enabled within the DockLayoutControl.\nThis is a dependency property.")]
		public bool AllowItemSizing
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(AllowItemSizingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(AllowItemSizingProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the style applied to visual elements used to re-size the DockLayoutControl’s items vertically or horizontally.")]
		public Style ItemSizerStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return (Style)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(ItemSizerStyleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(ItemSizerStyleProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected ElementSizers ItemSizers { get; private set; }

		public static bool GetAllowHorizontalSizing(UIElement element)
		{
			return (bool)element.GetValue(AllowHorizontalSizingProperty);
		}

		public static void SetAllowHorizontalSizing(UIElement element, bool value)
		{
			element.SetValue(AllowHorizontalSizingProperty, value);
		}

		public static bool GetAllowVerticalSizing(UIElement element)
		{
			return (bool)element.GetValue(AllowVerticalSizingProperty);
		}

		public static void SetAllowVerticalSizing(UIElement element, bool value)
		{
			element.SetValue(AllowVerticalSizingProperty, value);
		}

		public static Dock GetDock(UIElement element)
		{
			return (Dock)element.GetValue(DockProperty);
		}

		public static void SetDock(UIElement element, Dock value)
		{
			element.SetValue(DockProperty, value);
		}

		public static bool GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(UIElement element)
		{
			return (bool)element.GetValue(UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthProperty);
		}

		public static void SetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(UIElement element, bool value)
		{
			element.SetValue(UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthProperty, value);
		}

		public static bool GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(UIElement element)
		{
			return (bool)element.GetValue(UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightProperty);
		}

		public static void SetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(UIElement element, bool value)
		{
			element.SetValue(UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightProperty, value);
		}

		static DockLayoutControl()
		{
			AllowItemSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowItemSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((DockLayoutControl)(object)o).OnAllowItemSizingChanged();
			}));
			AllowHorizontalSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("AllowHorizontalSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			AllowVerticalSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("AllowVerticalSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			DockProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("Dock", typeof(Dock), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((object)Dock.Left, (PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			ItemSizerStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemSizerStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)((DockLayoutControl)(object)o).ItemSizers).ItemStyle = (Style)e.NewValue;
			}));
			UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth", typeof(bool), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight", typeof(bool), typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			LayoutControlBase.PaddingProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DockLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(new Thickness(0.0)));
		}

		public DockLayoutControl()
		{
			About.CheckLicenseShowNagScreen(typeof(DockLayoutControl));
			ItemSizers = CreateItemSizers();
			ItemSizers.SizingAreaWidth = base.ItemSpace;
		}

		protected override bool IsTempChild(UIElement child)
		{
			if (!((PanelBase)this).IsTempChild(child))
			{
				return ((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers).IsItem(child);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size result = base.OnMeasure(availableSize);
			InitChildrenMaxSizeAsDesiredSize();
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers).MarkItemsAsUnused();
			Size result = base.OnArrange(bounds);
			((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers).DeleteUnusedItems();
			return result;
		}

		protected override LayoutProviderBase CreateLayoutProvider()
		{
			return new DockLayoutProvider(this);
		}

		protected override LayoutParametersBase CreateLayoutProviderParameters()
		{
			return new DockLayoutParameters(base.ItemSpace, AllowItemSizing ? ItemSizers : null);
		}

		protected override void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml)
		{
			base.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, "Width", typeof(double));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, "Height", typeof(double));
		}

		protected override void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, "Width");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, "Height");
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new DockLayoutController(this);
		}

		protected virtual ElementSizers CreateItemSizers()
		{
			return new ElementSizers((PanelBase)(object)this);
		}

		protected virtual void InitChildrenMaxSizeAsDesiredSize()
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true))
			{
				if (GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(item) && item.DesiredSize.Width != 0.0 && double.IsInfinity(item.MaxWidth))
				{
					item.MaxWidth = item.DesiredSize.Width;
				}
				if (GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(item) && item.DesiredSize.Height != 0.0 && double.IsInfinity(item.MaxHeight))
				{
					item.MaxHeight = item.DesiredSize.Height;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowItemSizingChanged()
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowHorizontalSizingChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowVerticalSizingChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected override void OnAttachedPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty property, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnAttachedPropertyChanged(child, property, oldValue, newValue);
			if (property == AllowHorizontalSizingProperty)
			{
				OnAllowHorizontalSizingChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == AllowVerticalSizingProperty)
			{
				OnAllowVerticalSizingChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == DockProperty)
			{
				OnDockChanged(child, (Dock)oldValue, (Dock)newValue);
			}
			if (property == UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthProperty)
			{
				OnUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightProperty)
			{
				OnUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDockChanged(FrameworkElement child, Dock oldValue, Dock newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected override void OnItemSpaceChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			base.OnItemSpaceChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			if (ItemSizers != null)
			{
				ItemSizers.SizingAreaWidth = base.ItemSpace;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(child) && child.DesiredSize.Width != 0.0)
			{
				double width = child.Width;
				if (!double.IsNaN(width))
				{
					child.Width = double.NaN;
					((UIElement)(object)this).UpdateLayout();
				}
				child.MaxWidth = child.DesiredSize.Width;
				child.Width = width;
			}
			if (!GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(child))
			{
				child.MaxWidth = double.PositiveInfinity;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(child) && child.DesiredSize.Height != 0.0)
			{
				double height = child.Height;
				if (!double.IsNaN(height))
				{
					child.Height = double.NaN;
					((UIElement)(object)this).UpdateLayout();
				}
				child.MaxHeight = child.DesiredSize.Height;
				child.Height = height;
			}
			if (!GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(child))
			{
				child.MaxHeight = double.PositiveInfinity;
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new DockLayoutControlAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	public class DockLayoutParameters : LayoutParametersBase
	{
		public ElementSizers ItemSizers { get; private set; }

		public DockLayoutParameters(double itemSpace, ElementSizers itemSizers)
			: base(itemSpace)
		{
			ItemSizers = itemSizers;
		}
	}
	public class DockLayoutProvider : LayoutProviderBase
	{
		protected class Offsets : Dictionary<Dock, double>
		{
			protected double ItemSpace { get; private set; }

			public Offsets(double itemSpace)
			{
				ItemSpace = itemSpace;
				Add(Dock.Left, 0.0);
				Add(Dock.Top, 0.0);
				Add(Dock.Right, 0.0);
				Add(Dock.Bottom, 0.0);
			}

			public Rect GetClientBounds(Rect bounds)
			{
				Size clientSize = GetClientSize(RectHelper.Size(bounds));
				return new Rect(bounds.X + base[Dock.Left], bounds.Y + base[Dock.Top], clientSize.Width, clientSize.Height);
			}

			public Size GetClientSize(Size size)
			{
				return new Size(Math.Max(0.0, size.Width - (base[Dock.Left] + base[Dock.Right])), Math.Max(0.0, size.Height - (base[Dock.Top] + base[Dock.Bottom])));
			}

			public Size GetFullSize(Size size)
			{
				return new Size(base[Dock.Left] + size.Width + base[Dock.Right], base[Dock.Top] + size.Height + base[Dock.Bottom]);
			}

			public void Update(Dock itemDock, Size itemSize)
			{
				double num = ((itemDock != Dock.Left && itemDock != Dock.Right) ? itemSize.Height : itemSize.Width);
				base[itemDock] += num + ItemSpace;
			}
		}

		protected new IDockLayoutModel Model => (IDockLayoutModel)base.Model;

		protected new DockLayoutParameters Parameters => (DockLayoutParameters)base.Parameters;

		public DockLayoutProvider(IDockLayoutModel model)
			: base(model)
		{
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			Size result = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			Offsets offsets = new Offsets(Parameters.ItemSpace);
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Dock dock = DockLayoutControl.GetDock(item);
				switch (dock)
				{
				case Dock.None:
					item.Measure(maxSize);
					continue;
				case Dock.Client:
					continue;
				}
				item.Measure(offsets.GetClientSize(maxSize));
				Size desiredSize = UIElementExtensions.GetDesiredSize((UIElement)item);
				SizeHelper.UpdateMaxSize(ref result, offsets.GetFullSize(desiredSize));
				offsets.Update(dock, desiredSize);
			}
			Size clientSize = offsets.GetClientSize(maxSize);
			Size zero = SizeHelper.Zero;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item2 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				if (DockLayoutControl.GetDock(item2) == Dock.Client)
				{
					item2.Measure(clientSize);
					SizeHelper.UpdateMaxSize(ref zero, UIElementExtensions.GetDesiredSize((UIElement)item2));
				}
			}
			if (!SizeHelper.IsZero(zero))
			{
				SizeHelper.UpdateMaxSize(ref result, offsets.GetFullSize(zero));
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Offsets offsets = new Offsets(Parameters.ItemSpace);
			FrameworkElements val = new FrameworkElements();
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Dock dock = DockLayoutControl.GetDock(item);
				switch (dock)
				{
				case Dock.None:
					item.Arrange(new Rect(new Point(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetLeft(item), FrameworkElementExtensions.GetTop(item)), item.DesiredSize));
					continue;
				case Dock.Client:
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)val).Add(item);
					continue;
				}
				Rect bounds2 = RectHelper.New(item.DesiredSize);
				AlignBounds(ref bounds2, offsets.GetClientBounds(bounds), dock);
				item.Arrange(bounds2);
				offsets.Update(dock, RectHelper.Size(bounds2));
			}
			Rect clientBounds = offsets.GetClientBounds(bounds);
			foreach (FrameworkElement item2 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)val)
			{
				item2.Arrange(clientBounds);
			}
			if (Parameters.ItemSizers != null)
			{
				AddItemSizers(items);
			}
			return base.OnArrange(items, bounds, viewPortBounds);
		}

		protected virtual void AlignBounds(ref Rect bounds, Rect area, Dock dock)
		{
			if (dock == Dock.Left || dock == Dock.Right)
			{
				if (dock == Dock.Left)
				{
					bounds.X = area.Left;
				}
				else
				{
					bounds.X = area.Right - bounds.Width;
				}
				bounds.Y = area.Y;
				bounds.Height = area.Height;
			}
			else
			{
				bounds.X = area.X;
				bounds.Width = area.Width;
				if (dock == Dock.Top)
				{
					bounds.Y = area.Top;
				}
				else
				{
					bounds.Y = area.Bottom - bounds.Height;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void AddItemSizers(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Side? itemSizingSide = GetItemSizingSide(item);
				if (itemSizingSide.HasValue && IsItemSizeable(item, itemSizingSide.Value))
				{
					Parameters.ItemSizers.Add(item, itemSizingSide.Value);
				}
			}
		}

		protected Side? GetItemSizingSide(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			return DockLayoutControl.GetDock(item) switch
			{
				Dock.Left => (Side)2, 
				Dock.Top => (Side)4, 
				Dock.Right => (Side)0, 
				Dock.Bottom => (Side)1, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		protected bool IsItemSizeable(FrameworkElement item, Side side)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0005: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)side == 0 || (int)side == 2)
			{
				return DockLayoutControl.GetAllowHorizontalSizing(item);
			}
			return DockLayoutControl.GetAllowVerticalSizing(item);
		}
	}
	public class DockLayoutController : LayoutControllerBase
	{
		public DockLayoutController(IDockLayoutControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		public override bool IsScrollable()
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public interface IElementSizer : IControl
	{
		bool CollapseOnDoubleClick { get; }

		FrameworkElement Element { get; }

		Side Side { get; }

		bool UseSizingStep { get; }
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	public class ElementSizer : ControlBase, IElementSizer, IControl
	{
		public static int SizingStep;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CollapseOnDoubleClickProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ElementProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty UseSizingStepProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowResizingHandleProperty;

		private Side _Side;

		private double _SizingAreaWidth;

		private const string HorizontalRootElementName = "HorizontalRootElement";

		private const string VerticalRootElementName = "VerticalRootElement";

		public bool CollapseOnDoubleClick
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(CollapseOnDoubleClickProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(CollapseOnDoubleClickProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public ElementSizerController Controller => (ElementSizerController)(object)((ControlBase)this).Controller;

		public FrameworkElement Element
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ElementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ElementProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public double ElementSize => Controller.ElementSize;

		public bool IsSizing => Controller.IsSizing;

		public bool ShowResizingHandle
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ShowResizingHandleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ShowResizingHandleProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public Side Side
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				return _Side;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (_Side != value)
				{
					_Side = value;
					UpdateTemplate();
				}
			}
		}

		public double SizingAreaWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _SizingAreaWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				_SizingAreaWidth = Math.Max(0.0, value);
			}
		}

		public bool UseSizingStep
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(UseSizingStepProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(UseSizingStepProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement HorizontalRootElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement VerticalRootElement { get; set; }

		protected Orientation Orientation => SideExtentions.GetOrientation(Side);

		public event EventHandler ElementSizeChanging
		{
			add
			{
				Controller.ElementSizeChanging += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				Controller.ElementSizeChanging -= value;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler IsSizingChanged
		{
			add
			{
				Controller.IsSizingChanged += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				Controller.IsSizingChanged -= value;
			}
		}

		static ElementSizer()
		{
			SizingStep = 5;
			CollapseOnDoubleClickProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CollapseOnDoubleClick", typeof(bool), typeof(ElementSizer), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			ElementProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Element", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(ElementSizer), null);
			UseSizingStepProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UseSizingStep", typeof(bool), typeof(ElementSizer), null);
			ShowResizingHandleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowResizingHandle", typeof(bool), typeof(ElementSizer), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<ElementSizer>();
		}

		public void UpdateBounds()
		{
			if (((Visual)(object)this).VisualChildrenCount > 0)
			{
				(((Visual)(object)this).GetVisualChild(0) as FrameworkElement).Do(delegate(FrameworkElement x)
				{
					x.InvalidateMeasure();
				});
			}
			((UIElement)(object)LayoutTreeHelper.GetVisualChildren((DependencyObject)(object)this).OfType<Container>().FirstOrDefault())?.InvalidateMeasure();
			((UIElement)this).Measure(SizeHelper.Infinite);
			((UIElement)this).Arrange(CalculateBounds());
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			HorizontalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			VerticalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (HorizontalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)HorizontalRootElement, Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal);
				if (UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)HorizontalRootElement))
				{
					((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = HorizontalRootElement.Cursor;
				}
			}
			if (VerticalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)VerticalRootElement, Orientation == Orientation.Vertical);
				if (UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)VerticalRootElement))
				{
					((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = VerticalRootElement.Cursor;
				}
			}
			((UIElement)this).InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		protected virtual Rect CalculateBounds()
		{
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			if (Element == null)
			{
				return ((ControlBase)this).Bounds;
			}
			Rect layoutSlot = LayoutInformation.GetLayoutSlot(Element);
			Side side = Side;
			switch ((int)side)
			{
			case 0:
				layoutSlot.X = layoutSlot.Left - SizingAreaWidth;
				break;
			case 1:
				layoutSlot.Y = layoutSlot.Top - SizingAreaWidth;
				break;
			case 2:
				layoutSlot.X = layoutSlot.Right;
				break;
			case 4:
				layoutSlot.Y = layoutSlot.Bottom;
				break;
			}
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				layoutSlot.Height = SizingAreaWidth;
			}
			else
			{
				layoutSlot.Width = SizingAreaWidth;
			}
			return layoutSlot;
		}

		protected override ControlControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (ControlControllerBase)(object)new ElementSizerController(this);
		}
	}
	public class ElementSizerController : ControlControllerBase
	{
		private bool _IsSizing;

		public double ElementSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsElementAutoSize)
				{
					Size visualSize = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetVisualSize(IElementSizer.Element);
					if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
					{
						return visualSize.Width;
					}
					return visualSize.Height;
				}
				if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetRealWidth(IElementSizer.Element);
				}
				return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetRealHeight(IElementSizer.Element);
			}
			set
			{
				double elementSize = ElementSize;
				value = Math.Max(0.0, value);
				LastElementSize = value;
				if (IsUsingSizingStep())
				{
					value = Math.Round(value / (double)ElementSizer.SizingStep) * (double)ElementSizer.SizingStep;
				}
				if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					IElementSizer.Element.Height = value;
				}
				else
				{
					IElementSizer.Element.Width = value;
				}
				if (ElementSize != elementSize)
				{
					OnElementSizeChanging();
				}
			}
		}

		public IElementSizer IElementSizer => ((Controller)this).IControl as IElementSizer;

		public bool IsSizing
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsSizing;
			}
			protected set
			{
				if (IsSizing != value)
				{
					_IsSizing = value;
					OnIsSizingChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool IsElementAutoSize => double.IsNaN((Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? IElementSizer.Element.Height : IElementSizer.Element.Width);

		protected double LastElementSize { get; private set; }

		protected Orientation Orientation => SideExtentions.GetOrientation(IElementSizer.Side);

		protected Point AbsoluteStartDragPoint { get; private set; }

		protected override bool IsImmediateDragAndDrop => true;

		protected double OriginalElementSize { get; private set; }

		protected bool OriginalIsElementAutoSize { get; private set; }

		public event EventHandler ElementSizeChanging;

		public event EventHandler IsSizingChanged;

		public ElementSizerController(IElementSizer control)
			: base((IControl)control)
		{
		}

		protected virtual bool IsUsingSizingStep()
		{
			if (IElementSizer.UseSizingStep)
			{
				return !Keyboard2.IsControlPressed;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementSizeChanging()
		{
			if (this.ElementSizeChanging != null)
			{
				this.ElementSizeChanging(((ControlControllerBase)this).Control, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsSizingChanged()
		{
			if (this.IsSizingChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsSizingChanged(((ControlControllerBase)this).Control, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateElementSize()
		{
			if (IElementSizer.UseSizingStep && !double.IsNaN(LastElementSize))
			{
				ElementSize = LastElementSize;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseDoubleClick(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnMouseDoubleClick(e);
			ElementSize = ((IElementSizer.CollapseOnDoubleClick && IsElementAutoSize) ? 0.0 : double.NaN);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected override bool WantsDragAndDrop(Point p, out DragAndDropController controller)
		{
			controller = null;
			return IElementSizer.Element != null;
		}

		protected override void StartDragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			AbsoluteStartDragPoint = UIElementExtensions.MapPoint((UIElement)((ControlControllerBase)this).Control, ((Controller)this).StartDragPoint, (UIElement)null);
			OriginalElementSize = ElementSize;
			OriginalIsElementAutoSize = IsElementAutoSize;
			LastElementSize = double.NaN;
			IsSizing = true;
			((Controller)this).StartDragAndDrop(p);
		}

		protected override void DragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			((Controller)this).DragAndDrop(p);
			ElementSize = OriginalElementSize + GetElementSizeChange(UIElementExtensions.MapPoint((UIElement)((ControlControllerBase)this).Control, p, (UIElement)null));
		}

		protected override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			((Controller)this).EndDragAndDrop(accept);
			if (!accept)
			{
				ElementSize = (OriginalIsElementAutoSize ? double.NaN : OriginalElementSize);
			}
			IsSizing = false;
		}

		protected double GetElementSizeChange(Point absoluteDragPoint)
		{
			//IL_004f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0062: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			double num;
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				num = absoluteDragPoint.Y - AbsoluteStartDragPoint.Y;
			}
			else
			{
				num = absoluteDragPoint.X - AbsoluteStartDragPoint.X;
				if (((ControlControllerBase)this).Control.FlowDirection == FlowDirection.RightToLeft)
				{
					num = 0.0 - num;
				}
			}
			if ((int)IElementSizer.Side == 0 || (int)IElementSizer.Side == 1)
			{
				num = 0.0 - num;
			}
			if (!BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
			{
				num = PresentationSource.FromVisual(((ControlControllerBase)this).Control).CompositionTarget.TransformFromDevice.Transform(new Point(num, 0.0)).X;
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected override void OnDragAndDropKeyDown(DXKeyEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnDragAndDropKeyDown(e);
			if (e.Key == Key.LeftCtrl || e.Key == Key.RightCtrl)
			{
				UpdateElementSize();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnDragAndDropKeyUp(DXKeyEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnDragAndDropKeyUp(e);
			if (e.Key == Key.LeftCtrl || e.Key == Key.RightCtrl)
			{
				UpdateElementSize();
			}
		}
	}
	public class ElementSizers : ElementPool<ElementSizer>
	{
		private double _SizingAreaWidth;

		public double SizingAreaWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _SizingAreaWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				value = Math.Max(0.0, value);
				if (_SizingAreaWidth == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_SizingAreaWidth = value;
				foreach (ElementSizer item in base.Items)
				{
					item.SizingAreaWidth = SizingAreaWidth;
				}
			}
		}

		public ElementSizers(PanelBase container)
			: base((Panel)(object)container)
		{
		}

		public ElementSizer Add(FrameworkElement element, Side side)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ElementSizer elementSizer = base.Add();
			elementSizer.Element = element;
			elementSizer.Side = side;
			elementSizer.UpdateBounds();
			return elementSizer;
		}

		protected override ElementSizer CreateItem()
		{
			ElementSizer elementSizer = base.CreateItem();
			elementSizer.SizingAreaWidth = SizingAreaWidth;
			return elementSizer;
		}
	}

	}

	}

		void SendSeparatorsToBack();
	}

		[Description("Gets or sets the width/height of layers (columns/rows) into which items are arranged.")]
		public double LayerWidth
		{
			get
			{
				Size itemSize;
				return LayoutProvider.GetLayerWidth(((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false), out itemSize);
			}
			set
			{
				value = Math.Max(LayerMinWidth, Math.Min(value, LayerMaxWidth));
				FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false);
				if (LayoutProvider.GetLayerWidth(logicalChildren, out var itemSize) != value)
				{
					LayoutProvider.SetLayerWidth(logicalChildren, value, out var itemSize2);
					if (MaximizedElement != null)
					{
						MaximizedElementOriginalSize = new Size(double.IsInfinity(itemSize2.Width) ? MaximizedElementOriginalSize.Width : itemSize2.Width, double.IsInfinity(itemSize2.Height) ? MaximizedElementOriginalSize.Height : itemSize2.Height);
					}
					if (this.ItemsSizeChanged != null)
					{
						this.ItemsSizeChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<Size>(itemSize, itemSize2));
					}
				}
			}
		}

		FrameworkElement IFlowLayoutModel.MaximizedElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (MaximizedElement == null || !UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)MaximizedElement))
				{
					return null;
				}

			}
		}

		public static bool GetIsFlowBreak(UIElement element)
		{
			return (bool)element.GetValue(IsFlowBreakProperty);
		}
