		{
			element.SetValue(IsFlowBreakProperty, value);
		}

		static FlowLayoutControl()
		{
			DefaultLayerMinWidth = 20.0;
			ItemDropAnimationDuration = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500.0);
			ItemMaximizationAnimationDuration = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500.0);
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsAttachedBehavior", typeof(object), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), null);
			AllowAddFlowBreaksDuringItemMovingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowAddFlowBreaksDuringItemMoving", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), null);
			AllowItemMovingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowItemMoving", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), null);
			AnimateItemMaximizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnimateItemMaximization", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			AnimateItemMovingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnimateItemMoving", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), null);
			BreakFlowToFitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BreakFlowToFit", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnBreakFlowToFitChanged();
			}));
			IsFlowBreakProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsFlowBreak", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			ItemContainerStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemContainerStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnItemContainerStyleChanged(e);
			}));
			ItemMovingAnimationDurationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemMovingAnimationDuration", typeof(TimeSpan), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(200.0)));
			ItemsSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnItemsSourceChanged(e);
			}));
			ItemTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnItemTemplateChanged(e);
			}));
			ItemTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnItemTemplateSelectorChanged(e);
			}));
			LayerSeparatorStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayerSeparatorStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).LayerSeparators).ItemStyle = (Style)e.NewValue;
			}));
			LayerSizingCoverBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayerSizingCoverBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(127, byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue))));
			LayerSpaceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayerSpace", typeof(double), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(7.0, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				FlowLayoutControl flowLayoutControl = (FlowLayoutControl)(object)o;
				flowLayoutControl.OnLayerSpaceChanged((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
			}));
			MaximizedElementProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximizedElement", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (!_IgnoreMaximizedElementChange)
				{
					FlowLayoutControl flowLayoutControl = (FlowLayoutControl)(object)o;
					if (e.NewValue != null && (object)((FrameworkElement)e.NewValue).Parent != flowLayoutControl)
					{
						_IgnoreMaximizedElementChange = true;
						o.SetValue(e.Property, e.OldValue);
						_IgnoreMaximizedElementChange = false;
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("MaximizedElement");
					}
					flowLayoutControl.OnMaximizedElementChanged((FrameworkElement)e.OldValue, (FrameworkElement)e.NewValue);
				}
			}));
			MaximizedElementPositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximizedElementPosition", typeof(MaximizedElementPosition), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnMaximizedElementPositionChanged((MaximizedElementPosition)e.OldValue);
			}));
			MaximizedElementPositionIndicatorStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximizedElementPositionIndicatorStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), null);
			MovingItemPlaceHolderBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MovingItemPlaceHolderBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(LayoutControlBase.DefaultMovingItemPlaceHolderBrush));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnOrientationChanged();
			}));
			ShowLayerSeparatorsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowLayerSeparators", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnShowLayerSeparatorsChanged();
			}));
			StretchContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("StretchContent", typeof(bool), typeof(FlowLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((FlowLayoutControl)(object)o).OnStretchContentChanged();
			}));
			LayoutControlStrategyRegistrator.RegisterFlowLayoutControlStrategy();
		}

		public FlowLayoutControl()
		{
			About.CheckLicenseShowNagScreen(typeof(FlowLayoutControl));
			LayerSeparators = new LayerSeparators((Panel)(object)this);
			LayerSeparators.AreVisible = ShowLayerSeparators;
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement CreateItem()
		{
			return new ContentPresenter();
		}

		protected override Rect GetChildBounds(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (child == MaximizedElement)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			return base.GetChildBounds(child);
		}

		protected override Rect GetChildrenBounds()
		{
			Rect bounds = ((PanelBase)this).GetChildrenBounds();
			if (MaximizedElement != null)
			{
				LayoutProvider.UpdateChildrenBounds(ref bounds, base.GetChildBounds(MaximizedElement));
			}
			return bounds;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			foreach (UIElement item in BaseGetInternalElements())
			{
				yield return item;
			}
			foreach (UIElement internalElement in LayerSeparators.GetInternalElements())
			{
				yield return internalElement;
			}
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement GetNeighborChild(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true);
			int num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).IndexOf(child);
			if (num < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count - 1)
			{
				num++;
			}
			else
			{
				if (num <= 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				num--;
			}
			return ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[num];
		}

		protected virtual void InitItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			item.SetBinding(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, new Binding("DataContext")
			{
				Source = item
			});
		}

		protected override bool IsTempChild(UIElement child)
		{
			if (!((PanelBase)this).IsTempChild(child))
			{
				return ((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)LayerSeparators).IsItem(child);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void OnChildRemoved(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnChildRemoved(child);
			if (child == MaximizedElement)
			{
				MaximizedElement = null;
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<UIElement> BaseGetInternalElements()
		{
			return ((PanelBase)this).GetInternalElements();
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)LayerSeparators).MarkItemsAsUnused();
			Size result = base.OnArrange(bounds);
			((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)LayerSeparators).DeleteUnusedItems();
			return result;
		}

		protected override LayoutProviderBase CreateLayoutProvider()
		{
			return new FlowLayoutProvider(this);
		}

		protected override LayoutParametersBase CreateLayoutProviderParameters()
		{
			return new FlowLayoutParameters(base.ItemSpace, LayerSpace, BreakFlowToFit, StretchContent, ShowLayerSeparators, LayerSeparators);
		}

		protected override void ReadChildrenFromXML(XmlReader xml, out FrameworkElement lastChild)
		{
			base.ReadChildrenFromXML(xml, out lastChild);
			MoveLogicalChildrenToStart();
		}

		protected override void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			base.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, MaximizedElementProperty, "MaximizedElement", typeof(FrameworkElement));
			MaximizedElementOriginalSize = SizeHelper.Parse(xml["MaximizedElementOriginalSize"]);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, MaximizedElementPositionProperty, "MaximizedElementPosition", typeof(MaximizedElementPosition));
		}

		protected override void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml)
		{
			base.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, IsFlowBreakProperty, "IsFlowBreak", typeof(bool));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, "Width", typeof(double));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, "Height", typeof(double));
			if (element is GroupBox)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)element).ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(xml);
			}
		}

		protected override void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, MaximizedElementProperty, "MaximizedElement");
			xml.WriteAttributeString("MaximizedElementOriginalSize", MaximizedElementOriginalSize.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, MaximizedElementPositionProperty, "MaximizedElementPosition");
		}

		protected override void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, IsFlowBreakProperty, "IsFlowBreak");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, "Width");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, "Height");
			if (element is GroupBox)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)element).WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(xml);
			}
		}

		protected void MoveLogicalChildrenToStart()
		{
			for (int num = ((Panel)(object)this).Children.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = ((Panel)(object)this).Children[num];
				if (!((PanelBase)this).IsLogicalChild(uIElement))
				{
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.RemoveAt(num);
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add(uIElement);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new FlowLayoutController(this);
		}

		protected override void OnAttachedPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty property, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnAttachedPropertyChanged(child, property, oldValue, newValue);
			if (property == IsFlowBreakProperty)
			{
				OnIsFlowBreakChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnBreakFlowToFitChanged()
		{
			((ScrollControl)this).SetOffset(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsFlowBreakChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemContainerStyleChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<FlowLayoutControl, FrameworkElement>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemPositionChanged(int oldPosition, int newPosition)
		{
			if (newPosition != oldPosition)
			{
				ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<FlowLayoutControl, FrameworkElement>.OnTargetItemPositionChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty, oldPosition, newPosition);
			}
			if (this.ItemPositionChanged != null)
			{
				this.ItemPositionChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<int>(oldPosition, newPosition));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<FlowLayoutControl, FrameworkElement>.OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty, ItemTemplateProperty, ItemTemplateSelectorProperty, ItemContainerStyleProperty, (Func<FlowLayoutControl, IList>)((FlowLayoutControl control) => ((Panel)(object)control).Children), (Func<FlowLayoutControl, FrameworkElement>)((FlowLayoutControl control) => control.CreateItem()), (Action<int, object>)null, (Action<FrameworkElement>)delegate(FrameworkElement item)
			{
				InitItem(item);
			}, (ISupportInitialize)null, (Action<FrameworkElement, object>)null, false, true, (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null, false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemTemplateChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<FlowLayoutControl, FrameworkElement>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemTemplateSelectorChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<FlowLayoutControl, FrameworkElement>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayerSpaceChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnMaximizedElementChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this) && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)(object)this) && AnimateItemMaximization)
			{
				if (ItemMaximizationAnimation != null)
				{
					ItemMaximizationAnimation.Stop();
				}
				ItemMaximizationAnimation = new ElementBoundsAnimation(((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true));
				ItemMaximizationAnimation.StoreOldElementBounds();
			}
			if (oldValue == null)
			{
				OffsetBeforeElementMaximization = ((ScrollControl)this).Offset;
			}
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(MaximizedElementOriginalSize.Width))
				{
					oldValue.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
				}
				else
				{
					oldValue.Width = MaximizedElementOriginalSize.Width;
				}
				if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(MaximizedElementOriginalSize.Height))
				{
					oldValue.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
				}
				else
				{
					oldValue.Height = MaximizedElementOriginalSize.Height;
				}
				if (oldValue is IMaximizableElement)
				{
					((IMaximizableElement)oldValue).AfterNormalization();
				}
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				if (newValue is IMaximizableElement)
				{
					((IMaximizableElement)newValue).BeforeMaximization();
				}
				Size maximizedElementOriginalSize = new Size(newValue.Width, newValue.Height);
				if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)newValue, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty))
				{
					maximizedElementOriginalSize.Width = double.PositiveInfinity;
				}
				if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)newValue, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty))
				{
					maximizedElementOriginalSize.Height = double.PositiveInfinity;
				}
				MaximizedElementOriginalSize = maximizedElementOriginalSize;
				newValue.Width = double.NaN;
				newValue.Height = double.NaN;
			}
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
			if (oldValue == null)
			{
				((ScrollControl)this).BringChildIntoView(GetNeighborChild(newValue), false);
			}
			if (newValue == null)
			{
				((ScrollControl)this).SetOffset(OffsetBeforeElementMaximization);
				((ScrollControl)this).BringChildIntoView(oldValue, false);
			}
			if (ItemMaximizationAnimation != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)this).UpdateLayout();
				ItemMaximizationAnimation.StoreNewElementBounds();
				ItemMaximizationAnimation.Begin(ItemMaximizationAnimationDuration, new CubicEase(), delegate
				{
					ItemMaximizationAnimation = null;
				});
			}
			if (this.MaximizedElementChanged != null)
			{
				this.MaximizedElementChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<FrameworkElement>(oldValue, newValue));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMaximizedElementPositionChanged(MaximizedElementPosition oldValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
			if (this.MaximizedElementPositionChanged != null)
			{
				this.MaximizedElementPositionChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<MaximizedElementPosition>(oldValue, MaximizedElementPosition));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOrientationChanged()
		{
			((ScrollControl)this).SetOffset(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowLayerSeparatorsChanged()
		{
			LayerSeparators.AreVisible = ShowLayerSeparators;
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnStretchContentChanged()
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		void IFlowLayoutControl.BringSeparatorsToFront()
		{
			LayerSeparators.BringToFront();
		}

		bool IFlowLayoutControl.IsLayerSeparator(UIElement element)
		{
			return ((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)LayerSeparators).IsItem(element);
		}

		void IFlowLayoutControl.OnAllowLayerSizingChanged()
		{
			LayerSeparators.AreInteractive = Controller.AllowLayerSizing;
		}

		void IFlowLayoutControl.OnItemPositionChanged(int oldPosition, int newPosition)
		{
			OnItemPositionChanged(oldPosition, newPosition);
		}

		void IFlowLayoutControl.SendSeparatorsToBack()
		{
			LayerSeparators.SendToBack();
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new FlowLayoutControlAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	public class LayerSeparator : ControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ThicknessProperty;

		private bool _IsInteractive;

		private Orientation _Kind = Orientation.Vertical;

		private const string HorizontalRootElementName = "HorizontalRootElement";

		private const string VerticalRootElementName = "VerticalRootElement";

		public Orientation Kind
		{
			get
			{
				return _Kind;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Kind != value)
				{
					_Kind = value;
					UpdateTemplate();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsInteractive
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsInteractive;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_IsInteractive != value)
				{
					_IsInteractive = value;
					OnIsInteractiveChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double Thickness
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ThicknessProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ThicknessProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement HorizontalRootElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement VerticalRootElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement RootElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (Kind != Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					return VerticalRootElement;
				}
				return HorizontalRootElement;
			}
		}

		static LayerSeparator()
		{
			ThicknessProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Thickness", typeof(double), typeof(LayerSeparator), null);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<LayerSeparator>();
		}

		public LayerSeparator()
		{
			OnIsInteractiveChanged();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			HorizontalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			VerticalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsInteractiveChanged()
		{
			((UIElement)this).IsHitTestVisible = IsInteractive;
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (HorizontalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)HorizontalRootElement, Kind == Orientation.Horizontal);
			}
			if (VerticalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)VerticalRootElement, Kind == Orientation.Vertical);
			}
			if (RootElement != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = RootElement.Cursor;
			}
			((UIElement)this).InvalidateMeasure();
		}
	}
	public class LayerSeparators : ElementPool<LayerSeparator>
	{
		public const int SeparatorZIndex = -100;

		public const int TopMostSeparatorZIndex = 100;

		private bool _AreInteractive;

		private bool _AreVisible;

		public bool AreInteractive
		{
			get
			{
				return _AreInteractive;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_AreInteractive == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_AreInteractive = value;
				foreach (LayerSeparator item in base.Items)
				{
					item.IsInteractive = AreInteractive;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool AreVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return _AreVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (AreVisible != value)
				{
					_AreVisible = value;
					CheckStandardItem();
				}
			}
		}

		public double SeparatorThickness => StandardItem.Thickness;

		protected LayerSeparator StandardItem { get; private set; }

		public LayerSeparators(Panel container)
			: base(container)
		{
		}

		public LayerSeparator Add(Orientation kind, Rect bounds)
		{
			LayerSeparator layerSeparator = base.Add();
			layerSeparator.Kind = kind;
			((UIElement)(object)layerSeparator).Measure(SizeHelper.Infinite);
			((UIElement)(object)layerSeparator).Arrange(bounds);
			return layerSeparator;
		}

		public void BringToFront()
		{
			foreach (LayerSeparator item in base.Items)
			{
				FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)(object)item, 100);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			if (StandardItem != null)
			{
				yield return (UIElement)(object)StandardItem;
			}
		}

		public void SendToBack()
		{
			foreach (LayerSeparator item in base.Items)
			{
				FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)(object)item, -100);
			}
		}

		protected override LayerSeparator CreateItem()
		{
			LayerSeparator layerSeparator = base.CreateItem();
			layerSeparator.IsInteractive = AreInteractive;
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)(object)layerSeparator, -100);
			return layerSeparator;
		}

		protected override void OnItemStyleChanged()
		{
			base.OnItemStyleChanged();
			if (StandardItem != null)
			{
				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)StandardItem, FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, (object)base.ItemStyle);
			}
		}

		private void CheckStandardItem()
		{
			if (AreVisible)
			{
				CreateStandardItem();
			}
			else
			{
				DestroyStandardItem();
			}
		}

		private void CreateStandardItem()
		{
			if (StandardItem == null)
			{
				StandardItem = ((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)this).CreateItem();
				base.Container.Children.Add((UIElement)(object)StandardItem);
			}
		}

		private void DestroyStandardItem()
		{
			if (StandardItem != null)
			{
				base.Container.Children.Remove((UIElement)(object)StandardItem);
				StandardItem = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutParameters : LayoutParametersBase
	{
		public bool BreakFlowToFit { get; private set; }

		public LayerSeparators LayerSeparators { get; private set; }

		public double LayerSpace { get; private set; }

		public bool ShowLayerSeparators { get; private set; }

		public bool StretchContent { get; private set; }

		public FlowLayoutParameters(double itemSpace, double layerSpace, bool breakFlowToFit, bool stretchContent, bool showLayerSeparators, LayerSeparators layerSeparators)
			: base(itemSpace)
		{
			LayerSpace = layerSpace;
			BreakFlowToFit = breakFlowToFit;
			StretchContent = stretchContent;
			ShowLayerSeparators = showLayerSeparators;
			LayerSeparators = layerSeparators;
		}
	}
	public struct FlowLayoutItemPosition
	{
		public double LayerOffset;

		public double ItemOffset;

		public FlowLayoutItemPosition(double layerOffset, double itemOffset)
		{
			LayerOffset = layerOffset;
			ItemOffset = itemOffset;
		}
	}
	public struct FlowLayoutItemSize
	{
		public double Width;

		public double Length;

		public FlowLayoutItemSize(double width, double length)
		{
			Width = width;
			Length = length;
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutLayerInfo
	{
		public FlowLayoutItemSize Size;

		public int FirstItemIndex { get; private set; }

		public bool IsHardFlowBreak { get; private set; }

		public FlowLayoutItemPosition Position { get; private set; }

		public int SlotCount => SlotFirstItemIndexes.Count;

		public List<int> SlotFirstItemIndexes { get; private set; }

		public FlowLayoutLayerInfo(int firstItemIndex, bool isHardFlowBreak, FlowLayoutItemPosition position)
		{
			FirstItemIndex = firstItemIndex;
			IsHardFlowBreak = isHardFlowBreak;
			Position = position;
			SlotFirstItemIndexes = new List<int>();
		}
	}
	public enum FlowBreakKind
	{
		None,
		Existing,
		New
	}
	public class FlowLayoutProvider : LayoutProviderBase
	{
		protected delegate int CalculateMaximizedElementIndex(FrameworkElements items);

		protected delegate Size MeasureItem(FrameworkElement item);

		protected delegate void ArrangeItem(FrameworkElement item, ref FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition, ref FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize);

		public virtual bool CanAddHardFlowBreaks => Model.MaximizedElement == null;

		public double LayerSeparatorThickness { get; private set; }

		public FrameworkElements LayoutItems { get; protected set; }

		public virtual Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				if (Model.MaximizedElement == null)
				{
					return Model.Orientation;
				}
				if (Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Left || Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Right)
				{
					return Orientation.Vertical;
				}
				return Orientation.Horizontal;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool ShowLayerSeparators
		{
			get
			{
				if (!StretchContent)
				{
					return Parameters.ShowLayerSeparators;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool StretchContent
		{
			get
			{
				if (Model.MaximizedElement == null)
				{
					return Parameters.StretchContent;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected new IFlowLayoutModel Model => (IFlowLayoutModel)base.Model;

		protected List<FlowLayoutLayerInfo> LayerInfos { get; private set; }

		protected double LayerWidthChangeDirection
		{
			get
			{
				if (Model.MaximizedElement != null && (Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Left || Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Top))
				{
					return -1.0;
				}
				return 1.0;
			}
		}

		protected new FlowLayoutParameters Parameters => base.Parameters as FlowLayoutParameters;

		public FlowLayoutProvider(IFlowLayoutModel model)
			: base(model)
		{
			LayerInfos = new List<FlowLayoutLayerInfo>();
		}

		public override void CopyLayoutInfo(FrameworkElement from, FrameworkElement to)
		{
			base.CopyLayoutInfo(from, to);
			FlowLayoutControl.SetIsFlowBreak(to, FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(from));
		}

		public virtual void UpdateChildrenBounds(ref Rect bounds, Rect maximizedElementBounds)
		{
			if (bounds.IsEmpty)
			{
				bounds = maximizedElementBounds;
			}
			else if (Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Left || Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Right)
			{
				RectHelper.SetLeft(ref bounds, Math.Min(bounds.Left, maximizedElementBounds.Left));
				RectHelper.SetRight(ref bounds, Math.Max(bounds.Right, maximizedElementBounds.Right));
			}
			else
			{
				RectHelper.SetTop(ref bounds, Math.Min(bounds.Top, maximizedElementBounds.Top));
				RectHelper.SetBottom(ref bounds, Math.Max(bounds.Bottom, maximizedElementBounds.Bottom));
			}
		}

		public override void UpdateScrollableAreaBounds(ref Rect bounds)
		{
			base.UpdateScrollableAreaBounds(ref bounds);
			if (Model.MaximizedElement != null)
			{
				GetItemPositionAndSize(bounds, out var itemPosition, out var itemSize);
				GetItemPositionAndSize(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(Model.MaximizedElement), out var itemPosition2, out var itemSize2);
				if (Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Left || Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Top)
				{
					double num = itemPosition2.LayerOffset + itemSize2.Width - itemPosition.LayerOffset;
					itemPosition.LayerOffset += num;
					itemSize.Width -= num;
				}
				else
				{
					itemSize.Width -= itemPosition.LayerOffset + itemSize.Width - itemPosition2.LayerOffset;
				}
				itemSize.Width = Math.Max(0.0, itemSize.Width);
				bounds = GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize);
			}
		}

		public virtual double CalculateLayerWidthChange(LayerSeparator separator, Point positionChange)
		{
			int num = 1 + ((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)Parameters.LayerSeparators).IndexOf(separator);
			double num2 = ((Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? positionChange.Y : positionChange.X);
			return LayerWidthChangeDirection * Math.Round(num2 / (double)num);
		}

		public virtual int GetItemIndex(UIElementCollection items, int visibleIndex)
		{
			if (visibleIndex == ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count)
			{
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count - 1] == Model.MaximizedElement)
				{
					return items.IndexOf(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count - 2]) + 1;
				}
				return items.IndexOf(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count - 1]) + 1;
			}
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[visibleIndex] == Model.MaximizedElement)
			{
				if (visibleIndex == 0)
				{
					return items.IndexOf(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[visibleIndex + 1]);
				}
				return items.IndexOf(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[visibleIndex - 1]) + 1;
			}
			return items.IndexOf(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[visibleIndex]);
		}

		public virtual int GetItemPlaceIndex(FrameworkElement item, Rect bounds, Point p, out FlowBreakKind flowBreakKind)
		{
			flowBreakKind = FlowBreakKind.None;
			if (!bounds.Contains(p) || ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count == 0)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < LayerInfos.Count; i++)
			{
				FlowLayoutLayerInfo flowLayoutLayerInfo = LayerInfos[i];
				if (!GetElementHitTestBounds(GetLayerBounds(flowLayoutLayerInfo), bounds, isFront: true, isBack: true).Contains(p))
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < flowLayoutLayerInfo.SlotCount; j++)
				{
					Rect slotBounds = GetSlotBounds(flowLayoutLayerInfo, j);
					if (!GetElementHitTestBounds(slotBounds, bounds, j == 0, j == flowLayoutLayerInfo.SlotCount - 1).Contains(p))
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool isBeforeItemPlace;
					int num = GetSlotItemPlaceIndex(flowLayoutLayerInfo, j, item, bounds, p, out isBeforeItemPlace);
					if (isBeforeItemPlace)
					{
						if (num == 0 || FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[num]))
						{
							flowBreakKind = FlowBreakKind.Existing;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num++;
					}
					return num;
				}
			}
			foreach (FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo in LayerInfos)
			{
				if (layerInfo.IsHardFlowBreak && GetLayerSpaceBounds(layerInfo, bounds).Contains(p))
				{
					if (CanAddHardFlowBreaks)
					{
						flowBreakKind = FlowBreakKind.New;
					}
					return layerInfo.FirstItemIndex;
				}
			}
			if (GetRemainderBounds(bounds, GetLayerBounds(GetLayerInfo(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count - 1))).Contains(p))
			{
				if (CanAddHardFlowBreaks)
				{
					flowBreakKind = FlowBreakKind.New;
				}
				return ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count;
			}
			return -1;
		}

		public void OffsetLayerSeparators(double layerWidthChange)
		{
			if (layerWidthChange != 0.0)
			{
				layerWidthChange *= LayerWidthChangeDirection;
				for (int i = 0; i < ((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)Parameters.LayerSeparators).Count; i++)
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)(object)((ElementPool<LayerSeparator>)Parameters.LayerSeparators)[i];
					GetItemPositionAndSize(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(frameworkElement), out var itemPosition, out var itemSize);
					itemPosition.LayerOffset += (double)(1 + i) * layerWidthChange;
					frameworkElement.Arrange(GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize));
				}
			}
		}

		public double GetLayerMinWidth(Size itemsMinSize)
		{
			return GetItemSize(itemsMinSize).Width;
		}

		public double GetLayerMaxWidth(Size itemsMaxSize)
		{
			return GetItemSize(itemsMaxSize).Width;
		}

		public double GetLayerWidth(FrameworkElements items, out Size itemSize)
		{
			double num = double.PositiveInfinity;
			itemSize = new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				if (item != Model.MaximizedElement && item.IsVisible)
				{
					double width = GetItemSize(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize(item)).Width;
					if (!double.IsPositiveInfinity(num) && width != num)
					{
						return double.PositiveInfinity;
					}
					num = width;
				}
			}
			if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(num))
			{
				num = 0.0;
			}
			itemSize = GetItemSize(new FlowLayoutItemSize(num, double.PositiveInfinity));
			return num;
		}

		public void SetLayerWidth(FrameworkElements items, double value, out Size itemSize)
		{
			itemSize = GetItemSize(new FlowLayoutItemSize(value, double.PositiveInfinity));
			if (GetLayerWidth(items, out var _) == value)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				if (item != Model.MaximizedElement)
				{
					if (!double.IsPositiveInfinity(itemSize.Width))
					{
						item.Width = itemSize.Width;
					}
					if (!double.IsPositiveInfinity(itemSize.Height))
					{
						item.Height = itemSize.Height;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual double GetLayerDistance(bool isHardFlowBreak)
		{
			if (ShowLayerSeparators)
			{
				return GetLayerSpace(isHardFlowBreak) + LayerSeparatorThickness + GetLayerSpace(isHardFlowBreak);
			}
			return GetLayerSpace(isHardFlowBreak);
		}

		public virtual double GetLayerSpace(bool isHardFlowBreak)
		{
			return Parameters.LayerSpace;
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			CalculateLayout(items, RectHelper.New(maxSize), (FrameworkElements elements) => (Model.MaximizedElement != null) ? (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)elements).Count - 1) : (-1), delegate(FrameworkElement item)
			{
				Size availableSize;
				if (item == Model.MaximizedElement)
				{
					availableSize = CalculateMaximizedElementSize(items, RectHelper.New(maxSize));
				}
				else
				{
					FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize2 = new FlowLayoutItemSize(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
					if (StretchContent)
					{
						itemSize2.Width = GetLayerWidth(RectHelper.New(maxSize));
					}
					availableSize = GetItemSize(itemSize2);
				}
				item.Measure(availableSize);
				return UIElementExtensions.GetDesiredSize((UIElement)item);
			}, null);
			FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize = new FlowLayoutItemSize(0.0, 0.0);
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count != 0)
			{
				for (int num = 0; num < LayerInfos.Count; num++)
				{
					FlowLayoutLayerInfo flowLayoutLayerInfo = LayerInfos[num];
					if (num > 0)
					{
						itemSize.Width += GetLayerDistance(flowLayoutLayerInfo.IsHardFlowBreak);
					}
					itemSize.Width += flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Width;
					itemSize.Length = Math.Max(itemSize.Length, flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Length);
				}
				if (ShowLayerSeparators && LayerInfos.Count == 1)
				{
					itemSize.Width += GetLayerDistance(isHardFlowBreak: false);
				}
			}
			return GetItemSize(itemSize);
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			CalculateLayout(items, bounds, CalculateMaximizedElementIndexForArrange, (FrameworkElement item) => (item == Model.MaximizedElement) ? CalculateMaximizedElementSize(items, viewPortBounds) : UIElementExtensions.GetDesiredSize((UIElement)item), delegate(FrameworkElement item, ref FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition, ref FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
			{
				if (item == Model.MaximizedElement)
				{
					itemPosition.ItemOffset = GetLayerContentStart(viewPortBounds);
				}
				else
				{
					itemSize = GetItemSize(item.DesiredSize);
					if (StretchContent)
					{
						itemSize.Width = GetLayerWidth(viewPortBounds);
					}
				}
				item.Arrange(GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize));
			});
			if (ShowLayerSeparators && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count != 0)
			{
				AddLayerSeparators(bounds, viewPortBounds);
			}
			return RectHelper.Size(bounds);
		}

		protected override void OnParametersChanged()
		{
			base.OnParametersChanged();
			if (ShowLayerSeparators)
			{
				LayerSeparatorThickness = Parameters.LayerSeparators.SeparatorThickness;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void CalculateLayout(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, CalculateMaximizedElementIndex calculateMaximizedElementIndex, MeasureItem measureItem, ArrangeItem arrangeItem)
		{
			int num = calculateMaximizedElementIndex(items);
			if (num != -1)
			{
				PrepareMaximizedElement(items, num);
			}
			double layerContentStart = GetLayerContentStart(bounds);
			double num2 = layerContentStart;
			FlowLayoutItemPosition flowLayoutItemPosition = new FlowLayoutItemPosition(GetLayerStart(bounds), num2);
			LayoutItems = items;
			LayerInfos.Clear();
			FlowLayoutLayerInfo flowLayoutLayerInfo = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement item = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i];
				FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize = GetItemSize(measureItem(item));
				if (i == 0)
				{
					flowLayoutLayerInfo = new FlowLayoutLayerInfo(0, isHardFlowBreak: true, flowLayoutItemPosition);
					LayerInfos.Add(flowLayoutLayerInfo);
				}
				else
				{
					bool isHardFlowBreak = false;
					if ((num == -1 && IsFlowBreak(item, bounds, num2, GetSlotLength(items, i, itemSize), out isHardFlowBreak)) || (num != -1 && (i == num + 1 || i == num)))
					{
						num2 = layerContentStart;
						flowLayoutItemPosition.LayerOffset += flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Width + GetLayerDistance(isHardFlowBreak);
						flowLayoutItemPosition.ItemOffset = num2;
						flowLayoutLayerInfo = new FlowLayoutLayerInfo(i, isHardFlowBreak, flowLayoutItemPosition);
						LayerInfos.Add(flowLayoutLayerInfo);
					}
					else
					{
						flowLayoutItemPosition.ItemOffset = num2;
					}
				}
				flowLayoutLayerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes.Add(i);
				FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition = flowLayoutItemPosition;
				FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize2 = itemSize;
				if (IsSlotWithMultipleItems(items, i))
				{
					CalculateLayoutForSlotWithMultipleItems(items, ref i, itemPosition, ref itemSize2, measureItem, arrangeItem);
				}
				else
				{
					arrangeItem?.Invoke(item, ref itemPosition, ref itemSize2);
				}
				num2 = itemPosition.ItemOffset;
				flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Length = num2 + itemSize2.Length - layerContentStart;
				flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Width = Math.Max(flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Width, itemSize2.Width);
				num2 += GetSlotMaxLength(item, itemSize) + Parameters.ItemSpace;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void CalculateLayoutForSlotWithMultipleItems(FrameworkElements items, ref int itemIndex, FlowLayoutItemPosition slotPosition, ref FlowLayoutItemSize slotSize, MeasureItem measureItem, ArrangeItem arrangeItem)
		{
		}

		protected virtual FlowLayoutItemSize CalculateLayerSeparatorSize(Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			FlowLayoutItemSize result = new FlowLayoutItemSize(LayerSeparatorThickness, GetLayerContentLength(viewPortBounds));
			foreach (FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo in LayerInfos)
			{
				result.Length = Math.Max(result.Length, layerInfo.Size.Length);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual double GetSlotLength(FrameworkElements items, int itemIndex, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			return itemSize.Length;
		}

		protected virtual double GetSlotMaxLength(FrameworkElement item, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			return itemSize.Length;
		}

		protected virtual double GetSlotMaxWidth(FrameworkElement item, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			return itemSize.Width;
		}

		protected double GetSlotMaxWidth(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i];
				if (frameworkElement != Model.MaximizedElement)
				{
					FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize = GetItemSize(UIElementExtensions.GetDesiredSize((UIElement)frameworkElement));
					num = Math.Max(num, GetSlotMaxWidth(frameworkElement, itemSize));
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected virtual bool NeedsFullSlot(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual int CalculateMaximizedElementIndexForArrange(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			if (Model.MaximizedElement == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Left || Model.MaximizedElementPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Top)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - 1;
		}

		protected virtual Size CalculateMaximizedElementSize(FrameworkElements items, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			double width = Math.Max(0.0, GetLayerWidth(viewPortBounds) - (GetLayerDistance(isHardFlowBreak: false) + GetSlotMaxWidth(items)));
			return GetItemSize(new FlowLayoutItemSize(width, GetLayerContentLength(viewPortBounds)));
		}

		protected virtual void PrepareMaximizedElement(FrameworkElements items, int maximizedElementIndex)
		{
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Remove(Model.MaximizedElement);
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Insert(maximizedElementIndex, Model.MaximizedElement);
		}

		protected FlowLayoutLayerInfo GetLayerInfo(int itemIndex)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < LayerInfos.Count; i++)
			{
				if (itemIndex < LayerInfos[i].FirstItemIndex)
				{
					if (i != 0)
					{
						return LayerInfos[i - 1];
					}
					return null;
				}
			}
			if (LayerInfos.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return LayerInfos[LayerInfos.Count - 1];
		}

		protected int GetSlotLastItemIndex(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex)
		{
			if (slotIndex == layerInfo.SlotCount - 1)
			{
				int num = LayerInfos.IndexOf(layerInfo);
				if (num == LayerInfos.Count - 1)
				{
					return ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems).Count - 1;
				}
				return LayerInfos[num + 1].FirstItemIndex - 1;
			}
			return layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex + 1] - 1;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsFlowBreak(FrameworkElement item, Rect bounds, double slotOffset, double slotLength, out bool isHardFlowBreak)
		{
			isHardFlowBreak = FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(item);
			if (!StretchContent)
			{
				if (!isHardFlowBreak)
				{
					if (Parameters.BreakFlowToFit)
					{
						return slotOffset + slotLength > GetLayerContentEnd(bounds);
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected bool IsSlotWithMultipleItems(FrameworkElements items, int slotFirstItemIndex)
		{
			if (!NeedsFullSlot(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[slotFirstItemIndex]) && slotFirstItemIndex < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - 1)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[slotFirstItemIndex + 1];
				if (!NeedsFullSlot(frameworkElement) && !FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(frameworkElement))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected void AddLayerSeparator(Rect areaBounds, double layerEnd, FlowLayoutItemSize separatorSize)
		{
			Parameters.LayerSeparators.Add(Orientation, GetLayerSeparatorBounds(areaBounds, layerEnd, separatorSize));
		}

		protected virtual void AddLayerSeparators(Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			FlowLayoutItemSize separatorSize = CalculateLayerSeparatorSize(viewPortBounds);
			double num = GetLayerStart(bounds);
			for (int i = 0; i < Math.Max(1, LayerInfos.Count - 1); i++)
			{
				FlowLayoutLayerInfo flowLayoutLayerInfo = LayerInfos[i];
				if (i > 0)
				{
					num += GetLayerDistance(flowLayoutLayerInfo.IsHardFlowBreak);
				}
				num += flowLayoutLayerInfo.Size.Width;
				AddLayerSeparator(bounds, num, separatorSize);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetLayerSeparatorBounds(Rect areaBounds, double layerEnd, FlowLayoutItemSize separatorSize)
		{
			return GetItemBounds(new FlowLayoutItemPosition(layerEnd + Parameters.LayerSpace, GetLayerContentStart(areaBounds)), separatorSize);
		}

		protected Rect GetItemBounds(FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			double num = Math.Max(0.0, itemSize.Length);
			double num2 = Math.Max(0.0, itemSize.Width);
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return new Rect(itemPosition.ItemOffset, itemPosition.LayerOffset, num, num2);
			}
			return new Rect(itemPosition.LayerOffset, itemPosition.ItemOffset, num2, num);
		}

		protected void GetItemPositionAndSize(Rect itemBounds, out FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition, out FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				itemPosition = new FlowLayoutItemPosition(itemBounds.Y, itemBounds.X);
				itemSize = new FlowLayoutItemSize(itemBounds.Height, itemBounds.Width);
			}
			else
			{
				itemPosition = new FlowLayoutItemPosition(itemBounds.X, itemBounds.Y);
				itemSize = new FlowLayoutItemSize(itemBounds.Width, itemBounds.Height);
			}
		}

		protected FlowLayoutItemSize GetItemSize(Size itemSize)
		{
			GetItemPositionAndSize(RectHelper.New(itemSize), out var _, out var itemSize2);
			return itemSize2;
		}

		protected Size GetItemSize(FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			return RectHelper.Size(GetItemBounds(new FlowLayoutItemPosition(0.0, 0.0), itemSize));
		}

		protected double GetLayerStart(Rect bounds)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return bounds.Left;
			}
			return bounds.Top;
		}

		protected double GetLayerWidth(Rect bounds)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return bounds.Width;
			}
			return bounds.Height;
		}

		protected double GetLayerEnd(Rect bounds)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return bounds.Right;
			}
			return bounds.Bottom;
		}

		protected double GetLayerCenter(Rect bounds)
		{
			return (GetLayerStart(bounds) + GetLayerEnd(bounds)) / 2.0;
		}

		protected double GetLayerOffset(Point p)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return p.X;
			}
			return p.Y;
		}

		protected double GetLayerContentStart(Rect bounds)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return bounds.Top;
			}
			return bounds.Left;
		}

		protected double GetLayerContentLength(Rect bounds)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return bounds.Height;
			}
			return bounds.Width;
		}

		protected double GetLayerContentEnd(Rect bounds)
		{
			return GetLayerContentStart(bounds) + GetLayerContentLength(bounds);
		}

		protected double GetLayerContentCenter(Rect bounds)
		{
			return (GetLayerContentStart(bounds) + GetLayerContentEnd(bounds)) / 2.0;
		}

		protected double GetLayerContentOffset(Point p)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return p.Y;
			}
			return p.X;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetElementHitTestBounds(Rect elementBounds, Rect bounds, bool isFront, bool isBack)
		{
			GetItemPositionAndSize(elementBounds, out var itemPosition, out var itemSize);
			itemSize.Width += Parameters.ItemSpace;
			if (isFront)
			{
				double num = Math.Max(0.0, itemPosition.ItemOffset - GetLayerContentStart(bounds));
				itemPosition.ItemOffset -= num;
				itemSize.Length += num;
			}
			if (isBack)
			{
				itemSize.Length = Math.Max(0.0, GetLayerContentEnd(bounds) - itemPosition.ItemOffset);
			}
			else
			{
				itemSize.Length += Parameters.ItemSpace;
			}
			return GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize);
		}

		protected Rect GetLayerBounds(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo)
		{
			return GetItemBounds(layerInfo.Position, layerInfo.Size);
		}

		protected Rect GetLayerSpaceBounds(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, Rect bounds)
		{
			FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition = new FlowLayoutItemPosition(0.0, GetLayerContentStart(bounds));
			FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize = new FlowLayoutItemSize(0.0, GetLayerContentLength(bounds));
			if (layerInfo.FirstItemIndex == 0)
			{
				itemSize.Width = Math.Max(0.0, layerInfo.Position.LayerOffset - GetLayerStart(bounds));
			}
			else
			{
				itemSize.Width = GetLayerDistance(layerInfo.IsHardFlowBreak);
			}
			itemPosition.LayerOffset = layerInfo.Position.LayerOffset - itemSize.Width;
			return GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize);
		}

		protected Rect GetRemainderBounds(Rect bounds, Rect lastLayerBounds)
		{
			GetItemPositionAndSize(bounds, out var itemPosition, out var itemSize);
			double num = GetLayerEnd(lastLayerBounds) - itemPosition.LayerOffset;
			itemPosition.LayerOffset += num;
			itemSize.Width = Math.Max(0.0, itemSize.Width - num);
			return GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize);
		}

		protected Rect GetSlotBounds(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex)
		{
			GetItemPositionAndSize(GetLayerBounds(layerInfo), out var itemPosition, out var itemSize);
			int index = layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex];
			GetItemPositionAndSize(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[index]), out var itemPosition2, out var itemSize2);
			itemSize.Length = itemPosition.ItemOffset + itemSize.Length - itemPosition2.ItemOffset;
			itemPosition.ItemOffset = itemPosition2.ItemOffset;
			if (slotIndex < layerInfo.SlotCount - 1)
			{
				index = layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex + 1];
				GetItemPositionAndSize(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[index]), out itemPosition2, out itemSize2);
				itemSize.Length = itemPosition2.ItemOffset - Parameters.ItemSpace - itemPosition.ItemOffset;
			}
			return GetItemBounds(itemPosition, itemSize);
		}

		protected virtual int GetSlotItemPlaceIndex(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex, FrameworkElement item, Rect bounds, Point p, out bool isBeforeItemPlace)
		{
			int num = layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex];
			if (NeedsFullSlot(item) || NeedsFullSlot(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)LayoutItems)[num]))
			{
				isBeforeItemPlace = GetLayerContentOffset(p) < GetLayerContentCenter(GetSlotBounds(layerInfo, slotIndex));
				if (isBeforeItemPlace)
				{
					return num;
				}
				return GetSlotLastItemIndex(layerInfo, slotIndex);
			}
			isBeforeItemPlace = false;
			return -1;
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutController : LayoutControllerBase
	{
		private bool _AllowLayerSizing;

		public new IFlowLayoutControl ILayoutControl => base.ILayoutControl as IFlowLayoutControl;

		public new FlowLayoutProvider LayoutProvider => (FlowLayoutProvider)base.LayoutProvider;

		public bool AllowLayerSizing
		{
			get
			{
				return _AllowLayerSizing;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_AllowLayerSizing != value)
				{
					_AllowLayerSizing = value;
					ILayoutControl.OnAllowLayerSizingChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool AllowMaximizedElementMoving { get; set; }

		public FlowLayoutController(IFlowLayoutControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CanDragAndDropItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (base.CanDragAndDropItem(item))
			{
				return item != ILayoutControl.MaximizedElement;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual bool CanResizeLayers()
		{
			return !double.IsInfinity(ILayoutControl.LayerWidth);
		}

		protected override DragAndDropController CreateItemDragAndDropControler(Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
		{
			return (DragAndDropController)(object)new FlowLayoutItemDragAndDropController((Controller)(object)this, startDragPoint, dragControl);
		}

		protected override bool WantsDragAndDrop(Point p, out DragAndDropController controller)
		{
			if (AllowMaximizedElementMoving && ILayoutControl.MaximizedElement != null)
			{
				FrameworkElement moveableItem = GetMoveableItem(p);
				if (moveableItem == ILayoutControl.MaximizedElement)
				{
					controller = (DragAndDropController)(object)new FlowLayoutMaximizedElementDragAndDropController((Controller)(object)this, p);
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (AllowLayerSizing && CanResizeLayers())
			{
				UIElement uIElement = ((PanelControllerBase)this).IPanel.ChildAt(p, true, false, false);
				if (ILayoutControl.IsLayerSeparator(uIElement))
				{
					controller = (DragAndDropController)(object)new FlowLayoutLayerSizingController((Controller)(object)this, p, (LayerSeparator)(object)uIElement);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return base.WantsDragAndDrop(p, out controller);
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutItemDragAndDropController : LayoutItemDragAndDropControllerBase
	{
		protected static ElementBoundsAnimation ElementPositionsAnimation { get; set; }

		protected new FlowLayoutController Controller => (FlowLayoutController)base.Controller;

		private Dictionary<UIElement, bool> IsFlowBreakOriginalValues { get; set; }

		public FlowLayoutItemDragAndDropController(Controller controller, Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
			: base(controller, startDragPoint, dragControl)
		{
			if (ElementPositionsAnimation != null)
			{
				ElementPositionsAnimation.Stop();
				ElementPositionsAnimation = null;
			}
		}

		public override void DragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).DragAndDrop(p);
			if (ElementPositionsAnimation != null && ElementPositionsAnimation.IsActive)
			{
				return;
			}
			FlowBreakKind flowBreakKind;
			int itemPlaceIndex = Controller.LayoutProvider.GetItemPlaceIndex(base.DragControl, ((PanelControllerBase)Controller).ClientBounds, GetItemPlacePoint(p), out flowBreakKind);
			if (itemPlaceIndex == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = Controller.LayoutProvider.GetItemIndex(base.DragControlParent.Children, itemPlaceIndex);
			if (flowBreakKind == FlowBreakKind.New && !Controller.ILayoutControl.AllowAddFlowBreaksDuringItemMoving)
			{
				flowBreakKind = ((itemPlaceIndex == 0) ? FlowBreakKind.Existing : FlowBreakKind.None);
			}
			bool flag = flowBreakKind != FlowBreakKind.None;
			FrameworkElements layoutItems = Controller.LayoutProvider.LayoutItems;
			int num2 = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems).IndexOf(base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			int num3 = base.DragControlParent.Children.IndexOf(base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			bool flag2 = num2 == 0 || FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			bool flag3 = flag2 && (num2 == ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems).Count - 1 || FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems)[num2 + 1]));
			if (num > num3)
			{
				num--;
			}
			if (num3 == num && (!(num2 + 1 == itemPlaceIndex && flag) || (flag2 && flowBreakKind != FlowBreakKind.Existing)) && (itemPlaceIndex != num2 || (!(!flag && flag2) && (flowBreakKind != FlowBreakKind.New || flag3))))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Controller.ILayoutControl.AnimateItemMoving)
			{
				ElementPositionsAnimation = new ElementBoundsAnimation(layoutItems);
				ElementPositionsAnimation.StoreOldElementBounds();
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				if (flag3)
				{
					OnGroupFirstItemChanged(base.DragControlPlaceHolder, null);
				}
				else
				{
					OnGroupFirstItemChanged(base.DragControlPlaceHolder, ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems)[num2 + 1]);
				}
			}
			if (flowBreakKind == FlowBreakKind.Existing)
			{
				OnGroupFirstItemChanged(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems)[itemPlaceIndex], base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			}
			base.DragControlParent.Children.RemoveAt(num3);
			base.DragControlParent.Children.Insert(num, base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			if (flag2 && (num2 > 0 || itemPlaceIndex == 0) && num2 < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems).Count - 1)
			{
				SetIsFlowBreakAndStoreOriginalValue(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems)[num2 + 1], value: true);
			}
			if (flag && itemPlaceIndex < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems).Count)
			{
				SetIsFlowBreakAndStoreOriginalValue(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)layoutItems)[itemPlaceIndex], flowBreakKind == FlowBreakKind.New);
			}
			FlowLayoutControl.SetIsFlowBreak(base.DragControlPlaceHolder, flag && itemPlaceIndex > 0);
			if (Controller.ILayoutControl.AnimateItemMoving)
			{
				((Controller)Controller).Control.UpdateLayout();
				ElementPositionsAnimation.StoreNewElementBounds();
				ElementPositionsAnimation.Begin(Controller.ILayoutControl.ItemMovingAnimationDuration);
			}
		}

		public override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (ElementPositionsAnimation != null)
			{
				ElementPositionsAnimation.Stop();
				ElementPositionsAnimation = null;
			}
			if (Controller.ILayoutControl.AnimateItemMoving)
			{
				FrameworkElements val = new FrameworkElements();
				((Collection<FrameworkElement>)val).Add(base.DragControl);
				ElementPositionsAnimation = new ElementBoundsAnimation(val);
				ElementPositionsAnimation.StoreOldElementBounds(((Controller)Controller).Control);
			}
			int num = base.DragControlParent.Children.IndexOf(base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			bool isFlowBreak = FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
			((DragAndDropController)this).EndDragAndDrop(accept);
			if (accept)
			{
				if (base.DragControlIndex != num || FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(base.DragControl) != isFlowBreak)
				{
					int oldPosition = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((IPanel)Controller.ILayoutControl).GetLogicalChildren(false)).IndexOf(base.DragControl);
					base.DragControlParent.Children.Remove(base.DragControl);
					base.DragControlParent.Children.Insert(num, base.DragControl);
					FlowLayoutControl.SetIsFlowBreak(base.DragControl, isFlowBreak);
					int newPosition = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((IPanel)Controller.ILayoutControl).GetLogicalChildren(false)).IndexOf(base.DragControl);
					Controller.ILayoutControl.OnItemPositionChanged(oldPosition, newPosition);
				}
				SendIsFlowBreakChangeNotifications();
			}
			else
			{
				RestoreIsFlowBreakOriginalValues();
			}
			if (Controller.ILayoutControl.AnimateItemMoving)
			{
				object storedDragControlOpacity = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.DragControl, UIElement.OpacityProperty);
				base.DragControl.Opacity = 0.0;
				((Controller)Controller).Control.UpdateLayout();
				ElementPositionsAnimation.StoreNewElementBounds();
				object storedDragControlZIndex = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.DragControl, Panel.ZIndexProperty);
				FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex(base.DragControl, 1000);
				object storedDragControlIsHitTestVisible = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.DragControl, UIElement.IsHitTestVisibleProperty);
				base.DragControl.IsHitTestVisible = false;
				ElementPositionsAnimation.Begin(FlowLayoutControl.ItemDropAnimationDuration, new ExponentialEase
				{
					Exponent = 5.0
				}, delegate
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.DragControl, Panel.ZIndexProperty, storedDragControlZIndex);
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.DragControl, UIElement.IsHitTestVisibleProperty, storedDragControlIsHitTestVisible);
					ElementPositionsAnimation = null;
				});
				Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.DragControl, UIElement.OpacityProperty, storedDragControlOpacity);
				}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Point GetItemPlacePoint(Point p)
		{
			return p;
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupFirstItemChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
		}

		protected void RestoreIsFlowBreakOriginalValues()
		{
			if (IsFlowBreakOriginalValues == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<UIElement, bool> isFlowBreakOriginalValue in IsFlowBreakOriginalValues)
			{
				FlowLayoutControl.SetIsFlowBreak(isFlowBreakOriginalValue.Key, isFlowBreakOriginalValue.Value);
			}
		}

		protected void SendIsFlowBreakChangeNotifications()
		{
			if (IsFlowBreakOriginalValues == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((IPanel)Controller.ILayoutControl).GetLogicalChildren(false);
			foreach (KeyValuePair<UIElement, bool> isFlowBreakOriginalValue in IsFlowBreakOriginalValues)
			{
				if (FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(isFlowBreakOriginalValue.Key) != isFlowBreakOriginalValue.Value)
				{
					int num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).IndexOf((FrameworkElement)isFlowBreakOriginalValue.Key);
					Controller.ILayoutControl.OnItemPositionChanged(num, num);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void SetIsFlowBreakAndStoreOriginalValue(UIElement element, bool value)
		{
			if (FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(element) != value)
			{
				if (IsFlowBreakOriginalValues == null)
				{
					IsFlowBreakOriginalValues = new Dictionary<UIElement, bool>();
				}
				if (!IsFlowBreakOriginalValues.ContainsKey(element))
				{
					IsFlowBreakOriginalValues.Add(element, FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(element));
				}
				FlowLayoutControl.SetIsFlowBreak(element, value);
			}
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutMaximizedElementDragAndDropController : DragAndDropController
	{
		protected FlowLayoutController Controller => (FlowLayoutController)(object)((DragAndDropController)this).Controller;

		protected MaximizedElementPosition MaximizedElementPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return Controller.ILayoutControl.MaximizedElementPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				if (MaximizedElementPosition != value)
				{
					Controller.ILayoutControl.MaximizedElementPosition = value;
					if (DragImage != null)
					{
						DragImage.SelectedPosition = value;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected MaximizedElementPosition OriginalMaximizedElementPosition { get; private set; }

		protected MaximizedElementPositionIndicator DragImage => (MaximizedElementPositionIndicator)(object)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage;

		public FlowLayoutMaximizedElementDragAndDropController(Controller controller, Point startDragPoint)
			: base(controller, startDragPoint)
		{
		}

		public override void StartDragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			OriginalMaximizedElementPosition = MaximizedElementPosition;
			((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragAndDrop(p);
		}

		public override void DragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).DragAndDrop(p);
			MaximizedElementPosition = GetMaximizedElementPosition(p) ?? MaximizedElementPosition;
		}

		public override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).EndDragAndDrop(accept);
			if (!accept)
			{
				MaximizedElementPosition = OriginalMaximizedElementPosition;
			}
		}

		protected MaximizedElementPosition? GetMaximizedElementPosition(Point p)
		{
			Point point = PointHelper.Subtract(p, ((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragPoint);
			double num = DragImage.NoChangeAreaSize / 2.0;
			if (Math.Abs(point.X) <= num && Math.Abs(point.Y) <= num)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (Math.Abs(point.X) > Math.Abs(point.Y))
			{
				return (!(point.X < 0.0)) ? MaximizedElementPosition.Right : MaximizedElementPosition.Left;
			}
			return (point.Y < 0.0) ? MaximizedElementPosition.Top : MaximizedElementPosition.Bottom;
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement CreateDragImage()
		{
			return (FrameworkElement)(object)new MaximizedElementPositionIndicator
			{
				SelectedPosition = MaximizedElementPosition,
				Style = Controller.ILayoutControl.MaximizedElementPositionIndicatorStyle
			};
		}

		protected override Point GetDragImagePosition(Point p)
		{
			Point startDragPoint = ((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragPoint;
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)DragImage).ActualWidth == 0.0)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)DragImage).UpdateLayout();
			}
			startDragPoint.X -= Math.Round(((FrameworkElement)(object)DragImage).ActualWidth / 2.0);
			startDragPoint.Y -= Math.Round(((FrameworkElement)(object)DragImage).ActualHeight / 2.0);
			return startDragPoint;
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "NoChangeElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "LeftElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TopElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "RightElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "BottomElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	public class MaximizedElementPositionIndicator : ControlBase
	{
		public const double DefaultNoChangeAreaSize = 30.0;

		private MaximizedElementPosition _SelectedPosition;

		private const string NoChangeElementName = "NoChangeElement";

		private const string LeftElementName = "LeftElement";

		private const string TopElementName = "TopElement";

		private const string RightElementName = "RightElement";

		private const string BottomElementName = "BottomElement";

		public double NoChangeAreaSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (NoChangeElement == null)
				{
					return 30.0;
				}
				return NoChangeElement.ActualWidth;
			}
		}

		public MaximizedElementPosition SelectedPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return _SelectedPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				if (SelectedPosition != value)
				{
					_SelectedPosition = value;
					UpdateTemplate();
				}
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement NoChangeElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement LeftElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement TopElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement RightElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement BottomElement { get; set; }

		static MaximizedElementPositionIndicator()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<MaximizedElementPositionIndicator>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			NoChangeElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("NoChangeElement") as FrameworkElement;
			LeftElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("LeftElement") as FrameworkElement;
			TopElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("TopElement") as FrameworkElement;
			RightElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("RightElement") as FrameworkElement;
			BottomElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("BottomElement") as FrameworkElement;
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (LeftElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)LeftElement, SelectedPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Left);
			}
			if (TopElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)TopElement, SelectedPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Top);
			}
			if (RightElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)RightElement, SelectedPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Right);
			}
			if (BottomElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)BottomElement, SelectedPosition == MaximizedElementPosition.Bottom);
			}
		}
	}
	public class FlowLayoutLayerSizingController : DragAndDropController
	{
		private double _LayerWidthChange;

		public override Cursor DragCursor => ((FrameworkElement)(object)DragSeparator).Cursor;

		public override bool IsImmediateDragAndDrop => true;

		protected Rectangle ChildCover { get; set; }

		protected FlowLayoutController Controller => (FlowLayoutController)(object)((DragAndDropController)this).Controller;

		protected LayerSeparator DragSeparator { get; private set; }

		protected double LayerWidthChange
		{
			get
			{
				return _LayerWidthChange;
			}
			set
			{
				value = Math.Max(LayerWidthMinChange, Math.Min(value, LayerWidthMaxChange));
				if (_LayerWidthChange != value)
				{
					Controller.LayoutProvider.OffsetLayerSeparators(value - LayerWidthChange);
					_LayerWidthChange = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected double LayerWidthMinChange => Controller.ILayoutControl.LayerMinWidth - Controller.ILayoutControl.LayerWidth;

		protected double LayerWidthMaxChange => Controller.ILayoutControl.LayerMaxWidth - Controller.ILayoutControl.LayerWidth;

		public FlowLayoutLayerSizingController(Controller controller, Point startDragPoint, LayerSeparator dragSeparator)
			: base(controller, startDragPoint)
		{
			DragSeparator = dragSeparator;
		}

		public override void StartDragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			Controller.ILayoutControl.BringSeparatorsToFront();
			CreateChildCover();
			((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragAndDrop(p);
		}

		public override void DragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).DragAndDrop(p);
			LayerWidthChange = Controller.LayoutProvider.CalculateLayerWidthChange(DragSeparator, new Point(p.X - ((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragPoint.X, p.Y - ((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragPoint.Y));
		}

		public override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).EndDragAndDrop(accept);
			DestroyChildCover();
			Controller.ILayoutControl.SendSeparatorsToBack();
			if (accept)
			{
				Controller.ILayoutControl.LayerWidth += LayerWidthChange;
			}
			else
			{
				((Controller)Controller).Control.InvalidateArrange();
			}
		}

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			foreach (UIElement item in BaseGetInternalElements())
			{
				yield return item;
			}
			if (ChildCover != null)
			{
				yield return ChildCover;
			}
		}

		public override void OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).OnMeasure(availableSize);
			ChildCover.Measure(availableSize);
		}

		public override void OnArrange(Size finalSize)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).OnArrange(finalSize);
			ChildCover.Arrange(RectHelper.New(ChildCover.DesiredSize));
		}

		protected void CreateChildCover()
		{
			ChildCover = new Rectangle();
			ChildCover.Fill = Controller.ILayoutControl.LayerSizingCoverBrush;
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetSize((FrameworkElement)ChildCover, FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize(((Controller)Controller).Control));
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)ChildCover, 99);
			((PanelControllerBase)Controller).IPanel.Children.Add(ChildCover);
		}

		protected void DestroyChildCover()
		{
			((PanelControllerBase)Controller).IPanel.Children.Remove(ChildCover);
			ChildCover = null;
		}

		private IEnumerable<UIElement> BaseGetInternalElements()
		{
			return ((DragAndDropController)this).GetInternalElements();
		}
	}
	public abstract class MaximizableHeaderedContentControlBase : HeaderedContentControlBase
	{
		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CurrentContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CurrentContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(MaximizableHeaderedContentControlBase), null);

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaximizedContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximizedContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(MaximizableHeaderedContentControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((MaximizableHeaderedContentControlBase)(object)o).OnMaximizedContentTemplateChanged();
		}));

		private bool _IsMaximizedCore;

		public DataTemplate CurrentContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(CurrentContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(CurrentContentTemplateProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate MaximizedContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(MaximizedContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(MaximizedContentTemplateProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected bool IsMaximizedCore
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsMaximizedCore;
			}
			set
			{
				if (IsMaximizedCore != value)
				{
					_IsMaximizedCore = value;
					OnIsMaximizedCoreChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public MaximizableHeaderedContentControlBase()
		{
			UpdateCurrentContentTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual DependencyProperty GetCurrentContentTemplateProperty()
		{
			if (!IsMaximizedCore || MaximizedContentTemplate == null)
			{
				return ContentControlBase.ContentTemplateProperty;
			}
			return MaximizedContentTemplateProperty;
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsMaximizedCoreChanged()
		{
			UpdateCurrentContentTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnMaximizedContentTemplateChanged()
		{
			UpdateCurrentContentTemplate();
		}

		protected void UpdateCurrentContentTemplate()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(CurrentContentTemplateProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(GetCurrentContentTemplateProperty())
			});
		}
	}
	public enum GroupBoxButtonKind
	{
		Minimize,
		Unminimize,
		Maximize,
		Unmaximize
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "MinimizeElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "UnminimizeElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "MaximizeElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "UnmaximizeElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	public class GroupBoxButton : DXButton
	{
		private GroupBoxButtonKind _Kind;

		private const string MinimizeElementName = "MinimizeElement";

		private const string UnminimizeElementName = "UnminimizeElement";

		private const string MaximizeElementName = "MaximizeElement";

		private const string UnmaximizeElementName = "UnmaximizeElement";

		public GroupBoxButtonKind Kind
		{
			get
			{
				return _Kind;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Kind != value)
				{
					_Kind = value;
					UpdateTemplate();
				}
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement MinimizeElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement UnminimizeElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement MaximizeElement { get; set; }

		protected FrameworkElement UnmaximizeElement { get; set; }

		public GroupBoxButtonController Controller => (GroupBoxButtonController)(object)((DXButton)this).Controller;

		static GroupBoxButton()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<GroupBoxButton>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			MinimizeElement = (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("MinimizeElement");
			UnminimizeElement = (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("UnminimizeElement");
			MaximizeElement = (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("MaximizeElement");
			UnmaximizeElement = (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("UnmaximizeElement");
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (MinimizeElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)MinimizeElement, Kind == GroupBoxButtonKind.Minimize);
			}
			if (UnminimizeElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)UnminimizeElement, Kind == GroupBoxButtonKind.Unminimize);
			}
			if (MaximizeElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)MaximizeElement, Kind == GroupBoxButtonKind.Maximize);
			}
			if (UnmaximizeElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)UnmaximizeElement, Kind == GroupBoxButtonKind.Unmaximize);
			}
		}

		protected override ControlControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (ControlControllerBase)(object)new GroupBoxButtonController((IControl)(object)this);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new GroupBoxButtonAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	public class GroupBoxButtonController : DXButtonController
	{
		public GroupBoxButtonController(IControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		internal void InvokeClick()
		{
			((DXButtonController)this).OnClick();
		}
	}

			}
		}

		public event ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> StateChanged
		{
			[CompilerGenerated]
			add
			{
				ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> val = this.m_StateChanged;
				ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> val2;
				do
				{
					val2 = val;
					ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> value2 = (ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState>)(object)Delegate.Combine((Delegate)(object)val2, (Delegate)(object)value);
					val = Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref this.m_StateChanged, value2, val2);
				}
				while (val != val2);
			}
			[CompilerGenerated]
			remove
			{
				ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> val = this.m_StateChanged;
				ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> val2;
				do
				{
					val2 = val;
					ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState> value2 = (ValueChangedEventHandler<GroupBoxState>)(object)Delegate.Remove((Delegate)(object)val2, (Delegate)(object)value);
					val = Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref this.m_StateChanged, value2, val2);
