		}

		static GroupBox()
		{
			CornerRadiusProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CornerRadius", typeof(CornerRadius), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			MaximizeElementVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximizeElementVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)d).OnButtonVisibilityChanged();
			}));
			MinimizeElementVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimizeElementVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)d).OnButtonVisibilityChanged();
			}));
			MinimizationDirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimizationDirection", typeof(Orientation), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			SeparatorBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SeparatorBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			ShadowOffsetProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShadowOffset", typeof(double), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			ShadowVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShadowVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			ShowShadowProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowShadow", typeof(GroupBoxShadowVisibility), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)o).UpdateShadowVisibility();
			}));
			StateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("State", typeof(GroupBoxState), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				GroupBox groupBox = (GroupBox)(object)o;
				if (!groupBox._IsChangingState)
				{
					groupBox._IsChangingState = true;
					if (groupBox.OnStateChanging((GroupBoxState)e.OldValue, (GroupBoxState)e.NewValue))
					{
						groupBox.OnStateChanged((GroupBoxState)e.OldValue, (GroupBoxState)e.NewValue);
					}
					else
					{
						o.SetValue(e.Property, e.OldValue);
					}
					groupBox._IsChangingState = false;
				}
			}));
			TitleBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TitleBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			TitleForegroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TitleForeground", typeof(Brush), typeof(GroupBox), null);
			TitleVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TitleVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Visible));
			NormalTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NormalTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)d).UpdateCurrentTemplate();
			}));
			LightTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LightTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)d).UpdateCurrentTemplate();
			}));
			DisplayModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayMode", typeof(GroupBoxDisplayMode), typeof(GroupBox), new PropertyMetadata(GroupBoxDisplayMode.Default, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupBox)(object)d).UpdateCurrentTemplate();
			}));
			hasAutomationPeer = ReflectionHelper.CreateInstanceMethodHandler<FrameworkElement, Func<DependencyObject, bool>>((FrameworkElement)null, "get_HasAutomationPeer", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic, (int?)null, (Type[])null, true);
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<GroupBox>();
		}

		public GroupBox()
		{
			UpdateShadowVisibility();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (MaximizeElement != null)
			{
				((DXButton)MaximizeElement).Click -= OnButtonClick;
			}
			if (MinimizeElement != null)
			{
				((DXButton)MinimizeElement).Click -= OnButtonClick;
			}
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			BorderElement = ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetTemplateChild("BorderElement") as Border;
			MaximizeElement = ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetTemplateChild("MaximizeElement") as GroupBoxButton;
			MinimizeElement = ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetTemplateChild("MinimizeElement") as GroupBoxButton;
			if (MaximizeElement != null)
			{
				((DXButton)MaximizeElement).Click += OnButtonClick;
			}
			if (MinimizeElement != null)
			{
				((DXButton)MinimizeElement).Click += OnButtonClick;
			}
			UpdateButtons();
		}

		protected string GetLayoutState(GroupBoxState state)
		{
			return state switch
			{
				GroupBoxState.Normal => "NormalLayout", 
				GroupBoxState.Minimized => "MinimizedLayout", 
				_ => "MaximizedLayout", 
			};
		}

		private void OnButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnButtonClick((GroupBoxButton)sender);
		}

		protected internal virtual void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, StateProperty, "State", typeof(GroupBoxState));
		}

		protected internal virtual void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, StateProperty, "State");
		}

		protected override ControlControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (ControlControllerBase)(object)new GroupBoxController(this);
		}

		protected virtual Visibility GetShadowVisibility()
		{
			if (ShowShadow == GroupBoxShadowVisibility.Always || (ShowShadow == GroupBoxShadowVisibility.WhenHasMouse && ((Controller)((ControlBase)this).Controller).IsMouseEntered))
			{
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		protected virtual void OnButtonClick(GroupBoxButton button)
		{
			switch (button.Kind)
			{
			case GroupBoxButtonKind.Minimize:
				State = GroupBoxState.Minimized;
				break;
			case GroupBoxButtonKind.Unminimize:
			case GroupBoxButtonKind.Unmaximize:
				State = GroupBoxState.Normal;
				break;
			case GroupBoxButtonKind.Maximize:
				State = GroupBoxState.Maximized;
				break;
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnDesignTimeClick(DXMouseButtonEventArgs args)
		{
			if (MinimizeElementVisibility == Visibility.Visible && MinimizeElement != null && FrameworkElementExtensions.Contains((FrameworkElement)(object)MinimizeElement, ((DXMouseEventArgs)args).GetPosition((UIElement)null)))
			{
				OnButtonClick(MinimizeElement);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual bool OnStateChanging(GroupBoxState oldValue, GroupBoxState newValue)
		{
			if (newValue == GroupBoxState.Maximized)
			{
				return ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is IMaximizingContainer;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnStateChanged(GroupBoxState oldValue, GroupBoxState newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue == GroupBoxState.Maximized && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is IMaximizingContainer && (object)((IMaximizingContainer)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).MaximizedElement == this)
			{
				((IMaximizingContainer)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).MaximizedElement = null;
			}
			UpdateButtons();
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(true);
			UpdateSize(oldValue, newValue);
			base.IsMaximizedCore = newValue == GroupBoxState.Maximized;
			if (this.StateChanged != null)
			{
				this.StateChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<GroupBoxState>(oldValue, newValue));
			}
			if (newValue == GroupBoxState.Maximized)
			{
				((IMaximizingContainer)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).MaximizedElement = (FrameworkElement)(object)this;
			}
			((DispatcherObject)(object)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(ResetChildrenPeerCache));
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateButtons()
		{
			if (MinimizeElement != null)
			{
				MinimizeElement.Kind = ((State == GroupBoxState.Minimized) ? GroupBoxButtonKind.Unminimize : GroupBoxButtonKind.Minimize);
			}
			if (MaximizeElement != null)
			{
				MaximizeElement.Kind = ((State == GroupBoxState.Maximized) ? GroupBoxButtonKind.Unmaximize : GroupBoxButtonKind.Maximize);
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateLayoutState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).GoToState(GetLayoutState(State), useTransitions);
			UIElement uIElement = BorderElement;
			while (uIElement != null)
			{
				uIElement.InvalidateMeasure();
				if ((object)uIElement != this)
				{
					uIElement = (UIElement)VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(uIElement);
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateShadowVisibility()
		{
			((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(ShadowVisibilityProperty, (object)GetShadowVisibility());
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateSize(GroupBoxState oldState, GroupBoxState newState)
		{
			DependencyProperty dependencyProperty = ((MinimizationDirection == Orientation.Horizontal) ? FrameworkElement.WidthProperty : FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
			DependencyProperty dependencyProperty2 = ((MinimizationDirection == Orientation.Horizontal) ? FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty : FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty);
			if (newState == GroupBoxState.Minimized)
			{
				if (!double.IsNaN((double)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(dependencyProperty)))
				{
					_StoredSizePropertyValue = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, dependencyProperty);
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(dependencyProperty, (object)double.NaN);
				}
				if ((double)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(dependencyProperty2) != 0.0)
				{
					_StoredMinSizePropertyValue = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, dependencyProperty2);
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(dependencyProperty2, (object)0.0);
				}
			}
			if (oldState == GroupBoxState.Minimized)
			{
				if (_StoredSizePropertyValue != null)
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, dependencyProperty, _StoredSizePropertyValue);
					_StoredSizePropertyValue = null;
				}
				if (_StoredMinSizePropertyValue != null)
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, dependencyProperty2, _StoredMinSizePropertyValue);
					_StoredMinSizePropertyValue = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(useTransitions);
			UpdateLayoutState(useTransitions);
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCurrentTemplate()
		{
			if (NormalTemplate != null && LightTemplate != null)
			{
				ControlTemplate value = DisplayMode switch
				{
					GroupBoxDisplayMode.Light => LightTemplate, 
					_ => NormalTemplate, 
				};
				if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource((DependencyObject)(object)this, Control.TemplateProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Default)
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetCurrentValue(Control.TemplateProperty, (object)value);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnButtonVisibilityChanged()
		{
			ResetChildrenPeerCache();
		}

		private void ResetChildrenPeerCache()
		{
			if (HasAutomationPeer)
			{
				UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)(object)this)?.ResetChildrenCache();
			}
		}

		bool IGroupBox.DesignTimeClick(DXMouseButtonEventArgs args)
		{
			return OnDesignTimeClick(args);
		}

		void IGroupBox.UpdateShadowVisibility()
		{
			UpdateShadowVisibility();
		}

		void IMaximizableElement.AfterNormalization()
		{
			if (State == GroupBoxState.Maximized)
			{
				State = GroupBoxState.Normal;
			}
		}

		void IMaximizableElement.BeforeMaximization()
		{
			State = GroupBoxState.Maximized;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new DevExpress.Xpf.LayoutControl.UIAutomation.GroupBoxAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	public class GroupBoxController : ControlControllerBase
	{
		public IGroupBox IGroupBox => ((Controller)this).IControl as IGroupBox;

		public GroupBoxController(IGroupBox control)
			: base((IControl)control)
		{
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnMouseEnter(e);
			IGroupBox.UpdateShadowVisibility();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnMouseLeave(e);
			IGroupBox.UpdateShadowVisibility();
		}
	}
	public class GroupBoxShadow : Container
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CornerRadiusProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CornerRadius", typeof(CornerRadius), typeof(GroupBoxShadow), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((GroupBoxShadow)(object)o).OnCornerRadiusChanged();
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OffsetProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Offset", typeof(double), typeof(GroupBoxShadow), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((double)e.NewValue < 0.0)
			{
				o.SetValue(e.Property, 0.0);
			}
			else
			{
				((GroupBoxShadow)(object)o).OnOffsetChanged();
			}
		}));

		protected double StartOpacity = 0.07;

		protected double EndOpacity = 0.35;

		public CornerRadius CornerRadius
		{
			get
			{
				return (CornerRadius)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(CornerRadiusProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(CornerRadiusProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public double Offset
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(OffsetProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(OffsetProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public GroupBoxShadow()
		{
			((UIElement)this).IsHitTestVisible = false;
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement CreateElement(double margin, double opacity)
		{
			Border border = new Border();
			border.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Black);
			border.SetBinding(Border.CornerRadiusProperty, new Binding("CornerRadius")
			{
				Source = this
			});
			border.Margin = new Thickness(margin);
			border.Opacity = opacity;
			return border;
		}

		protected virtual void CreateElements()
		{
			((Panel)this).Children.Clear();
			double num = 1.0;
			for (int i = 0; (double)i < Offset; i++)
			{
				double num2 = StartOpacity + (0.5 + (double)i) * (EndOpacity - StartOpacity) / Offset;
				num2 = 1.0 - (1.0 - num2) / num;
				num *= 1.0 - num2;
				((Panel)this).Children.Add(CreateElement(i, num2));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCornerRadiusChanged()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnOffsetChanged()
		{
			((UIElement)this).RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform
			{
				X = Offset,
				Y = Offset
			};
			CreateElements();
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalRootElement", Type = typeof(FrameworkElement))]
	public class GroupSeparator : ControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		private const string HorizontalRootElementName = "HorizontalRootElement";

		private const string VerticalRootElementName = "VerticalRootElement";

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(OrientationProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement HorizontalRootElement { get; private set; }

		protected FrameworkElement VerticalRootElement { get; private set; }

		static GroupSeparator()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(GroupSeparator), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Horizontal, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupSeparator)(object)o).OnOrientationChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<GroupSeparator>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			HorizontalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("HorizontalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			VerticalRootElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("VerticalRootElement") as FrameworkElement;
			UpdateTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			if (HorizontalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)HorizontalRootElement, Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal);
			}
			if (VerticalRootElement != null)
			{
				UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)VerticalRootElement, Orientation == Orientation.Vertical);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOrientationChanged()
		{
			UpdateTemplate();
		}
	}

		internal class ItemsContainer : PanelBase
		{
			protected static DependencyProperty ChildVisibilityListener = PanelBase.RegisterChildPropertyListener("Visibility", typeof(ItemsContainer));

			public Action<FrameworkElement> ChildVisibilityChanged { get; set; }

			protected override void OnChildPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty propertyListener, object oldValue, object newValue)
			{
				((PanelBase)this).OnChildPropertyChanged(child, propertyListener, oldValue, newValue);
				if (propertyListener == ChildVisibilityListener && newValue != null && !newValue.Equals(oldValue))
				{
					OnChildVisibilityChanged(child);
				}
			}

			protected virtual void OnChildVisibilityChanged(FrameworkElement child)
			{
				if (ChildVisibilityChanged != null)
				{
					ChildVisibilityChanged(child);
				}
			}

			protected override void OnVisualChildrenChanged(DependencyObject visualAdded, DependencyObject visualRemoved)
			{
				((PanelBase)this).OnVisualChildrenChanged(visualAdded, visualRemoved);
				if (visualAdded is FrameworkElement)
				{
					PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener((FrameworkElement)visualAdded, "Visibility", ChildVisibilityListener);
				}
				if (visualRemoved is FrameworkElement)
				{
					PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener((FrameworkElement)visualRemoved, ChildVisibilityListener);
				}
			}
		}

		private string GetID(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(control.Name))
			{
				return control.Name;
			}
			string result = control.GetValue(IDProperty).ToString();
			if (AddRestoredItemsToAvailableItems)
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)control, StringIDProperty))
			{
				return result;
			}
			return (string)control.GetValue(StringIDProperty);
		}

		private void SetID(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			_LastID++;
			control.SetValue(IDProperty, _LastID);
		}

		private void SetID(FrameworkElement control, string id)
		{
			control.SetValue(StringIDProperty, id);
			if (int.TryParse(id, out var result))
			{
				control.SetValue(IDProperty, result);
				if (result > _LastID)
				{
					_LastID = result;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateID(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)control, IDProperty))
			{
				SetID(control);
			}
			else
			{
				int num = (int)control.GetValue(IDProperty);
				if (num > _LastID)
				{
					SetID(control);
				}
			}
			if (!control.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((IPanel)(ILayoutGroup)control).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				UpdateID(item);
			}
		}

		private FrameworkElement FindControl(string id, FrameworkElement exception = null)
		{
			if (AddRestoredItemsToAvailableItems ? int.TryParse(id, out var _) : (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(id)))
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = FindControl(AvailableItems, id, exception);
				if (frameworkElement == null)
				{
					frameworkElement = FindControl((ILayoutGroup)this, id, exception);
				}
				return frameworkElement;
			}
			return FindControlByName(id);
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement FindControlByName(string id)
		{
			return (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).FindName(id);
		}

		private FrameworkElement FindControl(ILayoutGroup group, string id, FrameworkElement exception)
		{
			return FindControl(((IPanel)group).GetLogicalChildren(false), id, exception);
		}

		private FrameworkElement FindControl(FrameworkElements controls, string id, FrameworkElement exception)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)controls)
			{
				if (item != exception && GetID(item) == id)
				{
					return item;
				}
				if (item.IsLayoutGroup())
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = FindControl((ILayoutGroup)item, id, exception);
					if (frameworkElement != null)
					{
						return frameworkElement;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		static LayoutControl()
		{
			ActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), null);
			AllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutControl)(object)o).OnAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomizationChanged();
			}));
			AllowItemMovingDuringCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowItemMovingDuringCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutControl)(object)o).OnAllowItemMovingDuringCustomizationChanged();
			}));
			AllowItemRenamingDuringCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowItemRenamingDuringCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowItemSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowItemSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutControl)(object)o).OnAllowItemSizingChanged();
			}));
			AllowItemSizingDuringCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowItemSizingDuringCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowNewItemsDuringCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowNewItemsDuringCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowHorizontalSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("AllowHorizontalSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			AllowVerticalSizingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("AllowVerticalSizing", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			CustomizationControlStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CustomizationControlStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ILayoutControl)o).InitCustomizationController();
			}));
			CustomizationLabelProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CustomizationLabel", typeof(object), typeof(LayoutControl), null);
			IsCustomizationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsCustomization", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool(o))
				{
					((LayoutControl)(object)o).Controller.IsCustomization = (bool)e.NewValue;
				}
			}));
			IsUserDefinedProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsUserDefined", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutControl), null);
			ItemCustomizationToolbarStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)

			ItemInsertionPointIndicatorStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemInsertionPointIndicatorStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutControl)(object)o).Controller.ItemInsertionPointIndicatorStyle = (Style)e.NewValue;
			}));
			ItemParentIndicatorStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemParentIndicatorStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ILayoutControl)o).InitCustomizationController();
			}));
			ItemSelectionIndicatorStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)

			}));
			ItemSizerStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemSizerStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)

		public static string GetCustomizationDefaultLabel(UIElement element)
		{
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string text = null;
			if (element is ContentControl)
			{
				text = ((ContentControl)element).Content as string;
			}
			else if (element is ContentControlBase)
			{
				text = ((ContentControlBase)element).Content as string;
			}
			else if (element is LayoutGroup)
			{
				text = ((LayoutGroup)(object)element).Header as string;
			}
			else if (element is LayoutItem)
			{
				text = ((LayoutItem)(object)element).Label as string;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					text = text.TrimEnd(':', ' ');
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = "[" + element.GetType().Name + "]";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public LayoutControl()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			_AvailableItems = new FrameworkElements();
			((ObservableCollection<FrameworkElement>)(object)AvailableItems).CollectionChanged += delegate(object o, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnAvailableItemsChanged(e);
			};
			VisibleAvailableItems = new FrameworkElements();
			AvailableItemsContainer = new ItemsContainer
			{
				Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed
			};
			AvailableItemsContainer.ChildVisibilityChanged = delegate(FrameworkElement child)
			{
				if (UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)child))
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)VisibleAvailableItems).Add(child);
				}
				else
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)VisibleAvailableItems).Remove(child);
				}
			};
			UpdateActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization();
			serializationControllerCore = CreateSerializationController();
		}

		public override void ReadFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			Locker val = updateIDLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				base.ReadFromXML(xml);
				if (!AddRestoredItemsToAvailableItems)
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)AvailableItems)
					{
						UpdateID(item);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
			OptimizeLayout(keepEmptyTabs: true);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement GetChildContainer(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			return (FrameworkElement)(((object)child.GetLayoutItem((FrameworkElement)(object)this, explicitOnly: false)) ?? ((object)((ScrollControl)this).GetChildContainer(child)));
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			foreach (UIElement internalElement in base.GetInternalElements())
			{
				yield return internalElement;
			}
			yield return (UIElement)(object)AvailableItemsContainer;
		}

		protected virtual void OnControlAdded(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			UpdateID(control);
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)AvailableItems).Remove(control);
		}

		protected virtual void OnControlRemoved(FrameworkElement control)
		{
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (!StretchContentHorizontally)
			{
				availableSize.Width = double.PositiveInfinity;
			}
			if (!StretchContentVertically)
			{
				availableSize.Height = double.PositiveInfinity;
			}
			return base.OnMeasure(availableSize);
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			Size result = RectHelper.Size(bounds);
			if (!StretchContentHorizontally || bounds.Width < ((PanelBase)this).OriginalDesiredSize.Width)
			{
				bounds.Width = ((PanelBase)this).OriginalDesiredSize.Width;
			}
			if (!StretchContentVertically || bounds.Height < ((PanelBase)this).OriginalDesiredSize.Height)
			{
				bounds.Height = ((PanelBase)this).OriginalDesiredSize.Height;
			}
			base.OnArrange(bounds);
			return result;
		}

		protected override void AddChildFromXML(IList children, FrameworkElement element, int index)
		{
			if (children != AvailableItems || !children.Contains(element))
			{
				base.AddChildFromXML(children, element, index);
			}
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement ReadChildFromXML(XmlReader xml, IList children, int index)
		{
			if (xml.Name == "AvailableItems")
			{
				ReadChildrenFromXML((IList)AvailableItems, xml, 0, out var _);
				return null;
			}
			return base.ReadChildFromXML(xml, children, index);
		}

		protected override void WriteToXMLCore(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteToXMLCore(xml);
			xml.WriteStartElement("AvailableItems");
			WriteChildrenToXML((IList)AvailableItems, xml);
			xml.WriteEndElement();
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new LayoutControlController(this);
		}

		protected override Type GetGroupType()
		{
			return typeof(LayoutGroup);
		}

		protected virtual void InitCustomizationController(LayoutControlCustomizationController controller)
		{
			controller.CustomizationControlStyle = CustomizationControlStyle;
			controller.ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle = ItemCustomizationToolbarStyle;
			controller.ItemParentIndicatorStyle = ItemParentIndicatorStyle;
			controller.ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle = ItemSelectionIndicatorStyle;
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomizationChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowItemMovingDuringCustomizationChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAvailableItemsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			IList list = ((args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add || args.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace) ? args.NewItems : ((IList)((args.Action != NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset) ? null : AvailableItems)));
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (FrameworkElement item in list)
				{
					FrameworkElementExtensions.SetParent(item, (DependencyObject)null);
					if (AddRestoredItemsToAvailableItems || !Locker.op_Implicit(updateIDLocker))
					{
						UpdateID(item);
					}
				}
			}
			SynchronizeWithAvailableItems((IList)VisibleAvailableItems, args, (FrameworkElement item) => UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)item));
			SynchronizeWithAvailableItems(((Panel)(object)AvailableItemsContainer).Children, args);
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)AvailableItemsContainer);
			((PanelBase)this).OnInitialized(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnInitNewElement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			SetIsUserDefined(element, value: true);
			if (this.InitNewElement != null)
			{
				this.InitNewElement(this, new LayoutControlInitNewElementEventArgs(element));
			}
		}

		protected override void OnItemLabelsAlignmentChanged()
		{
		}

		protected override void OnLayoutUpdated()
		{
			if (Controller.IsCustomization)
			{
				Controller.CustomizationController.CheckSelectedElementsAreInVisualTree();
			}
			((PanelBase)this).OnLayoutUpdated();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayoutUriChanged()
		{
			if (!(LayoutUri == null))
			{
				StreamResourceInfo resourceStream = Application.GetResourceStream(LayoutUri);
				if (resourceStream != null)
				{
					ReadFromXML(SafeXml.CreateReader(resourceStream.Stream));
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLoaded()
		{
			base.OnLoaded();
			if (_IsLayoutUriChanged)
			{
				_IsLayoutUriChanged = false;
				OnLayoutUriChanged();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPartStyleChanged(LayoutGroupPartStyle style)
		{
			((ILayoutGroup)this).UpdatePartStyle(style);
		}

		protected virtual void OnStretchContentChanged()
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnReadElementFromXML(XmlReader xml, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (this.ReadElementFromXML != null)
			{
				this.ReadElementFromXML(this, new LayoutControlReadElementFromXMLEventArgs(xml, element));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnWriteElementToXML(XmlWriter xml, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (this.WriteElementToXML != null)
			{
				this.WriteElementToXML(this, new LayoutControlWriteElementToXMLEventArgs(xml, element));
			}
		}

		protected void SynchronizeWithAvailableItems(IList items, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args, Predicate<FrameworkElement> isItemAllowed = null)
		{
			Action<IList> action = delegate(IList newItems)
			{
				foreach (FrameworkElement newItem in newItems)
				{
					if (isItemAllowed == null || isItemAllowed(newItem))
					{
						items.Add(newItem);
					}
				}
			};
			Action<IList> action2 = delegate(IList oldItems)
			{
				foreach (FrameworkElement oldItem in oldItems)
				{
					items.Remove(oldItem);
				}
			};
			switch (args.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				action(args.NewItems);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				action2(args.OldItems);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				action2(args.OldItems);
				action(args.NewItems);
				break;
			default:
				items.Clear();
				action((IList)AvailableItems);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void SetItemSizerStyle(Style value)
		{
			base.ItemSizerStyle = value;
		}

		private void UpdateActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization()
		{
			((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(ActualAllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomizationProperty, (object)(AllowItemMovingDuringCustomization && AllowAvailableItemsDuringCustomization));
		}

		void ILayoutControl.ControlAdded(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			OnControlAdded(control);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.ControlRemoved(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			OnControlRemoved(control);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.ControlVisibilityChanged(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			Controller.OnControlVisibilityChanged(control);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.DeleteAvailableItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (item.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				((ILayoutGroup)item).MoveNonUserDefinedChildrenToAvailableItems();
			}
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)AvailableItems).Remove(item);
		}

		FrameworkElement ILayoutControl.FindControl(string id)
		{
			return FindControl(id);
		}

		string ILayoutControl.GetID(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			return GetID(control);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.SetID(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			SetID(control);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.SetID(FrameworkElement control, string id)
		{
			SetID(control, id);
		}

		Style ILayoutControl.GetPartStyle(LayoutGroupPartStyle style)
		{
			return GetPartStyle(style);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.InitCustomizationController()
		{
			if (Controller.IsCustomization)
			{
				InitCustomizationController(Controller.CustomizationController);
			}
		}

		void ILayoutControl.InitNewElement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			OnInitNewElement(element);
		}

		bool ILayoutControl.MakeControlVisible(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			if (!UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)control))
			{
				return false;
			}
			LayoutItem layoutItem = control.GetLayoutItem();
			if (layoutItem != null)
			{
				control = (FrameworkElement)(object)layoutItem;
			}
			while (control.Parent is ILayoutGroup layoutGroup && layoutGroup.MakeChildVisible(control))
			{
				if (layoutGroup.IsRoot)
				{
					return true;
				}
				control = ((IControl)layoutGroup).Control;
			}
			return false;
		}

		void ILayoutControl.ModelChanged(LayoutControlModelChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			Controller.OnModelChanged(args);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.TabClicked(ILayoutGroup group, FrameworkElement selectedTabChild)
		{
			Controller.OnTabClicked(group, selectedTabChild);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.ReadElementFromXML(XmlReader xml, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			OnReadElementFromXML(xml, element);
		}

		void ILayoutControl.WriteElementToXML(XmlWriter xml, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			OnWriteElementToXML(xml, element);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new LayoutControlAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected virtual ISerializationController CreateSerializationController()
		{
			return new SerializationController(this);
		}

		private void SaveLayoutToStream(Stream stream)
		{
			SerializationController.SaveLayout(stream);
		}

		private void RestoreLayoutFromStream(Stream stream)
		{
			RestoreLayoutCore(stream);
		}

		private void SaveLayoutToXml(string path)
		{
			SerializationController.SaveLayout(path);
		}

		private void RestoreLayoutFromXml(string path)
		{
			RestoreLayoutCore(path);
		}

		private void RestoreLayoutCore(object path)
		{
			SerializationController.RestoreLayout(path);
		}
	}
	public abstract class LayoutControlModelChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public string ChangeDescription { get; protected set; }
	}
	public class LayoutControlModelStructureChangedEventArgs : LayoutControlModelChangedEventArgs
	{
		public FrameworkElement Element { get; private set; }

		public LayoutControlModelStructureChangedEventArgs(string changeDescription, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			base.ChangeDescription = changeDescription;
			Element = element;
		}
	}
	public class LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs : LayoutControlModelChangedEventArgs
	{
		public DependencyObject Object { get; private set; }

		public DependencyProperty Property { get; private set; }

		public string PropertyName { get; private set; }

		public LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs(DependencyObject obj, string propertyName, DependencyProperty property)
		{
			Object = obj;
			PropertyName = propertyName;
			Property = property;
			base.ChangeDescription = "Change " + PropertyName;
		}
	}
	public class LayoutControlController : LayoutGroupController
	{
		private bool _IsCustomization;

		public new ILayoutControl ILayoutControl => base.ILayoutControl as ILayoutControl;

		protected override bool NeedsUnloadedEvent => IsCustomization;

		public LayoutControlCustomizationController CustomizationController { get; private set; }

		public bool IsCustomization
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsCustomization;
			}
			set
			{
				if (IsCustomization != value)
				{
					_IsCustomization = value;
					if (IsCustomization)
					{
						CustomizationController = CreateCustomizationController();
						CustomizationController.BeginCustomization();
						ILayoutControl.InitCustomizationController();
					}
					ILayoutControl.IsCustomization = IsCustomization;
					OnIsCustomizationChanged();
					if (!IsCustomization)
					{
						CustomizationController.EndCustomization();
						CustomizationController = null;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Style ItemInsertionPointIndicatorStyle { get; set; }

		protected virtual bool ForwardDesignTimeInput => true;

		public event EventHandler IsCustomizationChanged;

		public event EventHandler<LayoutControlModelChangedEventArgs> ModelChanged;

		public LayoutControlController(ILayoutControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			foreach (UIElement item in <>n__0())
			{
				yield return item;
			}
			if (CustomizationController == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (UIElement internalElement in CustomizationController.GetInternalElements())
			{
				yield return internalElement;
			}
		}

		public override FrameworkElement GetItem(Point p, bool ignoreLayoutGroups, bool ignoreLocking)
		{
			if (!ignoreLocking && base.ILayoutGroup.IsLocked)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return base.GetItem(p, ignoreLayoutGroups, ignoreLocking);
		}

		public override void OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			((PanelControllerBase)this).OnMeasure(availableSize);
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnMeasure(availableSize);
			}
		}

		public override void OnArrange(Size finalSize)
		{
			((PanelControllerBase)this).OnArrange(finalSize);
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnArrange(finalSize);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnControlVisibilityChanged(FrameworkElement control)
		{
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnControlVisibilityChanged(control);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLayoutUpdated()
		{
			((Controller)this).OnLayoutUpdated();
			if (IsCustomization && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree(((Controller)this).Control))
			{
				CustomizationController.OnLayoutUpdated();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLoaded()
		{
			((Controller)this).OnLoaded();
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnLoaded();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnTabClicked(ILayoutGroup group, FrameworkElement selectedTabChild)
		{
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnTabClicked(group, selectedTabChild);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUnloaded()
		{
			((Controller)this).OnUnloaded();
			IsCustomization = false;
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(DXKeyEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnKeyDown(e);
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)((Controller)this).Control) && e.Key == Key.F2 && Keyboard2.IsControlPressed && Keyboard2.IsShiftPressed)
			{
				IsCustomization = !IsCustomization;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnMouseMove(e);
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnMouseMove(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			((Controller)this).OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			bool isMouseLeftButtonDown = ((Controller)this).IsMouseLeftButtonDown;
			((Controller)this).OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			if (e != null && IsCustomization && isMouseLeftButtonDown)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseRightButtonDown(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnMouseRightButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		public override bool IsScrollable()
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual LayoutControlCustomizationController CreateCustomizationController()
		{
			return new LayoutControlCustomizationController(this);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCustomizationChanged()
		{
			if (this.IsCustomizationChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsCustomizationChanged(((Controller)this).Control, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public virtual void DropElement(FrameworkElement element, LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = (ILayoutGroup)(insertionPoint.IsInternalInsertion ? insertionPoint.Element : insertionPoint.Element.Parent);
			layoutGroup.InsertElement(element, insertionPoint, insertionKind);
			base.ILayoutGroup.OptimizeLayout(keepEmptyTabs: true);
			((Controller)this).Control.InvalidateMeasure();
			if (!FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree(element))
			{
				element = null;
			}
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnDropElement(element);
			}
			OnModelChanged(new LayoutControlModelStructureChangedEventArgs("Drag & Drop", element));
		}

		public LayoutItemInsertionInfo GetInsertionInfo(FrameworkElement parentedElement, Point p)
		{
			FrameworkElement item = GetItem(p, ignoreLayoutGroups: false, DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)((Controller)this).Control));
			if (item == null)
			{
				return default(LayoutItemInsertionInfo);
			}
			ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = item as ILayoutGroup;
			ILayoutGroup layoutGroup2 = item.Parent as ILayoutGroup;
			p = UIElementExtensions.MapPoint((UIElement)((Controller)this).Control, p, (UIElement)item);
			LayoutItemInsertionKind layoutItemInsertionKind = ((item != ((Controller)this).Control) ? layoutGroup2.GetInsertionKind(item, p) : LayoutItemInsertionKind.None);
			if (item == ((Controller)this).Control || (item.IsLayoutGroup() && layoutItemInsertionKind == LayoutItemInsertionKind.None))
			{
				return layoutGroup.GetInsertionInfoForEmptyArea(parentedElement, p);
			}
			LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint = layoutGroup2.GetInsertionPoint(parentedElement, item, layoutItemInsertionKind, p);
			return new LayoutItemInsertionInfo(item, layoutItemInsertionKind, insertionPoint);
		}

		public void StartItemDragAndDrop(FrameworkElement item, MouseEventArgs mouseEventArgs, FrameworkElement source)
		{
			if (WantsItemDragAndDrop(new Point(0.0, 0.0), (Point p) => item, out var controller))
			{
				Point position = mouseEventArgs.GetPosition(source);
				((LayoutItemDragAndDropController)(object)controller).StartDragRelativePoint = new Point(position.X / source.ActualWidth, position.Y / source.ActualHeight);
				((Controller)this).DragAndDropController = controller;
				((Controller)this).StartDragAndDrop(mouseEventArgs.GetPosition(((Controller)this).Control));
			}
		}

		protected override bool CanItemDragAndDrop()
		{
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				return ILayoutControl.AllowItemMoving;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void StartDragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnStartDragAndDrop();
			}
			((Controller)this).StartDragAndDrop(p);
		}

		protected override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			((Controller)this).EndDragAndDrop(accept);
			if (IsCustomization)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnEndDragAndDrop(accept);
			}
		}

		protected override DragAndDropController CreateItemDragAndDropControler(Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
		{
			return (DragAndDropController)(object)new LayoutItemDragAndDropController((Controller)(object)this, startDragPoint, dragControl);
		}

		public void DesignTimeKeyDown(DXKeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).ProcessKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeKeyUp(DXKeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).ProcessKeyUp(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseCaptureCancelled()
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).OnMouseCaptureCancelled();
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseEnter(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).OnMouseEnter(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseLeave(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).OnMouseLeave(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseLeftButtonDown(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				CustomizationController.ProcessSelection(((Controller)this).Control, clearExistingSelection: true);
			}
			FrameworkElement selectableItem = CustomizationController.GetSelectableItem(((DXMouseEventArgs)e).GetPosition((UIElement)((Controller)this).Control));
			if (selectableItem.IsLayoutGroup() && ((ILayoutGroup)selectableItem).DesignTimeClick(e))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseLeftButtonUp(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseMove(DXMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				((Controller)this).OnMouseMove(e);
			}
		}

		public void DesignTimeMouseRightButtonDown(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ForwardDesignTimeInput)
			{
				OnMouseRightButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnModelChanged(LayoutControlModelChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)((Controller)this).Control) && IsCustomization && args is LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs e && e.Property == LayoutGroup.IsCollapsedProperty && ((LayoutGroup)(object)e.Object).IsCollapsed)
			{
				CustomizationController.OnGroupCollapsed((ILayoutGroup)e.Object);
			}
			if (this.ModelChanged != null)
			{
				this.ModelChanged(((Controller)this).Control, args);
			}
		}

		[CompilerGenerated]
		[DebuggerHidden]
		private IEnumerable<UIElement> <>n__0()
		{
			return ((Controller)this).GetInternalElements();
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemDragAndDropController : LayoutItemDragAndDropControllerBase
	{
		private ILayoutGroup _DestinationGroup;

		private int _DestinationGroupTabIndex = -1;

		private LayoutItemInsertionInfo _InsertionInfo;

		public static int GroupExpandingDelay = 1000;

		private Storyboard _GroupExpandingDelayTimer;

		public static int TabSelectionDelay = 750;

		private Storyboard _TabSelectionDelayTimer;

		protected new LayoutControlController Controller => (LayoutControlController)base.Controller;

		protected ILayoutGroup DestinationGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return _DestinationGroup;
			}
			set
			{
				if (DestinationGroup != value)
				{
					_DestinationGroup = value;
					OnDestinationGroupChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected int DestinationGroupTabIndex
		{
			get
			{
				return _DestinationGroupTabIndex;
			}
			set
			{
				if (DestinationGroupTabIndex != value)
				{
					_DestinationGroupTabIndex = value;
					OnDestinationGroupTabIndexChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected LayoutItemInsertionInfo InsertionInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return _InsertionInfo;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!InsertionInfo.Equals(value))
				{
					_InsertionInfo = value;
					OnInsertionInfoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected LayoutItemDragAndDropIndicator Indicator { get; private set; }

		public LayoutItemDragAndDropController(Controller controller, Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
			: base(controller, startDragPoint, dragControl)
		{
		}

		public override void StartDragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			InitializeIndicator();
			((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragAndDrop(p);
		}

		public override void DragAndDrop(Point p)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).DragAndDrop(p);
			InsertionInfo = Controller.GetInsertionInfo(base.DragControlPlaceHolder, p);
			if (DestinationGroup != null)
			{
				DestinationGroupTabIndex = DestinationGroup.GetTabIndex(UIElementExtensions.MapPoint((UIElement)((Controller)Controller).Control, p, (UIElement)null));
			}
		}

		public override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).EndDragAndDrop(accept);
			FinalizeIndicator();
			DestinationGroup = null;
			if (accept && InsertionInfo.InsertionPoint != null)
			{
				Controller.DropElement(base.DragControl, InsertionInfo.InsertionPoint, InsertionInfo.InsertionKind);
			}
		}

		public override void OnArrange(Size finalSize)
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).OnArrange(finalSize);
			InsertionInfo = default(LayoutItemInsertionInfo);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDestinationGroupChanged()
		{
			DestinationGroupTabIndex = -1;
			((DragAndDropController)this).CheckDelayTimer(ref _GroupExpandingDelayTimer, GroupExpandingDelay, (Func<bool>)IsGroupExpandingDelayNeeded, (Action)OnGroupExpandingDelayExpired);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDestinationGroupTabIndexChanged()
		{
			((DragAndDropController)this).CheckDelayTimer(ref _TabSelectionDelayTimer, TabSelectionDelay, (Func<bool>)IsTabSelectionDelayNeeded, (Action)OnTabSelectionDelayExpired);
		}

		protected virtual void OnInsertionInfoChanged()
		{
			Indicator.InsertionInfo = InsertionInfo;
			UIElementExtensions.SetVisible((UIElement)(object)Indicator, InsertionInfo.DestinationItem != null);
			DestinationGroup = (InsertionInfo.DestinationItem.IsLayoutGroup() ? ((ILayoutGroup)InsertionInfo.DestinationItem) : null);
		}

		protected virtual LayoutItemDragAndDropIndicator CreateIndicator()
		{
			return new LayoutItemDragAndDropIndicator(base.DragControlPlaceHolder);
		}

		protected void InitializeIndicator()
		{
			Indicator = CreateIndicator();
			Indicator.InsertionPointIndicatorStyle = Controller.ItemInsertionPointIndicatorStyle;
			((UIElement)(object)Indicator).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			Controller.CustomizationController.CustomizationCanvas.Children.Add((UIElement)(object)Indicator);
		}

		protected void FinalizeIndicator()
		{
			Controller.CustomizationController.CustomizationCanvas.Children.Remove((UIElement)(object)Indicator);
			Indicator = null;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsGroupExpandingDelayNeeded()
		{
			if (DestinationGroup != null)
			{
				return DestinationGroup.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.NoChildrenVisible;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupExpandingDelayExpired()
		{
			DestinationGroup.IsCollapsed = false;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsTabSelectionDelayNeeded()
		{
			if (DestinationGroup != null && DestinationGroupTabIndex != -1)
			{
				return DestinationGroup.SelectedTabIndex != DestinationGroupTabIndex;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnTabSelectionDelayExpired()
		{
			DestinationGroup.SelectedTabIndex = DestinationGroupTabIndex;
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemDragAndDropIndicator : PanelBase
	{
		private LayoutItemInsertionInfo _InsertionInfo;

		public LayoutItemInsertionInfo InsertionInfo
		{
			get
			{
				return _InsertionInfo;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!InsertionInfo.Equals(value))
				{
					LayoutItemInsertionInfo insertionInfo = InsertionInfo;
					_InsertionInfo = value;
					OnInsertionInfoChanged(insertionInfo);
				}
			}
		}

		public Style InsertionPointIndicatorStyle { get; set; }

		protected ILayoutGroup DestinationItemGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (InsertionInfo.DestinationItem == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return InsertionInfo.DestinationItem.Parent as ILayoutGroup;
			}
		}

		protected FrameworkElement ParentedDragControl { get; private set; }

		protected Storyboard ShowingStoryboard { get; private set; }

		public LayoutItemDragAndDropIndicator(FrameworkElement parentedDragControl)
		{
			ParentedDragControl = parentedDragControl;
			ShowingStoryboard = CreateShowingStoryboard();
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in ((Panel)this).Children)
			{
				child.Measure(SizeHelper.Infinite);
			}
			return ((PanelBase)this).OnMeasure(availableSize);
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Panel)this).Children.Count; i++)
			{
				LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator layoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator = (LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator)(object)((Panel)this).Children[i];
				Rect insertionPointIndicatorBounds = GetInsertionPointIndicatorBounds(DestinationItemGroup.GetInsertionPointZoneBounds(InsertionInfo.DestinationItem, InsertionInfo.InsertionKind, i, ((Panel)this).Children.Count), GetInsertionPointBounds(layoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator.InsertionPoint));
				if (!insertionPointIndicatorBounds.Size.IsEmpty)
				{
					((UIElement)(object)layoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator).Arrange(insertionPointIndicatorBounds);
				}
			}
			return ((PanelBase)this).OnArrange(bounds);
		}

		protected Rect GetInsertionPointBounds(LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint)
		{
			if (insertionPoint.Element.IsLayoutGroup() || insertionPoint.Element == InsertionInfo.DestinationItem.GetLayoutControl())
			{
				return ((ILayoutGroup)insertionPoint.Element).GetInsertionPointBounds(insertionPoint.IsInternalInsertion, InsertionInfo.DestinationItem);
			}
			return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(insertionPoint.Element, InsertionInfo.DestinationItem);
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetInsertionPointIndicatorBounds(Rect insertionPointZoneBounds, Rect insertionPointBounds)
		{
			//IL_001e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Rect result = insertionPointZoneBounds;
			Side? side = InsertionInfo.InsertionKind.GetSide();
			if (side.HasValue)
			{
				if (SideExtentions.GetOrientation(side.Value) == Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					result.X = insertionPointBounds.X;
					result.Width = insertionPointBounds.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					result.Y = insertionPointBounds.Y;
					result.Height = insertionPointBounds.Height;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected LayoutItemInsertionPoints GetInsertionPoints()
		{
			LayoutItemInsertionPoints layoutItemInsertionPoints = new LayoutItemInsertionPoints();
			if (DestinationItemGroup != null)
			{
				DestinationItemGroup.GetInsertionPoints(ParentedDragControl, InsertionInfo.DestinationItem, InsertionInfo.DestinationItem, InsertionInfo.InsertionKind, layoutItemInsertionPoints);
			}
			return layoutItemInsertionPoints;
		}

		protected void ActivateInsertionPointIndicator(LayoutItemInsertionPoint oldInsertionPoint, LayoutItemInsertionPoint newInsertionPoint)
		{
			LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator insertionPointIndicator = GetInsertionPointIndicator(oldInsertionPoint);
			if (insertionPointIndicator != null)
			{
				insertionPointIndicator.IsActive = false;
			}
			insertionPointIndicator = GetInsertionPointIndicator(newInsertionPoint);
			if (insertionPointIndicator != null)
			{
				insertionPointIndicator.IsActive = true;
			}
		}

		protected virtual LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator CreateInsertionPointIndicator(LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint)
		{
			return new LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator(insertionPoint, InsertionInfo.InsertionKind);
		}

		protected void CreateInsertionPointIndicators()
		{
			((Panel)this).Children.Clear();
			foreach (LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint in GetInsertionPoints())
			{
				LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator layoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator = CreateInsertionPointIndicator(insertionPoint);
				((FrameworkElement)(object)layoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator).Style = InsertionPointIndicatorStyle;
				((Panel)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)layoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator);
			}
		}

		protected LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator GetInsertionPointIndicator(LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint)
		{
			foreach (LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator child in ((Panel)this).Children)
			{
				if (child.InsertionPoint.Equals(insertionPoint))
				{
					return child;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected void UpdateInsertionPointIndicators(LayoutItemInsertionPoint oldInsertionPoint)
		{
			if (ShowingStoryboard != null)
			{
				ShowingStoryboard.Stop();
			}
			ActivateInsertionPointIndicator(oldInsertionPoint, null);
			CreateInsertionPointIndicators();
			if (ShowingStoryboard != null)
			{
				ShowingStoryboard.Begin();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnInsertionInfoChanged(LayoutItemInsertionInfo oldInsertionInfo)
		{
			if (InsertionInfo.DestinationItem != oldInsertionInfo.DestinationItem && InsertionInfo.DestinationItem != null)
			{
				FrameworkElementExtensions.SetBounds((FrameworkElement)(object)this, FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(InsertionInfo.DestinationItem, (FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)this).Parent));
			}
			if (InsertionInfo.DestinationItem != oldInsertionInfo.DestinationItem || InsertionInfo.InsertionKind != oldInsertionInfo.InsertionKind)
			{
				UpdateInsertionPointIndicators(oldInsertionInfo.InsertionPoint);
				oldInsertionInfo.InsertionPoint = null;
			}
			if (InsertionInfo.InsertionPoint != oldInsertionInfo.InsertionPoint)
			{
				ActivateInsertionPointIndicator(oldInsertionInfo.InsertionPoint, InsertionInfo.InsertionPoint);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Storyboard CreateShowingStoryboard()
		{
			DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation();
			Storyboard.SetTarget(doubleAnimation, (DependencyObject)(object)this);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath("Opacity"));
			doubleAnimation.From = 0.0;
			doubleAnimation.To = 1.0;
			doubleAnimation.Duration = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250.0);
			Storyboard storyboard = new Storyboard();
			storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
			return storyboard;
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator : ControlBase
	{
		private bool _IsActive;

		public LayoutItemInsertionKind InsertionKind { get; private set; }

		public LayoutItemInsertionPoint InsertionPoint { get; private set; }

		public bool IsActive
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsActive;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_IsActive != value)
				{
					_IsActive = value;
					OnIsActiveChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		static LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator>();
		}

		public LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator()
		{
		}

		public LayoutItemDragAndDropInsertionPointIndicator(LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			InsertionPoint = insertionPoint;
			InsertionKind = insertionKind;
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsActiveChanged()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(true);
		}

		protected override void UpdateState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).GoToState(IsActive ? "MouseOver" : "Normal", useTransitions);
		}
	}
	internal static class ILayoutControlExtensions
	{
		internal static void RequestEditorValidation(this ILayoutControl layoutControl)
		{
			LayoutItems layoutItems = new LayoutItems();
			GetLayoutItems(((IPanel)layoutControl).GetLogicalChildren(false), layoutItems);
			foreach (LayoutItem item in layoutItems)
			{
				(item as DataLayoutItem)?.DoValidate();
			}
		}

		private static void GetLayoutItems(FrameworkElements items, LayoutItems layoutItems)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item2 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				if (item2 is LayoutItem)
				{
					LayoutItem item = (LayoutItem)(object)item2;
					layoutItems.Add(item);
				}
				else if (item2.IsLayoutGroup())
				{
					GetLayoutItems(((IPanel)(ILayoutGroup)item2).GetLogicalChildren(false), layoutItems);
				}
			}
		}
	}
