	[LicenseProvider(typeof(DX_WPF_LicenseProvider))]
	public abstract class LayoutControlBase : ScrollControl, ILayoutControlBase, IScrollControl, IPanel, IControl, ILayoutModelBase, ISerializableItem, ISerializableCollectionItem
	{
		public const double DefaultItemSpace = 4.0;

		public const double DefaultPadding = 12.0;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemSpaceProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaddingProperty;

		protected static readonly Brush DefaultMovingItemPlaceHolderBrush;

		protected const string XMLIDName = "ID";

		[Description("This member supports the internal infrastructure, and is not intended to be used directly from your code.")]
		public LayoutControllerBase Controller => (LayoutControllerBase)(object)((ScrollControl)this).Controller;

		[Description("Gets or sets the distance between child items.\nThis is a dependency property.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public double ItemSpace
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ItemSpaceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ItemSpaceProperty, (object)Math.Max(0.0, value));
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets padding settings for the current control.\nThis is a dependency property.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Thickness Padding
		{
			get
			{
				return (Thickness)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(PaddingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(PaddingProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected LayoutProviderBase LayoutProvider { get; private set; }

		bool ILayoutControlBase.AllowItemMoving => false;

		LayoutProviderBase ILayoutControlBase.LayoutProvider => LayoutProvider;

		Brush ILayoutControlBase.MovingItemPlaceHolderBrush => null;

		FrameworkElement ISerializableItem.Element => (FrameworkElement)(object)this;

		string ISerializableCollectionItem.Name
		{
			get
			{
				return ((FrameworkElement)this).Name;
			}
			set
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).Name = value;
			}
		}

		string ISerializableCollectionItem.TypeName => SerializableItem.GetTypeName((DependencyObject)(object)this);

		string ISerializableCollectionItem.ParentName
		{
			get
			{
				return SerializableItem.GetParentName((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			}
			set
			{
				SerializableItem.SetParentName((DependencyObject)(object)this, value);
			}
		}

		string ISerializableCollectionItem.ParentCollectionName
		{
			get
			{
				return SerializableItem.GetParentCollectionName((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			}
			set
			{
				SerializableItem.SetParentCollectionName((DependencyObject)(object)this, value);
			}
		}

		static LayoutControlBase()
		{
			ItemSpaceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemSpace", typeof(double), typeof(LayoutControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(4.0, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				LayoutControlBase layoutControlBase = (LayoutControlBase)(object)o;
				layoutControlBase.OnItemSpaceChanged((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
			}));
			PaddingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Padding", typeof(Thickness), typeof(LayoutControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(new Thickness(12.0), delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				LayoutControlBase layoutControlBase = (LayoutControlBase)(object)o;
				layoutControlBase.OnPaddingChanged((Thickness)e.OldValue, (Thickness)e.NewValue);
			}));
			DefaultMovingItemPlaceHolderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(51, 0, 0, 0));
			DefaultMovingItemPlaceHolderBrush.Freeze();
			DXSerializer.SerializationProviderProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(LayoutControlBase), new UIPropertyMetadata(new LayoutControlSerializationProviderBase()));
		}

		public LayoutControlBase()
		{
			if (((Controller)Controller).IsScrollable())
			{
				SetScrollBehavior();
			}
			LayoutProvider = CreateLayoutProvider();
			SerializableItem.SetTypeName((DependencyObject)(object)this, ((object)this).GetType().Name);
		}

		private void SetScrollBehavior()
		{
			((DependencyObject)this).SetCurrentValue(ScrollBarExtensions.ScrollBehaviorProperty, (object)new LayoutControlBaseScrollBehavior());
			((DependencyObject)this).SetCurrentValue(ScrollBarExtensions.AllowMouseScrollingProperty, (object)true);
			((DependencyObject)this).SetCurrentValue(ScrollBarExtensions.AllowShiftKeyModeProperty, (object)true);
		}

		public virtual void ReadFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			if (xml.IsStartElement(((object)this).GetType().Name))
			{
				ReadFromXMLCore(xml);
			}
		}

		public virtual void WriteToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			xml.WriteStartElement(((object)this).GetType().Name);
			WriteToXMLCore(xml);
			xml.WriteEndElement();
		}

		protected override Rect GetChildBounds(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			Rect bounds = ((PanelBase)this).GetChildBounds(child);
			LayoutProvider.UpdateChildBounds(child, ref bounds);
			return bounds;
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				if (item.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					item.Measure(availableSize);
				}
			}
			return LayoutProvider.Measure(((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true), availableSize, CreateLayoutProviderParameters());
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			Rect viewPortBounds = bounds;
			if (((Controller)Controller).IsScrollable())
			{
				RectHelper.Offset(ref bounds, 0.0 - ((ScrollControl)this).HorizontalOffset, 0.0 - ((ScrollControl)this).VerticalOffset);
			}
			return LayoutProvider.Arrange(((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true), bounds, viewPortBounds, CreateLayoutProviderParameters());
		}

		protected override Thickness GetContentPadding()
		{
			Thickness contentPadding = ((PanelBase)this).GetContentPadding();
			ThicknessHelper.Inc(ref contentPadding, Padding);
			return contentPadding;
		}

		protected abstract LayoutProviderBase CreateLayoutProvider();

		protected virtual LayoutParametersBase CreateLayoutProviderParameters()
		{
			return new LayoutParametersBase(ItemSpace);
		}

		protected virtual void ReadFromXMLCore(XmlReader xml)
		{
			ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(xml);
			ReadChildrenFromXML(xml, out var _);
		}

		protected virtual void WriteToXMLCore(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(xml);
			WriteChildrenToXML((IList)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false), xml);
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement ReadChildFromXML(XmlReader xml, IList children, int index)
		{
			string text = xml["ID"];
			FrameworkElement element = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				element = FindByXMLID(text);
			}
			return ReadChildFromXMLCore(element, xml, children, index, text);
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement ReadChildFromXMLCore(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml, IList children, int index, string id)
		{
			if (xml.Name == "Element" && element != null)
			{
				AddChildFromXML(children, element, index);
				ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(element, xml);
				return element;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual void WriteChildToXML(FrameworkElement child, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			xml.WriteStartElement("Element");
			xml.WriteAttributeString("ID", GetXMLID(child));
			WriteChildToXMLCore(child, xml);
			xml.WriteEndElement();
		}

		protected virtual void WriteChildToXMLCore(FrameworkElement child, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(child, xml);
		}

		protected virtual void ReadChildrenFromXML(XmlReader xml, out FrameworkElement lastChild)
		{
			MoveLogicalChildrenToEnd(out var firstChildIndex);
			ReadChildrenFromXML(((Panel)this).Children, xml, firstChildIndex, out lastChild);
		}

		protected void ReadChildrenFromXML(IList children, XmlReader xml, int firstChildIndex, out FrameworkElement lastChild)
		{
			lastChild = null;
			if (xml.IsEmptyElement)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = firstChildIndex;
			while (xml.Read() && xml.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ReadChildFromXML(xml, children, num);
				if (frameworkElement != null)
				{
					lastChild = frameworkElement;
					num++;
				}
			}
		}

		protected void WriteChildrenToXML(IList children, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement child in children)
			{
				WriteChildToXML(child, xml);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlWriter xml)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void AddChildFromXML(IList children, FrameworkElement element, int index)
		{
			int num = children.IndexOf(element);
			if (num != index)
			{
				FrameworkElementExtensions.SetParent(element, (DependencyObject)null);
				children.Insert(index, element);
			}
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement FindByXMLID(string id)
		{
			return (((FrameworkElement)this).FindName(id) as FrameworkElement) ?? ((IEnumerable)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false)).OfType<FrameworkElement>().FirstOrDefault((FrameworkElement x) => x.Name == id);
		}

		protected virtual string GetXMLID(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return element.Name;
		}

		protected void MoveLogicalChildrenToEnd(out int firstChildIndex)
		{
			firstChildIndex = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Panel)this).Children.Count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = ((Panel)this).Children[i];
				if (!((PanelBase)this).IsLogicalChild(uIElement))
				{
					if (i != firstChildIndex)
					{
						((Panel)this).Children.RemoveAt(i);
						((Panel)this).Children.Insert(firstChildIndex, uIElement);
					}
					firstChildIndex++;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new LayoutControllerBase(this);
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemSpaceChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void OnPaddingChanged(Thickness oldValue, Thickness newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		FrameworkElement ILayoutControlBase.GetMoveableItem(Point p)
		{
			return Controller.GetMoveableItem(p);
		}
	}
	public class LayoutParametersBase
	{
		public double ItemSpace { get; set; }

		public LayoutParametersBase(double itemSpace)
		{
			ItemSpace = itemSpace;
		}
	}
	public abstract class LayoutProviderBase
	{
		private LayoutParametersBase _Parameters;

		protected ILayoutModelBase Model { get; private set; }

		protected LayoutParametersBase Parameters
		{
			get
			{
				return _Parameters;
			}
			private set
			{
				_Parameters = value;
				OnParametersChanged();
			}
		}

		public LayoutProviderBase(ILayoutModelBase model)
		{
			Model = model;
		}

		public Size Measure(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize, LayoutParametersBase parameters)
		{
			Parameters = parameters;
			return OnMeasure(items, maxSize);
		}

		public Size Arrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds, LayoutParametersBase parameters)
		{
			Parameters = parameters;
			return OnArrange(items, bounds, viewPortBounds);
		}

		public virtual void CopyLayoutInfo(FrameworkElement from, FrameworkElement to)
		{
			to.Width = from.Width;
			to.Height = from.Height;
			to.MinWidth = from.DesiredSize.Width - (from.Margin.Left + from.Margin.Right);
			to.MinHeight = from.DesiredSize.Height - (from.Margin.Top + from.Margin.Bottom);
			to.MaxWidth = from.MaxWidth;
			to.MaxHeight = from.MaxHeight;
			to.Margin = from.Margin;
			to.HorizontalAlignment = from.HorizontalAlignment;
			to.VerticalAlignment = from.VerticalAlignment;
		}

		public virtual void UpdateChildBounds(FrameworkElement child, ref Rect bounds)
		{
		}

		public virtual void UpdateScrollableAreaBounds(ref Rect bounds)
		{
		}

		protected virtual Size OnMeasure(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			return new Size(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		protected virtual Size OnArrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			return RectHelper.Size(bounds);
		}

		protected virtual void OnParametersChanged()
		{
		}
	}
	public class LayoutControllerBase : ScrollControlController
	{
		public ILayoutControlBase ILayoutControl => ((Controller)this).IControl as ILayoutControlBase;

		public LayoutProviderBase LayoutProvider => ILayoutControl.LayoutProvider;

		protected override Rect ScrollableAreaBounds
		{
			get
			{
				Rect bounds = ((PanelControllerBase)this).ScrollableAreaBounds;
				LayoutProvider.UpdateScrollableAreaBounds(ref bounds);
				return bounds;
			}
		}

		protected override bool CanProcessMouseWheel => false;

		internal bool CanScrollDown
		{
			get
			{
				if (!((Controller)this).Control.IsVisible)
				{
					return false;
				}
				ScrollParams scrollParams = GetScrollParams(horz: false);
				if (scrollParams.Enabled)
				{
					return scrollParams.Position != scrollParams.MaxPosition;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanScrollLeft
		{
			get
			{
				if (!((Controller)this).Control.IsVisible)
				{
					return false;
				}
				ScrollParams scrollParams = GetScrollParams(horz: true);
				if (scrollParams.Enabled)
				{
					return scrollParams.Position != scrollParams.Min;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanScrollRight
		{
			get
			{
				if (!((Controller)this).Control.IsVisible)
				{
					return false;
				}
				ScrollParams scrollParams = GetScrollParams(horz: true);
				if (scrollParams.Enabled)
				{
					return scrollParams.Position != scrollParams.MaxPosition;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool CanScrollUp
		{
			get
			{
				if (!((Controller)this).Control.IsVisible)
				{
					return false;
				}
				ScrollParams scrollParams = GetScrollParams(horz: false);
				if (scrollParams.Enabled)
				{
					return scrollParams.Position != scrollParams.Min;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public LayoutControllerBase(ILayoutControlBase control)
			: base((IScrollControl)control)
		{
		}

		internal bool ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(bool horz, int delta)
		{
			ScrollParams scrollParams = GetScrollParams(horz);
			return ((Controller)this).ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(scrollParams, delta);
		}

		private ScrollParams GetScrollParams(bool horz)
		{
			if (horz)
			{
				return ((Controller)this).HorzScrollParams.Enabled ? ((Controller)this).HorzScrollParams : ((Controller)this).VertScrollParams;
			}
			return ((Controller)this).VertScrollParams.Enabled ? ((Controller)this).VertScrollParams : ((Controller)this).HorzScrollParams;
		}

		public virtual FrameworkElement GetMoveableItem(Point p)
		{
			return (FrameworkElement)((PanelControllerBase)this).IPanel.ChildAt(p, true, true, false);
		}

		protected virtual bool CanItemDragAndDrop()
		{
			return ILayoutControl.AllowItemMoving;
		}

		protected virtual bool CanDragAndDropItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual DragAndDropController CreateItemDragAndDropControler(Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override bool WantsDragAndDrop(Point p, out DragAndDropController controller)
		{
			if (WantsItemDragAndDrop(p, (Point point) => GetMoveableItem(point), out controller))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ((ScrollControlController)this).WantsDragAndDrop(p, ref controller);
		}

		protected bool WantsItemDragAndDrop(Point p, Func<Point, FrameworkElement> getItem, out DragAndDropController controller)
		{
			if (CanItemDragAndDrop())
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = getItem(p);
				if (frameworkElement != null && CanDragAndDropItem(frameworkElement))
				{
					controller = CreateItemDragAndDropControler(p, frameworkElement);
					return true;
				}
			}
			controller = null;
			return false;
		}
	}
	public abstract class LayoutItemDragAndDropControllerBase : DragAndDropController
	{
		public static double DragImageOpacity = 0.7;

		private object _MaxWidthStoredValue;

		private object _MaxHeightStoredValue;

		private object _OpacityStoredValue;

		public Point StartDragRelativePoint { get; set; }

		protected override bool AllowAutoScrolling => true;

		protected LayoutControllerBase Controller => (LayoutControllerBase)(object)((DragAndDropController)this).Controller;

		protected FrameworkElement DragControl { get; private set; }

		protected int DragControlIndex { get; private set; }

		protected Point DragControlOrigin { get; private set; }

		protected Panel DragControlParent { get; private set; }

		protected FrameworkElement DragControlPlaceHolder { get; private set; }

		protected bool IsDragControlVisible
		{
			get
			{
				if (DragControlParent != null)
				{
					return UIElementExtensions.GetVisible((UIElement)DragControlParent);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected Size DragImageSize
		{
			get
			{
				Size size = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize(((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage);
				if (size == SizeHelper.Zero)
				{
					size = ((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.DesiredSize;
					SizeHelper.Deflate(ref size, ((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.Margin);
				}
				return size;
			}
		}

		public LayoutItemDragAndDropControllerBase(Controller controller, Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
			: base(controller, startDragPoint)
		{
			DragControl = dragControl;
			DragControlParent = (Panel)FrameworkElementExtensions.GetParent(DragControl);
			if (IsDragControlVisible)
			{
				DragControlOrigin = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetPosition(DragControl, ((Controller)Controller).Control);
				DragControlPlaceHolder = CreateDragControlPlaceHolder();
				InitDragControlPlaceHolder();
			}
			else
			{
				DragControlOrigin = PointHelper.Empty;
			}
			DragControlIndex = ((DragControlParent != null) ? DragControlParent.Children.IndexOf(DragControl) : (-1));
			if (!PointHelper.IsEmpty(DragControlOrigin))
			{
				StartDragRelativePoint = new Point((((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragPoint.X - DragControlOrigin.X) / DragControl.ActualWidth, (((DragAndDropController)this).StartDragPoint.Y - DragControlOrigin.Y) / DragControl.ActualHeight);
			}
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElement CreateDragControlPlaceHolder()
		{
			return new Canvas();
		}

		protected virtual void InitDragControlPlaceHolder()
		{
			if (DragControlPlaceHolder is Panel)
			{
				((Panel)DragControlPlaceHolder).Background = Controller.ILayoutControl.MovingItemPlaceHolderBrush;
			}
			Controller.LayoutProvider.CopyLayoutInfo(DragControl, DragControlPlaceHolder);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement CreateDragImage()
		{
			return DragControl;
		}

		protected override void InitializeDragImage()
		{
			_MaxWidthStoredValue = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage, FrameworkElement.MaxWidthProperty);
			_MaxHeightStoredValue = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage, FrameworkElement.MaxHeightProperty);
			_OpacityStoredValue = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage, UIElement.OpacityProperty);
			((DragAndDropController)this).InitializeDragImage();
			if (double.IsNaN(((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.Width))
			{
				((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.MaxWidth = Math.Min(((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.MaxWidth, ((PanelControllerBase)Controller).IPanel.ContentBounds.Width);
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.Height))
			{
				((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.MaxHeight = Math.Min(((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.MaxHeight, ((PanelControllerBase)Controller).IPanel.ContentBounds.Height);
			}
			((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.Opacity = DragImageOpacity;
			if (IsDragControlVisible)
			{
				DragControlParent.Children.Insert(DragControlIndex, DragControlPlaceHolder);
			}
		}

		protected override void FinalizeDragImage()
		{
			if (IsDragControlVisible)
			{
				DragControlParent.Children.Remove(DragControlPlaceHolder);
			}
			DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage, FrameworkElement.MaxWidthProperty, _MaxWidthStoredValue);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage, FrameworkElement.MaxHeightProperty, _MaxHeightStoredValue);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage, UIElement.OpacityProperty, _OpacityStoredValue);
			((DragAndDropController)this).FinalizeDragImage();
		}

		protected override Point GetDragImageOffset()
		{
			Size dragImageSize = DragImageSize;
			return new Point(0.0 - Math.Floor(StartDragRelativePoint.X * dragImageSize.Width) - ((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.Margin.Left, 0.0 - Math.Floor(StartDragRelativePoint.Y * dragImageSize.Height) - ((DragAndDropController)this).DragImage.Margin.Top);
		}
	}
	internal class LayoutControlBaseScrollBehavior : ScrollBehaviorBase
	{
		private LayoutControllerBase GetController(DependencyObject source)
		{
			return (source as LayoutControlBase).With((LayoutControlBase x) => x.Controller);
		}

		public override bool CanScrollDown(DependencyObject source)
		{
			LayoutControllerBase controller = GetController(source);
			return controller.Return((LayoutControllerBase x) => x.CanScrollDown, () => false);
		}

		public override bool CanScrollLeft(DependencyObject source)
		{
			LayoutControllerBase controller = GetController(source);
			return controller.Return((LayoutControllerBase x) => x.CanScrollLeft, () => false);
		}

		public override bool CanScrollRight(DependencyObject source)
		{
			LayoutControllerBase controller = GetController(source);
			return controller.Return((LayoutControllerBase x) => x.CanScrollRight, () => false);
		}

		public override bool CanScrollUp(DependencyObject source)
		{
			LayoutControllerBase controller = GetController(source);
			return controller.Return((LayoutControllerBase x) => x.CanScrollUp, () => false);
		}

		public override bool CheckHandlesMouseWheelScrolling(DependencyObject source)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public override void MouseWheelDown(DependencyObject source, int lineCount)
		{
			ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(source, -lineCount, horz: false);
		}

		public override void MouseWheelLeft(DependencyObject source, int lineCount)
		{
			ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(source, lineCount, horz: true);
		}

		public override void MouseWheelRight(DependencyObject source, int lineCount)
		{
			ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(source, -lineCount, horz: true);
		}

		public override void MouseWheelUp(DependencyObject source, int lineCount)
		{
			ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(source, lineCount, horz: false);
		}

		public override bool PreventMouseScrolling(DependencyObject source)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public override void ScrollToHorizontalOffset(DependencyObject source, double offset)
		{
			GetController(source).Do(delegate(LayoutControllerBase x)
			{
				x.ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(horz: true, -(int)offset);
			});
		}

		public override void ScrollToVerticalOffset(DependencyObject source, double offset)
		{
			GetController(source).Do(delegate(LayoutControllerBase x)
			{
				x.ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(horz: false, (int)offset);
			});
		}

		private void ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(DependencyObject source, int lineCount, bool horz)
		{
			GetController(source).Do(delegate(LayoutControllerBase x)
			{
				x.ProcessMouseWheelScrolling(horz, 120 * lineCount);
			});
		}
	}
	public enum ItemAlignment
	{
		Start,
		Center,
		End,
		Stretch
	}
	public class ItemInfo
	{
		public double Offset = double.NaN;

		public ItemAlignment Alignment { get; private set; }

		public bool IsProcessed { get; set; }

		public double Length { get; set; }

		public double MinLength { get; set; }

		public double MaxLength { get; private set; }

		public ItemInfo(double length, double minLength, double maxLength, ItemAlignment alignment)
		{
			MinLength = minLength;
			MaxLength = maxLength;
			Length = length;
			Alignment = alignment;
		}
	}
	public class ItemInfos : List<ItemInfo>
	{
		public double AvailableLength { get; private set; }

		public double ItemSpace { get; private set; }

		protected double CenteredLength => GetLength(ItemAlignment.Center);

		protected double FixedLength => Length - GetLength(ItemAlignment.Stretch);

		protected double Length => GetLength(null);

		public ItemInfos(double availableLength, double itemSpace)
		{
			AvailableLength = availableLength;
			ItemSpace = itemSpace;
		}

		public virtual void ArrangeItems(FrameworkElements items, Func<FrameworkElement, ItemAlignment> getItemAlignment)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			while (num3 < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - (num + num2))
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[num3];
				switch (getItemAlignment(frameworkElement))
				{
				case ItemAlignment.Center:
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).RemoveAt(num3);
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Insert(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - num2, frameworkElement);
					num++;
					break;
				case ItemAlignment.End:
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).RemoveAt(num3);
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Insert(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count, frameworkElement);
					num2++;
					break;
				default:
					num3++;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void Calculate()
		{
			CalculateStretchedLengths();
			CalculateOffsets();
		}

		protected virtual void CalculateOffsets()
		{
			double num = CalculateOffsets(0.0, forward: true, (ItemInfo itemInfo) => itemInfo.Alignment == ItemAlignment.Start || itemInfo.Alignment == ItemAlignment.Stretch);
			double num2 = CalculateOffsets(Math.Max(Length, AvailableLength), forward: false, (ItemInfo itemInfo) => itemInfo.Alignment == ItemAlignment.End);
			CalculateOffsets(Math.Round((num + num2 - CenteredLength) / 2.0), forward: true, (ItemInfo itemInfo) => itemInfo.Alignment == ItemAlignment.Center);
		}

		protected virtual double CalculateOffsets(double offset, bool forward, Predicate<ItemInfo> isItemForProcessing)
		{
			int num = ((!forward) ? (base.Count - 1) : 0);
			while ((forward && num < base.Count) || (!forward && num >= 0))
			{
				ItemInfo itemInfo = base[num];
				if (isItemForProcessing(itemInfo))
				{
					if (!forward)
					{
						offset -= itemInfo.Length;
					}
					itemInfo.Offset = offset;
					offset = ((!forward) ? (offset - ItemSpace) : (offset + (itemInfo.Length + ItemSpace)));
				}
				num = ((!forward) ? (num - 1) : (num + 1));
			}
			return offset;
		}

		protected virtual void CalculateStretchedLengths()
		{
			List<ItemInfo> stretchedItems = GetStretchedItems();
			if (stretchedItems.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			StretchedLengthsCalculator stretchedLengthsCalculator = new StretchedLengthsCalculator(Math.Max(0.0, AvailableLength - FixedLength - (double)(stretchedItems.Count - 1) * ItemSpace));
			stretchedLengthsCalculator.AddRange(stretchedItems);
			if (stretchedLengthsCalculator.CanFitItemsUniformly())
			{
				stretchedLengthsCalculator.ForEach(delegate(ItemInfo item)
				{
					item.MinLength = item.Length;
					item.Length = 0.0;
				});
			}
			stretchedLengthsCalculator.Calculate();
		}

		protected virtual double GetLength(ItemAlignment? alignment)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			using (Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					ItemInfo current = enumerator.Current;
					if (!alignment.HasValue || current.Alignment == alignment)
					{
						num += current.Length + ItemSpace;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num != 0.0)
			{
				num -= ItemSpace;
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected List<ItemInfo> GetStretchedItems()
		{
			List<ItemInfo> list = new List<ItemInfo>();
			using Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				ItemInfo current = enumerator.Current;
				if (current.Alignment == ItemAlignment.Stretch)
				{
					list.Add(current);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class StretchedLengthsCalculator : List<ItemInfo>
	{
		public double AvailableLength { get; private set; }

		public bool NeedsMoreLength { get; private set; }

		protected double ItemsLength
		{
			get
			{
				double num = 0.0;
				using Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					ItemInfo current = enumerator.Current;
					if (!current.IsProcessed)
					{
						num += current.Length;
					}
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public StretchedLengthsCalculator(double availableLength)
		{
			AvailableLength = availableLength;
		}

		public void Calculate()
		{
			if (base.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			double availableLength = (double.IsInfinity(AvailableLength) ? ItemsLength : AvailableLength);
			while (true)
			{
				double itemsLength = ItemsLength;
				double num = 0.0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				double num4 = 0.0;
				for (int i = 0; i < base.Count; i++)
				{
					ItemInfo itemInfo = base[i];
					if (!itemInfo.IsProcessed)
					{
						double num5 = ((itemsLength == 0.0) ? GetUniformStretchedOffset(i + 1) : Math.Round(availableLength * (num + itemInfo.Length) / itemsLength));
						num += itemInfo.Length;
						itemInfo.Length = num5 - num2;
						num2 += itemInfo.Length;
						if (itemInfo.Length < itemInfo.MinLength)
						{
							num3 += itemInfo.MinLength - itemInfo.Length;
						}
						if (itemInfo.Length > itemInfo.MaxLength)
						{
							num4 += itemInfo.Length - itemInfo.MaxLength;
						}
					}
				}
				if (num3 != 0.0 || num4 != 0.0)
				{
					NeedsMoreLength = num3 != 0.0;
					ProcessMinOrMaxLengthItems(num3 > num4, ref availableLength);
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public bool CanFitItemsUniformly()
		{
			return ItemsLength != AvailableLength;
		}

		protected double GetUniformStretchedOffset(int index)
		{
			if (base.Count == 1)
			{
				return AvailableLength * (double)index;
			}
			return Math.Round(AvailableLength * (double)index / (double)base.Count);
		}

		protected void ProcessMinOrMaxLengthItems(bool processMinLengthItems, ref double availableLength)
		{
			using (Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					ItemInfo current = enumerator.Current;
					if (!current.IsProcessed && ((processMinLengthItems && current.Length < current.MinLength) || (!processMinLengthItems && current.Length > current.MaxLength)))
					{
						if (processMinLengthItems)
						{
							current.Length = current.MinLength;
						}
						else
						{
							current.Length = current.MaxLength;
						}
						current.IsProcessed = true;
						availableLength -= current.Length;
					}
				}
			}
			availableLength = Math.Max(0.0, availableLength);
		}
	}
	public enum LayoutItemInsertionKind
	{
		None,
		Left,
		Top,
		Right,
		Bottom,
		Inside
	}
	public class LayoutItemInsertionPoint : IEquatable<LayoutItemInsertionPoint>
	{
		public FrameworkElement Element { get; set; }

		public bool IsInternalInsertion { get; set; }

		public LayoutItemInsertionPoint(FrameworkElement element, bool isInternalInsertion)
		{
			Element = element;
			IsInternalInsertion = isInternalInsertion;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is LayoutItemInsertionPoint)
			{
				return Equals((LayoutItemInsertionPoint)obj);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public bool Equals(LayoutItemInsertionPoint other)
		{
			if (other != null && other.Element == Element)
			{
				return other.IsInternalInsertion == IsInternalInsertion;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItemInsertionPoints : List<LayoutItemInsertionPoint>
	{
		public LayoutItemInsertionPoint Find(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.Count; i++)
			{
				if (base[i].Element == element)
				{
					return base[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public struct LayoutItemInsertionInfo : IEquatable<LayoutItemInsertionInfo>
	{
		public FrameworkElement DestinationItem;

		public LayoutItemInsertionKind InsertionKind;

		public LayoutItemInsertionPoint InsertionPoint;

		public LayoutItemInsertionInfo(FrameworkElement destinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind, LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint)
		{
			DestinationItem = destinationItem;
			InsertionKind = insertionKind;
			InsertionPoint = insertionPoint;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is LayoutItemInsertionInfo)
			{
				return Equals((LayoutItemInsertionInfo)obj);
			}
			return base.Equals(obj);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public bool Equals(LayoutItemInsertionInfo info)
		{
			if (DestinationItem == info.DestinationItem && InsertionKind == info.InsertionKind)
			{
				if (InsertionPoint != null || info.InsertionPoint != null)
				{
					if (InsertionPoint != null)
					{
						return InsertionPoint.Equals(info.InsertionPoint);
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	{
		protected class BorderContentFiller : Control
		{
			private bool _IsEmpty;

			protected readonly Size EmptyDesiredSize = new Size(60.0, 30.0);

			public Size AvailableSize { get; private set; }

			public bool IsEmpty
			{
				get
				{
					return _IsEmpty;
				}
				set
				{
					if (IsEmpty != value)
					{
						_IsEmpty = value;
						FrameworkElement frameworkElement = this;
						do
						{
							frameworkElement.InvalidateMeasure();
							frameworkElement = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetVisualParent(frameworkElement);
						}
						while (frameworkElement != null && (object)frameworkElement != Owner);
					}
				}
			}

			public LayoutGroup Owner { get; private set; }

			public BorderContentFiller(LayoutGroup owner)
			{
				base.IsTabStop = false;
				Owner = owner;
			}

			protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
			{
				AvailableSize = availableSize;
				if (IsEmpty)
				{
					SizeHelper.UpdateMinSize(ref availableSize, EmptyDesiredSize);
					return availableSize;
				}
				return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
			}

			protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeBounds)
			{
				if (!((PanelBase)Owner).IsArranging)
				{
					((UIElement)(object)Owner).InvalidateArrange();
				}
				return base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeBounds);
			}
		}

			}
		}

		{

				}
				return LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None;
			}

		protected virtual Orientation VisibleOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001b: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001f: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				if (HasTabs)
				{
					HeaderLocation headerLocation = TabControl.View.HeaderLocation;
					if ((int)headerLocation != 2 && (int)headerLocation != 4)
					{
						return Orientation.Vertical;
					}
					return Orientation.Horizontal;
				}
				return Orientation;
			}
		}

		Orientation ILayoutGroupModel.CollapseDirection => CollapseDirection;

		Orientation ILayoutGroupModel.Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return Orientation;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Orientation == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value == Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(OrientationProperty);
					if (Orientation == value)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				Orientation = value;
			}
		}

		bool ILayoutGroup.HasNewChildren
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasNewChildren)
				{
					return true;
				}
				foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
				{
					if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && ((ILayoutGroup)item).HasNewChildren)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public LayoutGroup()
		{
			ItemSizers = CreateItemSizers();
			ItemSizers.SizingAreaWidth = base.ItemSpace;
			_IsActuallyCollapsed = IsActuallyCollapsed;
			About.CheckLicenseShowNagScreen(typeof(LayoutGroup));
		}

		public void ApplyItemStyle()
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				ApplyItemStyle(item);
			}
		}

		public virtual LayoutGroup CreateGroup()
		{
			LayoutGroup layoutGroup = (LayoutGroup)GetGroupType().GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes).Invoke(null);
			if (GroupBoxDisplayMode != GroupBoxDisplayMode.Default)
			{
				layoutGroup.GroupBoxDisplayMode = GroupBoxDisplayMode;
			}
			return layoutGroup;
		}

		public FrameworkElements GetArrangedLogicalChildren(bool visibleOnly)
		{
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(visibleOnly);
			LayoutProvider.ArrangeItems(logicalChildren);
			return logicalChildren;
		}

		public ILayoutGroup MoveChildrenToNewGroup()
		{
			LayoutGroup layoutGroup = CreateGroup();
			((ILayoutGroupModel)layoutGroup).Orientation = Orientation;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				((Panel)(object)this).Children.Remove(item);
				((Panel)(object)layoutGroup).Children.Add(item);
			}
			((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup);
			return layoutGroup;
		}

		public bool MoveChildrenToParent()
		{
			if (IsLocked)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is Panel panel))
			{
				return false;
			}
			int index = panel.Children.IndexOf((UIElement)(object)this);
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false);
			for (int num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				FrameworkElement element = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[num];
				((Panel)(object)this).Children.Remove(element);
				panel.Children.Insert(index, element);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public ILayoutGroup MoveChildToNewGroup(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			int index = ((Panel)(object)this).Children.IndexOf(child);
			((Panel)(object)this).Children.RemoveAt(index);
			LayoutGroup layoutGroup = CreateGroup();
			InitGroupForChild(layoutGroup, child);
			((Panel)(object)layoutGroup).Children.Add(child);
			((Panel)(object)this).Children.Insert(index, (UIElement)(object)layoutGroup);
			return layoutGroup;
		}

		public bool OptimizeLayout()
		{
			return OptimizeLayout(keepEmptyTabs: false);
		}

		public bool OptimizeLayout(bool keepEmptyTabs)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int num = 0;
			while (true)
			{
				FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false);
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count == num)
				{
					break;
				}
				foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)
				{
					if (item.IsLayoutGroup())
					{
						flag = ((ILayoutGroup)item).OptimizeLayout(keepEmptyTabs) || flag;
					}
				}
				num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count;
			}
			return OnOptimizeLayout(keepEmptyTabs) || flag;
		}

		public bool SelectTab(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (!HasTabs)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true)).IndexOf(child);
			bool flag = num != -1;
			if (flag)
			{
				((ILayoutGroup)this).SelectedTabIndex = num;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected override void OnLoaded()
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnLoaded();
			UpdateActualGroupBoxDisplayMode();
		}

		protected static void InvalidateParents(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = child;
			do
			{
				frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)frameworkElement.Parent;
				frameworkElement?.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			while (frameworkElement.IsLayoutGroup());
		}

		protected void CheckUnfocusableState(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (IsChildUnfocusable(child))
			{
				InitializeUnfocusableState(child);
			}
			else
			{
				FinalizeUnfocusableState(child);
			}
		}

		protected void CheckUnfocusableStateForChildren()
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement child in ((Panel)(object)this).Children)
			{
				CheckUnfocusableState(child);
			}
		}

		protected void InitializeUnfocusableState(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
			{
				StorePropertyValue(child, KeyboardNavigation.ControlTabNavigationProperty, ControlTabNavigationInfoProperty);
				StorePropertyValue(child, KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty, TabNavigationInfoProperty);
				if (child.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && Keyboard.FocusedElement is DependencyObject)
				{
					DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope((DependencyObject)Keyboard.FocusedElement);
					FocusManager.SetFocusedElement(focusScope, null);
				}
				KeyboardNavigation.SetTabNavigation(child, KeyboardNavigationMode.None);
				KeyboardNavigation.SetControlTabNavigation(child, KeyboardNavigationMode.None);
			}
		}

		protected void FinalizeUnfocusableState(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			RestorePropertyValue(child, ControlTabNavigationInfoProperty, KeyboardNavigation.ControlTabNavigationProperty);
			RestorePropertyValue(child, TabNavigationInfoProperty, KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty);
		}

		protected override void AttachChildPropertyListeners(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).AttachChildPropertyListeners(child);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "HorizontalAlignment", ChildHorizontalAlignmentListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "VerticalAlignment", ChildVerticalAlignmentListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "Width", ChildWidthListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "Height", ChildHeightListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "MaxWidth", ChildMaxWidthListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "MaxHeight", ChildMaxHeightListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "Style", ChildStyleListener);
			PanelBase.AttachChildPropertyListener(child, "Visibility", ChildVisibilityListener);
		}

		protected override void DetachChildPropertyListeners(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).DetachChildPropertyListeners(child);
			if (!(child.Parent is LayoutGroup))
			{
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildHorizontalAlignmentListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildVerticalAlignmentListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildWidthListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildHeightListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildMaxWidthListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildMaxHeightListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildStyleListener);
				PanelBase.DetachChildPropertyListener(child, ChildVisibilityListener);
			}
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<UIElement> GetInternalElements()
		{
			foreach (UIElement item in BaseGetInternalElements())
			{
				yield return item;
			}
			if (Border != null)
			{
				yield return Border;
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool IsChildBorderless(ILayoutGroup child)
		{
			return !HasTabs;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsChildPermanent(ILayoutGroup child, bool keepTabs)
		{
			return HasTabs && keepTabs;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsChildUnfocusable(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (((PanelBase)this).IsLogicalChild((UIElement)child))
			{
				return IsLogicalChildUnfocusable(child);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsLogicalChildUnfocusable(FrameworkElement logicalChild)
		{
			if (!IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				if (HasTabs)
				{
					return logicalChild != SelectedTabChild;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override bool IsTempChild(UIElement child)
		{
			if (!((PanelBase)this).IsTempChild(child))
			{
				return ((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers).IsItem(child);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual bool MakeChildVisible(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (HasGroupBox)
			{
				if (IsActuallyCollapsed)
				{
					IsCollapsed = false;
				}
				return !IsActuallyCollapsed;
			}
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				return SelectTab(child);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void OnChildAdded(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnChildAdded(child);
			if (child.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				OnChildGroupAdded((ILayoutGroup)child);
			}
			if (Root != null)
			{
				Root.ControlAdded(child);
			}
			ApplyItemStyle(child);
			CheckUnfocusableState(child);
		}

		protected override void OnChildRemoved(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnChildRemoved(child);
			if (Root != null)
			{
				Root.ControlRemoved(child);
			}
			if (child.Parent == null)
			{
				ClearItemStyle(child);
			}
			if (!(child.Parent is LayoutGroup) && child is Control)
			{
				FinalizeUnfocusableState((Control)child);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildGroupAdded(ILayoutGroup childGroup)
		{
			childGroup.IsCustomization = IsCustomization;
			childGroup.ItemSizerStyle = ItemSizerStyle;
			foreach (LayoutGroupPartStyle value in EnumExtensions.GetValues(typeof(LayoutGroupPartStyle)))
			{
				childGroup.UpdatePartStyle(value);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildGroupHeaderChanged(LayoutGroup childGroup)
		{
			UpdateTab((FrameworkElement)(object)childGroup, updateStyle: false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildGroupHeaderTemplateChanged(LayoutGroup childGroup)
		{
			UpdateTab((FrameworkElement)(object)childGroup, updateStyle: false);
		}

		protected override void OnChildPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty propertyListener, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnChildPropertyChanged(child, propertyListener, oldValue, newValue);
			if (propertyListener == ChildHorizontalAlignmentListener)
			{
				OnChildHorizontalAlignmentChanged(child);
			}
			if (propertyListener == ChildVerticalAlignmentListener)
			{
				OnChildVerticalAlignmentChanged(child);
			}
			if (propertyListener == ChildWidthListener || propertyListener == ChildHeightListener || propertyListener == ChildMaxWidthListener || propertyListener == ChildMaxHeightListener)
			{
				OnChildSizeChanged(child);
			}
			if (propertyListener == ChildStyleListener)
			{
				OnChildStyleChanged(child);
			}
			if (propertyListener == ChildVisibilityListener && newValue != null && !newValue.Equals(oldValue))
			{
				OnChildVisibilityChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildHorizontalAlignmentChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			InvalidateParents(child);
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildVerticalAlignmentChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			InvalidateParents(child);
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildSizeChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			InvalidateParents(child);
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildStyleChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (child is LayoutItem layoutItem)
			{
				if (((FrameworkElement)(object)layoutItem).Style == null)
				{
					ApplyItemStyle(layoutItem);
				}
				else if (GetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(layoutItem) && ((FrameworkElement)(object)layoutItem).Style != GetItemStyle())
				{
					SetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(layoutItem, value: false);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnChildVisibilityChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			UpdateDesiredAlignment();
			if (Root != null)
			{
				Root.ControlVisibilityChanged(child);
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<UIElement> BaseGetInternalElements()
		{
			return ((PanelBase)this).GetInternalElements();
		}

		private void RestorePropertyValue(DependencyObject dObj, DependencyProperty from, DependencyProperty to)
		{
			object value = dObj.GetValue(from);
			if (value != null)
			{
				DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue(dObj, to, value);
				dObj.ClearValue(from);
			}
		}

		private void StorePropertyValue(DependencyObject dObj, DependencyProperty from, DependencyProperty to)
		{
			object value = dObj.GetValue(to);
			if (value == null)
			{
				dObj.SetValue(to, DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue(dObj, from));
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDesiredAlignment()
		{
			HorizontalAlignment? lastDesiredHorizontalAlignment = _lastDesiredHorizontalAlignment;
			HorizontalAlignment desiredHorizontalAlignment = DesiredHorizontalAlignment;
			if (lastDesiredHorizontalAlignment.GetValueOrDefault() != desiredHorizontalAlignment || !lastDesiredHorizontalAlignment.HasValue || _lastDesiredVerticalAlignment != DesiredVerticalAlignment)
			{
				_lastDesiredHorizontalAlignment = DesiredHorizontalAlignment;
				_lastDesiredVerticalAlignment = DesiredVerticalAlignment;
				if (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is LayoutGroup layoutGroup)
				{
					((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup).InvalidateMeasure();
					layoutGroup.UpdateDesiredAlignment();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size result = ((PanelBase)this).MeasureOverride(availableSize);
			if ((result.Width > availableSize.Width || result.Height > availableSize.Height) && !IsRoot && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is LayoutGroup && !((PanelBase)(LayoutGroup)(object)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).IsMeasuring)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)(LayoutGroup)(object)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			HasNewChildren = false;
			return ((PanelBase)this).ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}

		protected override void OnBeforeMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			CheckTabControlIndex();
			UpdateTabs(updateStyle: false);
			UpdateTabControlSelectedIndex();
			CheckSelectedTabChildChanged();
			if (Border != null && BorderContent != null)
			{
				Thickness borderContentPadding = BorderContentPadding;
				BorderContentPadding = new Thickness(0.0);
				Size size = RectHelper.Size(((PanelBase)this).CalculateClientBounds(availableSize));
				Size size2 = size;
				BorderContent.IsEmpty = LayoutProvider.IsContentEmpty(((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true));
				UIElementExtensions.InvalidateParentsOfModifiedChildren((UIElement)Border);
				Border.Measure(size.RoundToDpi(DXMidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
				if (IsBorderContentVisible)
				{
					bool flag = BorderContent.AvailableSize.Width == 0.0 && double.IsNaN(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Width) && ActualHorizontalAlignment != HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
					bool flag2 = BorderContent.AvailableSize.Height == 0.0 && double.IsNaN(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Height) && ActualVerticalAlignment != VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
					if (flag || flag2)
					{
						if (flag)
						{
							size.Width = double.PositiveInfinity;
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							size.Height = double.PositiveInfinity;
						}
						Border.Measure(size.RoundToDpi(DXMidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
					}
				}
				BorderMinSize = Border.DesiredSize;
				if (double.IsInfinity(size.Width) || double.IsInfinity(size.Height))
				{
					if (double.IsInfinity(size.Width))
					{
						size.Width = Border.DesiredSize.Width;
					}
					if (double.IsInfinity(size.Height))
					{
						size.Height = Border.DesiredSize.Height;
					}
					if (IsBorderContentVisible)
					{
						BorderContent.InvalidateMeasure();
					}
					Border.Measure(size.RoundToDpi(DXMidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
				}
				if (IsBorderContentVisible)
				{
					if (!BorderContent.IsMeasureValid)
					{
						BorderContentPadding = borderContentPadding;
					}
					else
					{
						BorderContentPadding = new Thickness(size.Width - BorderContent.AvailableSize.Width, size.Height - BorderContent.AvailableSize.Height, 0.0, 0.0);
					}
				}
				Border.Measure(size2.RoundToDpi(DXMidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
			}
			((PanelBase)this).OnBeforeMeasure(availableSize);
		}

		protected override void OnBeforeArrange(Size finalSize)
		{
			if (Border != null && BorderContent != null)
			{
				Thickness borderContentPadding = BorderContentPadding;
				BorderContentPadding = new Thickness(0.0);
				Rect rect = ((PanelBase)this).CalculateClientBounds(finalSize);
				Border.Arrange(rect);
				if (IsBorderContentVisible && FrameworkElementExtensions.GetVisualParent((FrameworkElement)BorderContent) != null)
				{
					Rect bounds = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds((FrameworkElement)BorderContent, (FrameworkElement)(object)this);
					if (bounds == default(Rect))
					{
						BorderContentPadding = borderContentPadding;
					}
					else
					{
						BorderContentPadding = RectHelper.Padding(rect, bounds);
					}
				}
			}
			((PanelBase)this).OnBeforeArrange(finalSize);
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size result = base.OnMeasure(availableSize);
			InitChildrenMaxSizeAsDesiredSize();
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers).MarkItemsAsUnused();
			Size result = base.OnArrange(bounds);
			((ElementPool<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers).DeleteUnusedItems();
			if (!IsItemLabelsAlignmentScope)
			{
				ItemLabelsAlignmentChanged();
			}
			else
			{
				LayoutProvider.AlignLayoutItemLabels((FrameworkElement)(object)this, GetChildrenForItemLabelsAlignment());
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Thickness GetClientPadding()
		{
			Thickness clientPadding = ((PanelBase)this).GetClientPadding();
			if (Border != null)
			{
				ThicknessHelper.Inc(ref clientPadding, BorderContentPadding);
			}
			return clientPadding;
		}

		protected override void UpdateOriginalDesiredSize(ref Size originalDesiredSize)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).UpdateOriginalDesiredSize(ref originalDesiredSize);
			if (Border != null)
			{
				Thickness contentPadding = ((PanelBase)this).ContentPadding;
				originalDesiredSize.Width = Math.Max(originalDesiredSize.Width, BorderMinSize.Width - BorderContentPadding.Left - (contentPadding.Left + contentPadding.Right));
				originalDesiredSize.Height = Math.Max(originalDesiredSize.Height, BorderMinSize.Height - BorderContentPadding.Top - (contentPadding.Top + contentPadding.Bottom));
			}
		}

		protected override LayoutProviderBase CreateLayoutProvider()
		{
			return new LayoutGroupProvider(this);
		}

		protected override LayoutParametersBase CreateLayoutProviderParameters()
		{
			return new LayoutGroupParameters(base.ItemSpace, CanItemSizing() ? ItemSizers : null);
		}

		protected virtual bool IsRemovableForOptimization(bool considerContent, bool keepEmptyTabs)
		{
			bool flag = considerContent && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false)).Count == 0;
			if (!GetIsPermanent(!flag || keepEmptyTabs))
			{
				return !considerContent || flag;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual bool OnOptimizeLayout(bool keepEmptyTabs)
		{
			bool result = false;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && ((ILayoutGroup)item).IsRemovableForOptimization(considerContent: true, keepEmptyTabs))
				{
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.Remove(item);
					result = true;
				}
			}
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false);
			if (IsRoot || !IsRemovableForOptimization(considerContent: false, keepEmptyTabs))
			{
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count == 1 && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[0].IsLayoutGroup())
				{
					ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = (ILayoutGroup)((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[0];
					if (layoutGroup.IsRemovableForOptimization(considerContent: false, keepEmptyTabs) && layoutGroup.ActualHorizontalAlignment == ActualHorizontalAlignment && layoutGroup.ActualVerticalAlignment == ActualVerticalAlignment)
					{
						if (Orientation != layoutGroup.Orientation)
						{
							((ILayoutGroupModel)this).Orientation = layoutGroup.Orientation;
							result = true;
						}
						if (layoutGroup.MoveChildrenToParent())
						{
							if (Header == null && HeaderTemplate == null)
							{
								if (layoutGroup.Header != null)
								{
									Header = layoutGroup.Header;
								}
								if (layoutGroup.HeaderTemplate != null)
								{
									HeaderTemplate = layoutGroup.HeaderTemplate;
								}
							}
							((Panel)(object)this).Children.Remove(((IControl)layoutGroup).Control);
							result = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if ((((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count == 1 || (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count > 1 && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is ILayoutGroup && ((ILayoutGroup)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).Orientation == Orientation)) && ((ILayoutGroup)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).ActualHorizontalAlignment == ActualHorizontalAlignment && ((ILayoutGroup)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).ActualVerticalAlignment == ActualVerticalAlignment)
			{
				MoveChildrenToParent();
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected void CheckGroupBox()
		{
			if (HasGroupBox && GroupBox == null)
			{
				GroupBox = CreateGroupBox();
				InitGroupBox();
				((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)GroupBox);
			}
			if (!HasGroupBox && GroupBox != null)
			{
				((Panel)(object)this).Children.Remove((UIElement)(object)GroupBox);
				GroupBox = null;
			}
		}

		protected virtual GroupBox CreateGroupBox()
		{
			return new GroupBox();
		}

		protected virtual HorizontalAlignment? GetGroupBoxHorizontalAlignment()
		{
			if (IsActuallyCollapsed && CollapseDirection == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return HorizontalAlignment.Left;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual VerticalAlignment? GetGroupBoxVerticalAlignment()
		{
			if (IsActuallyCollapsed && CollapseDirection == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return VerticalAlignment.Top;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual GroupBoxState GetGroupBoxState()
		{
			if (!IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				return GroupBoxState.Normal;
			}
			return GroupBoxState.Minimized;
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateActualGroupBoxDisplayMode()
		{
			GroupBoxDisplayMode actualGroupBoxDisplayMode = ((GroupBoxDisplayMode == GroupBoxDisplayMode.Default && Root != this) ? ((Root != null) ? Root.ActualGroupBoxDisplayMode : GroupBoxDisplayMode) : GroupBoxDisplayMode);
			ActualGroupBoxDisplayMode = actualGroupBoxDisplayMode;
		}

		protected virtual void InitGroupBox()
		{
			//IL_006b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0075: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((ContentControlBase)GroupBox).Content = new BorderContentFiller(this);
			((BorderContentFiller)((ContentControlBase)GroupBox).Content).InvalidateMeasure();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)GroupBox).SetBinding(HeaderedContentControlBase.HeaderProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Header")
			{
				Source = this
			});
			InitGroupBoxHeaderTemplate();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)GroupBox).SetBinding(GroupBox.MinimizeElementVisibilityProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("IsCollapsible")
			{
				Source = this,
				Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			InitGroupBoxAlignment();
			InitGroupBoxDisplayMode();
			InitGroupBoxState();
			InitGroupBoxStyle();
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetZIndex((FrameworkElement)(object)GroupBox, -1000);
			GroupBox.StateChanged += delegate
			{
				OnGroupBoxStateChanged();
			};
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(CollapseDirectionProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("MinimizationDirection")
			{
				Source = GroupBox
			});
		}

		protected void InitGroupBoxAlignment()
		{
			if (GroupBox != null)
			{
				GroupBox groupBox = GroupBox;
				DependencyProperty horizontalAlignmentProperty = FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty;
				HorizontalAlignment? groupBoxHorizontalAlignment = GetGroupBoxHorizontalAlignment();
				((DependencyObject)(object)groupBox).SetValue(horizontalAlignmentProperty, groupBoxHorizontalAlignment.HasValue ? ((object)groupBoxHorizontalAlignment.GetValueOrDefault()) : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
				GroupBox groupBox2 = GroupBox;
				DependencyProperty verticalAlignmentProperty = FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty;
				VerticalAlignment? groupBoxVerticalAlignment = GetGroupBoxVerticalAlignment();
				((DependencyObject)(object)groupBox2).SetValue(verticalAlignmentProperty, groupBoxVerticalAlignment.HasValue ? ((object)groupBoxVerticalAlignment.GetValueOrDefault()) : DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
			}
		}

		protected void InitGroupBoxHeaderTemplate()
		{
			if (GroupBox != null)
			{
				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)GroupBox, HeaderedContentControlBase.HeaderTemplateProperty, (object)HeaderTemplate);
			}
		}

		protected void InitGroupBoxState()
		{
			if (GroupBox != null)
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)GroupBox).SetCurrentValue(GroupBox.StateProperty, (object)GetGroupBoxState());
			}
		}

		protected void InitGroupBoxDisplayMode()
		{
			UpdateActualGroupBoxDisplayMode();
			if (GroupBox != null)
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)GroupBox).SetCurrentValue(GroupBox.DisplayModeProperty, (object)ActualGroupBoxDisplayMode);
			}
		}

		protected void InitGroupBoxStyle()
		{
			if (GroupBox != null)

				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)GroupBox, FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, (object)GetPartActualStyle(LayoutGroupPartStyle.GroupBox));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCollapseDirectionChanged()
		{
			InitGroupBoxAlignment();
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
			InvalidateParents((FrameworkElement)(object)this);
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupBoxStateChanged()
		{
			if (!IsCollapsible)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = GroupBox.State == GroupBoxState.Minimized;
			if (IsCollapsed != flag)
			{
				IsCollapsed = flag;
				if (Root != null)
				{
					Root.ModelChanged(new LayoutControlModelPropertyChangedEventArgs((DependencyObject)(object)this, "IsCollapsed", IsCollapsedProperty));
				}
			}
		}

		protected void CheckHasTabsChanged()
		{
			if (HasTabs != _HasTabs)
			{
				_HasTabs = HasTabs;
				OnHasTabsChanged();
			}
		}

		protected void CheckSelectedTabChildChanged()
		{
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				FrameworkElement selectedTabChild = SelectedTabChild;
				if (selectedTabChild != _SelectedTabChild)
				{
					FrameworkElement selectedTabChild2 = _SelectedTabChild;
					_SelectedTabChild = selectedTabChild;
					OnSelectedTabChildChanged(selectedTabChild2);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void CheckTabControl()
		{
			//IL_0096: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a0: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0079: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (HasTabs && TabControl == null)
			{
				TabControl = CreateTabControl();
				ThemedWindowsHelper.SetAllowThemedWindowIntegration((DependencyObject)(object)TabControl, false);
				InitTabControl();
				if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
				{
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)TabControl);
				}
				else
				{
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.Insert(0, (UIElement)(object)TabControl);
				}
				BindTabControlForeground();
				ThemeManager.AddThemeChangedHandler((DependencyObject)(object)TabControl, new ThemeChangedRoutedEventHandler(OnTabControlThemeChanged));
			}
			if (!HasTabs && TabControl != null)
			{
				ThemeManager.RemoveThemeChangedHandler((DependencyObject)(object)TabControl, new ThemeChangedRoutedEventHandler(OnTabControlThemeChanged));
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(Control.ForegroundProperty);
				FinalizeTabControl();
				((Panel)(object)this).Children.Remove((UIElement)(object)TabControl);
				((DependencyObject)(object)TabControl).ClearValue(ThemedWindowsHelper.AllowThemedWindowIntegrationProperty);
				TabControl = null;
			}
		}

		private void OnTabControlThemeChanged(DependencyObject sender, ThemeChangedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			BindTabControlForeground();
		}

		private void BindTabControlForeground()
		{
			if (TabControl != null)
			{
				if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource((DependencyObject)(object)TabControl, Control.ForegroundProperty).BaseValueSource > BaseValueSource.Inherited)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(Control.ForegroundProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Foreground")
					{
						Source = TabControl
					});
				}
				else
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(Control.ForegroundProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void CheckTabControlIndex()
		{
			if (HasTabs && !DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
			{
				int num = ((Panel)(object)this).Children.IndexOf((UIElement)(object)TabControl);
				if (num > 0)
				{
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.RemoveAt(num);
					((Panel)(object)this).Children.Insert(0, (UIElement)(object)TabControl);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void CopyTabHeaderInfo(FrameworkElement fromChild, FrameworkElement toElement)
		{
			LayoutGroup layoutGroup = toElement as LayoutGroup;
			object tabHeader = GetTabHeader(fromChild, useDefaultValues: false);
			DataTemplate tabHeaderTemplate = GetTabHeaderTemplate(fromChild);
			if (layoutGroup != null && !layoutGroup.IsRoot)
			{
				if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)layoutGroup, HeaderProperty, tabHeader);
					DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)layoutGroup, HeaderTemplateProperty, (object)tabHeaderTemplate);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				LayoutControl.SetTabHeader(toElement, tabHeader);
				LayoutControl.SetTabHeaderTemplate(toElement, tabHeaderTemplate);
			}
		}

		protected virtual object GetTabHeader(FrameworkElement child, bool useDefaultValues)
		{
			if (child == null)
			{
				if (!useDefaultValues)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return LocalizationRes.LayoutGroup_TabHeader_Empty;
			}
			if (LayoutControl.GetTabHeader(child) != null)
			{
				return LayoutControl.GetTabHeader(child);
			}
			if (child is LayoutGroup { IsRoot: false, Header: not null } layoutGroup)
			{
				return layoutGroup.Header;
			}
			if (!useDefaultValues)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return LocalizationRes.LayoutGroup_TabHeader_Default;
		}

		protected virtual DataTemplate GetTabHeaderTemplate(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (child == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (LayoutControl.GetTabHeaderTemplate(child) != null)
			{
				return LayoutControl.GetTabHeaderTemplate(child);
			}
			if (child is LayoutGroup { IsRoot: false, HeaderTemplate: not null } layoutGroup)
			{
				return layoutGroup.HeaderTemplate;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected int GetTabIndex(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			for (int i = 0; i < ((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Count; i++)
			{
				if (((FrameworkElement)(DXTabItem)((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items[i]).Tag == child)
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		protected int GetTabIndex(Point absolutePosition)
		{
			UIElement uIElement = FrameworkElementExtensions.FindElement((FrameworkElement)(object)TabControl, absolutePosition, (Func<UIElement, bool>)((UIElement element) => element is DXTabItem && ((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Contains(element)));
			if (uIElement == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return ((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.IndexOf(uIElement);
		}
