			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitTab(DXTabItem tab, FrameworkElement child, bool initStyle)
		{
			((HeaderedSelectorItemBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)tab).Header = GetTabHeader(child, useDefaultValues: true);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)tab, HeaderedSelectorItemBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>.HeaderTemplateProperty, (object)GetTabHeaderTemplate(child));
			BorderContentFiller borderContentFiller = ((ContentControl)(object)tab).Content as BorderContentFiller;
			if (borderContentFiller == null)
			{
				borderContentFiller = new BorderContentFiller(this);
			}
			borderContentFiller.InvalidateMeasure();
			((ContentControl)(object)tab).Content = borderContentFiller;
			if (initStyle)
			{
				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)tab, FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, (object)GetPartActualStyle(LayoutGroupPartStyle.Tab));
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)tab).Tag = child;
			if (child != null)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)tab).Visibility = child.Visibility;
				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetBinding((FrameworkElement)(object)tab, (DependencyObject)child, "IsEnabled", UIElement.IsEnabledProperty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void FinalizeTab(DXTabItem tab)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)tab).Tag = null;
		}

		protected virtual void InitTabs(bool initStyle)
		{
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_009b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a2: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0124: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_012e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true);
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count == 0 && ((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Count == 1 && GetTabIndex(null) == 0)
			{
				InitTab((DXTabItem)((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items[0], null, initStyle);
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement child = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[i];
				int tabIndex = GetTabIndex(child);
				DXTabItem val;
				if (tabIndex == -1)
				{
					val = CreateTab();
					((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Insert(i, val);
					flag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					val = (DXTabItem)((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items[tabIndex];
					if (tabIndex != i)
					{
						((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.RemoveAt(tabIndex);
						((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Insert(i, val);
					}
				}
				InitTab(val, child, tabIndex == -1 || initStyle);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedIndex = -1;
			}
			for (int num = ((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Count - 1; num >= ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count; num--)
			{
				FinalizeTab((DXTabItem)((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items[num]);
				((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.RemoveAt(num);
			}
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count == 0)
			{
				DXTabItem val2 = CreateTab();
				((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Add(val2);
				InitTab(val2, null, initStyle: true);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void FinalizeTabs()
		{
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (DXTabItem item in (IEnumerable)((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items)
			{
				DXTabItem tab = item;
				FinalizeTab(tab);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHasTabsChanged()
		{
			CheckTabControl();
			IsItemLabelsAlignmentScopeChanged();
			CheckUnfocusableStateForChildren();
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedTabChildChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue)
		{
			UnfocusTabChild(oldValue);
			CheckUnfocusableStateForChildren();
			if (MeasureSelectedTabChildOnly)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			else
			{
				((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
			}
			if (this.SelectedTabChildChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectedTabChildChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<FrameworkElement>(oldValue, SelectedTabChild));
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnTabControlDesignTimeClick(DXMouseButtonEventArgs args)
		{
			int tabIndex = GetTabIndex(((DXMouseEventArgs)args).GetPosition((UIElement)null));
			if (tabIndex != -1)
			{
				if (((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedIndex != tabIndex)
				{
					((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedIndex = tabIndex;
				}
				else
				{
					Root.TabClicked(this, SelectedTabChild);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnTabControlSelectionChanged()
		{
			((ILayoutGroup)this).SelectedTabIndex = ((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedIndex;
			if (((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedIndex != -1 && Root != null)
			{
				Root.TabClicked(this, SelectedTabChild);
			}
		}

		protected void UnfocusTabChild(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (child != null && FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(FocusManager.GetFocusScope(child)) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement && DependencyObjectExtensions.FindIsInParents((DependencyObject)frameworkElement, (DependencyObject)child))
			{
				((UIElement)(object)TabControl).Focus();
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateTab(FrameworkElement child, bool updateStyle)
		{
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				int tabIndex = GetTabIndex(child);
				if (tabIndex != -1)
				{
					InitTab((DXTabItem)((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items[tabIndex], child, updateStyle);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateTabControlSelectedIndex()
		{
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				DisableTabControlSelectionChangedNotification = true;
				((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedIndex = Math.Min(Math.Max(0, SelectedTabIndex), ((ItemsControl)(object)TabControl).Items.Count - 1);
				DisableTabControlSelectionChangedNotification = false;
				CheckSelectedTabChildChanged();
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateTabs(bool updateStyle)
		{
			if (HasTabs && TabControl != null && ((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedContainer != null)
			{
				DisableTabControlSelectionChangedNotification = true;
				FrameworkElement item = ((FrameworkElement)(object)((SelectorBase<DXTabControl, DXTabItem>)(object)TabControl).SelectedContainer).Tag as FrameworkElement;
				InitTabs(updateStyle);
				if (KeepSelectionOnTabRemoval)
				{
					SelectedTabIndex = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true)).IndexOf(item);
				}
				DisableTabControlSelectionChangedNotification = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnTabControlSelectionChanged(object sender, TabControlSelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!DisableTabControlSelectionChangedNotification)
			{
				OnTabControlSelectionChanged();
			}
		}

		protected static bool GetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(LayoutItem item)
		{
			return (bool)((DependencyObject)(object)item).GetValue(IsItemStyleSetByGroupProperty);
		}

		protected static void SetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(LayoutItem item, bool value)
		{
			if (value)
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)item).SetValue(IsItemStyleSetByGroupProperty, (object)value);
			}
			else
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)item).ClearValue(IsItemStyleSetByGroupProperty);
			}
		}

		protected void ApplyItemStyle(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element is LayoutItem)
			{
				ApplyItemStyle((LayoutItem)(object)element);
			}
			else if (element.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				((ILayoutGroup)element).ApplyItemStyle();
			}
		}

		protected void ApplyItemStyle(LayoutItem item)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)item).Style == null || GetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(item))
			{
				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)item, FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, (object)GetItemStyle());
				SetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(item, value: true);
			}
		}

		protected void ClearItemStyle()
		{
			if (GetItemStyle() != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				ClearItemStyle(item);
			}
		}

		protected void ClearItemStyle(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element is LayoutItem)
			{
				ClearItemStyle((LayoutItem)(object)element);
			}
			else if (element.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				((ILayoutGroup)element).ClearItemStyle();
			}
		}

		protected void ClearItemStyle(LayoutItem item)
		{
			if (GetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(item))
			{
				SetIsItemStyleSetByGroup(item, value: false);
				((DependencyObject)(object)item).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Style GetItemStyle()
		{
			Style itemStyle = ItemStyle;
			if (itemStyle == null && !IsRoot && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is ILayoutGroup)
			{
				itemStyle = ((ILayoutGroup)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).GetItemStyle();
			}
			return itemStyle;
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemStyleChanged()
		{
			ApplyItemStyle();
		}

		protected override void WriteToXMLCore(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			if (IsPermanent)
			{
				xml.WriteAttributeString("ID", GetXMLID((FrameworkElement)(object)this));
			}
			base.WriteToXMLCore(xml);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement ReadChildFromXMLCore(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml, IList children, int index, string id)
		{
			if (xml.Name == GetGroupType().Name)
			{
				LayoutGroup layoutGroup = element as LayoutGroup;
				if (layoutGroup == null)
				{
					layoutGroup = CreateGroup();
					if (element == null)
					{
						Root?.SetID((FrameworkElement)(object)layoutGroup, id);
					}
					LayoutControl.SetIsUserDefined((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup, value: true);
					element = (FrameworkElement)(object)layoutGroup;
				}
				AddChildFromXML(children, (FrameworkElement)(object)layoutGroup, index);
				layoutGroup.ReadFromXML(xml);
			}
			else
			{
				element = base.ReadChildFromXMLCore(element, xml, children, index, id);
			}
			return element;
		}

		protected override void WriteChildToXML(FrameworkElement child, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			if (child.GetType() == GetGroupType())
			{
				((LayoutGroup)(object)child).WriteToXML(xml);
			}
			else
			{
				base.WriteChildToXML(child, xml);
			}
		}

		protected override void ReadChildrenFromXML(XmlReader xml, out FrameworkElement lastChild)
		{
			base.ReadChildrenFromXML(xml, out lastChild);
			MoveChildrenToAvailableItems(lastChild);
		}

		protected override void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			base.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, HeaderProperty, "Header", typeof(object));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, IsCollapsedProperty, "IsCollapsed", typeof(bool));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, IsLockedProperty, "IsLocked", typeof(bool));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, LayoutControlBase.ItemSpaceProperty, "ItemSpace", typeof(double));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, OrientationProperty, "Orientation", typeof(Orientation));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, LayoutControlBase.PaddingProperty, "Padding", typeof(Thickness));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, SelectedTabIndexProperty, "SelectedTabIndex", typeof(int));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, ViewProperty, "View", typeof(LayoutGroupView));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, KeepSelectionOnTabRemovalProperty, "KeepSelectedTab", typeof(bool));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, StoredSizeProperty, "StoredSize", typeof(double));
			if (!IsRoot)
			{
				ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML((FrameworkElement)(object)this, xml);
			}
			else
			{
				Root.ReadElementFromXML(xml, (FrameworkElement)(object)this);
			}
		}

		protected override void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml)
		{
			base.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, "HorizontalAlignment", typeof(HorizontalAlignment));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty, "VerticalAlignment", typeof(VerticalAlignment));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, "Width", typeof(double));
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, "Height", typeof(double));
			if (element is LayoutItem)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)element).ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(xml);
			}
			Root?.ReadElementFromXML(xml, element);
		}

		protected override void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, HeaderProperty, "Header");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, IsCollapsedProperty, "IsCollapsed");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, IsLockedProperty, "IsLocked");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, LayoutControlBase.ItemSpaceProperty, "ItemSpace");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, OrientationProperty, "Orientation");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, LayoutControlBase.PaddingProperty, "Padding");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, SelectedTabIndexProperty, "SelectedTabIndex");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, ViewProperty, "View");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, KeepSelectionOnTabRemovalProperty, "KeepSelectedTab");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, StoredSizeProperty, "StoredSize");
			if (!IsRoot)
			{
				WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML((FrameworkElement)(object)this, xml);
			}
			else
			{
				Root.WriteElementToXML(xml, (FrameworkElement)(object)this);
			}
		}

		protected override void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, "HorizontalAlignment");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty, "VerticalAlignment");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, "Width");
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, "Height");
			if (element is LayoutItem)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)element).WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(xml);
			}
			Root?.WriteElementToXML(xml, element);
		}

		protected override void AddChildFromXML(IList children, FrameworkElement element, int index)
		{
			DependencyObject parent = element.Parent;
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)Root?.AvailableItems).Remove(element);
			base.AddChildFromXML(children, element, index);
			if ((object)element.Parent == this && (object)parent != this)
			{
				HasNewChildren = true;
			}
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement FindByXMLID(string id)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = Root.FindControl(id);
			if (frameworkElement != null && LayoutControl.GetIsUserDefined(frameworkElement) && (Root as LayoutControl).Return((LayoutControl x) => x.AddRestoredItemsToAvailableItems, () => true))
			{
				Root.SetID(frameworkElement);
				frameworkElement = null;
			}
			return frameworkElement;
		}

		protected override string GetXMLID(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return Root.GetID(element);
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new LayoutGroupController(this);
		}

		protected virtual ElementSizers CreateItemSizers()
		{
			return new ElementSizers((PanelBase)(object)this);
		}

		protected virtual Type GetGroupType()
		{
			return ((object)this).GetType();
		}

		protected virtual bool CanItemSizing()
		{
			if (Root != null)
			{
				return Root.AllowItemSizing;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected void CheckIsActuallyCollapsedChanged()
		{
			if (IsActuallyCollapsed != _IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				_IsActuallyCollapsed = IsActuallyCollapsed;
				OnIsActuallyCollapsedChanged();
			}
		}

		protected void ForEachGroup(Action<ILayoutGroup> action)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				if (item.IsLayoutGroup())
				{
					action((ILayoutGroup)item);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual FrameworkElements GetChildrenForItemLabelsAlignment()
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0016: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				return new FrameworkElements();
			}
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				FrameworkElements val = new FrameworkElements();
				if (SelectedTabChild != null)
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)val).Add(SelectedTabChild);
				}
				return val;
			}
			return ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true);
		}

		protected Rect GetClipBounds(FrameworkElement relativeTo)
		{
			if (!IsRoot && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is ILayoutGroup)
			{
				return ((ILayoutGroup)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).GetClipBounds((FrameworkElement)(object)this, relativeTo);
			}
			return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetVisualBounds((FrameworkElement)(object)this, relativeTo);
		}

		protected virtual bool GetIsPermanent()
		{
			if (View == LayoutGroupView.Group)
			{
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Name);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected bool GetIsPermanent(bool keepTabs)
		{
			if (!GetIsPermanent())
			{
				if (!IsRoot && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is ILayoutGroup)
				{
					return ((ILayoutGroup)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).IsChildPermanent(this, keepTabs);
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected ILayoutGroup GetItemLabelsAlignmentScope(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			while (item != null && (!(item is ILayoutGroup) || !((ILayoutGroup)item).IsItemLabelsAlignmentScope))
			{
				item = item.Parent as FrameworkElement;
			}
			return (ILayoutGroup)item;
		}

		protected Style GetPartActualStyle(LayoutGroupPartStyle style)
		{
			Style partStyle = GetPartStyle(style);
			if (partStyle == null)
			{
				if (Root == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				partStyle = Root.GetPartStyle(style);
			}
			return partStyle;
		}

		protected Style GetPartStyle(LayoutGroupPartStyle style)
		{
			return style switch
			{
				LayoutGroupPartStyle.GroupBox => GroupBoxStyle, 
				LayoutGroupPartStyle.Tabs => TabsStyle, 
				LayoutGroupPartStyle.Tab => TabStyle, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		protected virtual void InitChildrenMaxSizeAsDesiredSize()
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true))
			{
				if (LayoutControl.GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(item) && item.DesiredSize.Width != 0.0 && double.IsInfinity(item.MaxWidth))
				{
					item.MaxWidth = item.DesiredSize.Width;
				}
				if (LayoutControl.GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(item) && item.DesiredSize.Height != 0.0 && double.IsInfinity(item.MaxHeight))
				{
					item.MaxHeight = item.DesiredSize.Height;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InitGroupForChild(LayoutGroup group, FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				CopyTabHeaderInfo(child, (FrameworkElement)(object)group);
			}
			else
			{
				group.Orientation = Orientation;
			}
			LayoutProvider.InitGroupForChild(group, child);
		}

		protected void InitPartStyle(LayoutGroupPartStyle style)
		{
			switch (style)
			{
			case LayoutGroupPartStyle.GroupBox:
				InitGroupBoxStyle();
				break;
			case LayoutGroupPartStyle.Tabs:
				InitTabControlStyle();
				break;
			case LayoutGroupPartStyle.Tab:
				UpdateTabs(updateStyle: true);
				break;
			}
		}

		protected void IsItemLabelsAlignmentScopeChanged()
		{
			GetItemLabelsAlignmentScope((FrameworkElement)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent)?.LayoutItemLabelsAlignmentChanged();
			if (IsItemLabelsAlignmentScope)
			{
				UpdateItemLabelsAlignment();
			}
		}

		protected void ItemLabelsAlignmentChanged()
		{
			GetItemLabelsAlignmentScope((FrameworkElement)(object)this)?.LayoutItemLabelsAlignmentChanged();
		}

		protected void MoveChildrenToAvailableItems(FrameworkElement lastRemainingChild = null)
		{
			FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false);
			int num = ((lastRemainingChild != null) ? (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).IndexOf(lastRemainingChild) + 1) : 0);
			for (int i = num; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren)[i];
				if (frameworkElement.GetType() == GetGroupType() && !((LayoutGroup)(object)frameworkElement).IsPermanent)
				{
					((LayoutGroup)(object)frameworkElement).MoveChildrenToAvailableItems();
				}
				else
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)Root.AvailableItems).Add(frameworkElement);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void MoveNonUserDefinedChildrenToAvailableItems()
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(false))
			{
				if (!LayoutControl.GetIsUserDefined(item))
				{
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)Root.AvailableItems).Add(item);
				}
				else if (item.IsLayoutGroup())
				{
					((ILayoutGroup)item).MoveNonUserDefinedChildrenToAvailableItems();
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowItemSizingChanged()
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
			ForEachGroup(delegate(ILayoutGroup group)
			{
				group.AllowItemSizingChanged();
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowHorizontalSizingChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowVerticalSizingChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected override void OnAttachedPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty property, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnAttachedPropertyChanged(child, property, oldValue, newValue);
			if (property == LayoutControl.AllowHorizontalSizingProperty)
			{
				OnAllowHorizontalSizingChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == LayoutControl.AllowVerticalSizingProperty)
			{
				OnAllowVerticalSizingChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == LayoutControl.TabHeaderProperty)
			{
				OnTabHeaderChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == LayoutControl.TabHeaderTemplateProperty)
			{
				OnTabHeaderTemplateChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == LayoutControl.UseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthProperty)
			{
				OnUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthChanged(child);
			}
			if (property == LayoutControl.UseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightProperty)
			{
				OnUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCollapsed()
		{
			if (this.Collapsed != null)
			{
				this.Collapsed(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnDesignTimeClick(DXMouseButtonEventArgs args)
		{
			if (!HasGroupBox || !((IGroupBox)GroupBox).DesignTimeClick(args))
			{
				if (HasTabs)
				{
					return OnTabControlDesignTimeClick(args);
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnExpanded()
		{
			if (this.Expanded != null)
			{
				this.Expanded(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<Rect> OnGetLiveCustomizationAreas(FrameworkElement relativeTo)
		{
			foreach (ElementSizer item in (IEnumerable<ElementSizer>)ItemSizers)
			{
				yield return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds((FrameworkElement)(object)item, relativeTo);
			}
			if (HasGroupBox && GroupBox.MinimizeElementVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				Rect minimizeElementBounds = ((IGroupBox)GroupBox).MinimizeElementBounds;
				if (!minimizeElementBounds.IsEmpty)
				{
					yield return UIElementExtensions.MapRect((UIElement)(object)GroupBox, minimizeElementBounds, (UIElement)relativeTo);
				}
			}
			if (HasTabs)
			{
				if (TabControl.TabPanel != null && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)(object)TabControl.TabPanel))
				{
					yield return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds((FrameworkElement)(object)TabControl.TabPanel, relativeTo);
				}
				if (TabControl.PrevButton != null && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)TabControl.PrevButton))
				{
					yield return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds((FrameworkElement)TabControl.PrevButton, relativeTo);
				}
				if (TabControl.NextButton != null && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)TabControl.NextButton))
				{
					yield return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds((FrameworkElement)TabControl.NextButton, relativeTo);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderChanged()
		{
			if (!IsRoot && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is LayoutGroup)
			{
				((LayoutGroup)(object)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).OnChildGroupHeaderChanged(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderTemplateChanged()
		{
			InitGroupBoxHeaderTemplate();
			if (!IsRoot && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is LayoutGroup)
			{
				((LayoutGroup)(object)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).OnChildGroupHeaderTemplateChanged(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsActuallyCollapsedChanged()
		{
			InitGroupBoxAlignment();
			InitGroupBoxState();
			UpdateCollapsedSize();
			IsItemLabelsAlignmentScopeChanged();
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is UIElement)
			{
				((UIElement)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			CheckUnfocusableStateForChildren();
			if (IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				OnCollapsed();
				return;
			}
			OnExpanded();
			InvalidateParents((FrameworkElement)(object)this);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCollapsedChanged()
		{
			CheckIsActuallyCollapsedChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCollapsibleChanged()
		{
			CheckIsActuallyCollapsedChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidthChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (LayoutControl.GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(child) && child.DesiredSize.Width != 0.0)
			{
				double width = child.Width;
				if (!double.IsNaN(width))
				{
					child.Width = double.NaN;
					((UIElement)(object)this).UpdateLayout();
				}
				child.MaxWidth = child.DesiredSize.Width;
				child.Width = width;
			}
			if (!LayoutControl.GetUseDesiredWidthAsMaxWidth(child))
			{
				child.MaxWidth = double.PositiveInfinity;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeightChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (LayoutControl.GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(child) && child.DesiredSize.Height != 0.0)
			{
				double height = child.Height;
				if (!double.IsNaN(height))
				{
					child.Height = double.NaN;
					((UIElement)(object)this).UpdateLayout();
				}
				child.MaxHeight = child.DesiredSize.Height;
				child.Height = height;
			}
			if (!LayoutControl.GetUseDesiredHeightAsMaxHeight(child))
			{
				child.MaxHeight = double.PositiveInfinity;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewChanged()
		{
			CheckGroupBox();
			CheckIsActuallyCollapsedChanged();
			CheckHasTabsChanged();
			((PanelBase)this).Changed();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCollapsedSize()
		{
			DependencyProperty dp = ((CollapseDirection == Orientation.Horizontal) ? FrameworkElement.WidthProperty : FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
			DependencyProperty dependencyProperty = ((CollapseDirection == Orientation.Horizontal) ? FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty : FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty);
			if (IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				double num = (double)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(dp);
				double num2 = (double)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(dependencyProperty);
				if (!double.IsNaN(num))
				{
					StoredSize = num;
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(dp, (object)double.NaN);
				}
				if (num2 != 0.0)
				{
					_StoredMinSizePropertyValue = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, dependencyProperty);
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(dependencyProperty, (object)0.0);
				}
			}
			if (!IsActuallyCollapsed)
			{
				if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)(object)this, StoredSizeProperty))
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(dp, (object)StoredSize);
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(StoredSizeProperty);
				}
				if (_StoredMinSizePropertyValue != null)
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)(object)this, dependencyProperty, _StoredMinSizePropertyValue);
					_StoredMinSizePropertyValue = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateItemLabelsAlignment()
		{
			if (!((PanelBase)this).IsArranging)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
			}
		}

		protected internal ItemAlignment GetItemAlignment(LayoutItem item, bool horizontal)

		}

		}

		protected virtual void InvalidateChildrenMeasure()
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateMeasure();
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true))
			{
				if (item is LayoutGroup layoutGroup)
				{
					layoutGroup.InvalidateChildrenMeasure();
				}
			}
		}

		LayoutItemInsertionPoint ILayoutGroup.GetInsertionPoint(FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement destinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind, Point p)
		{
			LayoutItemInsertionPoints layoutItemInsertionPoints = new LayoutItemInsertionPoints();
			((ILayoutGroup)this).GetInsertionPoints(element, destinationItem, destinationItem, insertionKind, layoutItemInsertionPoints);
			for (int i = 0; i < layoutItemInsertionPoints.Count; i++)
			{
				if (((ILayoutGroup)this).GetInsertionPointZoneBounds(destinationItem, insertionKind, i, layoutItemInsertionPoints.Count).Contains(p))
				{
					return layoutItemInsertionPoints[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		Rect ILayoutGroup.GetInsertionPointBounds(bool isInternalInsertion, FrameworkElement relativeTo)

		void ILiveCustomizationAreasProvider.GetLiveCustomizationAreas(IList<Rect> areas, FrameworkElement relativeTo)
		{
			Rect clipBounds = GetClipBounds(relativeTo);
			if (clipBounds.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Rect item in OnGetLiveCustomizationAreas(relativeTo))
			{
				item.Intersect(clipBounds);
				if (!item.IsEmpty)
				{
					areas.Add(item);
				}
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item2 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((PanelBase)this).GetLogicalChildren(true))
			{
				if (item2 is ILiveCustomizationAreasProvider)
				{
					((ILiveCustomizationAreasProvider)item2).GetLiveCustomizationAreas(areas, relativeTo);
				}
			}
		}

		FrameworkElement ILayoutControlCustomizableItem.AddNewItem()
		{
			if (!HasTabs)
			{
				return null;
			}
			LayoutGroup layoutGroup = CreateGroup();
			((Panel)(object)this).Children.Add((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup);
			return (FrameworkElement)(object)layoutGroup;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new LayoutGroupAutomationPeer(this);
		}

	public class LayoutGroupParameters : LayoutParametersBase
	{
