	public static class LayoutGroupExtensions
	{
		public static ILayoutControl GetLayoutControl(this FrameworkElement element)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = element;
			while (frameworkElement != null && !frameworkElement.IsLayoutControl())
			{
				frameworkElement = frameworkElement.Parent as FrameworkElement;
				if (!(frameworkElement is ILayoutGroup) && !(frameworkElement is LayoutControl.ItemsContainer))
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return frameworkElement as ILayoutControl;
		}

		public static bool IsLayoutControl(this UIElement element)
		{
			if (element is ILayoutGroup)
			{
				return ((ILayoutGroup)element).IsRoot;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsLayoutGroup(this UIElement element)
		{
			if (element is ILayoutGroup)
			{
				return !((ILayoutGroup)element).IsRoot;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public static class ItemAlignmentExtensions
	{
		public static ItemAlignment GetItemAlignment(this HorizontalAlignment alignment)
		{
			return alignment switch
			{
				HorizontalAlignment.Left => ItemAlignment.Start, 
				HorizontalAlignment.Center => ItemAlignment.Center, 
				HorizontalAlignment.Right => ItemAlignment.End, 
				HorizontalAlignment.Stretch => ItemAlignment.Stretch, 
				_ => ItemAlignment.Start, 
			};
		}

		public static ItemAlignment GetItemAlignment(this VerticalAlignment alignment)
		{
			return alignment switch
			{
				VerticalAlignment.Top => ItemAlignment.Start, 
				VerticalAlignment.Center => ItemAlignment.Center, 
				VerticalAlignment.Bottom => ItemAlignment.End, 
				VerticalAlignment.Stretch => ItemAlignment.Stretch, 
				_ => ItemAlignment.Start, 
			};
		}

		public static HorizontalAlignment GetHorizontalAlignment(this ItemAlignment alignment)
		{
			return alignment switch
			{
				ItemAlignment.Start => HorizontalAlignment.Left, 
				ItemAlignment.Center => HorizontalAlignment.Center, 
				ItemAlignment.End => HorizontalAlignment.Right, 
				ItemAlignment.Stretch => HorizontalAlignment.Stretch, 
				_ => HorizontalAlignment.Left, 
			};
		}

		public static VerticalAlignment GetVerticalAlignment(this ItemAlignment alignment)
		{
			return alignment switch
			{
				ItemAlignment.Start => VerticalAlignment.Top, 
				ItemAlignment.Center => VerticalAlignment.Center, 
				ItemAlignment.End => VerticalAlignment.Bottom, 
				ItemAlignment.Stretch => VerticalAlignment.Stretch, 
				_ => VerticalAlignment.Top, 
			};
		}
	}
	public static class LayoutItemInsertionKindExtensions
	{
		public static Side? GetSide(this LayoutItemInsertionKind kind)
		{
			return kind switch
			{
				LayoutItemInsertionKind.Left => (Side)0, 
				LayoutItemInsertionKind.Top => (Side)1, 
				LayoutItemInsertionKind.Right => (Side)2, 
				LayoutItemInsertionKind.Bottom => (Side)4, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}
	}
	public class LayoutGroupController : LayoutControllerBase
	{
		public ILayoutGroup ILayoutGroup => ((Controller)this).IControl as ILayoutGroup;

		protected new LayoutGroupProvider LayoutProvider => (LayoutGroupProvider)base.LayoutProvider;

		public LayoutGroupController(ILayoutGroup control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		public virtual FrameworkElement GetItem(Point p, bool ignoreLayoutGroups, bool ignoreLocking)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)((PanelControllerBase)this).IPanel.ChildAt(p, true, true, true);
			if (frameworkElement == null && !ignoreLayoutGroups && RectHelper.New(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize(((Controller)this).Control)).Contains(p))
			{
				return ((Controller)this).Control;
			}
			if (!ignoreLocking && ILayoutGroup.IsLocked)
			{
				return ((Controller)this).Control;
			}
			if (frameworkElement.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				frameworkElement = ((ILayoutGroup)frameworkElement).GetItem(UIElementExtensions.MapPoint((UIElement)((Controller)this).Control, p, (UIElement)frameworkElement), ignoreLayoutGroups, ignoreLocking);
			}
			if (frameworkElement == null && !ILayoutGroup.IsBorderless && !ILayoutGroup.IsRoot)
			{
				frameworkElement = ((Controller)this).Control;
			}
			return frameworkElement;
		}

		public override bool IsScrollable()
		{
			return false;
		}

		public override FrameworkElement GetMoveableItem(Point p)
		{
			return GetItem(p, ignoreLayoutGroups: true, DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)((Controller)this).Control));
		}

		protected override bool CanItemDragAndDrop()
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class LayoutGroupProvider : LayoutProviderBase
	{
		private List<List<LayoutItems>> previousColumnsList = new List<List<LayoutItems>>();

		private int repeatCount;

		public static bool DetectCyclicColumnsAlignment { get; set; }

		protected new ILayoutGroupModel Model => (ILayoutGroupModel)base.Model;

		protected new LayoutGroupParameters Parameters => (LayoutGroupParameters)base.Parameters;

		static LayoutGroupProvider()
		{
			DetectCyclicColumnsAlignment = true;
		}

		public LayoutGroupProvider(ILayoutGroupModel model)
			: base(model)
		{
		}

		public void ArrangeItems(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			CreateItemInfos(0.0, 0.0).ArrangeItems(items, GetItemAlignment);
		}

		public override void CopyLayoutInfo(FrameworkElement from, FrameworkElement to)
		{
			base.CopyLayoutInfo(from, to);
			LayoutControl.SetAllowHorizontalSizing(to, LayoutControl.GetAllowHorizontalSizing(from));
			LayoutControl.SetAllowVerticalSizing(to, LayoutControl.GetAllowVerticalSizing(from));
			if (from.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = (ILayoutGroup)from;
				Size actualMaxSize = layoutGroup.ActualMaxSize;
				to.MaxWidth = actualMaxSize.Width;
				to.MaxHeight = actualMaxSize.Height;
				to.HorizontalAlignment = layoutGroup.ActualHorizontalAlignment;
				to.VerticalAlignment = layoutGroup.ActualVerticalAlignment;
			}
			if (from.Parent is ILayoutGroup layoutGroup2)
			{
				layoutGroup2.CopyTabHeaderInfo(from, to);
			}
		}

		public virtual Size GetActualMinOrMaxSize(FrameworkElements items, bool getMinSize, LayoutParametersBase parameters)
		{
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count == 0)
			{
				if (!getMinSize)
				{
					return SizeHelper.Infinite;
				}
				return SizeHelper.Zero;
			}
			if (Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				return GetActualMinOrMaxSizeWhenUncollapsed(items, getMinSize, parameters);
			}
			return GetActualMinOrMaxSizeWhenCollapsed(items, getMinSize, parameters);
		}

		public virtual Rect GetClipBounds(FrameworkElement control, FrameworkElement child, FrameworkElement relativeTo)
		{
			return FrameworkElementExtensions.GetVisualBounds(child, relativeTo);
		}

		public virtual HorizontalAlignment GetDesiredHorizontalAlignment(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			HorizontalAlignment horizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
			bool flag = Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.OneChildVisible || Model.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical;
			bool flag2 = true;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				HorizontalAlignment itemHorizontalAlignment = GetItemHorizontalAlignment(item);
				if ((flag && itemHorizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Stretch) || (!flag && !flag2 && itemHorizontalAlignment != horizontalAlignment))
				{
					horizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
					break;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					flag2 = false;
					if (!flag && (!(Model is LayoutGroup layoutGroup) || !(layoutGroup.Root is LayoutControl) || ((LayoutControl)layoutGroup.Root).UseContentBasedAlignment))
					{
						horizontalAlignment = itemHorizontalAlignment;
					}
				}
			}
			if (horizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Stretch && Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.NoChildrenVisible && Model.CollapseDirection == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				horizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
			}
			return horizontalAlignment;
		}

		public virtual VerticalAlignment GetDesiredVerticalAlignment(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			VerticalAlignment verticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
			bool flag = Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.OneChildVisible || Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal;
			bool flag2 = true;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				VerticalAlignment itemVerticalAlignment = GetItemVerticalAlignment(item);
				if ((flag && itemVerticalAlignment == VerticalAlignment.Stretch) || (!flag && !flag2 && itemVerticalAlignment != verticalAlignment))
				{
					verticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch;
					break;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					flag2 = false;
					if (!flag && (!(Model is LayoutGroup layoutGroup) || !(layoutGroup.Root is LayoutControl) || ((LayoutControl)layoutGroup.Root).UseContentBasedAlignment))
					{
						verticalAlignment = itemVerticalAlignment;
					}
				}
			}
			if (verticalAlignment == VerticalAlignment.Stretch && Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.NoChildrenVisible && Model.CollapseDirection == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				verticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
			}
			return verticalAlignment;
		}

		public virtual HorizontalAlignment GetItemHorizontalAlignment(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			HorizontalAlignment horizontalAlignment = (item.IsLayoutGroup() ? ((ILayoutGroup)item).ActualHorizontalAlignment : item.HorizontalAlignment);
			if (horizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Stretch && !double.IsNaN(item.Width))
			{
				horizontalAlignment = ((Model.Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal) ? HorizontalAlignment.Center : HorizontalAlignment.Left);
			}
			return horizontalAlignment;
		}

		public virtual VerticalAlignment GetItemVerticalAlignment(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			VerticalAlignment verticalAlignment = (item.IsLayoutGroup() ? ((ILayoutGroup)item).ActualVerticalAlignment : item.VerticalAlignment);
			if (verticalAlignment == VerticalAlignment.Stretch && !double.IsNaN(item.Height))
			{
				verticalAlignment = ((Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? VerticalAlignment.Center : VerticalAlignment.Top);
			}
			return verticalAlignment;
		}

		public void InitChildFromGroup(FrameworkElement child, FrameworkElement group)
		{
			ItemAlignment itemAlignment = GetItemAlignment(child);
			ItemAlignment itemAlignment2 = GetItemAlignment(group);
			if (itemAlignment2 == ItemAlignment.Center || itemAlignment2 == ItemAlignment.End)
			{
				SetItemAlignment(child, itemAlignment2);
			}
			else if (itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.Center || itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.End)
			{
				SetItemAlignment(child, ItemAlignment.Start);
			}
		}

		public virtual void InitGroupForChild(LayoutGroup group, FrameworkElement child)
		{
			Func<ItemAlignment, bool> func = (ItemAlignment alignment) => alignment == ItemAlignment.Center || alignment == ItemAlignment.End;
			if ((Model.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None || Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) && func(GetItemHorizontalAlignment(child).GetItemAlignment()))
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)group).HorizontalAlignment = child.HorizontalAlignment;
			}
			if ((Model.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None || Model.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) && func(GetItemVerticalAlignment(child).GetItemAlignment()))
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)group).VerticalAlignment = child.VerticalAlignment;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool IsContentEmpty(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			bool flag = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count == 0;
			if (!flag && Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.OneChildVisible)
			{
				foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
				{
					flag = item.IsLayoutGroup() && ((ILayoutGroup)item).IsUIEmpty;
					if (!flag)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public virtual bool IsLayoutUniform(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			if (Model.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				return true;
			}
			ItemAlignment? itemAlignment = null;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				ItemAlignment itemAlignment2 = GetItemAlignment(item);
				if (itemAlignment2 == ItemAlignment.Stretch)
				{
					itemAlignment2 = ItemAlignment.Start;
				}
				if (!itemAlignment.HasValue)
				{
					itemAlignment = itemAlignment2;
				}
				else if (itemAlignment2 != itemAlignment)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public virtual void SetItemHorizontalAlignment(FrameworkElement item, HorizontalAlignment value, bool updateWidth)
		{
			bool flag = DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)item, FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty);
			HorizontalAlignment horizontalAlignment = item.HorizontalAlignment;
			if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && value == ((ILayoutGroup)item).DesiredHorizontalAlignment)
			{
				item.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				item.HorizontalAlignment = value;
			}
			if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && item.HorizontalAlignment == horizontalAlignment && DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)item, FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty) != flag)
			{
				((ILayoutGroup)item.Parent).ChildHorizontalAlignmentChanged(item);
			}
			if (updateWidth && value == HorizontalAlignment.Stretch && !double.IsNaN(item.Width))
			{
				item.Width = double.NaN;
			}
		}

		public virtual void SetItemVerticalAlignment(FrameworkElement item, VerticalAlignment value, bool updateHeight)
		{
			bool flag = DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)item, FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty);
			VerticalAlignment verticalAlignment = item.VerticalAlignment;
			if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && value == ((ILayoutGroup)item).DesiredVerticalAlignment)
			{
				item.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				item.VerticalAlignment = value;
			}
			if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && item.VerticalAlignment == verticalAlignment && DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)item, FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty) != flag)
			{
				((ILayoutGroup)item.Parent).ChildVerticalAlignmentChanged(item);
			}
			if (updateHeight && value == VerticalAlignment.Stretch && !double.IsNaN(item.Height))
			{
				item.Height = double.NaN;
			}
		}

		protected virtual Size ArrangeWhenCollapsed(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds)
		{
			FrameworkElement uncollapsedChild = Model.UncollapsedChild;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Rect finalRect;
				if (item.IsLayoutGroup() && ((ILayoutGroup)item).IsBorderless)
				{
					finalRect = bounds;
				}
				else
				{
					finalRect = RectHelper.New(GetItemDesiredSize(item));
					RectHelper.AlignHorizontally(ref finalRect, bounds, GetItemHorizontalAlignment(item));
					RectHelper.AlignVertically(ref finalRect, bounds, GetItemVerticalAlignment(item));
				}
				if (item != uncollapsedChild)
				{
					if (Model.MeasureUncollapsedChildOnly && SizeHelper.IsZero(item.RenderSize) && (!item.IsLayoutGroup() || !((ILayoutGroup)item).HasNewChildren))
					{
						continue;
					}
					finalRect = UIElementExtensions.InvisibleBounds;
				}
				item.Arrange(finalRect);
			}
			return RectHelper.Size(bounds);
		}

		protected virtual Size ArrangeWhenUncollapsed(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds)
		{
			ItemInfos itemInfos = CreateItemInfos(GetItemLength(RectHelper.Size(bounds)), Parameters.ItemSpace);
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				itemInfos.Add(new ItemInfo(GetItemLength(GetItemDesiredSize(item)), GetItemLength(GetItemMinSize(item, getActualMinSize: true)), GetItemLength(GetItemMaxSize(item, getActualMaxSize: true)), GetItemAlignment(item)));
			}
			itemInfos.Calculate();
			double itemOffset = GetItemOffset(bounds);
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count; i++)
			{
				Point itemLocation = default(Point);
				Size itemSize = GetItemDesiredSize(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]);
				SetItemOffset(ref itemLocation, itemOffset + itemInfos[i].Offset);
				SetItemLength(ref itemSize, itemInfos[i].Length);
				Rect finalRect = new Rect(itemLocation, itemSize);
				if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					RectHelper.AlignVertically(ref finalRect, bounds, GetItemVerticalAlignment(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]));
				}
				else
				{
					RectHelper.AlignHorizontally(ref finalRect, bounds, GetItemHorizontalAlignment(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]));
				}
				((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i].Arrange(finalRect);
			}
			if (Parameters.ItemSizers != null)
			{
				ArrangeItems(items);
				AddItemSizers(items);
			}
			return RectHelper.Size(bounds);
		}

		protected virtual ItemInfos CreateItemInfos(double availableLength, double itemSpace)
		{
			return new ItemInfos(availableLength, itemSpace);
		}

		protected virtual void MeasureItem(FrameworkElement item, Size maxSize)
		{
			Size itemMinSize = GetItemMinSize(item, getActualMinSize: false);
			SetItemLength(ref maxSize, Math.Max(GetItemLength(itemMinSize), GetItemLength(maxSize)));
			if (GetItemOrthogonalAlignment(item) == ItemAlignment.Stretch)
			{
				SetItemWidth(ref maxSize, Math.Max(GetItemWidth(itemMinSize), GetItemWidth(maxSize)));
			}
			else
			{
				SetItemWidth(ref maxSize, double.PositiveInfinity);
			}
			item.Measure(maxSize);
		}

		protected void MeasureItem(FrameworkElement item, Size maxSize, ref double totalLength, ref double totalWidth)
		{
			MeasureItem(item, maxSize);
			UpdateTotalSize(ref totalLength, ref totalWidth, GetItemDesiredSize(item));
		}

		protected virtual void MeasureStretchedItems(FrameworkElements stretchedItems, double availableLength, double availableWidth, ref double totalLength, ref double totalWidth)
		{
			if (double.IsInfinity(availableLength))
			{
				Size itemSize = GetItemSize(availableLength, availableWidth);
				{
					foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)
					{
						MeasureItem(item, itemSize, ref totalLength, ref totalWidth);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			StretchedLengthsCalculator stretchedLengthsCalculator = new StretchedLengthsCalculator(availableLength);
			foreach (FrameworkElement item2 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)
			{
				stretchedLengthsCalculator.Add(new ItemInfo(0.0, GetItemLength(GetItemMinSize(item2, getActualMinSize: true)), GetItemLength(GetItemMaxSize(item2, getActualMaxSize: true)), ItemAlignment.Stretch));
			}
			stretchedLengthsCalculator.Calculate();
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems).Count; i++)
			{
				double itemLength = stretchedLengthsCalculator[i].Length;
				if (Model is LayoutGroup layoutGroup && (layoutGroup.Root as LayoutControl).Return((LayoutControl x) => x.UseContentMinSize, () => false))
				{
					itemLength = double.PositiveInfinity;
				}
				MeasureItem(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)[i], GetItemSize(itemLength, availableWidth));
				flag = flag || GetItemLength(GetItemDesiredSize(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)[i])) == stretchedLengthsCalculator[i].Length;
			}
			if (flag && !stretchedLengthsCalculator.NeedsMoreLength)
			{
				UpdateTotalSize(ref totalLength, ref totalWidth, GetItemSize(availableLength, 0.0));
			}
			else
			{
				List<double> list = new List<double>();
				double num = 0.0;
				foreach (FrameworkElement item3 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)
				{
					double itemLength2 = GetItemLength(GetItemDesiredSize(item3));
					list.Add(itemLength2);
					num = Math.Max(num, GetItemLength(GetItemDesiredSize(item3)));
				}
				double num2 = 0.0;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				for (int num4 = 0; num4 < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems).Count; num4++)
				{
					if (stretchedLengthsCalculator[num4].Length < num && stretchedLengthsCalculator[num4].Length != stretchedLengthsCalculator[num4].MaxLength)
					{
						MeasureItem(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)[num4], GetItemSize(num, availableWidth));
					}
					num2 += Math.Min(num, stretchedLengthsCalculator[num4].MaxLength);
					double maxLength = stretchedLengthsCalculator[num4].MaxLength;
					num3 = (double.IsInfinity(maxLength) ? (num3 + list[num4]) : (num3 + Math.Min(num, maxLength)));
					num2 = ((num2 - num3 <= 1.0) ? num3 : num2);
				}
				UpdateTotalSize(ref totalLength, ref totalWidth, GetItemSize(num2, 0.0));
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item4 in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)stretchedItems)
			{
				UpdateTotalSize(ref totalLength, ref totalWidth, GetItemSize(0.0, GetItemWidth(GetItemDesiredSize(item4))));
			}
		}

		protected virtual Size MeasureWhenCollapsed(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			Size zero = SizeHelper.Zero;
			bool flag = Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.NoChildrenVisible;
			if (flag)
			{
				maxSize = RectHelper.Size(UIElementExtensions.InvisibleBounds);
			}
			FrameworkElement uncollapsedChild = Model.UncollapsedChild;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				if (flag || !Model.MeasureUncollapsedChildOnly || item == uncollapsedChild)
				{
					MeasureItem(item, maxSize);
					if (!flag)
					{
						SizeHelper.UpdateMaxSize(ref zero, GetItemDesiredSize(item));
					}
				}
			}
			return zero;
		}

		protected virtual Size MeasureWhenUncollapsed(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0063: Expected O, but got Unknown
			double num = (double)(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - 1) * Parameters.ItemSpace;
			SetItemLength(ref maxSize, Math.Max(0.0, GetItemLength(maxSize) - num));
			double totalLength;
			double totalWidth;
			while (true)
			{
				Size itemSize = maxSize;
				SetItemLength(ref itemSize, double.PositiveInfinity);
				totalLength = 0.0;
				totalWidth = 0.0;
				FrameworkElements val = new FrameworkElements();
				foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
				{
					if (GetItemAlignment(item) == ItemAlignment.Stretch)
					{
						((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)val).Add(item);
					}
					else
					{
						MeasureItem(item, itemSize, ref totalLength, ref totalWidth);
					}
				}
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)val).Count != 0)
				{
					MeasureStretchedItems(val, Math.Max(0.0, GetItemLength(maxSize) - totalLength), GetItemWidth(maxSize), ref totalLength, ref totalWidth);
				}
				if (totalWidth <= GetItemWidth(maxSize))
				{
					break;
				}
				SetItemWidth(ref maxSize, totalWidth);
			}
			return GetItemSize((totalLength != 0.0) ? (totalLength + num) : 0.0, totalWidth);
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			if (Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				return MeasureWhenUncollapsed(items, maxSize);
			}
			return MeasureWhenCollapsed(items, maxSize);
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			if (Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				return ArrangeWhenUncollapsed(items, bounds);
			}
			return ArrangeWhenCollapsed(items, bounds);
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTotalSize(ref double totalLength, ref double totalWidth, Size itemSize)
		{
			totalLength += GetItemLength(itemSize);
			totalWidth = Math.Max(totalWidth, GetItemWidth(itemSize));
		}

		protected internal ItemAlignment GetItemAlignment(FrameworkElement item, bool horizontal)
		{
			if (horizontal)
			{
				return GetItemHorizontalAlignment(item).GetItemAlignment();
			}
			return GetItemVerticalAlignment(item).GetItemAlignment();
		}

		protected internal ItemAlignment GetItemAlignment(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			return GetItemAlignment(item, Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal);
		}

		protected ItemAlignment GetItemOrthogonalAlignment(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return GetItemVerticalAlignment(item).GetItemAlignment();
			}
			return GetItemHorizontalAlignment(item).GetItemAlignment();
		}

		protected void SetItemAlignment(FrameworkElement item, ItemAlignment alignment)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				SetItemHorizontalAlignment(item, alignment.GetHorizontalAlignment(), updateWidth: false);
			}
			else
			{
				SetItemVerticalAlignment(item, alignment.GetVerticalAlignment(), updateHeight: false);
			}
		}

		protected double GetItemOffset(Rect itemBounds)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return itemBounds.Y;
			}
			return itemBounds.X;
		}

		protected double GetItemOffsetPlusLength(Rect itemBounds)
		{
			return GetItemOffset(itemBounds) + GetItemLength(itemBounds);
		}

		protected double GetItemLength(Size itemSize)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return itemSize.Height;
			}
			return itemSize.Width;
		}

		protected double GetItemLength(Rect itemBounds)
		{
			return GetItemLength(RectHelper.Size(itemBounds));
		}

		protected double GetItemWidth(Size itemSize)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return itemSize.Width;
			}
			return itemSize.Height;
		}

		protected Size GetItemSize(double itemLength, double itemWidth)
		{
			Size itemSize = default(Size);
			SetItemLength(ref itemSize, itemLength);
			SetItemWidth(ref itemSize, itemWidth);
			return itemSize;
		}

		protected void SetItemOffset(ref Point itemLocation, double offset)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				itemLocation.X = offset;
			}
			else
			{
				itemLocation.Y = offset;
			}
		}

		protected void SetItemOffset(ref Rect itemBounds, double offset)
		{
			Point itemLocation = RectHelper.Location(itemBounds);
			SetItemOffset(ref itemLocation, offset);
			RectHelper.SetLocation(ref itemBounds, itemLocation);
		}

		protected void SetItemOffsetPlusLength(ref Rect itemBounds, double offsetPlusLength)
		{
			SetItemLength(ref itemBounds, offsetPlusLength - GetItemOffset(itemBounds));
		}

		protected void SetItemLength(ref Size itemSize, double length)
		{
			length = Math.Max(0.0, length);
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				itemSize.Width = length;
			}
			else
			{
				itemSize.Height = length;
			}
		}

		protected void SetItemLength(ref Rect itemBounds, double length)
		{
			Size itemSize = RectHelper.Size(itemBounds);
			SetItemLength(ref itemSize, length);
			RectHelper.SetSize(ref itemBounds, itemSize);
		}

		protected void SetItemWidth(ref Size itemSize, double width)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				itemSize.Height = width;
			}
			else
			{
				itemSize.Width = width;
			}
		}

		protected virtual Size GetActualMinOrMaxSizeWhenCollapsed(FrameworkElements items, bool getMinSize, LayoutParametersBase parameters)
		{
			if (Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.NoChildrenVisible)
			{
				if (!getMinSize)
				{
					return SizeHelper.Infinite;
				}
				return SizeHelper.Zero;
			}
			Size zero = SizeHelper.Zero;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Size size = (getMinSize ? GetItemMinSize(item, getActualMinSize: true) : GetItemMaxSize(item, getActualMaxSize: true));
				SizeHelper.UpdateMaxSize(ref zero, size);
			}
			return zero;
		}

		protected virtual Size GetActualMinOrMaxSizeWhenUncollapsed(FrameworkElements items, bool getMinSize, LayoutParametersBase parameters)
		{
			double totalLength = (getMinSize ? ((double)(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - 1) * parameters.ItemSpace) : double.PositiveInfinity);
			double totalWidth = 0.0;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Size itemSize = (getMinSize ? GetItemMinSize(item, getActualMinSize: true) : GetItemMaxSize(item, getActualMaxSize: true));
				Size itemDesiredSize = GetItemDesiredSize(item);
				if (getMinSize && GetItemAlignment(item) != ItemAlignment.Stretch && GetItemLength(itemDesiredSize) != 0.0)
				{
					SetItemLength(ref itemSize, GetItemLength(itemDesiredSize));
				}
				UpdateTotalSize(ref totalLength, ref totalWidth, itemSize);
			}
			return GetItemSize(totalLength, totalWidth);
		}

		protected Size GetItemMinSize(FrameworkElement item, bool getActualMinSize)
		{
			return GetItemMinOrMaxSize(item, (getActualMinSize && item.IsLayoutGroup()) ? ((ILayoutGroup)item).ActualMinSize : FrameworkElementExtensions.GetMinSize(item));
		}

		protected Size GetItemMaxSize(FrameworkElement item, bool getActualMaxSize)
		{
			return GetItemMinOrMaxSize(item, (getActualMaxSize && item.IsLayoutGroup()) ? ((ILayoutGroup)item).ActualMaxSize : FrameworkElementExtensions.GetMaxSize(item));
		}

		private Size GetItemMinOrMaxSize(FrameworkElement item, Size originalMinOrMaxSize)
		{
			Size result = originalMinOrMaxSize;
			if (!double.IsNaN(item.Width))
			{
				result.Width = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetRealWidth(item);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(item.Height))
			{
				result.Height = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetRealHeight(item);
			}
			SizeHelper.Inflate(ref result, item.Margin);
			return result;
		}

		protected Size GetItemDesiredSize(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (!item.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				return UIElementExtensions.GetDesiredSize((UIElement)item);
			}
			return ((IPanel)(ILayoutGroup)item).ActualDesiredSize;
		}

		protected virtual void AddItemSizers(FrameworkElements items)
		{
			//IL_007e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count <= 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			FrameworkElement[] array = new FrameworkElement[((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count + 1];
			DefineItemSizerElements(items, ItemAlignment.Start, array);
			DefineItemSizerElements(items, ItemAlignment.End, array);
			DefineItemSizerElements(items, ItemAlignment.Stretch, array);
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i];
				ItemAlignment itemAlignment = GetItemAlignment(frameworkElement);
				bool isStartSizer;
				if (itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.Center)
				{
					if (!IsItemSizable(frameworkElement))
					{
						continue;
					}
					isStartSizer = false;
				}
				else if (array[1 + i] == frameworkElement)
				{
					isStartSizer = false;
				}
				else
				{
					if (array[i] != frameworkElement)
					{
						continue;
					}
					isStartSizer = true;
				}
				Parameters.ItemSizers.Add(frameworkElement, GetItemSizerSide(isStartSizer));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DefineItemSizerElements(FrameworkElements items, ItemAlignment alignment, FrameworkElement[] itemSizerElements)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i];
				ItemAlignment itemAlignment = GetItemAlignment(frameworkElement);
				if (itemAlignment != alignment || !IsItemSizable(frameworkElement))
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num = 1 + i;
				if (itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.End)
				{
					num--;
				}
				else if (i > 0 && i == ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - 1)
				{
					if (itemSizerElements[num - 1] == null)
					{
						num--;
					}
					else if (itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.Stretch)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (itemSizerElements[num] == null)
				{
					itemSizerElements[num] = frameworkElement;
				}
			}
		}

		protected Side GetItemSizerSide(bool isStartSizer)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				if (isStartSizer)
				{
					return (Side)0;
				}
				return (Side)2;
			}
			if (isStartSizer)
			{
				return (Side)1;
			}
			return (Side)4;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsItemSizable(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				if (item.MinWidth < item.MaxWidth)
				{
					return LayoutControl.GetAllowHorizontalSizing(item);
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (item.MinHeight < item.MaxHeight)
			{
				return LayoutControl.GetAllowVerticalSizing(item);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void AlignLayoutItemLabels(FrameworkElement scope, FrameworkElements items)
		{
			if (Model is LayoutGroup layoutGroup)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup).LayoutUpdated -= LayoutGroup_LayoutUpdated;
				((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup).LayoutUpdated += LayoutGroup_LayoutUpdated;
			}
			LayoutItems layoutItems = new LayoutItems();
			GetLayoutItems(items, layoutItems);
			if (layoutItems.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<LayoutItems> list = CalculateColumns(scope, layoutItems);
			if (previousColumnsList.Count > 1 && IsLayoutRepeated(previousColumnsList, list))
			{
				repeatCount++;
			}
			if (repeatCount >= 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			previousColumnsList.Add(list);
			foreach (LayoutItems item in list)
			{
				SetLabelWidth(item, CalculateCommonLabelWidth(item));
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsLayoutRepeated(List<List<LayoutItems>> previousColumnsList, List<LayoutItems> columns)
		{
			if (!DetectCyclicColumnsAlignment)
			{
				return false;
			}
			foreach (List<LayoutItems> previousColumns in previousColumnsList)
			{
				if (AreColumnsListsEqual(previousColumns, columns))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static bool AreColumnsListsEqual(List<LayoutItems> previousColumns, List<LayoutItems> columns)
		{
			if (previousColumns.Count != columns.Count)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < previousColumns.Count; i++)
			{
				if (!AreColumnsEqual(previousColumns[i], columns[i]))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool AreColumnsEqual(LayoutItems previousColumn, LayoutItems column)
		{
			bool flag = previousColumn.Count == column.Count;
			if (flag)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < previousColumn.Count; i++)
				{
					if (previousColumn[i] != column[i])
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public virtual void GetLayoutItems(FrameworkElements items, LayoutItems layoutItems)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				if (item is LayoutItem)
				{
					LayoutItem layoutItem = (LayoutItem)(object)item;
					if (CanAlignLayoutItemLabel(layoutItem))
					{
						layoutItems.Add(layoutItem);
					}
				}
				else if (item.IsLayoutGroup())
				{
					((ILayoutGroup)item).GetLayoutItems(layoutItems);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual List<LayoutItems> CalculateColumns(FrameworkElement scope, LayoutItems layoutItems)
		{
			List<LayoutItems> list = new List<LayoutItems>();
			List<double> list2 = new List<double>();
			foreach (LayoutItem layoutItem in layoutItems)
			{
				double item = Math.Round(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetPosition((FrameworkElement)(object)layoutItem, scope).X, 6);
				int num = list2.IndexOf(item);
				if (num == -1)
				{
					num = list2.Count;
					list2.Add(item);
					list.Add(new LayoutItems());
				}
				list[num].Add(layoutItem);
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected virtual double CalculateCommonLabelWidth(LayoutItems layoutItems)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (LayoutItem layoutItem in layoutItems)
			{
				num = Math.Max(num, layoutItem.LabelWidth);
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected virtual bool CanAlignLayoutItemLabel(LayoutItem layoutItem)
		{
			HorizontalAlignment itemHorizontalAlignment = GetItemHorizontalAlignment((FrameworkElement)(object)layoutItem);
			if ((itemHorizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Left || itemHorizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Stretch) && layoutItem.IsLabelVisible)
			{
				return layoutItem.LabelPosition == LayoutItemLabelPosition.Left;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void SetLabelWidth(LayoutItems layoutItems, double labelWidth)
		{
			foreach (LayoutItem layoutItem in layoutItems)
			{
				layoutItem.LabelWidth = labelWidth;
			}
		}

		public virtual LayoutItemInsertionInfo GetInsertionInfoForEmptyArea(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement element, Point p)
		{
			LayoutItemInsertionInfo result = default(LayoutItemInsertionInfo);
			CalculateInsertionInfoForEmptyArea(control, ((IPanel)control).ClientBounds, p, ((IPanel)control).GetLogicalChildren(true), out result.InsertionKind, out var insertionPoint);
			result.DestinationItem = insertionPoint;
			LayoutItemInsertionPoints layoutItemInsertionPoints = new LayoutItemInsertionPoints();
			GetInsertionPoints(control, element, result.DestinationItem, result.DestinationItem, result.InsertionKind, layoutItemInsertionPoints);
			LayoutItemInsertionPoint layoutItemInsertionPoint = layoutItemInsertionPoints.Find(insertionPoint);
			if (layoutItemInsertionPoint == null)
			{
				layoutItemInsertionPoint = ((layoutItemInsertionPoints.Count > 0) ? layoutItemInsertionPoints[layoutItemInsertionPoints.Count - 1] : null);
			}
			result.InsertionPoint = layoutItemInsertionPoint;
			return result;
		}

		public virtual LayoutItemInsertionKind GetInsertionKind(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement destinationItem, Point p)
		{
			if (destinationItem == control && control.IsBorderless)
			{
				return LayoutItemInsertionKind.None;
			}
			for (LayoutItemInsertionKind layoutItemInsertionKind = LayoutItemInsertionKind.Left; layoutItemInsertionKind <= LayoutItemInsertionKind.Bottom; layoutItemInsertionKind++)
			{
				if (GetInsertionZoneBounds(control, destinationItem, layoutItemInsertionKind).Contains(p))
				{
					return layoutItemInsertionKind;
				}
			}
			return LayoutItemInsertionKind.None;
		}

		public virtual void GetInsertionPoints(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement destinationItem, FrameworkElement originalDestinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind, LayoutItemInsertionPoints points)
		{
			if (IsInsertionPoint(control, element, destinationItem, originalDestinationItem, insertionKind))
			{
				points.Add(new LayoutItemInsertionPoint(destinationItem, insertionKind == LayoutItemInsertionKind.Inside));
			}
			if (IsExternalInsertionPoint(control, element, destinationItem, insertionKind))
			{
				GetExternalInsertionPoints(control, element, originalDestinationItem, insertionKind, points);
			}
		}

		public virtual Rect GetInsertionPointZoneBounds(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement destinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind, int pointIndex, int pointCount)
		{
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0060: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0099: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			Rect insertionZoneBounds = GetInsertionZoneBounds(control, destinationItem, insertionKind);
			if (insertionKind == LayoutItemInsertionKind.Inside)
			{
				return insertionZoneBounds;
			}
			if (!insertionKind.GetSide().HasValue)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			Side value = insertionKind.GetSide().Value;
			double num = ((SideExtentions.GetOrientation(value) == Orientation.Horizontal) ? insertionZoneBounds.Height : insertionZoneBounds.Width);
			double num2 = Math.Floor(num / (double)pointCount);
			Rect result = insertionZoneBounds;
			RectHelper.Inflate(ref result, value, (double)(-(pointCount - 1 - pointIndex)) * num2);
			if (pointIndex == 0)
			{
				num2 += num - (double)pointCount * num2;
			}
			switch ((int)value)
			{
			case 0:
				result.Width = num2;
				break;
			case 2:
				RectHelper.SetLeft(ref result, result.Right - num2);
				break;
			case 1:
				result.Height = num2;
				break;
			case 4:
				RectHelper.SetTop(ref result, result.Bottom - num2);
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual void InsertElement(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement element, LayoutItemInsertionPoint insertionPoint, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			//IL_0110: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_015d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			Side? side = insertionKind.GetSide();
			if (insertionPoint.Element == control && insertionPoint.IsInternalInsertion)
			{
				if (insertionKind == LayoutItemInsertionKind.Inside)
				{
					Orientation orientation = ((element.Parent is ILayoutGroup) ? ((ILayoutGroup)element.Parent).Orientation : Model.Orientation);
					((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)control.Root.AvailableItems).Remove(element);
					FrameworkElementExtensions.SetParent(element, (DependencyObject)((IControl)control).Control);
					Model.Orientation = orientation;
					return;
				}
				if (side.HasValue)
				{
					ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = control.MoveChildrenToNewGroup();
					LayoutControl.SetIsUserDefined(((IControl)layoutGroup).Control, value: true);
					insertionPoint.Element = ((IControl)layoutGroup).Control;
					insertionPoint.IsInternalInsertion = false;
					Model.Orientation = OrientationExtensions.OrthogonalValue(Model.Orientation);
				}
			}
			if (!side.HasValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Model.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None || Model.Orientation == SideExtentions.GetOrientation(side.Value))
			{
				ILayoutGroup layoutGroup2 = control.MoveChildToNewGroup(insertionPoint.Element);
				LayoutControl.SetIsUserDefined(((IControl)layoutGroup2).Control, value: true);
				layoutGroup2.Orientation = OrientationExtensions.OrthogonalValue(SideExtentions.GetOrientation(side.Value));
				layoutGroup2.InsertElement(element, insertionPoint, insertionKind);
				return;
			}
			((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)control.Root.AvailableItems).Remove(element);
			FrameworkElementExtensions.SetParent(element, (DependencyObject)null);
			int num = ((IPanel)control).Children.IndexOf(insertionPoint.Element);
			if (SideExtentions.IsEnd(side.Value))
			{
				num++;
			}
			((IPanel)control).Children.Insert(num, element);
			InitInsertedElement(element, insertionPoint.Element);
		}

		public virtual bool IsExternalInsertionPoint(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement destinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0040: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!insertionKind.GetSide().HasValue || control.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Side value = insertionKind.GetSide().Value;
			Rect childAreaBounds = control.ChildAreaBounds;
			Rect bounds = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(destinationItem, ((IControl)control).Control);
			if (RectHelper.GetSideOffset(childAreaBounds, value) == RectHelper.GetSideOffset(bounds, value))
			{
				if (SideExtentions.GetOrientation(value) == Model.Orientation)
				{
					FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((IPanel)control).GetLogicalChildren(true);
					if (!((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Contains(element))
					{
						return ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count >= 2;
					}
					return ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count >= 3;
				}
				if (control.IsBorderless)
				{
					return ((ILayoutGroup)((IControl)control).Control.Parent).IsChildBorderless(control);
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void CalculateInsertionInfoForEmptyArea(ILayoutGroup control, Rect bounds, Point p, FrameworkElements items, out LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind, out FrameworkElement insertionPoint)
		{
			if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count == 0 && bounds.Contains(p))
			{
				insertionKind = LayoutItemInsertionKind.Inside;
				insertionPoint = ((IControl)control).Control;
				return;
			}
			if (control.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				ArrangeItems(items);
				Rect itemBounds = bounds;
				for (int i = 0; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count; i++)
				{
					Rect bounds2 = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]);
					if (i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count - 1)
					{
						SetItemOffsetPlusLength(ref itemBounds, (GetItemOffsetPlusLength(bounds2) + GetItemOffset(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i + 1]))) / 2.0);
					}
					else
					{
						SetItemOffsetPlusLength(ref itemBounds, GetItemOffsetPlusLength(bounds));
					}
					if (itemBounds.Contains(p))
					{
						insertionPoint = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i];
						insertionKind = GetInsertionKindForEmptyArea(bounds2, p);
						return;
					}
					SetItemOffset(ref itemBounds, GetItemOffsetPlusLength(itemBounds));
				}
			}
			else if (control.UncollapsedChild != null)
			{
				insertionPoint = control.UncollapsedChild;
				insertionKind = GetInsertionKindForEmptyArea(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(insertionPoint), p);
				return;
			}
			insertionKind = LayoutItemInsertionKind.None;
			insertionPoint = null;
		}

		protected virtual void GetExternalInsertionPoints(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement originalDestinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind, LayoutItemInsertionPoints points)
		{
			ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = ((IControl)control).Control.Parent as ILayoutGroup;
			if (control.IsBorderless && layoutGroup.IsChildBorderless(control))
			{
				layoutGroup.GetInsertionPoints(element, ((IControl)control).Control, originalDestinationItem, insertionKind, points);
			}
			else
			{
				points.Add(new LayoutItemInsertionPoint(((IControl)control).Control, isInternalInsertion: true));
			}
		}

		protected virtual LayoutItemInsertionKind GetInsertionKindForEmptyArea(Rect itemBounds, Point p)
		{
			LayoutItemInsertionKind result = LayoutItemInsertionKind.None;
			Action action = delegate
			{
				if (p.X < itemBounds.Left)
				{
					result = LayoutItemInsertionKind.Left;
				}
				if (p.X >= itemBounds.Right)
				{
					result = LayoutItemInsertionKind.Right;
				}
			};
			Action action2 = delegate
			{
				if (p.Y < itemBounds.Top)
				{
					result = LayoutItemInsertionKind.Top;
				}
				if (p.Y >= itemBounds.Bottom)
				{
					result = LayoutItemInsertionKind.Bottom;
				}
			};
			if (Model.CollapseMode == LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None && Model.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				action2();
				action();
			}
			else
			{
				action();
				action2();
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetInsertionZoneBounds(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement destinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			Rect contentBounds = Rect.Empty;
			if (destinationItem == control && !control.IsBorderless && control.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.NoChildrenVisible)
			{
				contentBounds = ((IPanel)control).ContentBounds;
			}
			return GetInsertionZoneBounds(RectHelper.New(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize(destinationItem)), contentBounds, insertionKind);
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetInsertionZoneBounds(Rect bounds, Rect contentBounds, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			//IL_0030: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01d2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01d4: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00d2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01e6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01e8: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00e8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ea: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0216: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_011b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_011d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (insertionKind == LayoutItemInsertionKind.Inside)
			{
				if (!contentBounds.IsEmpty)
				{
					return contentBounds;
				}
				return bounds;
			}
			if (!insertionKind.GetSide().HasValue)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			Side value = insertionKind.GetSide().Value;
			Rect result = bounds;
			if (result.Width > result.Height)
			{
				if (contentBounds.IsEmpty)
				{
					double num = Math.Ceiling(0.25 * bounds.Width);
					contentBounds = default(Rect);
					contentBounds.X = bounds.Left + num;
					contentBounds.Width = bounds.Width - 2.0 * num;
					contentBounds.Y = Math.Floor((bounds.Top + bounds.Bottom) / 2.0);
					contentBounds.Height = 0.0;
				}
				if ((int)value == 0)
				{
					RectHelper.SetRight(ref result, contentBounds.Left);
				}
				else if ((int)value == 2)
				{
					RectHelper.SetLeft(ref result, contentBounds.Right);
				}
				else
				{
					result.X = contentBounds.X;
					result.Width = contentBounds.Width;
					if ((int)value == 1)
					{
						RectHelper.SetBottom(ref result, contentBounds.Top);
					}
					else
					{
						RectHelper.SetTop(ref result, contentBounds.Bottom);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (contentBounds.IsEmpty)
				{
					contentBounds = default(Rect);
					contentBounds.X = Math.Floor((bounds.Left + bounds.Right) / 2.0);
					contentBounds.Width = 0.0;
					double num2 = Math.Ceiling(0.25 * bounds.Height);
					contentBounds.Y = bounds.Top + num2;
					contentBounds.Height = bounds.Height - 2.0 * num2;
				}
				if ((int)value == 1)
				{
					RectHelper.SetBottom(ref result, contentBounds.Top);
				}
				else if ((int)value == 4)
				{
					RectHelper.SetTop(ref result, contentBounds.Bottom);
				}
				else
				{
					result.Y = contentBounds.Y;
					result.Height = contentBounds.Height;
					if ((int)value == 0)
					{
						RectHelper.SetRight(ref result, contentBounds.Left);
					}
					else
					{
						RectHelper.SetLeft(ref result, contentBounds.Right);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsInsertionPoint(ILayoutGroup control, FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement destinationItem, FrameworkElement originalDestinationItem, LayoutItemInsertionKind insertionKind)
		{
			//IL_0040: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0085: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (destinationItem == element)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (insertionKind == LayoutItemInsertionKind.Inside && (destinationItem == control || destinationItem.IsLayoutGroup()))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (control.CollapseMode != LayoutGroupCollapseMode.None)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!insertionKind.GetSide().HasValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Side value = insertionKind.GetSide().Value;
			if (SideExtentions.GetOrientation(value) != Model.Orientation)
			{
				FrameworkElements arrangedLogicalChildren = control.GetArrangedLogicalChildren(visibleOnly: true);
				int num = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)arrangedLogicalChildren).IndexOf(element);
				if (num != -1)
				{
					int num2 = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)arrangedLogicalChildren).IndexOf(destinationItem);
					if ((SideExtentions.IsStart(value) && num2 == num + 1) || (SideExtentions.IsEnd(value) && num2 == num - 1))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			if (destinationItem.IsLayoutGroup())
			{
				ILayoutGroup layoutGroup = (ILayoutGroup)destinationItem;
				if (SideExtentions.GetOrientation(value) == layoutGroup.Orientation)
				{
					if (destinationItem != originalDestinationItem)
					{
						FrameworkElements logicalChildren = ((IPanel)layoutGroup).GetLogicalChildren(true);
						if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Contains(element) && ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)logicalChildren).Count <= 2)
						{
							return originalDestinationItem == element;
						}
						return true;
					}
					return true;
				}
				if (destinationItem != originalDestinationItem)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)((IPanel)layoutGroup).GetLogicalChildren(true)).Contains(element))
				{
					return !layoutGroup.IsExternalInsertionPoint(element, element, insertionKind);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void InitInsertedElement(FrameworkElement element, FrameworkElement insertionPoint)
		{
			ItemAlignment itemAlignment = GetItemAlignment(insertionPoint);
			if (itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.Start || itemAlignment == ItemAlignment.Stretch)
			{
				ItemAlignment itemAlignment2 = GetItemAlignment(element);
				if (itemAlignment2 != ItemAlignment.Start && itemAlignment2 != ItemAlignment.Stretch)
				{
					SetItemAlignment(element, ItemAlignment.Start);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				SetItemAlignment(element, itemAlignment);
			}
		}

		private void LayoutGroup_LayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (Model is LayoutGroup layoutGroup)
			{
				((UIElement)(object)layoutGroup).LayoutUpdated -= LayoutGroup_LayoutUpdated;
			}
			previousColumnsList.Clear();
			repeatCount = 0;
		}
	}

		[Description("Gets or sets the horizontal alignment of the label within the layout item’s label region.\nThis is a dependency property.")]

		protected LayoutItemLabel LabelElement { get; private set; }

		protected Size AvailableSize { get; private set; }

		protected internal double LabelWidth
		{
			get
			{
				if (LabelElement == null)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return LabelElement.DesiredWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (LabelElement != null)
				{
					double num = double.PositiveInfinity;
					double num2 = ((FrameworkElement)this).Margin.Left + ((FrameworkElement)this).ActualWidth + ((FrameworkElement)this).Margin.Right;
					bool flag = ((FrameworkElement)this).Parent is LayoutGroup layoutGroup && layoutGroup.GetItemAlignment(this, horizontal: true) == ItemAlignment.Stretch;
					if (double.IsInfinity(AvailableSize.Width) && num2 > ((UIElement)this).DesiredSize.Width && !flag)
					{
						num = UIElementExtensions.GetRoundedSize(num2) - (((UIElement)this).DesiredSize.Width - ((UIElement)(object)LabelElement).DesiredSize.Width);
					}
					LabelElement.CustomWidth = ((value - num < 1E-09) ? value : double.NaN);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				List<object> list = new List<object>();
				if (Content != null)
				{
					list.Add(Content);
				}
				if (Label != null)
				{
					list.Add(Label);
				}
				return list.GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		static LayoutItem()
		{
			AddColonToLabelProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AddColonToLabel", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)o).OnAddColonToLabelChanged();
			}));
			ContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Content", typeof(UIElement), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)o).OnContentChanged((UIElement)e.OldValue, (UIElement)e.NewValue);
			}));
			ElementSpaceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ElementSpace", typeof(double), typeof(LayoutItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure));
			IsRequiredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsRequired", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)o).OnIsRequiredChanged();
			}));
			LabelProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Label", typeof(object), typeof(LayoutItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsParentArrange, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)o).OnLabelChanged(e.OldValue);
			}));
			LabelHorizontalAlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelHorizontalAlignment", typeof(HorizontalAlignment), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(HorizontalAlignment.Left));
			LabelVerticalAlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelVerticalAlignment", typeof(VerticalAlignment), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(VerticalAlignment.Center));
			LabelPositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelPosition", typeof(LayoutItemLabelPosition), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)o).OnLabelPositionChanged((LayoutItemLabelPosition)e.OldValue);
			}));
			LabelStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(LayoutItem), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItem)(object)o).OnLabelStyleChanged();
			}));
			LabelTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(LayoutItem), null);
			ActualLabelProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActualLabel", typeof(object), typeof(LayoutItem), null);
			CalculatedLabelVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CalculatedLabelVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(LayoutItem), null);
			IsActuallyRequiredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsActuallyRequired", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItem), null);
			DXSerializer.SerializationProviderProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(LayoutItem), new UIPropertyMetadata(new LayoutControlSerializationProviderBase()));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItem>();
			DependencyPropertyRegistrator<LayoutItem>.New().OverrideMetadata(FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, (Action<LayoutItem>)delegate(LayoutItem x)
			{
				OnHorizontalAlignmentChanged(x);
			});
		}

		private static void OnHorizontalAlignmentChanged(LayoutItem item)
		{
			if (item.LabelElement != null)
			{
				item.LabelElement.CustomWidth = double.NaN;
			}
		}

		public LayoutItem()
		{
			UpdateActualLabel();
			CalculateLabelVisibility();
			UpdateIsActuallyRequired();
			SerializableItem.SetTypeName((DependencyObject)(object)this, ((object)this).GetType().Name);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (LabelElement != null)
			{
				LabelElement.DesiredWidthChanged = null;
				((DependencyObject)(object)LabelElement).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty);
			}
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			LabelElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("LabelElement") as LayoutItemLabel;
			UpdateLabelElementStyle();
			if (LabelElement != null)
			{
				LabelElement.DesiredWidthChanged = OnLabelWidthChanged;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateLabelElementStyle()
		{
			if (LabelElement != null)
			{
				if (LabelStyle != null)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)(object)LabelElement).Style = LabelStyle;
				}
				else
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)LabelElement).ClearValue(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			AvailableSize = availableSize;
			return ((ControlBase)this).MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected internal virtual void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, LabelProperty, "Label", typeof(object));
		}

		protected internal virtual void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, LabelProperty, "Label");
		}

		protected void CalculateLabelVisibility()
		{
			((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(CalculatedLabelVisibilityProperty, (object)((!IsLabelVisible) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible));
		}

		protected virtual object GetActualLabel()
		{
			string text = Label as string;
			if (text == null)
			{
				return Label;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && AddColonToLabel)
			{
				text += ":";
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected virtual bool GetIsActuallyRequired()
		{
			return IsRequired;
		}

		protected virtual bool GetIsLabelVisible(object label)
		{
			if (label != null)
			{
				if (label is string)
				{
					return !string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)label);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected internal virtual FrameworkElement GetSelectableElement(Point p)
		{
			if (Content != null && UIElementExtensions.GetVisible(Content) && FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds((FrameworkElement)Content, (FrameworkElement)(object)this).Contains(p))
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)Content;
			}
			return (FrameworkElement)(object)this;
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentChanged(UIElement oldValue, UIElement newValue)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).RemoveLogicalChild((object)oldValue);
			((FrameworkElement)this).AddLogicalChild((object)newValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnAddColonToLabelChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualLabel();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsRequiredChanged()
		{
			UpdateIsActuallyRequired();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).RemoveLogicalChild(oldValue);
			((FrameworkElement)this).AddLogicalChild(Label);
			UpdateActualLabel();
			CalculateLabelVisibility();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelPositionChanged(LayoutItemLabelPosition oldValue)
		{
			LabelWidth = double.NaN;
			((UIElement)this).InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelStyleChanged()
		{
			UpdateLabelElementStyle();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelWidthChanged()
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)this).Parent is ILayoutGroup layoutGroup)
			{
				layoutGroup.LayoutItemLabelWidthChanged((FrameworkElement)(object)this);
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateActualLabel()
		{
			ActualLabel = GetActualLabel();
		}

		protected void UpdateIsActuallyRequired()
		{
			IsActuallyRequired = GetIsActuallyRequired();
		}

		internal virtual void OnEndDeserializing()
		{
		}

		FrameworkElement ILayoutControlCustomizableItem.AddNewItem()
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new LayoutItemAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	public class LayoutItems : List<LayoutItem>
	{
	}
	public static class LayoutItemExtensions
	{
		public static LayoutItem GetLayoutItem(this FrameworkElement content, FrameworkElement root = null, bool explicitOnly = true)
		{
			LayoutItem layoutItem = DependencyObjectExtensions.FindElementByTypeInParents<LayoutItem>((DependencyObject)content, (DependencyObject)root);
			if (layoutItem == null || (explicitOnly && layoutItem.Content != content))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return layoutItem;
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(GroupName = "RequiredStates", Name = "NotRequired")]
	[TemplateVisualState(GroupName = "RequiredStates", Name = "Required")]
	public class LayoutItemLabel : ContentControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsRequiredProperty;

		private double _CustomWidth = double.NaN;

		private double _DesiredWidth = double.NaN;

		public Action DesiredWidthChanged;

		private const string RequiredStates = "RequiredStates";

		private const string NotRequiredState = "NotRequired";

		private const string RequiredState = "Required";

		public double CustomWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _CustomWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!CustomWidth.Equals(value))
				{
					_CustomWidth = value;
					((UIElement)this).InvalidateMeasure();
					LayoutTreeHelper.GetVisualParents((DependencyObject)(object)this, (DependencyObject)null).OfType<LayoutGroup>().FirstOrDefault()
						.Do(delegate(LayoutGroup x)
						{
							((UIElement)(object)x).InvalidateMeasure();
						});
					LayoutTreeHelper.GetVisualParents((DependencyObject)(object)this, (DependencyObject)null).OfType<LayoutItem>().FirstOrDefault()
						.Do(delegate(LayoutItem x)
						{
							((UIElement)(object)x).InvalidateMeasure();
						});
					LayoutTreeHelper.GetVisualParents((DependencyObject)(object)this, (DependencyObject)null).OfType<LayoutItemPanel>().FirstOrDefault()
						.Do(delegate(LayoutItemPanel x)
						{
							x.InvalidateMeasure();
						});
				}
			}
		}

		public double DesiredWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _DesiredWidth;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (DesiredWidth != value)
				{
					double desiredWidth = _DesiredWidth;
					_DesiredWidth = value;
					if (!double.IsNaN(desiredWidth))
					{
						OnDesiredWidthChanged();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsRequired
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(IsRequiredProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(IsRequiredProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected override bool IsContentInLogicalTree => false;

		static LayoutItemLabel()
		{
			IsRequiredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsRequired", typeof(bool), typeof(LayoutItemLabel), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((LayoutItemLabel)(object)o).OnIsRequiredChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<LayoutItemLabel>();
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size result = ((ControlBase)this).OnMeasure(availableSize);
			DesiredWidth = UIElementExtensions.GetRoundedSize(result.Width);
			if (!double.IsNaN(CustomWidth))
			{
				result.Width = CustomWidth;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDesiredWidthChanged()
		{
			if (DesiredWidthChanged != null)
			{
				DesiredWidthChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsRequiredChanged()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(false);
		}

		protected override void UpdateState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(useTransitions);
			((ControlBase)this).GoToState(IsRequired ? "Required" : "NotRequired", useTransitions);
		}
	}
	public enum LayoutItemLabelPosition
	{
		Left,
		Top
	}
	public class LayoutItemPanel : Panel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ElementSpaceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ElementSpace", typeof(double), typeof(LayoutItemPanel), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((LayoutItemPanel)o).OnElementSpaceChanged();
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LabelPositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelPosition", typeof(LayoutItemLabelPosition), typeof(LayoutItemPanel), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((LayoutItemPanel)o).OnLabelPositionChanged();
		}));

		private UIElement _Content;

		private UIElement _Label;

		public UIElement Content
		{
			get
			{
				return _Content;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Content != value)
				{
					if (Content != null)
					{
						base.Children.Remove(Content);
					}
					_Content = value;
					if (Content != null)
					{
						base.Children.Add(Content);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public double ElementSpace
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ElementSpaceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ElementSpaceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public UIElement Label
		{
			get
			{
				return _Label;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Label != value)
				{
					if (Label != null)
					{
						base.Children.Remove(Label);
					}
					_Label = value;
					if (Label != null)
					{
						base.Children.Add(Label);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public LayoutItemLabelPosition LabelPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutItemLabelPosition)GetValue(LabelPositionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LabelPositionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected double ActualElementSpace
		{
			get
			{
				if (Label == null || !UIElementExtensions.GetVisible(Label))
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return ElementSpace;
			}
		}

		protected Orientation Orientation { get; private set; }

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (Label == null || Content == null)
			{
				return new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			Label.Measure(GetLabelAvailableSize(availableSize));
			Content.Measure(GetContentAvailableSize(availableSize));
			return GetDesiredSize();
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			if (Label == null || Content == null)
			{
				return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
			}
			Rect bounds = RectHelper.New(finalSize);
			Label.Arrange(GetLabelBounds(bounds));
			Content.Arrange(GetContentBounds(bounds));
			return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}

		protected virtual Size GetContentAvailableSize(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size size = availableSize;
			SetLength(ref size, Math.Max(0.0, GetLength(size) - (GetLength(Label.DesiredSize) + ActualElementSpace)));
			return size;
		}

		protected virtual Size GetLabelAvailableSize(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size size = availableSize;
			SetLength(ref size, double.PositiveInfinity);
			return size;
		}

		protected virtual Size GetDesiredSize()
		{
			Size size = default(Size);
			SetLength(ref size, GetLength(Label.DesiredSize) + ActualElementSpace + GetLength(Content.DesiredSize));
			SetWidth(ref size, Math.Max(GetWidth(Label.DesiredSize), GetWidth(Content.DesiredSize)));
			return size;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetContentBounds(Rect bounds)
		{
			Rect rect = bounds;
			double num = GetLength(Label.DesiredSize) + ActualElementSpace;
			SetOffset(ref rect, num);
			SetLength(ref rect, Math.Max(0.0, GetLength(rect) - num));
			return rect;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetLabelBounds(Rect bounds)
		{
			Rect rect = bounds;
			SetLength(ref rect, GetLength(Label.DesiredSize));
			return rect;
		}

		protected double GetLength(Size size)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return size.Height;
			}
			return size.Width;
		}

		protected double GetLength(Rect rect)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return rect.Height;
			}
			return rect.Width;
		}

		protected double GetWidth(Size size)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return size.Width;
			}
			return size.Height;
		}

		protected void SetOffset(ref Rect rect, double value)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				rect.X = value;
			}
			else
			{
				rect.Y = value;
			}
		}

		protected void SetLength(ref Size size, double value)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				size.Width = value;
			}
			else
			{
				size.Height = value;
			}
		}

		protected void SetLength(ref Rect rect, double value)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				rect.Width = value;
			}
			else
			{
				rect.Height = value;
			}
		}

		protected void SetWidth(ref Size size, double value)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				size.Height = value;
			}
			else
			{
				size.Width = value;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementSpaceChanged()
		{
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelPositionChanged()
		{
			Orientation = ((LabelPosition != LayoutItemLabelPosition.Left) ? Orientation.Vertical : Orientation.Horizontal);
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}
	}

		public ScrollBox()
		{
			About.CheckLicenseShowNagScreen(typeof(ScrollBox));
		}

		protected override void OnAttachedPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty property, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((PanelBase)this).OnAttachedPropertyChanged(child, property, oldValue, newValue);
			if (property == LeftProperty || property == TopProperty)
			{
				((PanelBase)this).Changed();
			}
		}
	}
	public class ScrollBoxLayoutProvider : LayoutProviderBase
	{
		public ScrollBoxLayoutProvider(ILayoutControlBase control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		public override void UpdateChildBounds(FrameworkElement child, ref Rect bounds)
		{
			base.UpdateChildBounds(child, ref bounds);
			RectHelper.IncLeft(ref bounds, 0.0 - ScrollBox.GetLeft(child));
			RectHelper.IncTop(ref bounds, 0.0 - ScrollBox.GetTop(child));
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(FrameworkElements items, Size maxSize)
		{
			maxSize = SizeHelper.Infinite;
			Size result = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				item.Measure(maxSize);
				Size desiredSize = item.DesiredSize;
				desiredSize.Width += ScrollBox.GetLeft(item);
				desiredSize.Height += ScrollBox.GetTop(item);
				SizeHelper.UpdateMaxSize(ref result, UIElementExtensions.GetRoundedSize(desiredSize));
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)
			{
				Rect finalRect = RectHelper.New(item.DesiredSize);
				finalRect.X = bounds.Left + ScrollBox.GetLeft(item);
				finalRect.Y = bounds.Top + ScrollBox.GetTop(item);
				item.Arrange(finalRect);
			}
			return base.OnArrange(items, bounds, viewPortBounds);
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutControlItemsSourceWrapper : List<object>, INotifyCollectionChanged, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public event NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler CollectionChanged;

		public TileLayoutControlItemsSourceWrapper(IEnumerable enumerable)
			: base(enumerable.Cast<object>())
		{
			if (enumerable is INotifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				CollectionChangedEventManager.AddListener((INotifyCollectionChanged)enumerable, this);
			}
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (managerType == typeof(CollectionChangedEventManager))
			{
				OnSourceCollectionChanged(sender as IEnumerable, (NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs)e);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnSourceCollectionChanged(IEnumerable sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e2 = e;
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				AddRange(e.NewItems.Cast<object>());
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				Clear();
				if (sender != null)
				{
					AddRange(sender.Cast<object>());
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				if (e.OldItems.Count > 0)
				{
					int num3 = IndexOf(e.OldItems[0]);
					RemoveRange(num3, e.OldItems.Count);
					e2 = new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(e.Action, e.OldItems, num3);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				if (e.OldItems.Count > 0)
				{
					int num2 = IndexOf(e.OldItems[0]);
					RemoveAt(num2);
					Insert(e.NewStartingIndex, e.OldItems[0]);
					e2 = new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(e.Action, e.OldItems, e.NewStartingIndex, num2);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				if (e.OldItems.Count > 0)
				{
					int num = IndexOf(e.OldItems[0]);
					base[num] = e.NewItems[0];
					e2 = new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(e.Action, e.NewItems, e.OldItems, num);
				}
				break;
			}
			RaiseCollectionChanged(e2);
		}

		private void RaiseCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.CollectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.CollectionChanged(this, e);
			}
		}
	}
	public enum TileSize
	{
		ExtraSmall,
		Small,
		Large,
		ExtraLarge
	}

	}
