		protected Storyboard ContentChangeStoryboard { get; private set; }

		protected bool IsContentChangeStoryboardActive { get; private set; }

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				List<object> list = new List<object>();
				IEnumerator logicalChildren = ((HeaderedContentControlBase)this).LogicalChildren;
				if (logicalChildren != null)
				{
					while (logicalChildren.MoveNext())
					{
						list.Add(logicalChildren.Current);
					}
				}
				if (((ContentControlBase)this).Content != null && !list.Contains(((ContentControlBase)this).Content))
				{
					list.Add(((ContentControlBase)this).Content);
				}
				foreach (object item in _contentSource)
				{
					if (!list.Contains(item))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				return list.GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		private DispatcherTimer ContentChangeTimer { get; set; }

		private IEnumerator ContentEnumerator { get; set; }

		private bool IsContentChangeActive => ContentEnumerator != null;

		public event EventHandler Click;

		public event EventHandler IsMaximizedChanged;

		static Tile()
		{
			CalculatedBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CalculatedBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(Tile), null);
			CalculatedHeaderVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CalculatedHeaderVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(Tile), null);
			PreviousContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PreviousContent", typeof(object), typeof(Tile), null);
			AnimateContentChangeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnimateContentChange", typeof(bool), typeof(Tile), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			CommandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Command", typeof(ICommand), typeof(Tile), null);
			CommandParameterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CommandParameter", typeof(object), typeof(Tile), null);
			ContentChangeIntervalProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ContentChangeInterval", typeof(TimeSpan), typeof(Tile), new PropertyMetadata(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5.0), delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((Tile)(object)o).OnContentChangeIntervalChanged();
			}));
			ContentSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ContentSource", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(Tile), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((Tile)(object)o).OnContentSourceChanged((IEnumerable)e.OldValue, (IEnumerable)e.NewValue);
			}));
			HorizontalHeaderAlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HorizontalHeaderAlignment", typeof(HorizontalAlignment), typeof(Tile), null);
			IsMaximizedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsMaximized", typeof(bool), typeof(Tile), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				Tile tile = (Tile)(object)o;
				if (!tile._IsChangingIsMaximized)
				{
					tile._IsChangingIsMaximized = true;
					if ((bool)e.NewValue && !(((FrameworkElement)(object)tile).Parent is IMaximizingContainer))
					{
						o.SetValue(e.Property, e.OldValue);
					}
					else
					{
						tile.OnIsMaximizedChanged();
					}
					tile._IsChangingIsMaximized = false;
				}
			}));
			SizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Size", typeof(TileSize), typeof(Tile), new PropertyMetadata(TileSize.Large, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((Tile)(object)o).OnSizeChanged();
			}));
			VerticalHeaderAlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VerticalHeaderAlignment", typeof(VerticalAlignment), typeof(Tile), null);
			BackgroundListener = ControlBase.RegisterPropertyListener("Background");
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<Tile>();
		}

		public Tile()
		{
			CalculateHeaderVisibility();
			((ControlBase)this).AttachPropertyListener("Background", BackgroundListener, (object)null);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			ContentChangeStoryboard = ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).GetTemplateChild("ContentChangeStoryboard") as Storyboard;
		}

		protected override Size OnMeasure(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (IsMaximized)
			{
				return ((ControlBase)this).OnMeasure(availableSize);
			}
			Size size = default(Size);
			switch (Size)
			{
			case TileSize.ExtraSmall:
			{
				double width = (size.Height = 70.0);
				size.Width = width;
				break;
			}
			case TileSize.Small:
				size.Width = 150.0;
				size.Height = 150.0;
				break;
			case TileSize.Large:
				size.Width = 310.0;
				size.Height = 150.0;
				break;
			case TileSize.ExtraLarge:
			{
				double width = (size.Height = 310.0);
				size.Width = width;
				break;
			}
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Width))
			{
				size.Width = Math.Max(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Width, ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).MinWidth);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Height))
			{
				size.Height = Math.Max(((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Height, ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).MinHeight);
			}
			((ControlBase)this).OnMeasure(size);
			return size;
		}

		protected override ControlControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (ControlControllerBase)(object)new TileController((IControl)(object)this);
		}

		protected virtual Color CalculateGradientEndColor(Color startColor)
		{
			return Color.FromArgb(startColor.A, CalculateGradientEndColorChannel(startColor.R), CalculateGradientEndColorChannel(startColor.G), CalculateGradientEndColorChannel(startColor.B));
		}

		protected virtual byte CalculateGradientEndColorChannel(byte startColorChannel)
		{
			if (startColorChannel < 8 || startColorChannel > 247)
			{
				return startColorChannel;
			}
			int num = ((startColorChannel >= 128) ? (63 - startColorChannel / 4) : (startColorChannel / 4 - 1));
			return (byte)(startColorChannel + num);
		}

		protected void CalculateHeaderVisibility()
		{
			((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(CalculatedHeaderVisibilityProperty, (object)((!IsHeaderVisible()) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible));
		}

		protected virtual bool CanAnimateContentChange()
		{
			if (AnimateContentChange && !IsMaximized)
			{
				if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)(object)this))
				{
					return IsContentChangeActive;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual Brush GetCalculatedBackground()
		{
			if (!(((Control)(object)this).Background is SolidColorBrush { Color: var color }))
			{
				return null;
			}
			Color color2 = CalculateGradientEndColor(color);
			if (color2 == color)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new LinearGradientBrush(new GradientStopCollection
			{
				new GradientStop
				{
					Offset = 0.0,
					Color = color
				},
				new GradientStop
				{
					Offset = 1.0,
					Color = color2
				}
			}, 0.0);
		}

		protected virtual bool IsHeaderVisible()
		{
			return Size != TileSize.ExtraSmall;
		}

		protected virtual void OnBackgroundChanged()
		{
			UpdateCalculatedBackground();
		}

		protected virtual void OnClick()
		{
			if (this.Click != null)
			{
				this.Click(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			if (Command != null && Command.CanExecute(CommandParameter))
			{
				Command.Execute(CommandParameter);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((ContentControlBase)this).OnContentChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			PreviousContent = oldValue;
			if (CanAnimateContentChange() && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).IsLoaded && FrameworkElementExtensions.IsInVisualTree((FrameworkElement)(object)this) && ContentChangeStoryboard != null)
			{
				if (IsContentChangeStoryboardActive)
				{
					ContentChangeStoryboard.SkipToFill();
				}
				ContentChangeStoryboard.Begin();
				IsContentChangeStoryboardActive = true;
			}
			if (oldValue != null && !_contentSource.Contains(oldValue))
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)this).RemoveLogicalChild(oldValue);
			}
			if (newValue != null && !_contentSource.Contains(newValue))
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)this).AddLogicalChild(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentChangeIntervalChanged()
		{
			UpdateContentChangeInterval();
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentSourceChanged(IEnumerable oldValue, IEnumerable newValue)
		{
			INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged = oldValue as INotifyCollectionChanged;
			INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged2 = newValue as INotifyCollectionChanged;
			if (notifyCollectionChanged != null)
			{
				CollectionChangedEventManager.RemoveListener(notifyCollectionChanged, this);
			}
			if (notifyCollectionChanged2 != null)
			{
				CollectionChangedEventManager.AddListener(notifyCollectionChanged2, this);
			}
			SyncCollectionHelper.PopulateCore(_contentSource, newValue as ICollection, ConvertItemAction, null, ClearItemAction);
			if (ContentSource != null)
			{
				StartContentChange();
			}
			else
			{
				StopContentChange();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsMaximizedChanged()
		{
			if (!IsMaximized && ((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is IMaximizingContainer && (object)((IMaximizingContainer)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).MaximizedElement == this)
			{
				((IMaximizingContainer)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).MaximizedElement = null;
			}
			base.IsMaximizedCore = IsMaximized;
			if (this.IsMaximizedChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsMaximizedChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			if (IsMaximized)
			{
				((IMaximizingContainer)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).MaximizedElement = (FrameworkElement)(object)this;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLoaded()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnLoaded();
			UpdateCalculatedBackground();
			UpdateContentChangeState();
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyProperty propertyListener, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnPropertyChanged(propertyListener, oldValue, newValue);
			if (propertyListener == BackgroundListener)
			{
				OnBackgroundChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSizeChanged()
		{
			CalculateHeaderVisibility();
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateMeasure();
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is ITileLayoutControl)
			{
				((ITileLayoutControl)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).OnTileSizeChanged(this);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUnloaded()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).OnUnloaded();
			UpdateContentChangeState();
		}

		protected void UpdateCalculatedBackground()
		{
			CalculatedBackground = GetCalculatedBackground();
		}

		private void OnContentSourceCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(ContentSource is ICollection))
			{
				return;
			}
			SyncCollectionHelper.SyncCollection(e, _contentSource, ContentSource as IList, ConvertItemAction, null, null, ClearItemAction);
			if (_contentSource.Contains(((ContentControlBase)this).Content))
			{
				ContentEnumerator = _contentSource.GetEnumerator();
				while (ContentEnumerator.MoveNext() && ContentEnumerator.Current != ((ContentControlBase)this).Content)
				{
				}
			}
			else
			{
				StartContentChange();
			}
		}

		private object ConvertItemAction(object item)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).AddLogicalChild(item);
			return item;
		}

		private void ClearItemAction(object item)
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).RemoveLogicalChild(item);
		}

		private void ChangeContent()
		{
			if (!ContentEnumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				ContentEnumerator.Reset();
				if (!ContentEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					((ContentControlBase)this).Content = null;
					return;
				}
			}
			((ContentControlBase)this).Content = ContentEnumerator.Current;
		}

		private void StartContentChange()
		{
			if (IsContentChangeActive)
			{
				StopContentChange();
			}
			ContentEnumerator = ContentSource.GetEnumerator();
			ChangeContent();
			ContentChangeTimer = new DispatcherTimer();
			ContentChangeTimer.Interval = ContentChangeInterval;
			ContentChangeTimer.Tick += delegate
			{
				ChangeContent();
			};
			UpdateContentChangeState();
		}

		private void StopContentChange()
		{
			if (IsContentChangeActive)
			{
				ContentChangeTimer.Stop();
				ContentChangeTimer = null;
				ContentEnumerator = null;
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).ClearValue(ContentControlBase.ContentProperty);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateContentChangeInterval()
		{
			if (IsContentChangeActive)
			{
				ContentChangeTimer.Interval = ContentChangeInterval;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateContentChangeState()
		{
			if (IsContentChangeActive)
			{
				if (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).IsLoaded)
				{
					ContentChangeTimer.Start();
				}
				else
				{
					ContentChangeTimer.Stop();
				}
			}
		}

		void ITile.Click()
		{
			OnClick();
			if (((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent is ITileLayoutControl)
			{
				((ITileLayoutControl)((FrameworkElement)(object)this).Parent).TileClick(this);
			}
		}

		void IMaximizableElement.AfterNormalization()
		{
			IsMaximized = false;
		}

		void IMaximizableElement.BeforeMaximization()
		{
			IsMaximized = true;
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new TileAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected internal virtual void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(XmlReader xml)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, SizeProperty, "Size", typeof(TileSize));
		}

		protected internal virtual void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)(object)this, xml, SizeProperty, "Size");
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (managerType == typeof(CollectionChangedEventManager))
			{
				OnContentSourceCollectionChanged((NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs)e);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class TileController : ControlControllerBase
	{
		public ITile ITile => ((ControlControllerBase)this).Control as ITile;

		public TileController(IControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		internal void InvokeClick()
		{
			OnClick();
		}

		private void OnClick()
		{
			ITile.Click();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			bool isMouseLeftButtonDown = ((Controller)this).IsMouseLeftButtonDown;
			((Controller)this).OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			if (isMouseLeftButtonDown)
			{
				OnClick();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TileClickEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public Tile Tile { get; private set; }

		public TileClickEventArgs(Tile tile)
		{
			Tile = tile;
		}
	}
	public interface ITileLayoutModel : IFlowLayoutModel, ILayoutModelBase

	}
	public interface ITileLayoutControl : IFlowLayoutControl, ILayoutControlBase, IScrollControl, IPanel, IControl, ILayoutModelBase, IFlowLayoutModel, ITileLayoutModel
	{
		void OnTileSizeChanged(ITile tile);

		void StopGroupHeaderEditing();

		void TileClick(ITile tile);
	}
	[StyleTypedProperty(Property = "GroupHeaderStyle", StyleTargetType = typeof(TileGroupHeader))]
	[LicenseProvider(typeof(DX_WPF_LicenseProvider))]
	[DXToolboxBrowsable]
	public class TileLayoutControl : FlowLayoutControl, ITileLayoutControl, IFlowLayoutControl, ILayoutControlBase, IScrollControl, IPanel, IControl, ILayoutModelBase, IFlowLayoutModel, ITileLayoutModel
	{
		public static readonly Brush DefaultBackground;

		public const double DefaultGroupHeaderSpace = 11.0;

		public new const double DefaultItemSpace = 10.0;

		public new const double DefaultLayerSpace = 70.0;

		public new static readonly Thickness DefaultPadding;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowGroupHeaderEditingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupHeaderProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupHeaderSpaceProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupHeaderStyleProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupHeaderTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TileClickCommandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowGroupHeadersProperty;

		[Description("Gets or sets whether end-users are allowed to edit group headers. This is a dependency property.")]
		public bool AllowGroupHeaderEditing
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(AllowGroupHeaderEditingProperty);
			}
			set

				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(AllowGroupHeaderEditingProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the vertical distance between tile group headers and tiles.\nThis is a dependency property.")]
		public double GroupHeaderSpace
		{
			get

				return (double)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(GroupHeaderSpaceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(GroupHeaderSpaceProperty, (object)value);

		}

		[Description("Gets or sets a style applied to tile group headers.\nThis is a dependency property.")]

		[Description("Gets or sets the command to invoke when a tile is clicked. This is a dependency property.")]

		public bool ShowGroupHeaders
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(ShowGroupHeadersProperty);
			}
			set

			}
		}

		protected TileGroupHeaders GroupHeaders { get; private set; }

		public event EventHandler<TileClickEventArgs> TileClick;

		public static object GetGroupHeader(UIElement element)
		{
			return element.GetValue(GroupHeaderProperty);
		}

		public static void SetGroupHeader(UIElement element, object value)
		{
			element.SetValue(GroupHeaderProperty, value);
		}

		static TileLayoutControl()
		{
			DefaultBackground = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Transparent);
			DefaultPadding = new Thickness(120.0, 110.0, 120.0, 110.0);
			AllowGroupHeaderEditingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowGroupHeaderEditing", typeof(bool), typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((TileLayoutControl)(object)o).OnAllowGroupHeaderEditingChanged();
			}));
			GroupHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("GroupHeader", typeof(object), typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata((PropertyChangedCallback)PanelBase.OnAttachedPropertyChanged));
			GroupHeaderSpaceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupHeaderSpace", typeof(double), typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(11.0, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((TileLayoutControl)(object)o).OnGroupHeaderSpaceChanged();
			}));
			GroupHeaderStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupHeaderStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ElementPool<TileGroupHeader>)((TileLayoutControl)(object)o).GroupHeaders).ItemStyle = (Style)e.NewValue;
			}));
			GroupHeaderTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupHeaderTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((TileLayoutControl)(object)o).GroupHeaders.ItemContentTemplate = (DataTemplate)e.NewValue;
			}));
			TileClickCommandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TileClickCommand", typeof(ICommand), typeof(TileLayoutControl), null);
			ShowGroupHeadersProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowGroupHeaders", typeof(bool), typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((TileLayoutControl)(object)o).OnShowGroupHeadersChanged();
			}));
			LayoutControlStrategyRegistrator.RegisterTileLayoutControlStrategy();
			FlowLayoutControl.AllowAddFlowBreaksDuringItemMovingProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			FlowLayoutControl.AllowItemMovingProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			FlowLayoutControl.AnimateItemMovingProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			Panel.BackgroundProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DefaultBackground));
			LayoutControlBase.ItemSpaceProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(10.0));
			FlowLayoutControl.LayerSpaceProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(70.0));
			LayoutControlBase.PaddingProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TileLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(DefaultPadding));
		}

		public TileLayoutControl()
		{
			GroupHeaders = new TileGroupHeaders((Panel)(object)this);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement CreateItem()
		{
			return (FrameworkElement)(object)new Tile();
		}

		protected override void InitItem(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			item.SetBinding(ContentControlBase.ContentProperty, new Binding());
		}

		protected override bool IsTempChild(UIElement child)
		{
			if (!base.IsTempChild(child))
			{
				return ((ElementPool<TileGroupHeader>)GroupHeaders).IsItem(child);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(Rect bounds)
		{
			((ElementPool<TileGroupHeader>)GroupHeaders).MarkItemsAsUnused();
			Size result = base.OnArrange(bounds);
			((ElementPool<TileGroupHeader>)GroupHeaders).DeleteUnusedItems();
			return result;
		}

		protected override LayoutProviderBase CreateLayoutProvider()
		{
			return new TileLayoutProvider(this);
		}

		protected override LayoutParametersBase CreateLayoutProviderParameters()
		{
			return new TileLayoutParameters(base.ItemSpace, base.LayerSpace, GroupHeaderSpace, base.BreakFlowToFit, GroupHeaders);
		}

		protected override void ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlReader xml)
		{
			base.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.ReadPropertyFromXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, GroupHeaderProperty, "GroupHeader", typeof(object));
			(element as Tile).Do(delegate(Tile x)
			{
				x.ReadCustomizablePropertiesFromXML(xml);
			});
		}

		protected override void WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(FrameworkElement element, XmlWriter xml)
		{
			base.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(element, xml);
			DependencyObjectExtensions.WritePropertyToXML((DependencyObject)element, xml, GroupHeaderProperty, "GroupHeader");
			(element as Tile).Do(delegate(Tile x)
			{
				x.WriteCustomizablePropertiesToXML(xml);
			});
		}

		protected override PanelControllerBase CreateController()
		{
			return (PanelControllerBase)(object)new TileLayoutController(this);
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowGroupHeaderEditingChanged()
		{
			GroupHeaders.AreEditable = AllowGroupHeaderEditing;
		}

		protected override void OnAttachedPropertyChanged(FrameworkElement child, DependencyProperty property, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			base.OnAttachedPropertyChanged(child, property, oldValue, newValue);
			if (property == GroupHeaderProperty)
			{
				OnGroupHeaderChanged(child);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupHeaderChanged(FrameworkElement child)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupHeaderSpaceChanged()
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowGroupHeadersChanged()
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateArrange();
		}

		protected virtual void OnTileClick(Tile tile)
		{
			if (this.TileClick != null)
			{
				this.TileClick(this, new TileClickEventArgs(tile));
			}
			if (TileClickCommand != null && TileClickCommand.CanExecute(tile))
			{
				TileClickCommand.Execute(tile);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e2 = e;
			if (e.NewValue != null && !(e.NewValue is IList))
			{
				TileLayoutControlItemsSourceWrapper newValue = new TileLayoutControlItemsSourceWrapper((IEnumerable)e.NewValue);
				e2 = new DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs(e.Property, e.OldValue, newValue);
			}
			base.OnItemsSourceChanged(e2);
		}

		void ITileLayoutControl.OnTileSizeChanged(ITile tile)
		{
			((UIElement)(object)this).InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		void ITileLayoutControl.StopGroupHeaderEditing()
		{
			GroupHeaders.StopEditing();
		}

		void ITileLayoutControl.TileClick(ITile tile)
		{
			if (tile is Tile)
			{
				OnTileClick((Tile)tile);
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return (AutomationPeer)(object)new TileLayoutControlAutomationPeer(this);
		}
	}
	public enum TileGroupHeaderState
	{
		NonEditable,
		Editable,
		Editing
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "EditorElement", Type = typeof(TextBox))]
	[TemplateVisualState(GroupName = "EditingStates", Name = "NonEditable")]
	[TemplateVisualState(GroupName = "EditingStates", Name = "Editable")]
	[TemplateVisualState(GroupName = "EditingStates", Name = "Editing")]
	public class TileGroupHeader : ContentControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty StateProperty;

		private const string EditorElementName = "EditorElement";

		private const string EditingStates = "EditingStates";

		private const string NonEditableState = "NonEditable";

		private const string EditableState = "Editable";

		private const string EditingState = "Editing";

		public TileGroupHeaderState State
		{
			get
			{
				return (TileGroupHeaderState)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(StateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(StateProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		protected TextBox EditorElement { get; private set; }

		protected override bool IsContentInLogicalTree => false;

		static TileGroupHeader()
		{
			StateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("State", typeof(TileGroupHeaderState), typeof(TileGroupHeader), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((TileGroupHeader)(object)o).OnStateChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<TileGroupHeader>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (EditorElement != null)
			{
				EditorElement.GotFocus -= OnEditorElementGotFocus;
				EditorElement.KeyDown -= OnEditorElementKeyDown;
				EditorElement.LostFocus -= OnEditorElementLostFocus;
			}
			((ControlBase)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			EditorElement = ((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("EditorElement") as TextBox;
			if (EditorElement != null)
			{
				EditorElement.GotFocus += OnEditorElementGotFocus;
				EditorElement.KeyDown += OnEditorElementKeyDown;
				EditorElement.LostFocus += OnEditorElementLostFocus;
			}
			UpdateEditor();
		}

		private string GetStateName(TileGroupHeaderState state)
		{
			return state switch
			{
				TileGroupHeaderState.NonEditable => "NonEditable", 
				TileGroupHeaderState.Editable => "Editable", 
				TileGroupHeaderState.Editing => "Editing", 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		private void OnEditorElementGotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)this).IsLoaded)
			{
				State = TileGroupHeaderState.Editing;
			}
			else
			{
				((UIElement)this).Focus();
			}
		}

		private void OnEditorElementKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return || e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				StopEditing(e.Key == Key.Return);
			}
		}

		private void OnEditorElementLostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			StopEditing(accept: true);
		}

		internal void StopEditing(bool accept)
		{
			if (State == TileGroupHeaderState.Editing)
			{
				if (accept)
				{
					((ContentControlBase)this).Content = ((EditorElement.Text == "") ? null : EditorElement.Text);
				}
				State = TileGroupHeaderState.Editable;
				EditorElement.Select(0, 0);
				DependencyObject focusScope = FocusManager.GetFocusScope((DependencyObject)(object)this);
				object focusedElement = FocusManager.GetFocusedElement(focusScope);
				if (focusedElement == EditorElement)
				{
					FocusManager.SetFocusedElement(focusScope, null);
					Keyboard.ClearFocus();
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateEditor()
		{
			if (EditorElement != null && State != TileGroupHeaderState.NonEditable)
			{
				string text = ((ContentControlBase)this).Content as string;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					EditorElement.Text = ((State == TileGroupHeaderState.Editable) ? LocalizationRes.TileLayoutControl_GroupHeader_Empty : "");
				}
				else
				{
					EditorElement.Text = text;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			((ContentControlBase)this).OnContentChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			UpdateEditor();
		}

		protected virtual void OnStateChanged()
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(false);
			UpdateEditor();
		}

		protected override void UpdateState(bool useTransitions)
		{
			((ControlBase)this).UpdateState(useTransitions);
			((ControlBase)this).GoToState(GetStateName(State), useTransitions);
		}
	}
	public class TileGroupHeaders : ElementPool<TileGroupHeader>
	{
		private bool _AreEditable;

		private DataTemplate _ItemContentTemplate;

		public bool AreEditable
		{
			get
			{
				return _AreEditable;
			}
			set
			{
				if (AreEditable != value)
				{
					StopEditing();
					_AreEditable = value;
					base.Items.ForEach(UpdateItemState);
				}
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate ItemContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return _ItemContentTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ItemContentTemplate != value)
				{
					_ItemContentTemplate = value;
					OnItemContentTemplateChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public TileGroupHeaders(Panel container)
			: base(container)
		{
		}

		public void StopEditing()
		{
			if (!AreEditable)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (TileGroupHeader item in base.Items)
			{
				item.StopEditing(accept: true);
			}
		}

		protected override TileGroupHeader CreateItem()
		{
			TileGroupHeader tileGroupHeader = base.CreateItem();
			if (ItemContentTemplate != null)
			{
				UpdateItemContentTemplate(tileGroupHeader);
			}
			UpdateItemState(tileGroupHeader);
			return tileGroupHeader;
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemContentTemplateChanged()
		{
			base.Items.ForEach(UpdateItemContentTemplate);
		}

		private void UpdateItemContentTemplate(TileGroupHeader item)
		{
			DependencyObjectExtensions.SetValueIfNotDefault((DependencyObject)(object)item, ContentControlBase.ContentTemplateProperty, (object)ItemContentTemplate);
		}

		private void UpdateItemState(TileGroupHeader item)
		{
			item.State = (AreEditable ? TileGroupHeaderState.Editable : TileGroupHeaderState.NonEditable);
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutParameters : FlowLayoutParameters
	{
		public TileGroupHeaders GroupHeaders { get; private set; }

		public double GroupHeaderSpace { get; private set; }

		public TileLayoutParameters(double itemSpace, double layerSpace, double groupHeaderSpace, bool breakFlowToFit, TileGroupHeaders groupHeaders)
			: base(itemSpace, layerSpace, breakFlowToFit, stretchContent: false, showLayerSeparators: false, null)
		{
			GroupHeaders = groupHeaders;
			GroupHeaderSpace = groupHeaderSpace;
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutProvider : FlowLayoutProvider
	{
		public virtual bool ShowGroupHeaders
		{
			get
			{
				if (Model.MaximizedElement == null)
				{
					return Model.ShowGroupHeaders;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected new TileLayoutParameters Parameters => base.Parameters as TileLayoutParameters;

		protected new ITileLayoutModel Model => (ITileLayoutModel)base.Model;

		public TileLayoutProvider(IFlowLayoutModel model)
			: base(model)
		{
		}

		public override double GetLayerSpace(bool isHardFlowBreak)
		{
			if (!isHardFlowBreak)
			{
				return Parameters.ItemSpace;
			}
			return base.GetLayerSpace(isHardFlowBreak);
		}

		protected override Size OnArrange(FrameworkElements items, Rect bounds, Rect viewPortBounds)
		{
			Size result = base.OnArrange(items, bounds, viewPortBounds);
			if (ShowGroupHeaders)
			{
				AddGroupHeaders();
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void CalculateLayoutForSlotWithMultipleItems(FrameworkElements items, ref int itemIndex, FlowLayoutItemPosition slotPosition, ref FlowLayoutItemSize slotSize, MeasureItem measureItem, ArrangeItem arrangeItem)
		{
			List<int> itemPositionsInSlot = GetItemPositionsInSlot(items, itemIndex);
			FlowLayoutItemPosition flowLayoutItemPosition = slotPosition;
			FlowLayoutItemSize flowLayoutItemSize = slotSize;
			FlowLayoutItemPosition flowLayoutItemPosition2 = slotPosition;
			FlowLayoutItemSize flowLayoutItemSize2 = slotSize;
			for (int i = itemIndex; i < itemIndex + itemPositionsInSlot.Count; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement item = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i];
				FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize = ((i == itemIndex) ? flowLayoutItemSize2 : GetItemSize(measureItem(item)));
				FlowLayoutItemPosition itemPosition = flowLayoutItemPosition2;
				switch (itemPositionsInSlot[i - itemIndex])
				{
				case 1:
				case 3:
				case 5:
				case 7:
					itemPosition.LayerOffset += flowLayoutItemSize2.Width + Parameters.ItemSpace;
					break;
				case 2:
				case 6:
					itemPosition.LayerOffset = flowLayoutItemPosition.LayerOffset;
					itemPosition.ItemOffset += flowLayoutItemSize2.Length + Parameters.ItemSpace;
					break;
				case 4:
					flowLayoutItemPosition.LayerOffset += GetHalfSlotMaxWidth(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[itemIndex], slotSize) + Parameters.ItemSpace;
					itemPosition = flowLayoutItemPosition;
					slotSize.Length = flowLayoutItemSize.Length;
					flowLayoutItemSize = itemSize;
					break;
				}
				flowLayoutItemSize.Width = Math.Max(flowLayoutItemSize.Width, itemPosition.LayerOffset + itemSize.Width - flowLayoutItemPosition.LayerOffset);
				flowLayoutItemSize.Length = Math.Max(flowLayoutItemSize.Length, itemPosition.ItemOffset + itemSize.Length - flowLayoutItemPosition.ItemOffset);
				arrangeItem?.Invoke(item, ref itemPosition, ref itemSize);
				flowLayoutItemPosition2 = itemPosition;
				flowLayoutItemSize2 = itemSize;
			}
			itemIndex += itemPositionsInSlot.Count - 1;
			slotSize.Width = flowLayoutItemPosition.LayerOffset + flowLayoutItemSize.Width - slotPosition.LayerOffset;
			slotSize.Length = Math.Max(slotSize.Length, flowLayoutItemSize.Length);
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetHalfSlotBounds(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex, bool isFirstHalf)
		{
			Rect slotBounds = GetSlotBounds(layerInfo, slotIndex);
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.LayoutItems)[layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex]];
			double halfSlotMaxWidth = GetHalfSlotMaxWidth(frameworkElement, GetItemSize(FrameworkElementExtensions.GetSize(frameworkElement)));
			if (isFirstHalf)
			{
				slotBounds.Width = Math.Min(slotBounds.Width, halfSlotMaxWidth);
			}
			else
			{
				RectHelper.IncLeft(ref slotBounds, halfSlotMaxWidth + Parameters.ItemSpace);
			}
			return slotBounds;
		}

		protected virtual void GetHalfSlotFirstAndLastItemIndexes(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex, bool isFirstHalf, out int firstItemIndex, out int lastItemIndex)
		{
			int num = layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex];
			List<int> itemPositionsInSlot = GetItemPositionsInSlot(base.LayoutItems, num);
			firstItemIndex = num;
			lastItemIndex = num + itemPositionsInSlot.Count - 1;
			for (int i = 0; i < itemPositionsInSlot.Count; i++)
			{
				if (itemPositionsInSlot[i] == 4)
				{
					if (isFirstHalf)
					{
						lastItemIndex = num + i - 1;
					}
					else
					{
						firstItemIndex = num + i;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual int GetHalfSlotItemPlaceIndex(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex, bool isFirstHalf, FrameworkElement item, Rect bounds, Point p, out bool isBeforeItemPlace)
		{
			Rect halfSlotBounds = GetHalfSlotBounds(layerInfo, slotIndex, isFirstHalf);
			if (!GetElementHitTestBounds(halfSlotBounds, bounds, slotIndex == 0, slotIndex == layerInfo.SlotCount - 1).Contains(p))
			{
				isBeforeItemPlace = false;
				return -1;
			}
			if (NeedsHalfSlot(item))
			{
				isBeforeItemPlace = GetLayerOffset(p) < GetLayerCenter(halfSlotBounds);
			}
			else
			{
				isBeforeItemPlace = false;
			}
			GetHalfSlotFirstAndLastItemIndexes(layerInfo, slotIndex, isFirstHalf, out var firstItemIndex, out var lastItemIndex);
			if (!isBeforeItemPlace)
			{
				return lastItemIndex;
			}
			return firstItemIndex;
		}

		protected virtual double GetHalfSlotMaxWidth(FrameworkElement item, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			if (((ITile)item).Size == TileSize.ExtraSmall)
			{
				return 2.0 * itemSize.Width + Parameters.ItemSpace;
			}
			return itemSize.Width;
		}

		protected virtual List<int> GetItemPositionsInSlot(FrameworkElements items, int slotFirstItemIndex)
		{
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			int num = 0;
			list.Add(num);
			for (int i = slotFirstItemIndex + 1; i < ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items).Count && !NeedsFullSlot(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]) && !FlowLayoutControl.GetIsFlowBreak(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]); i++)
			{
				int num2 = ((((ITile)((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i - 1]).Size == TileSize.Small) ? (num + 4) : ((((ITile)((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[i]).Size != TileSize.Small) ? (num + 1) : (num - num % 4 + 4)));
				if (num2 >= 8)
				{
					break;
				}
				list.Add(num2);
				num = num2;
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override int GetSlotItemPlaceIndex(FlowLayoutLayerInfo layerInfo, int slotIndex, FrameworkElement item, Rect bounds, Point p, out bool isBeforeItemPlace)
		{
			int slotItemPlaceIndex = base.GetSlotItemPlaceIndex(layerInfo, slotIndex, item, bounds, p, out isBeforeItemPlace);
			if (slotItemPlaceIndex != -1)
			{
				return slotItemPlaceIndex;
			}
			if (!NeedsHalfSlot(item))
			{
				for (int i = layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex]; i <= GetSlotLastItemIndex(layerInfo, slotIndex); i++)
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.LayoutItems)[i];
					Rect bounds2 = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(frameworkElement);
					bool flag = slotIndex == layerInfo.SlotCount - 1;
					if (flag && !NeedsHalfSlot(frameworkElement))
					{
						List<int> itemPositionsInSlot = GetItemPositionsInSlot(base.LayoutItems, layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex]);
						int num = itemPositionsInSlot[i - layerInfo.SlotFirstItemIndexes[slotIndex]];
						flag = num == 2 || num == 3 || num == 6 || num == 7;
					}
					if (GetElementHitTestBounds(bounds2, bounds, slotIndex == 0, flag).Contains(p))
					{
						isBeforeItemPlace = GetLayerOffset(p) < GetLayerCenter(bounds2);
						return i;
					}
				}
			}
			slotItemPlaceIndex = GetHalfSlotItemPlaceIndex(layerInfo, slotIndex, isFirstHalf: true, item, bounds, p, out isBeforeItemPlace);
			if (slotItemPlaceIndex == -1)
			{
				slotItemPlaceIndex = GetHalfSlotItemPlaceIndex(layerInfo, slotIndex, isFirstHalf: false, item, bounds, p, out isBeforeItemPlace);
			}
			return slotItemPlaceIndex;
		}

		protected override double GetSlotLength(FrameworkElements items, int itemIndex, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			double slotLength = base.GetSlotLength(items, itemIndex, itemSize);
			double slotMaxLength = GetSlotMaxLength(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)items)[itemIndex], itemSize);
			if (slotLength < slotMaxLength)
			{
				foreach (int item in GetItemPositionsInSlot(items, itemIndex))
				{
					if (item > 1)
					{
						return slotMaxLength;
					}
				}
			}
			return slotLength;
		}

		protected override double GetSlotMaxLength(FrameworkElement item, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			if (!NeedsFullSlot(item) && ((ITile)item).Size == TileSize.ExtraSmall)
			{
				return 2.0 * itemSize.Length + Parameters.ItemSpace;
			}
			return base.GetSlotMaxLength(item, itemSize);
		}

		protected override double GetSlotMaxWidth(FrameworkElement item, FlowLayoutItemSize itemSize)
		{
			if (NeedsFullSlot(item))
			{
				return base.GetSlotMaxWidth(item, itemSize);
			}
			return 2.0 * GetHalfSlotMaxWidth(item, itemSize) + Parameters.ItemSpace;
		}

		protected override bool NeedsFullSlot(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			ITile tile = item as ITile;
			if (item != Model.MaximizedElement)
			{
				if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical && tile != null)
				{
					if (tile.Size != TileSize.ExtraSmall)
					{
						return tile.Size != TileSize.Small;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual bool NeedsHalfSlot(FrameworkElement item)
		{
			if (item is ITile)
			{
				return ((ITile)item).Size == TileSize.Small;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected void AddGroupHeader(object headerSource, Rect headerAreaBounds)
		{
			TileGroupHeader tileGroupHeader = ((ElementPool<TileGroupHeader>)Parameters.GroupHeaders).Add();
			((FrameworkElement)(object)tileGroupHeader).SetBinding(ContentControlBase.ContentProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding
			{
				Source = headerSource,
				Path = new PropertyPath(TileLayoutControl.GroupHeaderProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
			});
			UIElementExtensions.InvalidateParentsOfModifiedChildren((UIElement)(object)tileGroupHeader);
			((UIElement)(object)tileGroupHeader).Measure(RectHelper.Size(headerAreaBounds));
			((UIElement)(object)tileGroupHeader).Arrange(GetGroupHeaderBounds(headerAreaBounds, ((UIElement)(object)tileGroupHeader).DesiredSize));
		}

		protected virtual void AddGroupHeaders()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.LayerInfos.Count; i++)
			{
				FlowLayoutLayerInfo flowLayoutLayerInfo = base.LayerInfos[i];
				if (flowLayoutLayerInfo.IsHardFlowBreak)
				{
					AddGroupHeader(((Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)base.LayoutItems)[flowLayoutLayerInfo.FirstItemIndex], GetGroupHeaderAreaBounds(i));
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetGroupBounds(int groupFirstLayerInfoIndex)
		{
			Rect empty = Rect.Empty;
			for (int i = groupFirstLayerInfoIndex; i < base.LayerInfos.Count; i++)
			{
				FlowLayoutLayerInfo flowLayoutLayerInfo = base.LayerInfos[i];
				if (i > groupFirstLayerInfoIndex && flowLayoutLayerInfo.IsHardFlowBreak)
				{
					break;
				}
				empty.Union(GetLayerBounds(flowLayoutLayerInfo));
			}
			return empty;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetGroupHeaderAreaBounds(int groupFirstLayerInfoIndex)
		{
			Rect groupBounds = GetGroupBounds(groupFirstLayerInfoIndex);
			groupBounds.Y -= Parameters.GroupHeaderSpace;
			groupBounds.Height = double.PositiveInfinity;
			return groupBounds;
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetGroupHeaderBounds(Rect headerAreaBounds, Size headerSize)
		{
			Rect result = headerAreaBounds;
			result.Y -= headerSize.Height;
			result.Height = headerSize.Height;
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutController : FlowLayoutController
	{
		public new ITileLayoutControl ILayoutControl => base.ILayoutControl as ITileLayoutControl;

		public TileLayoutController(IFlowLayoutControl control)
			: base(control)
		{
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(DXMouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			bool flag = ((Controller)this).IsMouseLeftButtonDown && !((Controller)this).IsDragAndDrop;
			((Controller)this).OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			if (flag)
			{
				ILayoutControl.StopGroupHeaderEditing();
			}
		}

		protected override DragAndDropController CreateItemDragAndDropControler(Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
		{
			return (DragAndDropController)(object)new TileLayoutItemDragAndDropController((Controller)(object)this, startDragPoint, dragControl);
		}
	}
	public class TileLayoutItemDragAndDropController : FlowLayoutItemDragAndDropController
	{
		private class TilePlaceHolder : Canvas, ITile
		{
			public TileSize Size { get; private set; }

			public TilePlaceHolder(ITile tile)
			{
				Size = tile.Size;
			}

			void ITile.Click()
			{
			}
		}

		protected new TileLayoutController Controller => (TileLayoutController)base.Controller;

		private Dictionary<UIElement, object> GroupHeaderOriginalValues { get; set; }

		public TileLayoutItemDragAndDropController(Controller controller, Point startDragPoint, FrameworkElement dragControl)
			: base(controller, startDragPoint, dragControl)
		{
		}

		public override void EndDragAndDrop(bool accept)
		{
			if (accept)
			{
				TileLayoutControl.SetGroupHeader(base.DragControl, TileLayoutControl.GetGroupHeader(base.DragControlPlaceHolder));
			}
			else
			{
				RestoreGroupHeaderOriginalValues();
			}
			base.EndDragAndDrop(accept);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement CreateDragControlPlaceHolder()
		{
			if (base.DragControl is ITile)
			{
				return new TilePlaceHolder((ITile)base.DragControl);
			}
			return base.CreateDragControlPlaceHolder();
		}

		protected override Point GetItemPlacePoint(Point p)
		{
			Point point = PointHelper.Subtract(base.StartDragRelativePoint, new Point(0.5, 0.5));
			Point point2 = PointHelper.Multiply(SizeHelper.ToPoint(base.DragImageSize), point);
			return PointHelper.Subtract(p, point2);
		}

		protected override void InitDragControlPlaceHolder()
		{
			Controller.ILayoutControl.StopGroupHeaderEditing();
			base.InitDragControlPlaceHolder();
			TileLayoutControl.SetGroupHeader(base.DragControlPlaceHolder, TileLayoutControl.GetGroupHeader(base.DragControl));
		}

		protected override void OnGroupFirstItemChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			base.OnGroupFirstItemChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			MoveGroupHeaderAndStoreOriginalValues(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		protected void MoveGroupHeaderAndStoreOriginalValues(UIElement from, UIElement to)
		{
			if (GroupHeaderOriginalValues == null)
			{
				GroupHeaderOriginalValues = new Dictionary<UIElement, object>();
			}
			object groupHeader = TileLayoutControl.GetGroupHeader(from);
			if (!GroupHeaderOriginalValues.ContainsKey(from))
			{
				GroupHeaderOriginalValues.Add(from, groupHeader);
			}
			TileLayoutControl.SetGroupHeader(from, DependencyProperty.UnsetValue);
			if (to != null)
			{
				if (!GroupHeaderOriginalValues.ContainsKey(to))
				{
					GroupHeaderOriginalValues.Add(to, TileLayoutControl.GetGroupHeader(to));
				}
				TileLayoutControl.SetGroupHeader(to, groupHeader);
			}
		}

		protected void RestoreGroupHeaderOriginalValues()
		{
			if (GroupHeaderOriginalValues == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<UIElement, object> groupHeaderOriginalValue in GroupHeaderOriginalValues)
			{
				TileLayoutControl.SetGroupHeader(groupHeaderOriginalValue.Key, groupHeaderOriginalValue.Value);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ElementBoundsAnimation
	{
		protected class ElementBounds : Dictionary<FrameworkElement, Rect>
		{
		}

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		protected static readonly DependencyProperty ClipRectWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ClipRectWidth", typeof(double), typeof(ElementBoundsAnimation), new PropertyMetadata(OnClipRectSizeChanged));

		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		protected static readonly DependencyProperty ClipRectHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ClipRectHeight", typeof(double), typeof(ElementBoundsAnimation), new PropertyMetadata(OnClipRectSizeChanged));

		public bool IsActive => Storyboard != null;

		protected FrameworkElements Elements { get; private set; }

		protected ElementBounds NewElementBounds { get; private set; }

		protected ElementBounds OldElementBounds { get; private set; }

		protected Storyboard Storyboard { get; private set; }

		protected Action StoryboardCompleted { get; private set; }

		private static void OnClipRectSizeChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!DependencyObjectExtensions.HasDefaultValue(o, e.Property) && o is UIElement { Clip: RectangleGeometry { Rect: var rect } clip })
			{
				if (e.Property == ClipRectWidthProperty)
				{
					rect.Width = (double)e.NewValue;
				}
				else
				{
					rect.Height = (double)e.NewValue;
				}
				clip.Rect = rect;
			}
		}

		public ElementBoundsAnimation(FrameworkElements elements)
		{
			Elements = elements;
		}

		public bool Begin(TimeSpan duration, IEasingFunction easingFunction = null, Action onCompleted = null)
		{
			if (IsActive)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Storyboard = new Storyboard();
			foreach (FrameworkElement key in OldElementBounds.Keys)
			{
				Rect rect = OldElementBounds[key];
				Rect rect2 = NewElementBounds[key];
				AnimateElementPosition(Storyboard, key, RectHelper.Location(rect), RectHelper.Location(rect2));
				AnimateElementSize(Storyboard, key, RectHelper.Size(rect), RectHelper.Size(rect2));
			}
			if (Storyboard.Children.Count == 0)
			{
				Storyboard = null;
				if (onCompleted != null)
				{
					onCompleted();
				}
				return false;
			}
			foreach (DoubleAnimation child in Storyboard.Children)
			{
				child.Duration = duration;
				child.EasingFunction = easingFunction;
			}
			StoryboardCompleted = delegate
			{
				Storyboard.Stop();
				foreach (FrameworkElement key2 in OldElementBounds.Keys)
				{
					if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)key2, UIElement.RenderTransformProperty))
					{
						key2.ClearValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty);
					}
					if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsPropertyAssigned((DependencyObject)key2, UIElement.ClipProperty))
					{
						key2.ClearValue(UIElement.ClipProperty);
					}
				}
				StoryboardCompleted = null;
				Storyboard = null;
				if (onCompleted != null)
				{
					onCompleted();
				}
			};
			Storyboard.Completed += delegate
			{
				if (StoryboardCompleted != null)
				{
					StoryboardCompleted();
				}
			};
			Storyboard.Begin();
			return true;
		}

		public void Stop()
		{
			if (IsActive)
			{
				Storyboard.SkipToFill();
				StoryboardCompleted();
			}
		}

		public void StoreNewElementBounds(FrameworkElement relativeTo = null)
		{
			NewElementBounds = GetElementBounds(relativeTo);
		}

		public void StoreOldElementBounds(FrameworkElement relativeTo = null)
		{
			OldElementBounds = GetElementBounds(relativeTo);
		}

		protected void AnimateElementPosition(Storyboard storyboard, FrameworkElement element, Point oldPosition, Point newPosition)
		{
			if (!(newPosition == oldPosition))
			{
				element.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform();
				storyboard.Children.Add(CreateDoubleAnimation(element, "(RenderTransform).X", oldPosition.X - newPosition.X, 0.0));
				storyboard.Children.Add(CreateDoubleAnimation(element, "(RenderTransform).Y", oldPosition.Y - newPosition.Y, 0.0));
			}
		}

		protected void AnimateElementSize(Storyboard storyboard, FrameworkElement element, Size oldSize, Size newSize)
		{
			if (!(newSize == oldSize) && !(newSize.Width < oldSize.Width) && !(newSize.Height < oldSize.Height))
			{
				element.Clip = new RectangleGeometry
				{
					Rect = SizeHelper.ToRect(oldSize)
				};
				storyboard.Children.Add(CreateDoubleAnimation(element, ClipRectWidthProperty, oldSize.Width, newSize.Width));
				storyboard.Children.Add(CreateDoubleAnimation(element, ClipRectHeightProperty, oldSize.Height, newSize.Height));
			}
		}

		protected DoubleAnimation CreateDoubleAnimation(FrameworkElement element, object property, double from, double to)
		{
			DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation();
			Storyboard.SetTarget(doubleAnimation, element);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, (property is string) ? new PropertyPath((string)property) : new PropertyPath(property));
			doubleAnimation.From = from;
			doubleAnimation.To = to;
			return doubleAnimation;
		}

		protected ElementBounds GetElementBounds(FrameworkElement relativeTo = null)
		{
			ElementBounds elementBounds = new ElementBounds();
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in (Collection<FrameworkElement>)(object)Elements)
			{
				elementBounds.Add(item, (relativeTo == null) ? FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(item) : FrameworkElementExtensions.GetBounds(item, relativeTo));
			}
			return elementBounds;
		}
	}
	public class Resources
	{
		private static LocalizationRes _Strings = new LocalizationRes();

		public LocalizationRes Strings => _Strings;
	}
	internal static class FrameworkElementExtensions
	{
		public static INameScope FindScope(DependencyObject d)
		{
			DependencyObject scopeOwner;
			return FindScope(d, out scopeOwner);
		}

		public static INameScope FindScope(DependencyObject d, out DependencyObject scopeOwner)
		{
			while (d != null)
			{
				INameScope nameScope = NameScope.GetNameScope(d);
				if (nameScope != null)
				{
					scopeOwner = d;
					return nameScope;
				}
				d = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(d);
			}
			scopeOwner = null;
			return null;
		}

		public static bool HasNameScope(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return FindScope(d) != null;
		}
	}
	public class CustomizationToolbarButtonBehavior : Behavior<ButtonBase>
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolbarProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Toolbar", typeof(LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar), typeof(CustomizationToolbarButtonBehavior), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnToolbarChanged(d, e);
		}));

		public LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar Toolbar
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(ToolbarProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(ToolbarProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnToolbarChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			CustomizationToolbarButtonBehavior customizationToolbarButtonBehavior = d as CustomizationToolbarButtonBehavior;
			LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar layoutItemCustomizationToolbar = e.NewValue as LayoutItemCustomizationToolbar;
			if (customizationToolbarButtonBehavior != null && ((Behavior<ButtonBase>)customizationToolbarButtonBehavior).AssociatedObject != null && layoutItemCustomizationToolbar != null)
			{
				((Behavior<ButtonBase>)customizationToolbarButtonBehavior).AssociatedObject.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown += layoutItemCustomizationToolbar.OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown;
				((Behavior<ButtonBase>)customizationToolbarButtonBehavior).AssociatedObject.PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp += layoutItemCustomizationToolbar.OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnAttached()
		{
			((AttachableObjectBase)this).OnAttached();
			if (Toolbar != null)
			{
				base.AssociatedObject.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown += Toolbar.OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown;
				base.AssociatedObject.PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp += Toolbar.OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnDetaching()
		{
			((AttachableObjectBase)this).OnDetaching();
			if (Toolbar != null)
			{
				base.AssociatedObject.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown -= Toolbar.OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown;
				base.AssociatedObject.PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp -= Toolbar.OnButtonPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp;
			}
		}
	}
	internal enum DXMidpointRounding
	{
		ToEven = 0,
		AwayFromZero = 1,
		ToPositiveInfinity = 4
	}
	internal static class DpiUtils
	{
		public static double RoundToDpi(this double value, DXMidpointRounding midpointRounding = DXMidpointRounding.ToEven)
		{
			if (double.IsInfinity(value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			double scaleX = ScreenHelper.ScaleX;
			double value2 = value * scaleX;
			double num = Math.Round(value2, 5);
			double num2 = Math.Floor(num);
			switch (midpointRounding)
			{
			case DXMidpointRounding.ToEven:
			case DXMidpointRounding.AwayFromZero:
				return (num2 + Math.Round(num - num2, (MidpointRounding)midpointRounding)) / scaleX;
			case DXMidpointRounding.ToPositiveInfinity:
				return (num2 + Math.Ceiling(num - num2)) / scaleX;
			default:
				return (num2 + Math.Round(num - num2, MidpointRounding.ToEven)) / scaleX;
			}
		}

		public static Size RoundToDpi(this Size size, DXMidpointRounding midpointRounding = DXMidpointRounding.ToEven)
		{
			return new Size(size.Width.RoundToDpi(midpointRounding), size.Height.RoundToDpi(midpointRounding));
		}
	}

namespace DevExpress.Xpf.LayoutControl.UIAutomation
{
	public abstract class BaseLayoutAutomationPeer : BaseNavigationAutomationPeer
	{
		protected delegate bool IteratorCallback(AutomationPeer peer);

		protected delegate bool FilterCallback(object obj);

		protected static bool Iterate(DependencyObject parent, IteratorCallback callback, FilterCallback filterCallback)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (parent != null)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = null;
				int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent);
				for (int i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						break;
					}
					DependencyObject child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, i);
					flag = ((filterCallback(child) && (automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)child)) != null) ? callback(automationPeer) : ((child == null || !(child is UIElement3D) || (automationPeer = UIElement3DAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement3D)child)) == null) ? Iterate(child, callback, filterCallback) : callback(automationPeer)));
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected BaseLayoutAutomationPeer(FrameworkElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected sealed override string GetNameCore()
		{
			string nameImpl = GetNameImpl();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(nameImpl))
			{
				return nameImpl;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)this).Owner.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected virtual string GetNameImpl()
		{
			string nameCore = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).GetNameCore();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nameCore))
			{
				bool flag = default(bool);
				object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag);
				if (flag)
				{
					return (string)obj;
				}
			}
			return nameCore;
		}

		protected sealed override string GetAutomationIdCore()
		{
			string automationIdImpl = GetAutomationIdImpl();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(automationIdImpl))
			{
				return automationIdImpl;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)this).Owner.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected virtual string GetAutomationIdImpl()
		{
			string automationIdCore = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).GetAutomationIdCore();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(automationIdCore))
			{
				bool flag = default(bool);
				object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.AutomationIdProperty, ref flag);
				if (flag)
				{
					return (string)obj;
				}
			}
			return automationIdCore;
		}
	}
	public abstract class BaseLayoutAutomationPeer<T> : BaseLayoutAutomationPeer where T : FrameworkElement
	{
		public T Owner => (T)((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner;

		protected BaseLayoutAutomationPeer(T owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}
	}
	public abstract class LayoutControlBaseAutomationPeer<T> : BaseLayoutAutomationPeer<T> where T : LayoutControlBase
	{
		protected virtual bool IncludeInternalElements => false;

		protected LayoutControlBaseAutomationPeer(T owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Group;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return ((object)base.Owner).GetType().Name;
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> children = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			IteratorCallback callback = delegate(AutomationPeer peer)
			{
				children.Add(peer);
				return false;
			};
			FilterCallback filterCallback = (object obj) => obj != null && obj is UIElement;
			BaseLayoutAutomationPeer.Iterate((DependencyObject)(object)base.Owner, callback, filterCallback);
			return children;
		}
	}
	public abstract class ScrollablePanelAutomationPeer<T> : LayoutControlBaseAutomationPeer<T>, IScrollProvider where T : LayoutControlBase
	{
		private LayoutControllerBase Controller => base.Owner.Controller;

		double IScrollProvider.HorizontalScrollPercent => ((Controller)Controller).HorzScrollParams.RelativePosition * 100.0;

		double IScrollProvider.HorizontalViewSize => ((Controller)Controller).HorzScrollParams.RelativePageSize * 100.0;

		bool IScrollProvider.HorizontallyScrollable => ((Controller)Controller).HorzScrollParams.Enabled;

		private IScrollProvider IScrollProvider => this;

		double IScrollProvider.VerticalScrollPercent => ((Controller)Controller).VertScrollParams.RelativePosition * 100.0;

		double IScrollProvider.VerticalViewSize => ((Controller)Controller).VertScrollParams.RelativePageSize * 100.0;

		bool IScrollProvider.VerticallyScrollable => ((Controller)Controller).VertScrollParams.Enabled;

		protected ScrollablePanelAutomationPeer(T owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Scroll)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		void IScrollProvider.Scroll(ScrollAmount horizontalAmount, ScrollAmount verticalAmount)
		{
			if (!((AutomationPeer)(object)this).IsEnabled())
			{
				throw new ElementNotEnabledException();
			}
			bool flag = horizontalAmount != ScrollAmount.NoAmount;
			bool flag2 = verticalAmount != ScrollAmount.NoAmount;
			if ((flag && !IScrollProvider.HorizontallyScrollable) || (flag2 && !IScrollProvider.VerticallyScrollable))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("OperationCannotBePerformed");
			}
			double num = ((Controller)Controller).HorzScrollParams.PageSize;
			double num2 = ((Controller)Controller).VertScrollParams.PageSize;
			switch (horizontalAmount)
			{
			case ScrollAmount.LargeDecrement:
				num = 0.0 - num;
				break;
			case ScrollAmount.SmallDecrement:
				num = -16.0;
				break;
			case ScrollAmount.SmallIncrement:
				num = -16.0;
				break;
			case ScrollAmount.NoAmount:
				num = 0.0;
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException("OperationCannotBePerformed");
			case ScrollAmount.LargeIncrement:
				break;
			}
			switch (verticalAmount)
			{
			case ScrollAmount.LargeDecrement:
				num2 = 0.0 - num2;
				break;
			case ScrollAmount.SmallDecrement:
				num2 = -16.0;
				break;
			case ScrollAmount.SmallIncrement:
				num2 = -16.0;
				break;
			case ScrollAmount.NoAmount:
				num2 = 0.0;
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException("OperationCannotBePerformed");
			case ScrollAmount.LargeIncrement:
				break;
			}
			if (flag || flag2)
			{
				((ScrollControl)base.Owner).SetOffset(new Point(((ScrollControl)base.Owner).Offset.X + num, ((ScrollControl)base.Owner).Offset.Y + num2));
			}
		}

		void IScrollProvider.SetScrollPercent(double horizontalPercent, double verticalPercent)
		{
			ScrollParams horzScrollParams = ((Controller)Controller).HorzScrollParams;
			ScrollParams vertScrollParams = ((Controller)Controller).VertScrollParams;
			((ScrollControlController)Controller).Scroll(Orientation.Horizontal, (horzScrollParams.Max - horzScrollParams.PageSize) * horizontalPercent * 0.01, false, true);
			((ScrollControlController)Controller).Scroll(Orientation.Vertical, (vertScrollParams.Max - vertScrollParams.PageSize) * verticalPercent * 0.01, false, true);
		}
	}
	public class LayoutGroupAutomationPeer : LayoutControlBaseAutomationPeer<LayoutGroup>, IExpandCollapseProvider, ISelectionProvider
	{
		private bool IsCollapsible
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Owner.View == LayoutGroupView.GroupBox)
				{
					return base.Owner.IsCollapsible;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected override bool IncludeInternalElements
		{
			get
			{
				if (!base.IncludeInternalElements)
				{
					return base.Owner.View == LayoutGroupView.Tabs;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		ExpandCollapseState IExpandCollapseProvider.ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (!base.Owner.IsCollapsible || !base.Owner.IsActuallyCollapsed)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		bool ISelectionProvider.CanSelectMultiple => false;

		bool ISelectionProvider.IsSelectionRequired => true;

		public LayoutGroupAutomationPeer(LayoutGroup owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetNameImpl()
		{
			string nameImpl = base.GetNameImpl();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nameImpl))
			{
				if (base.Owner.Header == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return base.Owner.Header.ToString();
			}
			return nameImpl;
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdImpl()
		{
			string automationIdImpl = base.GetAutomationIdImpl();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(automationIdImpl))
			{
				if (base.Owner.Header == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return base.Owner.Header.ToString();
			}
			return automationIdImpl;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse && IsCollapsible)
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Selection && base.Owner.View == LayoutGroupView.Tabs)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> childrenCore = base.GetChildrenCore();
			if (base.Owner.View == LayoutGroupView.GroupBox)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = ((IEnumerable<FrameworkElement>)((PanelBase)base.Owner).GetChildren(true, false, true)).Where((FrameworkElement x) => x is GroupBox).FirstOrDefault();
				if (frameworkElement != null && LayoutHelper.FindElementByName(frameworkElement, "MinimizeElement") is GroupBoxButton element)
				{
					AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)(object)element);
					if (automationPeer != null)
					{
						childrenCore.Add(automationPeer);
					}
				}
			}
			return childrenCore;
		}

		void IExpandCollapseProvider.Collapse()
		{
			if (base.Owner.IsCollapsible)
			{
				base.Owner.IsCollapsed = true;
			}
		}

		void IExpandCollapseProvider.Expand()
		{
			if (base.Owner.IsCollapsible)
			{
				base.Owner.IsCollapsed = false;
			}
		}

		IRawElementProviderSimple[] ISelectionProvider.GetSelection()
		{
			AutomationPeer peer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(base.Owner.SelectedTabChild);
			return new IRawElementProviderSimple[1] { ((AutomationPeer)(object)this).ProviderFromPeer(peer) };
		}
	}

		}

		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Invoke)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		void IInvokeProvider.Invoke()
		{
			if (!((AutomationPeer)(object)this).IsEnabled())
			{
				throw new ElementNotEnabledException();
			}
			((DispatcherObject)(object)base.Owner).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				base.Owner.Controller.InvokeClick();
			}, DispatcherPriority.Input);
		}
	}

	public class GroupBoxAutomationPeer : BaseLayoutAutomationPeer<GroupBox>, IExpandCollapseProvider
	{
		private bool IsCollapsible => true;

		ExpandCollapseState IExpandCollapseProvider.ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Owner.State != GroupBoxState.Minimized)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public GroupBoxAutomationPeer(GroupBox owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetNameImpl()
		{
			string nameImpl = base.GetNameImpl();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nameImpl))
			{
				if (((HeaderedContentControlBase)base.Owner).Header == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return ((HeaderedContentControlBase)base.Owner).Header.ToString();
			}
			return nameImpl;
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdImpl()
		{
			string automationIdImpl = base.GetAutomationIdImpl();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(automationIdImpl))
			{
				if (((HeaderedContentControlBase)base.Owner).Header == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return ((HeaderedContentControlBase)base.Owner).Header.ToString();
			}
			return automationIdImpl;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse && IsCollapsible)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> children = null;
			IteratorCallback callback = delegate(AutomationPeer peer)
			{
				if (children == null)
				{
					children = new List<AutomationPeer>();
				}
				children.Add(peer);
				return false;
			};
			FilterCallback filterCallback = (object obj) => obj != null && obj is UIElement && !(obj is LayoutGroup) && ((UIElement)obj).IsVisible;
			BaseLayoutAutomationPeer.Iterate((DependencyObject)(object)base.Owner, callback, filterCallback);
			return children;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Group;
		}

		void IExpandCollapseProvider.Collapse()
		{
			base.Owner.State = GroupBoxState.Minimized;
		}

		void IExpandCollapseProvider.Expand()
		{
			base.Owner.State = GroupBoxState.Normal;
		}
	}
